### [1] [How Vision Transformers Represent Position: A Mechanistic Comparison of Absolute and Rotary Positional Encodings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13992)
*Mahmoud Mannes,Nikhil Maturi,Parva Mehta,Jiwon Jeong,Aravind Singaram Kannappan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过机制分析揭示ViT中AP E与RoPE编码空间位置的不同内部机制：APE以显式特征存储位置，RoPE在注意力中构建并在被扣留时重建位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对位置编码如何塑造ViT内部表征知之甚少，需要理解不同位置嵌入选择对模型空间信息组织方式的影响。

Method: 结合稀疏自编码器(S A E)、因果干预与消融实验，分析AP E与RoPE模型中行/列空间信息的编码与重建机制，并检验其因果重要性。

Result: AP E模型可通过SAE恢复因果性的行、列特定特征；RoPE存在分别编码行与列的旋转分量，其行/列信息可被独立移除并在旋转恢复后部分重建。置换APE位置特征使top-1准确率下降24-31点；消融RoPE主要重建头的旋转，降幅大于其他任何头。

Conclusion: 位置编码的选择从根本上改变了ViT内部空间信息的组织与处理方式；机制分析可揭示模型如何构建、重建与维持结构化表征，为视觉模型的定向干预提供基础。

Abstract: Positional embeddings encode the spatial position of each image patch in a Vision Transformer (ViT), allowing the model to distinguish different spatial arrangements of visual content. However, we know little about how the choice of embedding shapes a vision transformer's internal representation. In this work, we show that Absolute Positional Embeddings (APE) and Rotary Positional Embeddings (RoPE) encode spatial position through different internal mechanisms: APE stores position in explicit features, while RoPE builds it inside attention and rebuilds it when it is withheld. Specifically, in APE models, sparse autoencoders recover causal row- and column-specific features, whereas interventions on RoPE reveal distinct rotary components that separately encode row and column information. Additionally, causal interventions selectively disabling RoPE's row or column rotations show that decodable row and column information can be independently removed and, once rotations are restored, partly reconstructed, in part by axis-specialized attention heads. Both mechanisms are causally important for classification: permuting APE's positional features across patches reduces top-1 accuracy by 24--31 points, and ablating the rotations of RoPE's primary rebuilding head reduces it more than ablating those of any other head. Our findings show that the choice of positional encoding fundamentally changes how spatial information is organized and processed inside Vision Transformers. More broadly, our work demonstrates how mechanistic analysis can reveal how models construct, reconstruct, and maintain structured representations, providing a basis for targeted interventions in vision models.

</details>


### [2] [SharedSAE: a unified sparse dictionary across u-net blocks and denoising time for diffusion models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13997)
*Longhao Lei,Lan Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SharedSAE，一个跨U-Net注意力块与去噪时间步共享的稀疏字典，实现扩散模型统一的概念定位与干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型中概念表示随网络深度和去噪时间漂移，而现有稀疏自编码器仅提供逐块或逐时间步字典，缺乏统一字典，无法进行网络级定位与干预。

Method: 学习一个跨SDXL 1.0四个U-Net注意力块和全部50个去噪步共享的稀疏字典；用块特定的低秩输入适配器对齐各块残差分布；用时间条件模块（共享时间步分支+块-时间步校正分支）吸收时间非平稳性。

Result: 保持字典结构连贯；局部特征抑制使I2P检测器阳性子集中NudeNet阳性生成从139降至1；追踪稀疏特征发现概念表示随生成在U-Net块间重分布，随去噪推进从最早下采样块向中间和较晚块迁移。

Conclusion: SharedSAE为扩散模型提供统一、可解释、可干预的表示空间，支持任意覆盖块和去噪步的概念分析与擦除。

Abstract: Concept representations in diffusion models drift across network depth and denoising time, yet existing sparse autoencoders offer only per-block or per-timestep dictionaries, leaving no single feature dictionary for localization and intervention in the network. We present Shared Sparse Autoencoder (SharedSAE), which learns one sparse dictionary shared across four U-Net attention blocks and all 50 denoising steps of SDXL 1.0. A block-specific low-rank input adapter aligns the residual distribution of each block before the shared encoder, and a temporal conditioning module with a shared timestep branch and a block-timestep correction branch absorbs temporal non-stationarity. Despite spanning both axes, Shared SAE preserves a coherent dictionary structure. Built on the shared dictionary, localized feature suppression reduces the number of NudeNet-positive generations in the I2P detector-positive subset from 139 to 1. Tracing sparse features across the trajectory further reveals that concept representations are redistributed across U-Net blocks during generation, shifting from the earliest downsampling block toward intermediate and later blocks as denoising proceeds. SharedSAE thus provides a unified, interpretable, and intervention-ready representation space for diffusion models, enabling concept analysis and erasure at any covered block and denoising step.

</details>


### [3] [Pristine Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14091)
*Anirudh Sundara Rajan,Krishna Kumar Singh,Richard Zhang,Yong Jae Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Pristine Models，一种在预训练阶段将安全与不安全概念知识分隔到独立参数分支的文本到图像生成架构，推理时可断开不安全分支。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练数据过滤保证安全但损失质量，因为含不安全概念的样本也提供有用知识；现有事后概念遗忘方法随概念增多会导致灾难性遗忘。

Method: 设计一种注意力机制，鼓励将安全和不安全知识分离到独立参数化的分支中，分支参数在训练时学习，推理时可断开不安全分支。

Result: 在ImageNet上实现更安全的生成，同时保留大部分全数据预训练的效用，图像质量和文本对齐优于仅用安全数据训练或事后概念移除；在CC12M上验证其有效性。

Conclusion: 不安全概念知识可在预训练阶段被分隔，从而在推理时禁用访问，兼顾安全性与生成质量，并可扩展至更大规模开放世界预训练。

Abstract: Training on broad, unfiltered data enables high-quality image generation, but can also teach models concepts that should not be generated; restricting the training data ensures safety, but reduces quality because examples containing unsafe concepts also provide useful knowledge that is not itself unsafe. Existing approaches largely address this trade-off post hoc through concept unlearning, which can lead to catastrophic forgetting as the number of concepts to remove grows. We instead ask whether unsafe concept knowledge can be compartmentalized during pretraining so that access to it can be disabled at inference. We introduce Pristine Models, a novel text-to-image generation architecture with an attention mechanism that encourages the separation of safe and unsafe knowledge into independently parameterized branches. The branch-specific parameters are learned during training, while the unsafe branch can be detached at inference. On ImageNet, Pristine Models enable safer generation while retaining much of the utility of full-data pretraining, achieving higher image quality and better text alignment than training only on safe data or applying post-hoc concept removal. We further scale our approach to CC12M, demonstrating that it remains effective in a substantially larger and more diverse open-world pretraining setting.

</details>


### [4] [Human Pose Estimation by Probabilistic Mixtures of Gaussian and Uniform Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14093)
*Santiago Reina Alguacil,Ezequiel López Rubio*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需重训练的后处理连续概率框架，将热图建模为GMM加均匀分布，提升HPE的精度、不确定性与3D扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代人体姿态估计依赖从离散热图中确定性提取坐标，在密集遮挡和分布外歧义下系统性地失败。

Method: 事后拦截原始热图，建模为高斯混合模型（GMM）加空间无关均匀分布，无需架构修改或重训练；用空间期望做坐标提取，测试时增强聚合概率分布而非离散激活，并借助多模态GMM参数化与MRF等几何正则器结合，扩展到多视角3D。

Result: GMM空间期望精度与领先亚像素解码器持平；概率聚合缓解多尺度热图污染；几何协方差体积在严重遮挡OoD检测上优于峰值强度启发式；自适应Softmax融合使AUSE降低或持平；可扩展至多视角3D并绕过O(N^3)体素内存瓶颈。

Conclusion: 连续密度建模为HPE提供统一的事后框架，改善精度、不确定性和3D扩展性，同时揭示2D运动先验在透视缩短与视觉退化下的局限。

Abstract: Despite advancements in sub-pixel decoding, modern Human Pose Estimation (HPE) architectures remain constrained by deterministic coordinate extraction from discrete heatmaps, which systematically fails under dense occlusions and Out-of-Distribution (OoD) ambiguities. To address these limitations, we propose a post-hoc continuous probabilistic framework that intercepts raw heatmaps and models them as a Gaussian Mixture Model (GMM) augmented with a spatially agnostic uniform distribution, requiring no architectural modifications or retraining. This continuous representation allows us to comprehensively explore the distinct contributions of density modeling to HPE. We show that the spatial expectation of our fitted GMM achieves precision parity with leading sub-pixel decoders without heuristic post-processing. Leveraging this continuous space, our test-time augmentation aggregates probability distributions rather than discrete activations, mitigating multi-scale heatmap poisoning. Furthermore, because our multimodal GMM parameterization isolates competing spatial hypotheses, it naturally interfaces with geometric regularizers such as Markov Random Fields. However, our analysis exposes the inherent vulnerabilities of 2D kinematic priors under perspective foreshortening and visual degradation. Crucially, this density formulation yields significant improvements in uncertainty quantification: our calibrated geometric covariance volume outperforms standard peak-intensity heuristics in OoD anomaly detection under severe occlusion. For in-distribution calibration, dynamically fusing our geometric metric via Adaptive Softmax reduces or matches baseline Area Under the Sparsification Error (AUSE) across benchmarks and degradation levels. Finally, we establish that this continuous parameterization is extensible to multi-view 3D architectures, bypassing the $\mathcal{O}(N^3)$ voxel memory bottleneck.

</details>


### [5] [Scenes as an Attack Surface: Uncovering Scene-Grounded Safety Failures in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14094)
*Tongxi Wu,Yuyang Cheng,Jiacheng Xian,Hu Huang,Zhihao Wu,Menglin Yang,Jian Zhang,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 现有MLLM越狱攻击多依赖特制视觉输入携带有害意图，本文揭示另一种漏洞：仅凭任务相关的场景语义即可使MLLM从拒答转为顺从，并提出SceneSafetyBench基准与SGA攻击方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数先前攻击依赖特别构造的视觉输入来承载或支撑有害意图。作者想探究：当视觉输入不引入额外攻击指令、原任务意图不变时，仅任务相关的场景语义是否足以让MLLM从拒答转向顺从。

Method: 将此类失败形式化为"场景锚定的视觉安全漏洞"，构建SceneSafetyBench基准，覆盖八类风险、九个白盒与黑盒MLLM进行评测；基于"任务相关视觉证据影响下游安全表征"的发现，提出Scene-Grounded Attacks (SGA)：在保持原任务不变的前提下构造经校验的场景锚定查询，并组合其独立回答。

Result: 在九个目标MLLM上，SGA平均攻击成功率(ASR)达86.17%，比最强基线高8.55个百分点，并在其中八个模型上取得最高ASR。

Conclusion: 场景语义是MLLM中被忽视的攻击面，凸显了更强视觉锚定能力与稳健多模态安全对齐之间日益紧张的关系。

Abstract: Existing jailbreak attacks have exposed serious safety weaknesses in multimodal large language models (MLLMs). Most prior attacks rely on specially constructed visual inputs to carry or support harmful intent. In this paper, we uncover a different vulnerability: task-relevant scene semantics alone can shift an MLLM from refusal to compliance, even when the visual input introduces no additional attack instructions and the original task intent remains unchanged. We formalize this failure as a scene-grounded visual safety vulnerability and introduce SceneSafetyBench to investigate it across eight risk categories and nine white- and black-box MLLMs. Our analysis reveals that this failure is associated with how task-relevant visual evidence influences downstream safety representations, suggesting that visual grounding alone cannot fully explain the observed safety shift. Building on this finding, we propose Scene-Grounded Attacks (SGA), which preserve the original task while constructing validated scene-grounded queries and combining their independent responses. Across nine target MLLMs, SGA achieves an average attack success rate (ASR) of 86.17%, outperforming the strongest baseline by 8.55 percentage points and achieving the highest ASR on eight models. Our results identify scene semantics as an overlooked attack surface in MLLMs and highlight a growing tension between stronger visual grounding and robust multimodal safety alignment.

</details>


### [6] [TrajCFG: Token-Wise Classifier-Free Guidance via Denoising Trajectory Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14153)
*Jingyu Liu,Hao Jiang,Yikang Ding,Huafeng Shi,Xu Yang,Xiaoqiang Liu,Guoxin Zhang,Pengfei Wan,Kun Gai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TrajCFG，一种无需训练的流匹配引导方法，依据历史方向变化对每个token自适应分配引导强度，在多种生成任务上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CFG对所有潜变量token使用相同引导尺度，但采样中它们的速度预测变化各异，均匀缩放并非最优。

Method: 基于引导速度方向的历史变化，将反馈映射为有界尺度：方向变化大则引导强，方向一致则引导弱；融合相邻步与区间归一化的长跨度反馈，复用标准CFG预测，无额外模型评估。

Result: 在文生视频、图生视频、文生音视频、文生图、图生3D等任务上均超过基线；文生视频中VBench总分较最强基线提升0.30-0.75分（三个骨干网络）。

Conclusion: 历史方向反馈是广泛适用的细粒度引导分配信号。

Abstract: Classifier-free guidance (CFG) typically applies a shared scale to all latent tokens, although their velocity predictions evolve differently during sampling. We introduce Trajectory-Adaptive Classifier-Free Guidance (TrajCFG), a training-free method for flow-matching models that adapts token-wise guidance using historical changes in guided velocity direction. TrajCFG maps this feedback to bounded scales, assigning stronger guidance to larger directional changes and weaker guidance to more consistent directions. It combines adjacent-step and interval-normalized longer-span feedback, reusing standard CFG predictions without additional model evaluations. Controlled diagnostics and ablations examine the roles of directional feedback, token-wise allocation, and history-span fusion. Experiments on text-to-video, image-to-video, text-to-audio-video, text-to-image, and image-to-3D generation show improvements over base samplers across multiple backbones. For text-to-video generation, TrajCFG improves VBench Total scores by 0.30-0.75 points over the strongest evaluated baseline on each of three backbones. These results support historical directional feedback as a broadly applicable signal for fine-grained guidance allocation. The project page is available at https://jyliu-98.github.io/trajcfg-project-page/.

</details>


### [7] [Video-Arena: A Scalable Agentic Video Benchmark for Spatiotemporal Capability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14158)
*Kuan Zhang,Yukun Chen,Zhihao Yang,Yue Su,Xiangnan Wu,Zirong Chen,Run Luo,Tao Tan,Yinhe Zheng,Yiming Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Video-Arena基准，将竞技游戏转为可扩展、可验证的视频世界状态理解测试，揭示现有模型在事件感知与状态跟踪上的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频基准难以判断模型能否持续理解演化的世界状态；用真实视频构建此类基准困难，因为世界状态部分可观测，标注难以获取。

Method: 受人类理解动态事件方式启发，将竞技游戏转化为基准，涵盖三类任务：感知关键事件、解读状态更新、跟踪结果状态；用游戏记录大规模生成视频及对齐参考标签；精选9款游戏共180个任务的黄金集，并在原生视觉理解与工具辅助的智能体分析两种协议下评测。

Result: 最优系统在两种协议下分别取得一定F1分数（原文数值缺失）；结果显示原生模型难以可靠感知事件，工具辅助系统能改善事件恢复，但在解读状态更新和持续跟踪状态上仍表现不佳。

Conclusion: Video-Arena为视频世界状态理解提供了可扩展、可验证的评测方式，指出当前模型在状态演化理解上的关键短板。

Abstract: Understanding and tracking a dynamic world are fundamental to visual intelligence. Existing video benchmarks provide limited insight into whether models can maintain a coherent understanding of evolving world state. Building such a benchmark from real-world videos is challenging because the underlying world state is partially observable, making annotations hard to obtain. To close this gap, we introduce Video-Arena, a benchmark that turns competitive games into scalable, verifiable tests of how well models understand evolving world states from videos. Specifically, inspired by how humans understand dynamic events, we evaluate spatiotemporal world state understanding and tracking through three task categories: perceiving critical events, interpreting state updates, and tracking the resulting states. Our pipeline can generate videos and aligned reference labels from game records at scale. To keep evaluation affordable and reproducible, we curate a golden set of 180 tasks across 9 games and run evaluations under two protocols: native visual understanding and tool-assisted agentic analysis. The best-performing systems achieve F1 scores of and under the two protocols, respectively. The result highlights that native models struggle to perceive events reliably, while tool-assisted systems improve event recovery but still struggle to interpret state updates and track the resulting states consistently over time. Our project page is in https://videoarena-bench.github.io

</details>


### [8] [Token-Budgeted Candidate Retrieval for 3D Scene-Grounded VLMs: Category Names vs. Pairwise Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14207)
*Michał Zakrzewski,Mariia Shpir,Taras Rumezhak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在固定上下文预算下，语义检索（类别名匹配/小编码器）在3D场景指代检索中优于手工几何配对评分，且上下文选择直接影响下游准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定token预算下，3D场景VLM应优先看到哪些对象和关系缺乏明确答案；需要评估候选检索与上下文选择策略对检索及下游任务的影响。

Method: 在ScanRefer gt_val及Nr3D/Sr3D的自定义20%划分（35,504查询）上，比较token预算候选检索：轴对齐框、token-ratio指标、无训练基线、两个小编码器（MiniLM、BGE-small，按类别名匹配）及QCRR（查询条件几何配对评分）；并用修正的3DGraphLLM框架测试上下文选择对下游精度的影响。

Result: 类别名匹配在各K均优于几何配对，高召回差距最大；ScanRefer上类别匹配R@10=0.864 vs QCRR 0.626；MiniLM/BGE-small达0.911–0.913（ScanRefer）、0.941–0.947（Nr3D）。3DGraphLLM中匹配对象预算下准确率：Random 0.44、QCRR 0.56、MiniLM 0.67、oracle 0.70。

Conclusion: 语义检索在检索和下游准确率上均胜过手工设计的几何方法；将发布协议、检索器实现和逐查询输出，便于替换新选择器。

Abstract: Under a fixed context budget, which objects and relations should a 3D scene-grounded VLM see first? On ScanNet indoor referring benchmarks (Dai et al., 2017), matching the query to object category names outperforms pairwise geometry scoring at every reported K. The gaps are largest at high recall. Analytic pairwise cues beat per-object cue scoring and query-agnostic kNN graphs, including on paraphrases. MiniLM and BGE-small still score higher on those splits. We evaluate token-budgeted candidate retrieval with axis-aligned boxes, a token-ratio metric, training-free baselines, two small encoders that match q to each object's category name, and QCRR (Query-Conditioned Relation Retrieval), a deterministic pairwise-geometry scorer. Across ScanRefer gt_val plus a custom 20% scan-id split of Nr3D and Sr3D (35,504 queries; not official ReferIt3D val), additive noun matching reaches R@10 0.864 on ScanRefer against QCRR's 0.626; MiniLM and BGE-small reach 0.911 to 0.913 on ScanRefer and 0.941 to 0.947 on Nr3D. A corrected 3DGraphLLM harness (native Acc@0.25 0.67 on the full graph, n=300 ScanRefer queries) shows that context selection changes downstream accuracy under a matched object budget (Random 0.44, QCRR 0.56, MiniLM 0.67, oracle 0.70). Semantic retrieval beats hand-designed geometry on that harness. We will release the protocol, retriever implementations, and per-query outputs so new selectors can be swapped in without rewriting the analysis.

</details>


### [9] [CAPTOR: a vision-language foundation model for Chest-Abdomen-Pelvis Oncologic CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14226)
*Séléna Jean-Mactoux,Sophie Loizillon,Ghina Jardali,Amandine Crombé,Nathalie Lassau,Alexandre Bône*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CAPTOR是一个用于肿瘤胸腹盆CT解读的对比式视觉-语言基础模型，在多中心数据预训练后于多个下游任务上超越现有CT基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肿瘤胸腹盆CT解读缺乏临床对齐的专用基础模型，需要验证在肿瘤学富集数据上预训练能否提升目标临床情境下的表现。

Method: 采用对比式视觉-语言预训练策略，在北美、欧洲和亚洲多中心回顾性肿瘤CAP CT数据集（单/多序列）上训练CAPTOR；在三项下游任务（疾病检测、生存预测、图像检索）上与最先进CT基础模型对比，分层评估并以bootstrap估计置信区间、置换检验做配对比较。

Result: 训练与测试分别使用25,512和10,794对CT-报告；TumorImagingBench各数据集含134-421项研究。CAPTOR在分类、检索上均优于所有基线，在四项肿瘤预后基准中三项排名第一（CAP子集AUC 0.767 vs 0.695，肿瘤子集AUC 0.784 vs 0.734，均p<0.001）。

Conclusion: 在肿瘤学富集的CAP CT数据上训练的基础模型在目标临床情境中优于现有最先进模型，凸显临床对齐预训练的重要性；已开源代码与模型权重。

Abstract: Purpose : To develop and evaluate CAPTOR, a deep-learning vision-language foundation model for oncologic chest-abdomen-pelvis (CAP) CT interpretation.
  Materials & Methods : CAPTOR (Chest-Abdomen-Pelvis foundaTion model for Oncologic Radiology) is a contrastive vision-language model, trained using a pretraining strategy for oncologic CAP CT (in single- or multiple-series acquisitions), on a multicenter retrospective dataset from North America, Europe and Asia, predominantly from tumor lesion examinations. CAPTOR was compared with state-of-the-art CT foundation models on three downstream tasks: disease detection, survival prediction using TumorImagingBench and image retrieval. Classification and retrieval were both evaluated on held-out internal and public test sets, stratified by anatomical coverage and indication. Bootstrap resampling was used to estimate confidence intervals, and pairwise comparisons were performed using permutation tests.
  Results : CAPTOR was trained and tested on 25,512 and 10,794 CT-report pairs respectively. TumorImagingBench datasets comprised between 134 and 421 studies each. CAPTOR outperformed all baseline models, on the classification task, on the CAP subset (AUC 0.767, 95%CI: 0.734-0.793, vs. 0.695, 95%CI: 0.645-0.736 for the next-best, p<0.001) and oncologic subset (AUC 0.784, 95%CI: 0.759-0.805, vs. 0.734, 95%CI: 0.703-0.767 p<0.001), ranked first on three of the four oncologic prognostic benchmarks, and achieved superior retrieval performance on CAP and oncologic subsets.
  Conclusion : A foundation model trained on oncology-enriched CAP CT data outperformed relevant state-of-the-art models in its target clinical context, underscoring the importance of clinically aligned pretraining.
  We release our preprocessing, training and inference code : https://github.com/Guerbet-AI/CAPTOR and model weights : https://huggingface.co/Guerbet-AI/CAPTOR

</details>


### [10] [Latent World Action Model for GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14228)
*Seungjun Yu,Hojun Choi,Seojeong Park,Jaeyo Shin,Hyunjung Shim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将GUI世界模型从像素空间重构改为潜在空间的"动作诱导未来信息"预测，用大模型共享表征加双头（动作选择+过渡建模）并结合动作感知对比目标缓解身份捷径问题，在离线/在线GUI任务上成功率提升最高31.9%/40.6%，推理延迟降低最高98.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有像素空间世界模型采用"先预测未来屏幕、再重新感知选择动作"的两阶段范式，存在两大问题：(L1) 像素重建计算成本高；(L2) 需重建大量与动作选择无关的外观细节。且屏幕生成目标易引发"身份捷径"问题，即预测状态基本保留当前状态而非捕捉动作诱导的变化。

Method: 将GUI世界建模的预测目标从全屏重构转为潜在空间中的动作诱导未来信息。使用大型多模态模型作为潜在世界模型，其共享表征支持两个专用头：动作头直接将潜在表征转为动作选择，过渡头捕捉动作条件下的未来信息。此外引入动作感知对比目标，促使过渡表征捕捉有判别性的动作诱导变化，以缓解身份捷径。

Result: 在离线与在线GUI设置下的大量实验表明，相比像素空间世界模型，成功率相对提升分别最高达31.9%和40.6%，推理延迟最多降低98.9%。

Conclusion: 通过在潜在空间建模动作诱导的未来信息而非重构像素屏幕，并借助双头设计与动作感知对比目标，可有效解决像素空间范式的计算成本与身份捷径问题，在提升GUI代理成功率的同时大幅降低推理延迟。

Abstract: Pixel-space world models have recently emerged as a promising approach for GUI agents, predicting future screens prior to execution and subsequently re-perceiving them for action selection. However, this two-stage paradigm faces two key challenges in GUI environments: (L1) the high computational cost of pixel reconstruction and (L2) the need to reconstruct large and diverse appearance-level details beyond what is required for action selection. In practice, this screen-generation objective can induce an identity shortcut problem, where future-state prediction largely preserves the current state rather than capturing action-induced changes. In this work, we reformulate GUI world modeling by shifting the prediction target from full-screen reconstruction to action-induced future information in latent space. Concretely, we leverage a large multimodal model as the latent world model, whose shared representations support two dedicated heads for both action selection and predictive transition modeling. The action head directly translates these latent representations into action selection, while the transition head captures action-conditioned future information. To mitigate the identity shortcut, we further introduce an action-aware contrastive objective that encourages the transition representations to capture discriminative action-induced changes. Extensive experiments across offline and online GUI settings show that our method achieves up to 31.9% and 40.6% relative gains in success rate over pixel-space world models, respectively, while substantially reducing inference latency by up to 98.9%. Our code is available at https://github.com/sjyu001/latent2world.

</details>


### [11] [SliderEdit++: Improved Slider Trajectories in Instruction-Based Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14253)
*Arman Zarei,Sayan Nag,Bryan Wang,Franck Dernoncourt,Varun Manjunatha,Nedim Lipka,Soheil Feizi,Samyadeep Basu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SlideBench基准与SliderEdit++方法，实现连续、平滑、解耦的滑块式图像编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于滑块的指令图像编辑无法提供可靠的连续可控性：滑动时出现突变和非单调变化，且多个滑块之间相互干扰，缺乏系统性评测手段。

Method: 提出SlideBench基准（覆盖多类编辑并含单调性、感知连续性、滑块解耦指标）；进而提出SliderEdit++，对中间编辑轨迹而非仅端点进行正则化，结合速度与语义空间轨迹正则化、频谱控制，并加入解耦目标以减少编辑间干扰。

Result: 在SlideBench上达到SOTA，综合得分较最强基线相对提升18.5%；采用少步编辑骨干，速度约为现有滑块方法的3至20倍。

Conclusion: 显式正则化编辑轨迹可显著提升滑块式编辑的连续可控性与解耦性，同时兼顾交互响应速度。

Abstract: Slider-based control aims to give users continuous, fine-grained control over the intensity of individual edits in instruction-based image editing. However, existing approaches fail to deliver reliable continuous controllability: traversing a slider can produce abrupt visual jumps and non-monotonic changes, while jointly controlled sliders can interfere with one another. To systematically study these limitations, we introduce SlideBench, a comprehensive benchmark for continuous image editing spanning diverse editing categories and equipped with a unified suite of metrics quantifying monotonic progression, perceptual continuity, and inter-slider disentanglement. Building on these observations, we propose SliderEdit++, which explicitly regularizes the intermediate editing trajectory rather than supervising only its endpoints. Our method combines velocity- and semantic-space trajectory regularization with spectral control to encourage smooth, predictable slider traversal, together with a disentanglement objective that reduces interference between independently controlled edits. On SlideBench, SliderEdit++ achieves state-of-the-art performance, improving the overall score by 18.5% relative to the strongest baseline. At the same time, using a few-step editing backbone makes our method approximately 3x to 20x faster than existing slider-based approaches, enabling substantially more responsive interactive editing.

</details>


### [12] [Image Diffusion Models Are Good 3D Human Motion Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14254)
*Siyuan Yang,Chong Wang,Hao Cheng,Jun Liu,Bihan Wen,Alex Kot,Hedvig Kjellstrom*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种图像兼容的表示方法，将根中心关节轨迹组织为拓扑感知的关节-时间图像，并配合潜在根动态流，用单一模型处理异构骨架布局的文本到动作生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到动作生成依赖数据集特定的骨架表示，不同关节布局的基准需分别训练模型，无法跨异构骨架共享建模。

Method: 将根中心关节轨迹组织为拓扑感知的关节-时间图像，根动态通过互补的潜在流建模；动作自编码器以根潜变量为条件解码局部动作，文本条件SiT联合生成两个潜在流，并对解码的3D关节位置施加监督。

Result: 在HumanML3D和KIT-ML上，数据集特定模型达到SOTA；在HumanML3D和KIT-ML上训练单个共享自编码器和SiT（无需骨架重定向）进一步提升了两个基准的性能。

Conclusion: 图像扩散架构可通过通用图像兼容表示有效建模3D人体动作，跨异构骨架布局的共享建模能带来额外收益。

Abstract: Text-to-motion generation typically relies on dataset-specific skeletal representations, with models trained separately for benchmarks with different joint layouts. We investigate whether image diffusion architectures can model 3D human motion through a common image-compatible representation that accommodates heterogeneous skeletal layouts while preserving motion-specific kinematic structure. To this end, we organize root-centered joint trajectories into topology-informed joint--time images and model global root dynamics through a complementary latent stream. A motion autoencoder conditions local-motion decoding on the root latent, while a text-conditioned Scalable Interpolant Transformer (SiT) jointly generates both latent streams, with supervision on decoded 3D joint positions further constraining generation in motion space. In addition to dataset-specific models, we train a single motion autoencoder on HumanML3D and KIT-ML, followed by a single text-conditioned SiT shared across both datasets, preserving their respective skeletal definitions without skeleton retargeting to a common joint layout. Experiments on HumanML3D and KIT-ML show that our dataset-specific models achieve state-of-the-art performance, while shared training yields further gains on both benchmarks, demonstrating the benefits of shared modeling across heterogeneous skeletal layouts.

</details>


### [13] [BigCell: Generating Gigapixel Whole-Slide Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14261)
*Srikar Yellapragada,Alexandros Graikos,Zilinghan Li,Kostas Triaridis,Tarak Nath Nandi,Ji Dong K Bai,Beatrice Knudsen,Tahsin Kurc,Prateek Prasanna,Rajarsi R. Gupta,Ravi K Madduri,Joel Saltz,Dimitris Samaras*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BigCell是首个能生成原生gigapixel分辨率全切片病理图像的方法，通过流匹配Transformer和滑动窗口推理，实现了从文本报告到8k图像的合成，并提升少样本分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有病理生成模型仅工作在patch尺度（至多1024×1024），而临床诊断的文本报告和切片级标签都在全切片尺度，二者存在尺度不匹配。

Method: BigCell在2048×2048像素上训练流匹配Transformer，使用超过12万张公开WSI，条件为病理基础模型embedding，并采用推理时滑动窗口生成至32768×32768；引入自适应调度优先处理重要区域；另训练模型从病理文本报告生成密集条件网格实现8k文本到图像合成。

Result: 生成图像在tile级和slide级保真度指标上超越先前tile生成器；自适应调度加速3倍以上且质量损失极小；合成样本使slide级少样本分类性能相比真实数据基线提升最多18%。

Conclusion: BigCell实现了原生gigapixel全切片病理图像生成及文本到图像合成，兼具效率与质量，并可有效增强下游少样本分类任务。

Abstract: Whole-slide images (WSIs) are gigapixel-scale digital scans of histopathology tissue and serve as the primary basis for cancer diagnosis. Existing histopathology generative models operate at the patch scale, producing fixed-size tiles of at most 1024$\times$1024 pixels which encompass only a small fraction of the WSI. In contrast, text reports for clinical diagnoses and slide-level diagnostic labels -- the supervision that exists at scale -- operate at the whole-slide level. We present BigCell, the first approach to generate coherent whole-slide histopathology images at native gigapixel resolution. BigCell trains a flow-matching transformer at 2048$\times$2048 pixels on over 120,000 publicly available WSIs spanning multiple organ types. Conditioning on embeddings from pathology foundation models and using an inference-time sliding-window pipeline, BigCell generates coherent images of up to 32,768$\times$32,768 pixels, outperforming prior tile generators on both tile-level and slide-level fidelity metrics. To make gigapixel synthesis practical, we introduce an adaptive scheduling strategy that prioritizes important slide regions, speeding up image generation by over 3\ttimes with minimal quality degradation. Furthermore, we propose the first text-to-image synthesis at 8k resolution by training a separate model that produces dense conditioning grids directly from pathology text reports. We demonstrate that augmenting with synthetic samples improves slide-level few-shot classification performance by up to $18 \%$ over baselines trained on real data.

</details>


### [14] [Thinking with Images without Coordinates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14265)
*Meng Cao,Haoze Zhao,Yi Wang,Ian Reid,Xiaodan Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TiCO 提出无需坐标的"图像思考"范式，将视觉推理重构为潜在空间中的语义证据发现，用探针token与重要性采样生成token级证据区域，在高分辨率与通用视觉推理基准上超越基于坐标的SOTA，且推理效率更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有"图像思考"方法依赖显式坐标定位，存在两个根本问题：一是语义鸿沟，稀疏回归难以捕捉高维潜在结构；二是分辨率鸿沟，patch级token与像素级坐标粒度不匹配。

Method: TiCO 将视觉推理重构为潜在空间内的语义证据发现过程，证据以token级区域形式注入而非像素坐标。具体使用专用探针token估计相似度分布，并在空间连通分量上做重要性采样，生成多样且语义主导的证据rollout。

Result: 在多个高分辨率与通用视觉推理基准上，TiCO 一致优于基于坐标的SOTA方法，并显著提升推理效率。

Conclusion: 通过去除坐标依赖、转向潜在空间语义证据发现，可有效缓解语义与分辨率两类鸿沟，实现更强的视觉推理能力与更高的推理效率。

Abstract: Recent advances in multi-modal large language models have shifted toward the "thinking with images" paradigm to improve reasoning faithfulness, yet existing methods rely heavily on explicit coordinate- based grounding. We identify two fundamental limitations in this approach, i.e., a semantic gap where sparse regressions miss high-dimensional latent structures, and a resolution gap due to the granularity mismatch between patch-level tokens and pixel-level coordinates. To address these, we propose TiCO (Thinking with images without COordinates). It reformulates visual reasoning as a semantic evidence discovery process within the latent space, and evidence is injected as token-level regions without pixel coordinates. Specifically, TiCO utilizes a dedicated probe token to estimate similarity distributions and performs importance sampling over spatially connected components to generate diverse, semantically dominant evidence rollouts. Extensive experiments across multiple high-resolution and general visual reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that our TiCO consistently outperforms state-of-the-art coordinate-based methods with significantly improved inference efficiency.

</details>


### [15] [RoboFake: Are Deepfake Detectors Ready for Humanoid Face Robots?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14269)
*Hao Chen,Junnan Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RoboFake概念：用真人脸机器人配合生成语音在镜头前制造人脸伪造（真实物体、像素未编辑）。作者用6个在FaceForensics++上训练的公开检测器测试物理唇形同步机器人、合成视频与渲染机器人脸，发现多数检测器会标记机器人脸，但并非识别合成，而是因机器人脸"不像人"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人已能唇形同步并做出细致表情。若赋予其真人面容并用生成语音驱动，就产生了一种"发生在镜头前"的人脸伪造（RoboFake）——相机拍摄的是真实物体，像素未被编辑，传统deepfake检测框架可能失效。作者想探究当前检测器面对机器人脸时会如何反应。

Method: 在FaceForensics++上固定所有阈值，测试6个公开检测器；输入包括物理唇形同步机器人视频、同一机器人的合成视频以及渲染机器人脸；并将每个结果与经过相同处理的真人脸对比。还进行了匹配的真人对照与掩码实验，并施加轻量JPEG压缩测试鲁棒性。

Result: 在仅标记5%真人脸的阈值下，3个检测器几乎标记所有物理机器人帧，另2个不足7%。匹配真人对照与掩码表明响应来自人脸而非处理或背景，但它并不追踪合成：仅露脸时，6个检测器中3个给物理机器人的假分高于其合成视频。响应脆弱，轻量JPEG压缩即可使多数机器人帧降至部分检测器阈值以下。

Conclusion: 当前检测器标记当今机器人是因为其脸"不像人"，而非识别出伪造。作者发布评估协议与代码，并针对真实、交互式RoboFake提出三个可检验假设。

Abstract: Humanoid face robots can now move their lips in sync with speech and show detailed facial expressions. Give such a robot the face of a real person and drive it with generated speech, and you get a face forgery that happens in front of the camera: the camera records a real object, and no pixel is edited. We call this a RoboFake. In this paper we ask what current deepfake detectors do when they see a robot face. We test six public detectors trained on FaceForensics++ on video of a physical lip-syncing face robot, on a synthesized video of the same robot, and on rendered robot faces. All thresholds are fixed on FaceForensics++, and every result is compared with real human faces that went through the same processing as the robot videos. At a threshold that flags 5% of real faces, three detectors flag almost every physical robot frame, while two flag fewer than 7%. A matched human control and masking show that the response comes from the face, not from the processing or the background. However, it does not track synthesis: with only the face visible, three of the six detectors give the physical robot a higher fake score than its synthesized video. The response is also fragile, and light JPEG compression is enough to push most robot frames below the threshold for some detectors. Our results suggest that current detectors flag today's robots because their faces do not look human, not because they recognize a forgery. We release our evaluation protocol and code, and state three testable hypotheses about realistic, interactive RoboFakes.

</details>


### [16] [TGSR: Text-Grounded Diffusion for Faithful Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14281)
*Zhiming Hu,Ran Zhang,Angela Ye,Raghav Goyal,Faraz Ali,Tristan Ty Aumentado-Armstrong,Samrudhdhi B. Rangrej,Konstantinos G. Derpanis,Iqbal Mohomed,Alex Levinshtein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出TGSR框架，将文本超分辨率重构为"选择性生成"问题，在生成过程中逐步识别并抑制可能产生幻觉的文本区域，从而在保持感知质量的同时提升文本保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在图像超分辨率中表现优异，但在文本内容上常产生幻觉（不存在的或扭曲的文字），改变场景语义并损害模型可靠性。现有方法虽着力增强文本生成，但采用"不加区分"的方式，未能从根本上解决幻觉问题。作者认为核心问题在于缺乏"选择性控制"。

Method: 提出TGSR（Text-Grounded Super-Resolution），一个测试时框架，在生成过程中逐步定位并抑制可能被幻觉化的文本区域；为此，作者从多种基于扩散的SR输出中构建大规模数据集，并训练专用检测器。

Result: TGSR在多种骨干模型上一致提升文本保真度，识别精度最高提升17.68个百分点，且不降低感知质量。

Conclusion: 研究强调了选择性控制在生成式超分辨率中的重要价值，为解决文本幻觉提供了新思路。

Abstract: Diffusion models have achieved remarkable success in image super-resolution (SR) by leveraging strong generative priors to enhance perceptual quality. However, for textual content, these priors often produce text hallucinations: non-existent or distorted text, which alter scene semantics and compromise model reliability. While recent methods focus on enhancing text generation, they do so indiscriminately, leaving hallucinations fundamentally unresolved. We argue the core problem is a lack of selective control. Therefore, we reformulate text SR as a selective generation problem: text is resolved only when reliable generation is expected, while other scene content is fully super-resolved. We introduce TGSR (Text-Grounded Super-Resolution), a test-time framework that progressively identifies and suppresses likely hallucinated text regions during generation. To enable this, we train dedicated detectors on a large-scale dataset curated from diverse diffusion-based SR outputs. TGSR consistently improves text fidelity across multiple backbones, increasing recognition precision by up to 17.68 points without degrading perceptual quality, underscoring the value of selective control in generative SR.

</details>


### [17] [GeoFix: Geometry Fixing for 4D Consistent Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14293)
*Yeobin Hong,Hyojun Go,Dohun Lee,Jeongjae Lee,Sunghwan Hong,Federico Tombari,Zhaochong An,Jong Chul Ye,Konrad Schindler,Dominik Narnhofer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GeoFix统一框架，用单一几何奖励同时提升4D生成的几何一致性与视觉质量，适用于训练和推理阶段的奖励对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型作为4D生成先验时，生成视频常缺乏几何一致性；现有后训练方法设计几何奖励会损害纹理细节，需引入额外外观奖励并精细平衡。

Method: 设计单一几何奖励，度量静态场景区域的跨视角深度一致性，只针对结构不一致而不约束外观或惩罚合理物体运动；并对奖励随相机视场重叠变化（重叠越深、深度越一致）的趋势进行校准，避免小相机位移被误判为更好几何。

Result: WorldScore 3D一致性提升39%，VBench质量分提升4%。

Conclusion: 通过校准的单一几何奖励即可同时改善几何一致性与视觉质量，无需辅助外观奖励与复杂奖励平衡。

Abstract: Video diffusion models increasingly serve as priors for 4D generation, but the generated videos often lack geometric consistency. Recent approaches address this through post-training by designing geometric rewards, yet this can degrade texture and fine detail, requiring auxiliary appearance rewards and careful reward balancing. To address this challenge, we propose GeoFix, a unified framework that improves geometric consistency and visual quality with a single geometric reward, applicable to both training and inference-time reward alignment. Our reward measures cross-view depth consistency in static scene regions, targeting structural inconsistencies without directly constraining appearance or penalizing valid object motion. Crucially, depth agreement increases with camera field-of-view overlap even in real videos and 4D datasets, allowing a smaller camera movement to be mistaken for better geometry. We account for this effect by calibrating the reward against the overlap-dependent trend. GeoFix achieves a 39% improvement in WorldScore 3D consistency and a 4% increase in VBench Quality Score.

</details>


### [18] [MvFFN: Multi-view Floor plan Feed-forward Network for Unposed Wide-Baseline Panorama Layout Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14315)
*Yuguang Li,Yichuan Deng,Zixuan Liu,Ivaylo Boyadzhiev,Xiuyuan Wu,Xinyu Zou,Linda Shapiro,Alex Colburn*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MOIS局部墙实例分割与MvFFN，从稀疏无位姿宽基线全景图重建多房间户型图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏、无位姿、宽基线RGB全景图重建相机位姿和户型图仍是难题，多房间直接预测因位姿、几何、语义与全局结构强耦合而困难。

Method: 核心思路是保持预测局部化：MOIS让每个全景列仅预测其观测到的墙及顺时针相邻的两面墙，全局墙划分与房间布局由重叠局部预测确定性聚合产生；基于MOIS提出MvFFN，联合预测粗相机位姿、稠密全局几何和逐墙语义；再用Layout Chaining后处理组装成共享全局坐标、墙身份一致、房间连通的多房间户型图。

Result: 稠密MOIS预测在跨视图墙身份和房间内局部连通性上优于模块化组合基线；组装布局在墙和交点级别精度上大幅超越先前方法。同时发布ZInD-CrossView数据集，为ZInD增加全局唯一墙实例和跨视图分割标签。

Conclusion: 通过局部化预测与确定性聚合，可有效解决稀疏宽基线全景下的多房间户型重建，并提供新基准数据集。

Abstract: Reconstructing accurate camera poses and floor-plan layouts from sparse, unposed, wide-baseline RGB panoramas remains a challenging open problem. Direct multi-room floor-plan prediction is especially difficult in this setting: pose, geometry, semantics, and global layout structure are tightly coupled, yet a complete floor-plan is hard to learn as a single global output. Our key insight is to keep the model's predictions strictly local. Multi-view Ordered Wall Instance Segmentation (MOIS) is designed around this concept. Each panorama column predicts only the wall it observes plus the next two adjacent walls in clockwise order. The global wall partition, room layouts, and shared coordinates emerge from deterministic aggregation of overlapping local predictions. Building on MOIS, we propose Multi-view floor-plan Feed-Forward Network (MvFFN), which jointly predicts coarse camera poses, dense global geometry, and per-wall semantic outputs. A downstream Layout Chaining (LC) post processing pipeline then assembles these predictions into multi-room floor-plans with shared global coordinates, consistent wall identities, and connected room structure. The dense MOIS predictions recover cross-view wall identity and local within-room connectivity more accurately than composed modular baselines, and the assembled layouts substantially outperform prior methods on wall and junction level accuracy. We also introduce the ZInD-CrossView dataset, which augments ZInD with globally unique wall instances and cross-view segmentation labels for this task.

</details>


### [19] [EV-JEPA: Elastic V-JEPA for Any Device and Cross-Device Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14324)
*Anxhelo Diko,Jieyu Lin,Stefano Ambrogio,Ziyun Li,Mahmoud Assran,Théo Moutakanni,Barbara De Salvo,Chiao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EV-JEPA：一次训练即可在边缘-云异构设备上弹性部署的宽度可重构、可跨设备分区的V-JEPA基础模型，核心为Transition-Aware Triplet Training (T3)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型如V-JEPA通常被训练为固定容量、绑定单一设备的刚性网络，不支持可重构计算预算和协作执行。现有方法仅解决单一维度：slimmable/NAS提供宽度弹性但仅限单设备推理，split-computing支持跨设备协作但仅限固定分区与静态容量。

Method: 提出EV-JEPA单一模型，宽度可重构且原生支持异构设备分区。核心是Transition-Aware Triplet Training (T3)：每步选取一个窄宽度和一个宽宽度，训练三条路径——两条单独宽度路径，及一条早期层窄、后续层宽的混合路径；混合切换点即设备交接点，使同一权重同时学习单设备与跨设备执行。

Result: 在多基准上，EV-JEPA与独立训练基线差距在1个准确点内，较先前弹性训练方法提升最高达11个准确点。窄到宽跨设备混合执行恢复至最高容量独立模型1.1点以内，支持多对一、任意对任意及可变分割配置。弹性原则还可扩展至弹性token压缩机制，实现设备端计算、延迟与准确率间的可控权衡。

Conclusion: EV-JEPA通过T3实现单一模型在边缘-云连续体上的弹性部署，兼顾宽度重构与跨设备分区，性能接近专用基线，并可将弹性原则延伸至token压缩层面。

Abstract: Foundation models like V-JEPA are increasingly deployed across a heterogeneous edge-cloud continuum spanning smart glasses, phones, robots, and servers. Yet, they are typically trained as rigid, fixed-capacity networks bound to one device, supporting neither reconfigurable compute budgets nor collaborative execution. Existing approaches address only one axis at a time: slimmable and NAS-based methods provide width elasticity but assume single-device inference, whereas split-computing methods enable cross-device collaboration only for fixed partitions and static capacities. We introduce Elastic V-JEPA (EV-JEPA), a single model trained once and deployable everywhere along the continuum. Its width is reconfigurable, and it is natively partitionable across heterogeneous devices. At its core is Transition-Aware Triplet Training (T3). At each step we pick a narrow and a wide width, and train three paths: each width alone, plus a hybrid path that runs the early layers narrow and the rest wide. The hybrid switch point is where one device hands off to another, so the same weights learn both single-device and cross-device execution. Across various benchmarks, EV-JEPA falls within 1 accuracy point of independently trained baselines while improving over previous elastic training approaches by up to 11 accuracy points. Narrow-to-wide cross-device hybrid execution recovers to within 1.1 points of the highest-capacity standalone model and supports many-to-one, any-to-any, and variable-split configurations. Finally, we show that the elastic principle extends beyond the backbone to an elastic token compression mechanism that exposes controllable trade-offs among on-device computation, latency, and accuracy.

</details>


### [20] [Manifold Navigation Learning Using Recurrent Networks: Efficient Training Techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14325)
*Sarah Harkins Dayton,Sathya Rengaswami,Vasileios Maroulas,David L. Boothe,Ioannis D. Schizas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将流形导航形式化为速度驱动的跟踪任务，提出基于坐标图的学习框架，用GRU在欧氏表示下预测轨迹并映射回流形，验证了自定义损失函数对长程预测的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 位置跟踪在欧氏导航中研究充分，但一般流形上的导航研究较少，其运动受空间几何约束。现有方法难以在保持几何一致性的同时高效学习，因此需要一种既避免显式流形约束、又能评估几何一致性的学习框架。

Method: 将流形导航形式化为速度驱动的跟踪任务；提出基于坐标图（coordinate chart）的学习框架，模型完全在欧氏表示中运行，预测轨迹通过浸入函数映射到流形后再计算误差；对比多种循环架构后选用GRU做序列位置预测；采用自定义损失函数，并与标准MSE损失及朴素向量相加基线对比。

Result: 在解析定义和数值定义的流形以及由LiDAR点云重建的曲面上进行评估；循环模型能从速度序列中学习近似流形约束动力学，并持续优于欧氏基线；某些自定义损失函数相比传统逐点损失显著提升长程预测精度。

Conclusion: 循环模型（GRU）可有效学习流形约束动力学，验证了基于坐标图的欧氏学习框架在流形导航中的可行性；合适的自定义损失函数对长程预测精度至关重要。

Abstract: Position tracking is a well-studied problem in Euclidean navigation, yet comparatively little attention has been given to navigation on general manifolds, where motion is governed by the underlying geometry of the space. In this work, we formalize the problem of manifold navigation as a velocity-driven tracking task and propose a coordinate-chart-based learning framework in which learning models operate entirely in a Euclidean representation. Predicted trajectories are mapped to the manifold via an immersion function prior to error computation, allowing geometric consistency to be evaluated without explicitly enforcing manifold constraints during learning. Within this framework, we focus on Gated Recurrent Units (GRUs) for sequential position prediction on curved manifolds after comparisons with alternative recurrent architectures. We evaluate our GRU models, trained with pertinent custom loss functions, against both a standard MSE loss function and a naive vector-addition baseline. We evaluate our approach on both analytically (and numerically) defined manifolds, as well as a surface reconstructed from a LiDAR point cloud. Our results show that recurrent models can learn to approximate manifold-constrained dynamics from velocity sequences and consistently outperform Euclidean baselines. Our results demonstrate that certain custom loss functions significantly improve long-horizon prediction accuracy compared to conventional point-wise loss functions.

</details>


### [21] [Real-Time Traffic Density Estimation: A Hybrid Computer Vision and Predictive Machine Learning Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14326)
*Prince Kwabena Appiah Boadu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两阶段流水线，将车辆检测/跟踪与空间逻辑层结合，再用LSTM、GRU和TCN进行短时交通密度预测，并基准测试了十种检测器及其速度-精度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从摄像头视频估计交通密度需要将视觉感知与短时预测相结合，但检测器速度与精度、以及短窗口交通遥测预测的局限尚缺系统评估。

Method: 两阶段流水线：先检测与跟踪车辆，空间逻辑层按预计算道路掩码过滤边界框并提取逐帧交通特征；再由LSTM、GRU、TCN基于10帧输入预测未来5步。在5,716帧数据集上基准测试六族十种检测器，并在29,243个留出窗口上评估时序模型。

Result: YOLOv5l达到最高mAP@50:95为0.524（56 FPS），YOLOv8n达258 FPS（0.487 mAP@50:95），均为不含跟踪和空间过滤的检测器推理速度。时序预测聚合R²：GRU 0.858、LSTM 0.856、TCN 0.840。评估流缺少道路掩码，限制空间过滤组件的验证。

Conclusion: 结果刻画了检测器速度-精度权衡及短窗口交通遥测预测的局限；指标主要反映方向方差而非绝对交通密度精度，空间滤波组件验证受限。

Abstract: Traffic density estimation from camera feeds requires connecting visual perception with short-horizon forecasting. We present a two-stage pipeline that combines vehicle detection and tracking with a spatial logic layer, followed by a temporal estimator. The spatial layer filters bounding boxes by their overlap with a precomputed road mask and extracts per-frame traffic features. LSTM, GRU, and Temporal Convolutional Network (TCN) estimators use these features to predict five future steps from a ten-frame input window. We benchmark ten detector variants from six families on a dataset of 5,716 annotated frames using a single NVIDIA GPU. YOLOv5l achieves the highest mAP@50:95 of 0.524 at 56 frames per second, while YOLOv8n reaches 258 frames per second at 0.487 mAP@50:95; these rates measure detector inference without tracking or spatial filtering. Temporal evaluation on 29,243 held-out windows yields aggregate R-squared values of 0.858 for GRU, 0.856 for LSTM, and 0.840 for TCN. These metrics measure forecasts of detector-derived telemetry and are dominated by direction variance, rather than establishing absolute traffic-density accuracy. No road mask was available for the evaluation stream, limiting validation of the spatial filtering component. A live run on a third camera stream examines forecasting behavior within the complete pipeline. The results characterize detector speed-accuracy trade-offs and the limitations of short-window traffic telemetry forecasting.

</details>


### [22] [TraceSampler: Making Sampling Errors Predictable and Correctable for Accelerated Generative Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14332)
*George Matthew,Guoqiang Zhang,Kenta Niwa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TraceSampler是一种后训练方法，通过将端点误差建模为样本相关的预测问题，在几乎零额外开销下显著提升少步采样质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间生成模型精确采样需大量模型评估；少步采样虽降成本，但粗更新会引入累积的端点误差，损害生成质量。

Method: 后训练校正，不改动预训练模型。将端点误差作为样本相关预测问题：用同区间更高步参考积分提供监督目标，并用共享残差结构、轨迹方向及低秩余项的紧凑表示，让浅层逐步MLP头预测样本相关系数；两阶段学习残差校正，中间骨干评估提供新轨迹信息并复用于下一步。

Result: 在CIFAR-10（Rectified Flow）、ImageNet-256（SiT-XL/2）和SD3.5 Medium上评估。10步时轨迹MSE较同步骤Euler分别降90.5%和85.6%，FID为2.692和2.189，接近120步Heun参考（2.601、2.056）且骨干评估少24倍。SD3.5上10步ImageReward从0.752升至0.906，轨迹MSE降70.7%。保持Euler评估预算，推理FLOPs增加<0.4%，墙钟开销可忽略。

Conclusion: 粗步采样误差可从采样轨迹中预测并被高效校正，实现低成本高质量生成。

Abstract: Accurate sampling from continuous-time generative models can require many model evaluations. Low-step sampling reduces cost, but coarse updates introduce endpoint errors that can accumulate and degrade generation quality. We introduce TraceSampler, a post-training method that corrects these errors without modifying the pretrained model. Rather than optimising low-step updates directly, TraceSampler models endpoint error as a sample-specific prediction problem. A higher-step reference integration over the same interval provides the supervised target, while a compact representation makes high-dimensional error predictable from the sampling trace. This representation combines shared residual structure, trace-derived directions, and a low-rank remainder, so shallow per-step MLP heads predict sample-dependent coefficients. These coefficients drive a two-stage learned residual correction: the first stage predicts the full endpoint residual, while an intermediate backbone evaluation provides new trace information to predict the remaining residual; this evaluation is reused by the following step. TraceSampler is evaluated with Rectified Flow on CIFAR-10, SiT-XL/2 on ImageNet-256, and SD3.5 Medium for text-to-image generation. At 10 steps, it reduces trajectory MSE relative to matched-step Euler by 90.5% on CIFAR-10 and 85.6% on ImageNet-256, while achieving FID scores of 2.692 and 2.189, close to 120-step Heun references of 2.601 and 2.056 with 24$\times$ fewer backbone evaluations. On SD3.5 Medium, TraceSampler improves image quality and prompt following; at 10 steps, ImageReward rises from 0.752 to 0.906 while trajectory MSE falls by 70.7%. These gains preserve Euler's backbone-evaluation budget, add less than 0.4% inference FLOPs, and incur negligible wall-clock overhead, showing that coarse-step sampling error can be made predictable from the sampling trace and efficiently corrected.

</details>


### [23] [Learning Transferable World-Action Models from Task-Paired Human Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14351)
*Jeongeun Park,Taekyung Kim,Dongyoon Han,Sangdoo Yun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WATCH 提出跨视频预训练方法，利用成对的人类演示视频学习可迁移的世界-动作表征，显著提升机器人策略的任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准视频内下一帧预测会让模型依赖上下文与未来帧之间的共享外观信息，而非学习可跨场景和跨具身迁移的交互规律。作者希望让模型学到更具迁移性的任务相关交互，且无需匹配的人-机器人演示数据。

Method: WATCH 使用跨多样环境采集的任务配对人类演示，在一个演示的视频与动作条件下，联合预测另一个演示的动作和其当前观察下的未来帧，从而迫使模型学习跨场景、跨演示者可迁移的任务交互。

Result: 仅用人类-人类配对即可学习该能力，无需人-机器人配对。机器人后训练前，动作对齐和跨具身预测更好，表征更接近机器人配对学到的表征；机器人后训练后，MimicDroid 上下文成功率从 40.9% 提升到 68.3%（Cosmos Policy），从 59.8% 提升到 77.7%（Cosmos3），未见层级增益最大，且在无上下文后训练下依然保持。

Conclusion: WATCH 优于视频内预训练和协同训练基线，表明跨视频预测是一种可扩展的学习可迁移世界-动作表征的方法。

Abstract: World-action models inherit broad visual and physical priors from video pre-training. However, standard within-video next-frame prediction allows them to rely on appearance shared between the context and future, rather than learning interactions that transfer across scenes and embodiments. We introduce WATCH, a cross-video pre-training that learns from task-paired human demonstrations collected across diverse environments. Conditioned on the video and actions of one demonstration, the model jointly predicts another demonstration's actions and future frames from its current observation. Predicting one demonstration from another encourages the model to learn task-relevant interactions that transfer across scenes and demonstrators. Our pre-training shows that these capabilities can be learned from readily scalable human-human pairs alone, without requiring matched human-robot demonstrations. Analyses before robot post-training show improved motion alignment across embodiments and better action and video prediction on unseen robots, with representations closer to those learned from robot pairs. After robot post-training, WATCH improves MimicDroid in-context success from 40.9% to 68.3% with Cosmos Policy and from 59.8% to 77.7% with Cosmos3, with the largest gains on the unseen levels. Gains persist under context-free post-training across robot embodiments. WATCH outperforms within-video pre-training and co-training baselines, supporting cross-video prediction as a scalable approach to learning transferable world-action representations.

</details>


### [24] [RealityBench: Simulated Environments to Evaluate a Vision Language Model's Ability to Document Human Physical Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14397)
*Andrew Perrault,Anmol Takiar,Dominik Wienecki,Arnab Nandi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文用 Blender 构建 11 类真实体力工作场景的合成基准，评估 26 个视觉语言模型在"文档化理解"与"可接受性判断"两项任务上的能力，发现模型在文本读取上表现好，但在测量、小部件和空间关系上较弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 智能体通过相机/智能眼镜辅助体力工作者的关键能力是理解工作者的行为和场景并协助文档记录。然而现有视觉语言模型在此类场景中的能力尚不明确，且难以基准化：真实物理工作数据多为专有，且方差极大。厨房、仓库等结构化场景有可预测的光照、几何和物体，而屋顶光伏安装、车辆维修等野外任务则无约束、高方差。因此需要一种可控的方法来系统研究真实物理工作。

Method: 在 Blender 中对真实世界物理工作进行建模，覆盖 11 种环境，并引入图像可接受性与文档化两方面的变体。可接受性任务判断某视角是否适合作为工作证明；文档化任务判断智能体能否从该视角理解任务相关的环境状态。对 26 个视觉语言模型进行评测。

Result: 顶尖模型在"答案为画面中文本"的文档化任务上基本能解决，但读取测量值、判断小部件和空间关系（如卷尺是否倾斜）时表现弱。可接受性任务因人类评分者之间意见不一而更难以界定：在与单个评分者一致度相当的评分器对照下，最佳模型无法与单个非作者评分者区分，成本处于人类通过范围内。在可追踪可接受性错误的三个环境中，多数错误拒绝源于误读场景而非误读要求。在有真实图像的三个文档化问题上，渲染帧上的模型排序可迁移到真实图像。

Conclusion: 该基准揭示了视觉语言模型在真实物理工作辅助中的能力短板，尤其是测量、细小部件和空间关系理解；可接受性判断本身具主观性，模型难以超越单个人类评分者。合成渲染帧上的评估结果可迁移到真实图像，为后续研究提供了可控的评测框架。

Abstract: Augmenting worker capabilities in the physical world through guidance has been a decades-long goal for AI agents. One key supporting capability is to understand what the worker is doing, seeing through the human's camera or smart glasses, and assist them in documentation. Despite advances in vision language models, their capabilities in these settings remain uncertain and are challenging to benchmark because most physical work data is proprietary, and involves too much variability to study in a principled manner. Unlike structured settings such as kitchens and warehouses that have predictable lighting, geometry, and objects, field-facing tasks such as rooftop solar installation and vehicle repair are unconstrained and high variance. Here, we model real-world physical work in Blender across eleven environments, incorporating variants in both image acceptability and documentation. The acceptability task asks whether a particular view would be suitable proof-of-work and documentation asks whether an agent can understand the task-relevant state of the environment from that view. Across 26 vision-language models, we find that top models largely solve documentation when the answer is text in frame, but are weak at reading measurements and at judging small parts and spatial relations, such as whether a tape measure leans. Acceptability is harder to pin down because human raters disagree with each other. Measured against a scorer that agrees with the raters' majority about as often as a single rater does, the best models cannot be distinguished from a single non-author rater, at a cost within the range of a human pass. In the three environments where we can trace acceptability errors, most wrong rejections come from misreading the scene rather than the requirement. On the three documentation questions for which we have real images, model rankings on rendered frames transfer to real images.

</details>


### [25] [VisualCoding: Learning Executable Skills for Reliable Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14403)
*Liu Jiaqi,Jin Ruinan,Li Xiaoxiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VisualCoding通过执行引导获取可复用视觉程序并直接采用其答案，提升视觉推理智能体的单次运行表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复用技能需智能体实现/调用流程并生成最终答案，步骤中的错误与幻觉会阻碍经验转化为可靠的单次运行性能。

Method: 包含两模块：Execution-Guided Program Acquisition将视觉原语与代码组合成家族级求解器，在标注训练实例上迭代测试修正；Scope-Aware Direct Execution运行保留的求解器并返回其有效的域内答案。

Result: 在4个模型、4个基准、69个任务家族上评估。Gemini-3.1-Pro平均准确率超出最强基线4.45个百分点；用Flash-Lite复用冻结的Pro获取产物时超出14.78个百分点。消融支持执行引导获取，并显示直接执行优于文本或可调用复用。

Conclusion: 执行引导获取程序并直接采用答案可有效提升视觉推理的可靠性，且程序产物可跨模型复用。

Abstract: Learning reusable skills can improve visual reasoning agents, yet applying a skill still requires the agent to implement or invoke a procedure and produce the final answer. Errors and hallucinations at these steps can prevent acquired experience from translating into reliable single-run performance. We propose VisualCoding, which combines execution-guided acquisition of reusable visual programs with direct adoption of their answers on applicable inputs. Execution-Guided Program Acquisition composes visual primitives and additional code into family-level solvers, then iteratively tests and revises them on labeled training instances. Scope-Aware Direct Execution runs a retained solver and returns its valid in-scope answer. We evaluate VisualCoding on four models across four benchmarks spanning 69 task families. VisualCoding exceeds the strongest evaluated baseline in mean accuracy by 4.45 percentage points with Gemini-3.1-Pro and by 14.78 points when reusing the frozen Pro-acquired artifacts with Flash-Lite. Ablations support execution-guided acquisition and show that direct execution outperforms textual or callable reuse of the same programs. Code is available at https://github.com/jiaq-liu/VisualCoding.

</details>


### [26] [SceneForesee: Learning Scene Evolution for Spatiotemporal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14439)
*Chaoyue Li,Shoudong Han,Haoyang Wu,Boxue Yang,Sijia Chen,Yufan Chen,Ruping Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出场景演化理解(SEU)任务并构建SceneForesee基准(144.57K时序对)，同时提出EvoSee时空推理框架，通过共享候选状态与联合分配学习未来几何与事件语义，在预测和4D推理任务上显著超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型多模态模型需理解场景发展中实体的运动与交互，要求跨时间跟踪同一实体并将其运动与后续变化关联，但缺乏相应任务定义与基准来评估实体连续性与场景演化推理能力。

Method: 提出EvoSee时空推理框架，通过共享候选状态和联合分配学习未来几何与事件语义；从观测中选择每个实体的一个延续，并利用所选变化进行实体精炼、保留和场景表示。

Result: 在SceneForesee上，EvoSee相比Qwen3.5-9B在Combined Point-F1提升10.45分、Event Global F1提升17.24分；在Dyn-Bench上问答准确率与grounding J&F分别提升4.8和3.58分。

Conclusion: 场景演化既是预测目标也是动态表示，将预测的实体变化引入时空推理能有效提升性能。

Abstract: Large multimodal models (LMMs) need to understand how entities move and interact as a scene develops. This requires following the same entities over time and relating their observed motion to subsequent changes. We formulate Scene Evolution Understanding (SEU), a task for predicting and interpreting this change. Given observed video, a model estimates entity histories and predicts one scene continuation through future trajectories and Events, accompanied by Visual Evidence from observed motion and a scene description. We construct SceneForesee, a benchmark for evaluating entity continuity and scene evolution reasoning through paired observed and future scenes with shared entity identities. It contains 144.57K temporal pairs, 1.05M entity correspondences, and 272.49K Events paired with observed evidence. We propose a spatiotemporal reasoning framework, EvoSee, that learns future geometry and Event semantics through shared candidate states and joint assignment. It selects one continuation per entity from observation and uses the selected changes for entity refinement, retention, and scene representation. On SceneForesee, EvoSee outperforms Qwen3.5-9B by 10.45 points in Combined Point-F1 and 17.24 points in Event Global F1. On the 4D reasoning benchmark Dyn-Bench, it improves question answering accuracy and grounding J&F by 4.8 and 3.58 points over their respective base systems. These results support scene evolution as both a prediction target and a dynamic representation that brings predicted entity changes into spatiotemporal reasoning.The project is available at https://github.com/mikubaka88/SceneForesee-Spatiotemporal-Reasoning.

</details>


### [27] [FreeCHROMA: Generative Chroma Keying from Unaligned Background References](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14445)
*Jiayi Wu,Tianfu Wang,Rui Zhao,Florian Schiffers,Zhimin Chen,Cornelia Fermuller,Yezhou Yang,Christopher Metzler,Yiannis Aloimonos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FreeCHROMA 用参数高效的方式微调预训练视频扩散模型，实现从1-3张未对齐背景参考进行生成式视频合成（色键抠像），无需单独的重打光阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统色键流程需要协调的背景板和专门的光照协调；现有生成方法要求预对齐背景、只做背景条件前景重打光，或仅用编辑后的首帧表示环境，限制了背景选择、场景构图和相机运动，并增加准备/编辑成本。

Method: 以参数高效方式微调预训练视频扩散模型；输入1-3张未对齐背景参考，将前景融入参考环境，保持前景身份、运动和细节，同时生成时空连贯背景；前后景协调在同一生成过程内完成，无需独立重打光；可选文本指令辅助消歧空间关系与相机行为。训练数据由真实单目视频经前景-背景分离与基础模型增强构建伪配对数据，原视频作为训练目标。

Result: 在参考保真度、前景保持、场景融合、光照协调和整体视频质量上均稳定优于近期合成与条件视频生成基线，尤其在有相机运动时增益明显；探索性实验显示其在机器人领域有潜力。

Conclusion: FreeCHROMA 放宽了现有工具的约束，实现从未对齐输入到端到端的生成式色键合成，并展示在机器人等领域的应用潜力。

Abstract: In film production, chroma keying composites an approved foreground performance into a new environment, but conventional workflows require coordinated background plates and dedicated harmonization. Recent generative methods often require pre-aligned backgrounds, focus on background-conditioned foreground relighting, or represent the environment only through an edited first frame. These restrictions limit background selection, scene composition, and camera motion while adding preparation or editing effort. We present FreeCHROMA, which adapts a pretrained video diffusion model in a parameter-efficient manner for generative video compositing from one to three unaligned background references. The model integrates the foreground into the referenced environment, preserving foreground identity, motion, and fine visual details while generating a spatiotemporally coherent background. Foreground-background harmonization occurs within the same generation process, without a separate relighting stage. Optional text instructions help disambiguate complex spatial relationships and camera behavior. To train the model, we construct pseudo-paired data from readily available real monocular videos through foreground-background separation and augmentation with existing foundation models. The original videos serve as training targets, while automated processing and quality verification enable scalable data construction. Across reference fidelity, foreground preservation, scene integration, harmonization, and overall video quality, FreeCHROMA consistently outperforms recent compositing and conditional video-generation baselines, with particularly clear gains under camera motion. FreeCHROMA relaxes the constraints of existing tools to enable end-to-end generative chroma keying from unaligned inputs, with exploratory experiments demonstrating its potential in robotics.

</details>


### [28] [DSR-Net: Learning Dynamic Skeletal Relations for 3D Human Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14499)
*Fan Li,Jian Xiang,LinLin Xue,Yuwei Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DSR-Net动态结构表示学习框架，通过动态骨骼关系建模提升3D人体姿态估计性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖GCN固定骨骼拓扑或注意力隐式捕捉关节关系，难以适应多样动作模式，缺乏对运动相关骨骼结构与关节依赖的自适应建模。

Method: 引入动态骨骼关系DSR模块学习超越预定义连接的自适应关节依赖；基于所学关系设计分层关系感知融合策略促进结构与时序表示交互；设计轻量残差骨骼重校准模块RSRM以最小开销增强结构感知特征稳定性。

Result: 在Human3.6M和MPI-INF-3DHP上分别达到37.9 mm和14.9 mm的MPJPE，持续优于强基线。

Conclusion: 验证了动态骨骼关系建模对鲁棒3D人体姿态估计的有效性。

Abstract: 3D human pose estimation requires accurate modeling of motion-dependent skeletal structures and inter-joint dependencies. Existing methods typically rely on fixed skeletal topologies in Graph Convolutional Networks (GCNs) or implicitly capture joint relations through attention mechanisms, limiting their adaptability to diverse motion patterns. To address this issue,we proposeDSR-Net, a dynamic structural representation learning framework for 3D human pose estimation. Specifically, a Dynamic Skeletal Relation (DSR) module is introduced to learn adaptive inter-joint dependencies beyond predefined skeletal connections. Based on the learned relations, a hierarchical relation-aware fusion strategy is developed to facilitate effective interaction between structural and temporal representations. In addition, a lightweight Residual Skeletal Recalibration Module (RSRM) is designed to enhance the stability of structure-aware features with minimal computational overhead. Extensive experiments on Human3.6M and MPI-INF-3DHP demonstrate that DSR-Net achieves MPJPEs of 37.9 mm and 14.9 mm, respectively, consistently improving over strong baselines. The results validate the effectiveness of dynamic skeletal relation modeling for robust 3D human pose estimation.

</details>


### [29] [Spatiotemporal Recognition of Abdominal Wall Layers During Optical Trocar Entry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14512)
*Shreeram Athreya,Andrew Melehy,Carter Prentice,Michael Robinson,Corey Arnold,James Wu,William Speier*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文评估空间上下文与时间建模是否能提升光学套管穿刺过程中的组织识别，通过极坐标网格分区、梯度提升分类器及隐马尔可夫模型等完整流程，显著改善了分类性能、空间一致性与起病时机定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学套管穿刺时多种腹壁组织可能同时可见，仅依赖外观特征的组织识别存在困难，因此需要探究空间上下文与时间建模能否提升识别效果。

Method: 回顾性分析71段录像（1,516个标注关键帧、29,151个临床标注扇区），每帧分为28个极坐标网格扇区并归入9类组织；使用颜色、邻域扇区与位置特征的梯度提升发射分类器，并加入学习式时间平滑、空间正则化、解剖有序隐马尔可夫模型和后验融合；采用按录像分组的五折交叉验证及交叉拟合防止泄漏。

Result: 完整流程准确率0.646、宏F1 0.507、宏AUROC 0.882（仅发射模型为0.600、0.440、0.861）；宏F1在五折均提升0.066；标签变化由66.2降至19.5次/扇区-分钟，起病误差中位数由15.58秒降至2.45秒；组织深度编码跨度≥3的帧由53%降至26%（临床标注为22%）；但腹膜前脂肪与肠管识别仍受限，完整模型仍有44%的过早起病比例。

Conclusion: 结合外观、空间上下文与时间约束可改善回顾性组织识别与起病定位；但存在组织特异性局限、残余时间误差及对未来观测的依赖，临床使用前需外部验证与因果模型的前瞻性评估。

Abstract: Objective. To evaluate whether spatial context and temporal modeling improve tissue recognition during optical trocar entry, where multiple abdominal-wall tissues may be visible simultaneously. Methods. We retrospectively analyzed 71 recordings with 1,516 annotated keyframes and 29,151 clinician-annotated sectors. Each frame was divided into 28 polar-grid sectors assigned to nine tissue classes. Color, neighboring-sector, and positional features were used by a gradient-boosted emission classifier. The full pipeline added learned temporal smoothing, spatial regularization, an anatomically ordered hidden Markov model, and posterior blending. Five-fold cross-validation was grouped by recording, with cross-fitting to prevent within-recording leakage. Primary evaluation used clinician-derived targets; ablations assessed classification, probability quality, spatial consistency, and peritoneum-or-deeper onset timing. Results. The complete pipeline achieved accuracy 0.646, macro-F1 0.507, and macro-AUROC 0.882, versus 0.600, 0.440, and 0.861 for emissions alone. Macro-F1 improved by 0.066 across all five folds. Label changes decreased from 66.2 to 19.5 per sector-minute, and median onset error decreased from 15.58 to 2.45 seconds. Frames with a tissue-depth-code span of at least three decreased from 53% to 26%, versus 22% for clinician annotations. Performance remained limited for preperitoneal fat and bowel, and the full model retained a 44% premature-onset proportion. Conclusions. Combining appearance, spatial context, and temporal constraints improved retrospective tissue recognition and onset localization. Tissue-specific limitations, residual timing errors, and reliance on future observations warrant external validation and prospective evaluation of causal models before clinical use.

</details>


### [30] [TartanMatch: Towards Universal Dense Correspondence Across Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14513)
*Hyeokjoon Kwon,Jiting Cai,Ruogu Li,Kritan Bhandari,Geethika Hemkumar,Parv Maheshwari,Yuheng Qiu,Yuchen Zhang,Sebastian Scherer,Wenshan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TartanMatch：一个统一模型，用单一共享网络实现RGB、event、thermal、depth、projected LiDAR五种模态全部25个有序模态对之间的稠密对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数稠密对应方法仅支持RGB-RGB或少量模态对，且为每个模态对单独训练模型随传感器数量增长而扩展性差，还丧失了模态对之间的正迁移潜力。

Method: 将RGB预训练的对应模型通过轻量级模态适配和真实+合成数据混合，适配到单一共享网络中的所有模态对。

Result: 在零样本真实基准上，相比最强基线，跨模态匹配EPE降低61.9%，同模态匹配降低49.6%；联合训练比各对单独训练平均精度更高，尤其困难跨模态对。

Conclusion: 单一模型即可实现广泛多模态覆盖并达到最先进匹配精度，联合训练带来跨模态正迁移。

Abstract: Robotic platforms combine multi-modal sensors to operate across diverse conditions, but most dense correspondence methods are limited to RGB-RGB or a small set of modality pairs. Training a model for each modality pair scales poorly with the number of sensors and forgoes potential positive transfer across modality pairs. We introduce TartanMatch, a unified model for dense correspondence across all 25 ordered pairs among RGB, event, thermal, depth, and projected LiDAR. TartanMatch adapts an RGB-pretrained correspondence model to all modality pairs within one shared network using lightweight modality adaptation and a mixture of real and synthetic data. On zero-shot real-world benchmarks, TartanMatch reduces Endpoint Error (EPE) over the strongest baselines by 61.9% for cross-modal matching and 49.6% for same-modal matching. We further show that joint training across all modality pairs improves average matching accuracy over pair-specific training, especially on challenging cross-modal pairs. These results demonstrate that a single model achieves broad multi-modal coverage and state-of-the-art matching accuracy.

</details>


### [31] [Compositional Concepts Erasing: Disentangling Object-Style via Domain Manifold Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14514)
*Yang Xiao,Kaiyuan Deng,Gen Li,Zinan Ling,Zhiwen Fan,Xiaolong Ma,Bo Hui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Domain Manifold Unlearning (DMU)，用EMA更新的特征代理表示保留的目标物体方向，实现扩散模型中物体-风格组合的选择性遗忘，缓解灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型可能生成违法内容和侵权内容，需要机器遗忘来去除有害概念或版权风格。但现有方法只关注物体级或风格级遗忘，无法处理物体-风格组合遗忘；且多个遗忘请求会导致灾难性遗忘。

Method: DMU利用从扩散中间特征中提取的、经EMA更新的特征代理，来表示被保留的目标物体方向。作为物体的轻量级估计表示，DMU能够选择性地遗忘特定物体中的领域相关成分，同时保留其他语义表示。

Result: 大量实验表明，DMU显著缓解灾难性遗忘，在批量遗忘和顺序遗忘任务上均优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: DMU通过特征代理实现物体-风格组合的精细化选择性遗忘，提升了多组合遗忘场景下的鲁棒性，为扩散模型的安全遗忘提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Diffusion models pose significant risks regarding the generation of illicit content and copyright violations. This has catalyzed the development of machine unlearning to erase harmful concepts or copyrighted styles from a model's generative repertoire without compromising its general performance. However, previous unlearning approaches on diffusion models have largely focused on either object-level or style-level removal and failed to address the challenge of object-style combination unlearning. At the same time, multiple unlearning requests often lead to catastrophic forgetting. To address these challenges, we propose Domain Manifold Unlearning (DMU), which uses an EMA-updated feature proxy extracted from intermediate diffusion features to represent the retained target-object direction. As a lightweight estimating representation of an object, DMU enables selective unlearning of domain-specific components in a specific object while preserving other semantic representations, thereby significantly enhancing robustness in multiple combination unlearning scenarios. Extensive experiments show that DMU significantly mitigates catastrophic forgetting, outperforming state-of-the-art methods of batch unlearning and sequential unlearning.

</details>


### [32] [We Have Been Interpreting Diffusion Transformer Backbone Behaviors Wrong](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14517)
*Benyuan Meng,Longtao Huang,Yang Liu,Liang Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文重新解释扩散模型中激活随层深的倒U形性能曲线：预测目标（如ε、u、x₀）才是核心，语义信号只是中间副产物，且训练目标的改变可预测地影响曲线形状，从而更好地指导REPA等应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社区普遍观察到扩散骨干在各层激活上存在倒U形深度性能曲线，但对其成因的既有解释（信号粒度由粗到细）缺乏DiT骨干中的证据，且研究多为静态视角，未考察不同训练设置下曲线的变化。

Method: 将预测目标置于中心，分析激活的语义信号是预测目标的中间副产物；系统对比不同训练设置（预测目标的选择）对深度曲线形状的影响，并据此预测REPA的最优放置位置。

Result: 提供了不同于"粒度变化"的机制解释，表明预测目标的选择可预测地改变曲线形状；并以此成功预测了REPA的近似最优放置位置。

Conclusion: 更新后的解释使深度曲线现象的应用（如REPA放置）更有理论依据且可预测，代码已开源。

Abstract: During a network forward call, diffusion backbones naturally produce many intermediate activations. If we evaluate each activation against an identical downstream benchmarking protocol, we can plot an inverted-U-shaped curve as the depth profile: the activation's performance goes up first and then down as we go into deeper layers. This is common knowledge in the diffusion model community, which inspires many applications including the widely-adopted REPA. However, the common interpretation of this depth profile may be inaccurate and incomplete: (i) The cause of this curve has long been treated as signal granularity changing from coarse to fine, but there has been little evidence in DiT backbones. (ii) The community has been mainly studying this phenomenon in a static way, without investigating how it changes across different training setups. In this work, we show a different mechanism picture for the profile, where the prediction target (e.g., $ε$, $u$, $x_0$) sits at the center. The semantically meaningful signal is only an intermediate by-product for predicting the target, while the prediction target is the real goal for the backbone. Furthermore, the choice of prediction targets as part of the training setup can predictably affect the curve's shape. With this updated interpretation, many applications of this depth profile can be better grounded and predicted. As an example, we show how to predict the near-optimal placement of REPA based on the prediction target of the training setup. The code is at https://github.com/Darkbblue/dit-compare.

</details>


### [33] [InfraWorld: Motion-Aware World Modeling for Roadside 4D Occupancy Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14544)
*Songkai Wang,Xiaokai Bai,Lei Yang,Hui-liang Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 InfraWorld 世界模型，将路侧 4D 占用预测形式化为选择性状态演化，兼顾全局场景上下文与稀疏局部运动，达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路侧场景全局结构化但局部动态演化，需同时具备全面场景级上下文和精确建模稀疏局部运动的能力，而现有预测范式难以兼顾这两点。

Method: 维护稠密 BEV 状态（全局）+ 持久运动查询（局部）；用动态查询初始化 DQI 在历史运动区域初始化查询；通过长短期时序融合 LST 更新稠密状态；用运动感知查询注入 MAQI 将置信加权查询信息注入对应 BEV token，实现定向动态更新而不扰动全局上下文。

Result: 在 InfraOcc 和 Occ3D-nus 数据集上达到路侧 4D 占用预测 SOTA，并在自车视角设置下仍保持有效性。

Conclusion: InfraWorld 通过选择性状态演化将预测能力集中于演化中的交通区域，有效解决全局上下文与局部动态兼顾的难题。

Abstract: Roadside 4D occupancy forecasting predicts future semantic occupancy in a fixed infrastructure coordinate system. Roadside scenes are globally structured yet locally evolving, requiring comprehensive scene-level context while precisely modeling sparse localized motion. However, existing forecasting paradigms struggle to tackle both requirements. To address this limitation, we propose InfraWorld, a world model formulating roadside occupancy forecasting as selective state evolution. InfraWorld maintains a dense BEV state for the complete scene and persistent motion queries for moving regions, assigning complementary states to global context and local dynamics. Specifically, we propose Dynamic Query Initiation (DQI) to initialize queries in regions supported by motion in the observed history. During rollout, the dense state is updated through Long--Short Temporal Fusion (LST), which combines short-term adjacent-frame continuity with long-term motion trends to preserve stable context while capturing scene evolution. Motion-Aware Query Injection (MAQI) then refines the queries and injects confidence-weighted query information into corresponding BEV tokens, enabling targeted dynamic updates without perturbing global context. Together, InfraWorld concentrates predictive capacity on evolving traffic regions. Extensive experiments on the InfraOcc and Occ3D--nus datasets demonstrate that InfraWorld achieves state-of-the-art performance for roadside 4D occupancy forecasting while retaining its effectiveness under ego-centric settings. Code will be released.

</details>


### [34] [Adversarial Gradient Forcing for Robust Few-Step Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14553)
*Chenxi Xia,Yixuan Xu,Shan Liu,Jiapeng Wang,Maohua Li,Hanlin Tang,Kan Liu,Tao Lan,Weiyi Lu,Xinggao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文识别出自回归视频生成中的"局部历史鲁棒性缺口"问题，并提出对抗梯度强制（AGF）框架，通过对抗扰动自生成历史提升长时程生成的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频生成模型逐个生成视频块，Self Forcing等方法通过对自身rollout训练缓解了曝光偏差，但在长时程生成中仍存在误差累积。作者认为核心问题在于模型对生成历史中的误差高度敏感，微小偏差会被后续生成放大，即"局部历史鲁棒性缺口"这一尚未被充分研究的问题。

Method: 提出对抗梯度强制（AGF）框架，旨在提升少步自回归视频模型对自身生成历史中扰动与累积误差的鲁棒性。AGF利用未来块预测的分布匹配梯度来选择自生成历史的对抗扰动，然后在原始历史与扰动历史上联合训练生成器，以提高未来生成的鲁棒性。

Result: 大量实验表明，AGF在60秒视频生成上取得最高的VBench总分，在4分钟视频生成上取得最佳视觉质量。值得注意的是，AGF仅需5秒rollout进行训练即可实现如此长时程的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: AGF有效解决了自回归视频生成中的局部历史鲁棒性缺口，显著提升长时程生成质量，且训练成本低（仅需5秒rollout），为长视频生成提供了高效鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Autoregressive video generation models generate videos sequentially, with each new block conditioned on previously generated blocks. By training on the model's own rollouts, Self Forcing and its follow-up methods substantially mitigate exposure bias caused by the mismatch between training- and inference-time history distributions. However, these models still suffer from error accumulation during long-horizon generation. We identify a key underlying limitation: the model remains highly sensitive to errors in its generated history, allowing small deviations to be amplified through subsequent generations. We refer to this underexplored problem as the local history robustness gap. To address this gap, we introduce Adversarial Gradient Forcing (AGF), a framework designed to improve the robustness of few-step autoregressive video models to perturbations and accumulated errors in their own generated histories. AGF uses distribution matching gradients from future block predictions to select adversarial perturbations of self-generated histories. It then jointly trains the generator on the original and perturbed histories to improve the robustness of future generation. Extensive experiments show that AGF achieves the highest VBench Total score on 60-second video generation and the best visual quality on four-minute video generation. Notably, AGF achieves such long-horizon robustness while requiring only 5-second rollouts for training.

</details>


### [35] [FrameFold: Response-Guided KV Cache Consolidation for Autoregressive Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14568)
*Muyu Liu,Mingming Liu,Hong Gu,Yuyao Zhang,Qin Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FrameFold 是一种免训练的 KV 缓存合并方法，通过对比合并前后相同历史查询检索到的内容来指导代表性构建与合并选择，在有限缓存预算下提升长视频生成的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频生成中保留全部历史 KV 状态会随视频增长而增加显存和每步注意力开销。固定缓存预算虽能限制成本，但如何在压缩缓存的同时保留支持高质量生成的历史内容仍是难题。现有方法在"保留什么"和"如何减少有用内容损失"上存在共同挑战。

Method: FrameFold 是免训练的 KV 缓存合并方法：利用相邻历史条目间的时间冗余，将其 KV 状态合并为共享代表，同时保留完整空间网格；通过匹配条件注意力响应以闭式求解值合并系数；选择剩余响应失真最低的合并方案，用最近历史查询在合并前后检索内容进行对比来指导构建与选择。

Result: 在匹配 KV 缓存预算下，Self Forcing 与 Causal Forcing 在 30 和 60 秒、LongLive 在 120 秒的实验均显示一致性和视觉质量提升；连续八分钟 Self Forcing rollout 相比基线质量退化更少。

Conclusion: FrameFold 通过基于注意力响应失真的 KV 缓存合并，在有限缓存预算下有效缓解长视频生成的质量退化，且无需训练，兼容多种基线方法。

Abstract: In autoregressive video generation, retaining all historical key-value (KV) states increases memory usage and per-step attention costs as videos grow longer. A fixed cache budget bounds these costs but raises the challenge of preserving historical content that supports consistent, high-quality generation. Existing methods control cache size through history selection or consolidation, yet deciding what to retain and how to limit the loss of useful content remains a shared challenge. Our analysis shows that spatial queries depend on different historical content and that higher current attention coverage does not necessarily yield better long-video quality. We introduce FrameFold, a training-free KV cache consolidation method that compares the content retrieved by the same recent historical queries before and after merging to guide both representative construction and merge selection. Exploiting temporal redundancy between adjacent historical entries, FrameFold merges their KV states into shared representatives while retaining complete spatial grids. It fits value-merging coefficients in closed form by matching conditional attention responses, then selects the merge with the lowest remaining response distortion.
