### [71] [CowCare: A Unified Visual Framework for Viewpoint-Robust Cattle Tracking and Spatiotemporal Heat-Stress Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15012)
*Xin Liu,Weiran Li,Minghao Zhu,Mina Han,Zhenbo Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CowCare统一框架，将视角鲁棒的多相机跟踪（CowTrack）与时空热应激识别（TSDF-Net）结合，实现奶牛个体自动化健康监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精准畜牧业中奶牛福利与热应激早期检测需可靠的个体监测，但原始多相机视频难以自动诊断：跟踪与识别流程割裂、密集遮挡与跨相机视角变化导致身份碎片化，加上相机振动等环境噪声和农业数据稀缺，细微热应激线索（如急促呼吸、腹部起伏）难以捕捉。

Method: CowCare包含两个模块：跟踪模块CowTrack通过视角解耦聚合网络（VDA-Net）在遮挡下聚合局部纹理，并用视角解耦Transformer（VDT）抑制瞬态视角变化，同时引入因果在线自监督实现动态场景自适应；识别模块TSDF-Net通过时序稳定聚合模块（TSAM）抑制高频抖动，并用扩散语义先验（DSP）借助冻结预训练扩散模型增强细微行为信号、缓解过拟合。

Result: 在两个真实农场基准上评测：CowTrack在MVCow上取得83.48% HOTA和87.72% IDF1的全局多目标跟踪性能；TSDF-Net在HSCow上取得87.72% Top-1准确率。

Conclusion: CowCare是面向自动化牲畜健康监测的鲁棒端到端框架，有效连接低级视觉特征与高级行为诊断。

Abstract: Reliable individual monitoring is essential for dairy cattle welfare and early heat stress detection in Precision Livestock Farming (PLF). However, translating raw multi-camera videos into automated diagnostics remains challenging due to disconnected tracking and recognition pipelines and complex physical environments. Dense mutual occlusions and cross-camera viewpoint variations fragment identities, while environmental noise, including camera vibrations, and limited agricultural data obscure subtle heat stress cues such as rapid breathing and flank undulations. To bridge low-level visual features and high-level behavioral diagnostics, we propose CowCare, a unified framework combining viewpoint-robust tracking with spatiotemporal heat stress recognition. Its tracking module, CowTrack, maintains cross-camera identity consistency through a View-Decoupled Aggregation Network (VDA-Net). Instead of relying on static global representations, VDA-Net aggregates localized textures under heavy occlusion and uses a Viewpoint Decoupling Transformer (VDT) to suppress transient viewpoint variations. CowTrack further incorporates a causal online self-supervision mechanism for continuous adaptation to dynamic farm scenes. The resulting tracklets are subsequently processed by TSDF-Net, a fine-grained video understanding network. Its Temporal Stabilization and Aggregation Module (TSAM) suppresses high-frequency jitter, while a Diffusion Semantic Prior (DSP) uses frozen pre-trained diffusion semantics to enhance subtle behavioral signals and mitigate overfitting. Extensive evaluations on two real-world farm benchmarks demonstrate the framework's effectiveness: CowTrack achieves 83.48% HOTA and 87.72% IDF1 for global multi-object tracking on MVCow, and TSDF-Net achieves 87.72% Top-1 accuracy on HSCow. These results establish CowCare as a robust end-to-end framework for automated livestock health monitoring.

</details>


### [72] [Appearance Compensation in Volume Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15030)
*Jianghong Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究"能重现所有外部RGB视图的体密度是否可能错误"，证明外观可补偿密度变化，导致密度不可唯一识别，并给出SH2下的显式物理歧义构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在发射-吸收渲染中，仅凭外部RGB视图是否能唯一确定体密度？本文质疑"渲染准确即密度正确"的假设，探究密度可恢复性依赖哪些因素。

Method: 在独立参数化、物理有界的有效源下研究发射-吸收渲染；用共享空间外观场刻画残余密度信息；通过有限阶球谐源重拟合给出最优密度增益，采用耦合张量规范与物理局域packets识别极限非线性逆系数；构造SH2精确物理歧义并与有限表示、连续像素测量及密度敏感采集相联系。

Result: 1) 有限阶球谐重拟合后给出最优密度-only增益，其倒数为锐利极限非线性逆系数；2) 固定密度下对残差集各向同性源可实现正增益，且增长角容量可隐藏固定非零密度误差同时保持物理性与光滑性约束；3) 构造SH2精确歧义：中心密度加倍、相对密度L²误差超14.52%，而所有外部RGB射线不变。实验验证SH0/1/2拟合区分、传输测量区分相同RGB场等问题。

Conclusion: 渲染精度本身不能证明密度正确；密度恢复依赖外观容量、空间分辨率与测量设计。局部可识别性与容量一致的恢复模量缺失可共存。

Abstract: Can a volume that reproduces every external RGB view still have an incorrect density? We study this question in emission-absorption rendering with an independently parameterized, physically bounded effective source. Appearance compensates a density change by replacing the radiance that it removes from the rest of each ray. We characterize the surviving density information with a shared spatial appearance field. Our first result is an optimal density-only gain after finite-degree spherical-harmonic source refitting. A coupled tensor gauge and physical localized packets identify the sharp limiting nonlinear inverse coefficient as its reciprocal. Our second result realizes positive gain at every fixed finite degree for a residual set of isotropic spatial sources at one fixed density. At the same references, growing angular capacity hides a fixed nonzero density error while retaining physicality and any prescribed finite set of smoothness bounds. Local identifiability and the absence of a capacity-uniform recovery modulus therefore coexist. Our third result constructs an exact physical SH2 ambiguity: central density doubles and relative density $L^2$ error exceeds $14.52\%$, yet every external RGB ray is unchanged. We connect these continuum results to shared finite representations, continuous pixel measurements and density-sensitive acquisition. Complete-class certificates separate SH0, SH1 and SH2 fits at one learned density. Finite-camera results expose the distinct role of spatial capacity, and transmission measurements separate otherwise identical RGB fields. Controlled training tests and captured-smoke profiles supply mechanism evidence with explicit observation scopes. The results show why rendering accuracy alone cannot certify density: recovery also depends on appearance capacity, spatial resolution and measurement design.

</details>


### [73] [What Really Works for Universal Multimodal Embeddings? A Controlled Study of Contrastive Training Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15035)
*Duo Li,Haojun Pan,Zuhao Yang,Xintong Wang,Chengfu Huo,Shijian Lu,Liang Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 系统评估多模态对比学习策略，发现多数策略增益有限甚至有害，并提出MLLM-as-a-Judge诊断框架揭示三种失效模式与最佳组合配方。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对比学习策略（负样本选择、批次构建、梯度调制）众多，但在受控可复现条件下其真实有效性缺乏理解，因此需要回答：复杂对比策略是否稳定带来下游增益？

Method: 在MMEB上以统一基准框架系统评估代表性策略；引入MLLM-as-a-Judge诊断框架，将top-1检索行为分解为标注正样本、假阴性（未标注的有效匹配）和困难/简单负样本错误。

Result: 多数策略相对标准对齐基线仅有边际增益，部分甚至降低性能。三点洞察：(1)梯度失真：对高相似负样本加权梯度会损害泛化；(2)分布错觉：正负相似度分布分离更宽未必提升检索；(3)协同训练：假阴性缓解或困难批次单独收益有限，三者结合达到最佳，Qwen2-VL-2B在MMEB平均提升6.0%。

Conclusion: 研究揭示流行对比策略的真实效果，为训练通用多模态嵌入模型提供可操作的配方。

Abstract: Contrastive learning has become the standard paradigm for multimodal representation learning, aligning semantically matched pairs while separating mismatched ones in a shared embedding space. Although various contrastive training strategies have been proposed across negative selection, batch construction, and gradient modulation, their actual effectiveness under controlled, reproducible conditions remains poorly understood. This motivates a fundamental question: Do sophisticated contrastive strategies consistently bring meaningful downstream gains in multimodal embedding learning? To address this, we systematically evaluate representative strategies under a unified benchmarking framework on MMEB. Surprisingly, direct evaluations reveal that most existing strategies offer marginal gains over a standard aligned baseline, with several even degrading overall performance. To uncover the underlying failure modes, we introduce an MLLM-as-a-Judge diagnostic framework that dissects top-1 retrieval behaviors into labeled positives, false negatives (unlabeled valid matches), and hard/easy negative errors. Our systematic analysis yields three key insights: (1) Gradient distortion: Upweighting gradients from high-similarity negatives consistently hurts downstream generalization; (2) Distribution illusion: Wider separation between positive and negative similarity distributions does not necessarily translate to better retrieval performance; and (3) Synergistic training: While false-negative mitigation or hard-batch construction yields limited gains in isolation, integrating hard-negative mining, false-negative mitigation, and hard-batch construction achieves optimal performance, delivering an average improvement of 6.0% on MMEB with Qwen2-VL-2B. Overall, our findings demystify popular contrastive strategies and provide actionable recipes for training universal multimodal embedding models.

</details>


### [74] [Use Of Convolutional Neural Networks In Detection Of Branch Dieback Disease In Pequi Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15049)
*Ronilson Rodrigues Pinho,Elua Ramos Coutinho,Nilza de Lima Pereira Sales,Sildenir Aalves Ribeiro,Sergio Manuel Serra da Cruz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 评估比较AlexNet、Inception、LeNet、VGG16、MobileNet五种CNN架构，用于诊断pequi树的枝枯病。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 枝枯病严重威胁pequi树的生长发育和果实产量，造成重大经济和生态损失，需要有效的诊断方法。

Method: 使用Python库（Scikit-learn、NumPy、TensorFlow、Matplotlib）实现计算机视觉技术，CNN模型分析叶片、枝条和花穗图像以检测纹理和颜色等病理模式。

Result: 通过对比分析识别出能最有效区分健康与患病植株的CNN架构。

Conclusion: 该对比分析为pequi树枝枯病的诊断确定了最优CNN架构。

Abstract: This study aims to evaluate and compare convolutional neural network (CNN) architectures, specifically AlexNet, Inception, LeNet, VGG16, and MobileNet, for diagnosing branch dieback (seca-dos-ponteiros) in pequi trees (Caryocar brasiliense). This disease represents a severe threat to tree development and fruit yield, causing substantial economic and ecological losses. The methodology implements computer vision techniques using Python libraries, including Scikit-learn, NumPy, TensorFlow, and Matplotlib, to process and evaluate visual data. The CNN models analyze images of leaves, branches, and flower spikes to detect pathological patterns, such as alterations in texture and coloration. Ultimately, this comparative analysis identifies the most effective architecture for accurately distinguishing healthy plants from diseased ones.

</details>


### [75] [Deep Learning-based Semantic Segmentation of Dental Radiographs: Comparative Evaluation of PSPNet, DeepLabv3, UNet, and UNet++](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15061)
*Malithi Nawarathne,Nadeesha Herath Mudiyanselage Lihini Sangeetha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对比了PSPNet、DeepLabV3、UNet和UNet++四种分割模型在牙科X光图像上的性能，在受控实验条件下发现UNet++表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 牙科X光图像分割对疾病诊断和临床决策至关重要，但因图像低对比度、噪声、质量差异等因素而具有挑战性。以往研究多用CNN模型，但实验设置差异导致难以直接比较，因此需要在公平受控条件下对比不同分割模型。

Method: 使用1269张牙科X光图像，先进行一系列预处理，然后在公平受控的实验设置下对比PSPNet、DeepLabV3、UNet、UNet++四种分割模型，考察不同图像尺寸和批大小的影响。

Result: UNet++表现最优，在图像尺寸300x300、批大小16时取得最高IoU 0.6719和DICE 0.7869。研究还探讨了图像分辨率和批大小对不同架构性能与准确率的影响。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了不同分割模型的优势，并展示了基于深度学习的语义分割方法在自动化牙科X光分析中的潜力。

Abstract: Accurate and efficient analysis of dental radiographs has become increasingly important for disease diagnosis and clinical decision making. In particular, dental radiographs segmentation plays a critical role as it is challenging due to several reasons, such as low contrast, noise in the images, variations in the image quality, etc. Previous studies have primarily addressed these challenges using convolutional neural networks (CNN)-based models. However, direct comparison of these segmentation models is still challenging due to the variations among the experimental settings. Therefore, we mainly compared four segmentation models, including Pyramid Scene Parsing Network (PSPNet), DeepLabV3, UNet, and UNet++, through a fair, controlled experimentation setting under varying conditions such as image sizes and batch sizes. For this, we used 1269 dental radiography images, and prior to training, we implemented a series of preprocessing techniques. To assess the models, we used several metrics, including Dice coefficient (DICE), Intersection over Union (IoU), accuracy, precision, training and validation losses. According to our results, UNet++ demonstrated the highest IoU score of 0.6719, and a DICE value of 0.7869, showing superior results in terms of segmentation, specifically when configured with an image size of 300x300 and a batch size of 16. In addition, we also explored the effect of image resolutions and batch sizes on model performance and accuracy under different architectures. Therefore, this study provides insights into the strengths of different segmentation models and the potential of deep learning-based semantic segmentation methods for improving automated dental radiograph analysis.

</details>


### [76] [Global Disaster Risk Assessment Using World Risk Index Data: A Geospatial and Visual Analytics Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15064)
*Malithi Nawarathne,Nadeesha Herath Mudiyanselage,Lihini Sangeetha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究利用Python数据可视化技术分析2000–2025年世界风险指数(WRI)数据，识别出菲律宾、墨西哥、印尼和印度为高WRI国家，为防灾准备提供洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球面临资源枯竭、粮食安全、贫困、城市化、移民和环境等多重威胁，WRI可衡量各国自然灾害及风险脆弱性，提前识别高风险区域以便采取应对措施。

Method: 分析2000至2025年相关WRI数据，采用数据可视化技术，使用Matplotlib、Seaborn和Geopandas等Python库进行高效分析。

Result: 分析发现菲律宾、墨西哥、印度尼西亚和印度具有较高的WRI指数值，表明这些国家应做好自然灾害准备。

Conclusion: 本研究通过数据可视化技术为理解自然灾害与威胁提供洞察，有助于采取必要防范措施。

Abstract: The world has seen several threats that include resource depletion, food security, poverty and inequality, difficulties associated with urbanization, migration, and environmental effects. The World Risk Index (WRI) is a metric that is used to measure the susceptibility of nations against natural disasters and related risks that is reported by the Humanitarian Data Exchange. The WRI is comprised of factors such as susceptibility, vulnerability to natural hazards, and coping mechanisms. The WRI index has allowed to recognize regions in advance in order to face threats and situations beforehand. This study was conducted by analyzing the data that was related to years from 2000 to 2025 using data visualization techniques. Python related libraries of Matplotlib, Seaborn, and Geopandas were used due to their efficiency and usability in analyzing WRI data. As per the analysis, it was identified that countries of Philippines, Mexico, Indonesia, and India were recorded with high WRI index values that showed these nations should be prepared for natural disasters and situations. Hence, this study has provided insights using data visualization techniques in understanding natural disasters and threats so that necessary measures can be taken

</details>


### [77] [BrainAid: Efficient Brain Tumor Detection Using Lightweight CNNs and Explainable AI for Resource-Constrained Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15077)
*Sai Sriram Gonthina,Ross Gore,Bikash Chandra Singh,Pronaya Bhattacharya,Sachin Shetty,Abhishek Phadke,Sandip Roy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BrainAid，结合CNN与ViT对四类脑肿瘤MRI进行分类，精度98%，并集成XAI、部署于树莓派实现低资源实时诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脑肿瘤分类依赖专家人工判读MRI，主观性强且资源消耗大导致诊断延迟；深度学习模型虽有效但计算需求高，亟需高效、可实时部署的诊断工具，尤适用于资源受限地区。

Method: BrainAid融合卷积神经网络（CNN）与视觉Transformer（ViT），对胶质瘤、脑膜瘤、垂体瘤和无肿瘤四类MRI进行多分类，并集成可解释AI（XAI）提升可解释性，最终部署于Raspberry Pi边缘设备。

Result: 在使用公开数据集上，模型达到98%的精确率、98%的F1分数和98%的准确率，性能优于VGG16、Inception-V3、ResNet-50等标准模型；在Raspberry Pi上运行验证了低资源环境下的效率。

Conclusion: 该系统证明了基于边缘设备的实时脑肿瘤分类可行性，可为医务人员提供快速诊断支持，改善资源有限及欠发达地区的患者护理。

Abstract: A brain tumor is an unusual growth of cells in the brain that can be cancerous or not, affecting normal brain function and causing serious risk to health. Existing solutions for brain tumor classification, such as expert manual evaluations of magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) scans, are generally inconsistent as different professionals have different criteria and can be resource-intensive, leading to delayed diagnoses. Additionally, while deep learning models have demonstrated potential, they typically require significant computational resources, motivating the need for more efficient and accessible diagnostic tools that can be deployed in real-time healthcare settings. To address these limitations, this study presents BrainAid, which makes efficient use of Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) and Vision Transformers (ViTs) for effective classification of four-class classification of glioma, meningioma, pituitary tumor, and no-tumor MRI scans, outperforming all the standard models such as VGG16, Inception-V3, ResNet-50 and so, while integrating Explainable AI (XAI) to improve interpretability. The BrainAid is trained to classify tumors with a high precision of 98%, achieving an F1 score of 98% and an accuracy of 98% using publicly available dataset. The model is deployed on a Raspberry Pi, demonstrating efficiency in low-resource environments. This system shows the feasibility of real-time, edge-based tumor classification, providing quick diagnostic support for medical professionals and enhancing patient care in resource-limited healthcare facilities and underserved areas.

</details>


### [78] [Region Aware Squeeze-and-Excitation Networks for Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15079)
*Jack Roberts,Praneeth Tummala,Kavi Sarna,Jeova Farias Sales Rocha Neto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将SE的全局池化替换为空间引导池化，实现区域感知的通道重校准，包含BASE、SPASE、MAPSE、PLEASE四个模块，在分割和分类任务上多数超越SE及空间-通道注意力方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SE模块通过通道依赖建模提升卷积网络性能，但全局平均池化将空间结构压缩为单通道描述符，限制了其在图像分割等密集预测任务上的效果。

Method: 将SE的全局池化推广为空间引导池化：BASE用规则网格实现Tiled Squeeze-and-Excite；SPASE基于SLIC超像素池化；MAPSE从冻结通用网络生成的语义图获取区域；PLEASE通过轻量分配网络端到端学习软区域分配。每个通道在每个区域得到一个门控，使像素注意力反映其区域归属，从而衔接通道与空间注意力。

Result: 在CUB、DUTS、ECSSD的二值分割，PASCAL VOC 2012、ADE20K的语义分割，以及mini-ImageNet分类任务上，本文模块在多数基准上优于SE及空间-通道注意力变体，取得大部分最佳结果。模块具有互补的计算权衡：部分几乎不增加参数，部分不增加可测量延迟。PLEASE的软分配作为前向传播副产物提供结构化空间可解释性。

Conclusion: 用区域感知的空间引导池化替代全局池化，可有效提升通道重校准在密集预测任务中的性能，并在多任务上验证了其有效性与计算灵活性。

Abstract: Squeeze-and-Excitation (SE) modules improve convolutional networks by modeling channel-wise dependencies, but global average pooling collapses spatial structure into a single per-channel descriptor, limiting their effectiveness for dense prediction tasks such as image segmentation. We generalize SE by replacing global pooling with spatially guided pooling, enabling region-aware channel recalibration through four modules with complementary cost profiles. BASE formalizes Tiled Squeeze-and-Excite using a regular grid; SPASE pools over SLIC superpixels for image-adaptive regions; MAPSE obtains regions from semantic maps produced by a frozen, general-purpose network; and PLEASE learns soft region assignments end-to-end through a lightweight assignment network. Each channel receives one gate per region, so attention at each pixel reflects its region membership, bridging channel and spatial attention. PLEASE also produces its soft assignments as a byproduct of the forward pass, providing structured spatial interpretability without post-hoc analysis. The modules offer complementary computational tradeoffs: some add essentially no parameters, while others add no measurable latency. Across binary segmentation on CUB, DUTS, and ECSSD, semantic segmentation on PASCAL VOC 2012 and ADE20K, and classification on mini-ImageNet, our modules outperform SE and related spatial-channel attention variants on most benchmarks, achieving the majority of best results.

</details>


### [79] [VLCQ: Post-training quantization for deep neural networks using variable length coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15092)
*Reem Abdel-Salam,Ahmed H. Abdel-Gawad,Amr G. Wassal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VLCQ，一种放宽定点编码约束、基于可变长编码的后训练量化方法，在无需训练数据和微调的情况下实现更高压缩率和更优精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高性能后训练量化多依赖INT8定点整数，难以在更低比特下保持性能；追求更高压缩时精度显著下降。需一种无需原始数据或重训练、又能在低比特下保持精度的灵活量化方案。

Method: VLCQ放宽定点编码约束，采用可变长编码（variable-length encoding）来更灵活地探索量化方案，属于后训练量化，不需训练数据或模型微调。

Result: 在图像分类、分割和检测任务上，VLCQ在压缩率与精度上优于SOTA后训练量化方法。逐通道量化下超越FP32精度和PWLQ，较FP32最高6倍压缩、较PWLQ最高1.7倍；优先压缩时较FP32最高12.25倍压缩且性能下降小于4%。逐张量量化下与DFQ相当，并更灵活支持更低比特率。

Conclusion: VLCQ通过可变长编码突破定点限制，在不访问训练数据、不微调的前提下实现了优于现有方法的压缩率与精度权衡，适用于多种任务和模型。

Abstract: Quantization plays a crucial role in efficiently deploying deep learning models on resource-constrained devices. Post-training quantization does not require access to the original dataset or retraining the full model. Current methods that achieve high performance close to baseline results typically require INT8 fixed-point integers. However, achieving higher model compression with lower bit-widths often leads to significant performance degradation. In this paper, we propose VLCQ, a post-training quantization method that relaxes the fixed-point encoding constraint, enabling more flexible exploration of quantization techniques through variable-length encoding. This approach achieves competitive or improved performance while reducing bit-widths, without requiring access to training data or model fine-tuning. Extensive experiments on image classification, segmentation, and object detection demonstrate that VLCQ improves model compression and accuracy compared with state-of-the-art post-training quantization approaches. For per-channel quantization, VLCQ surpasses FP32 accuracy and Piece-Wise Linear Quantization (PWLQ) on most evaluated models, achieving up to 6x compression compared with FP32 and up to 1.7x compared with PWLQ. When prioritizing compression with limited accuracy loss, VLCQ achieves up to 12.25x compression compared with FP32 with less than 4% performance degradation. For per-tensor quantization, VLCQ is competitive with Data-Free Quantization (DFQ), while providing greater flexibility to achieve lower bit rates across different tasks and models.

</details>


### [80] [TIDE: Trajectory-Informed Skill Distillation for Efficient Text-to-Video Prompt Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15098)
*Chengxing Zhou,Yuliang Huang,Jingwei Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TIDE 将昂贵的迭代提示优化搜索蒸馏为可复用、可解释的提示改写 Skill，用一次 LLM 调用加一次生成即可达到接近搜索方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: T2V 模型对提示具体性高度敏感，但现有提示优化需在推理时反复生成视频并用视觉语言模型评估，成本高昂；作者希望将这一昂贵搜索过程蒸馏为可解释、可复用的改写能力。

Method: 将提示改写建模为一组源条件规则模块，每个模块针对一个语义维度。蒸馏后冻结的 Skill 激活相关规则，在单次 LLM 调用中生成正、负提示，随后仅做一次 T2V 生成，无需测试时视觉搜索或骨干微调。仅用 100 条提示、14 小时进化得到。

Result: 提示优化效果与计算昂贵的搜索方法相当；迁移到未见过的视频生成模型时，优于专为这些模型设计或训练的方法。

Conclusion: TIDE 证明可将昂贵的推理时提示搜索蒸馏为轻量、可迁移的提示改写 Skill，提升 T2V 生成效率与泛化性。代码已开源。

Abstract: Text-to-video (T2V) models are highly sensitive to prompt specificity, while iterative prompt optimization typically improves generation quality only through repeated video generation and vision-language model evaluation at inference time. We propose TIDE, a framework that distills this costly search process into an interpretable and reusable prompt-rewriting Skill. TIDE formulates prompt rewriting as a set of source-conditioned rule modules, each targeting a distinct semantic dimension. Once distilled, the frozen Skill activates the relevant rules and generates both positive and negative prompts in a single LLM call, followed by only one T2V generation, without test-time visual search or backbone fine-tuning. Evolved using only 100 prompts over 14 hours, TIDE achieves prompt-optimization performance comparable to computationally expensive search-based methods. Moreover, when transferred to previously unseen video-generation models, TIDE outperforms existing approaches specifically designed or trained for those models. Code is available at https://github.com/xiningin/TIDE_T2V.

</details>


### [81] [Drive3R: Block-wise Streaming Geometric Foundation Models for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15112)
*Tianyi Yang,Haojun Yang,Jipeng Xiao,Xuesong Chen,Shaoshuai Shi,Li Jiang,Zhipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Drive3R，一种分块流式几何基础模型，通过分块因果注意力与置信度感知重锚定，实现长时程自动驾驶序列的高效高保真3D重建，并在多个数据集上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭环仿真依赖密集多视角3D重建，但现有前馈几何基础模型应用于连续长时程驾驶序列时，受GPU显存限制和全局注意力二次复杂度制约。已有扩展策略存在缺陷：因果流式会产生累积尺度漂移与轨迹偏移，"分块再对齐"启发式则割裂全局上下文、导致轨迹不连续。

Method: Drive3R采用分块流式架构：1) 分块因果注意力，既解决全双向注意力的可扩展性瓶颈，又避免纯因果流式丢失局部未来上下文；2) 置信度感知重锚定，通过检测不可靠的流式状态、重初始化KV缓存，并经SE(3)对齐拼接刷新后的片段，缓解长程漂移。

Result: 在nuScenes、KITTI和Waymo上取得新SOTA。nuScenes上相比DVGT-2基线：AbsRel由0.1806降至0.1081，点云Accuracy由0.6750升至0.5344，ATE由1.5812降至0.9092。

Conclusion: Drive3R通过分块因果注意力与置信度感知重锚定，有效解决了长时程驾驶序列3D重建的可扩展性与漂移问题，为高保真闭环仿真提供了新的几何基础模型方案。

Abstract: High-fidelity closed-loop simulation for autonomous driving increasingly relies on dense multi-view 3D reconstruction. While recent feed-forward geometric foundation models have revolutionized per-scene optimization, applying them to continuous, long-horizon driving sequences is computationally intractable due to GPU memory limits and the quadratic complexity of global attention. Existing scaling strategies are fundamentally limited, as causal streaming suffers from compounding scale drift and trajectory deviation, while rigid "chunk-then-align" heuristics fragment global context and produce trajectory discontinuities. To address these limitations, we introduce Drive3R, a block-wise streaming geometric foundation model. Its block-wise causal attention resolves the scalability bottleneck of fully bidirectional attention while avoiding the loss of local future context in purely causal streaming. Its confidence-aware re-anchoring further mitigates long-range drift by detecting unreliable streaming states, re-initializing the KV cache, and stitching refreshed segments through SE(3) alignment. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Drive3R establishes a new state-of-the-art on nuScenes, KITTI, and Waymo. Specifically, on nuScenes, it improves over the recent DVGT-2 baseline from 0.1806 to 0.1081 in AbsRel, from 0.6750 to 0.5344 in point-cloud Accuracy, and from 1.5812 to 0.9092 in ATE.

</details>


### [82] [RIECNN: Real-Time Image Enhanced CNN for Traffic Sign Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15127)
*Reem Abdel-Salam,Rana Mostafa,Ahmed H. Abdel-Gawad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Real-Time Image Enhanced CNN (RIE-CNN)，用于实时交通标志识别，在三个基准上取得最高识别率并保持实时性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通标志识别对自动驾驶安全至关重要，但现有深度学习算法受环境影响大、实时性差，难以应对多数据集场景。

Method: 提出实时图像增强CNN（RIE-CNN），通过图像增强结合CNN处理多种交通标志数据集。

Result: 在GTSRB、BTSC、rMASTIF上分别达到99.75%、99.25%、99.55%识别率；预处理与推理总时间不超过1.3 ms/图。

Conclusion: 方法在多个基准上实现高精度与实时性能，并对亮度和对比度变化等环境挑战具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: Traffic sign recognition plays a crucial role in the development of autonomous cars to reduce the accident rate and promote road safety. It has been a necessity to address traffic signs that are affected significantly by the environment as well as poor real-time performance for deep-learning state-of-the-art algorithms. In this paper, we introduce Real-Time Image Enhanced CNN ({\our}) for Traffic Sign Recognition. {\our} is a real-time, novel approach that tackles multiple, diverse traffic sign datasets, and out-performs the state-of-the-art architectures in terms of recognition rate and execution time. Experiments are conducted using the German Traffic Sign Benchmark (GTSRB), the Belgium Traffic Sign Classification (BTSC), and the Croatian Traffic Sign (rMASTIF) benchmark. Experimental results show that our approach has achieved the highest recognition rate for all Benchmarks, achieving a recognition accuracy of 99.75\% for GTSRB, 99.25\% for BTSC and 99.55\% for rMASTIF. In terms of latency and meeting the real-time constraint, the pre-processing time and inference time together do not exceed 1.3 ms per image. Not only have our proposed approach achieved remarkably high accuracy with real-time performance, but it also demonstrated robustness against traffic sign recognition challenges such as brightness and contrast variations in the environment.

</details>


### [83] [TRIG: Toward Measuring Texture Realism in Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15164)
*Zhiwei Xu,Jiali Cui,Zhiqiang Lao,Heather Yu,Liang Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TRIG指标，用纹理数据集上预训练的GAN判别器作骨干，衡量生成图像的纹理真实感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成图像质量评价指标的骨干网多针对分类或图文对齐等任务训练，这类目标鼓励对携带纹理的低层变化保持不变性，特征主要编码语义与全局结构，难以捕捉纹理真实感差异。

Method: TRIG以在纹理数据集上预训练的GAN判别器为骨干（提供自然纹理局部统计先验），微调一个plausibility head识别局部纹理退化，同时将自然的失焦与传感器噪声作为正例；在该骨干特征上计算真实图像参考集与生成图像集之间的标准分布距离。

Result: 在多个生成模型上应用TRIG，其与人类真实感评分的吻合度优于基线指标。

Conclusion: 用面向纹理的GAN判别器骨干替代通用骨干，可更显式地捕捉纹理差异，使评价指标更贴合人的真实感判断。

Abstract: This paper studies the backbone used by metrics for evaluating the quality of generated images. Although generative models have advanced rapidly, some outputs still exhibit unrealistic texture characteristics, and these differences are difficult to measure. Widely used evaluation metrics provide limited insight into texture realism, since they often rely on a backbone trained for another objective, such as image classification or image-text alignment. Such objectives typically encourage invariance to low-level variations that carry texture, so the resulting features primarily encode semantic and global structure. As a result, texture-specific discrepancies may not be explicitly captured. We propose TRIG, a metric directed at the texture realism of generated images. Its backbone is instead the discriminator of a generative adversarial network pre-trained on a texture dataset, which provides a learned prior for the local statistics of natural textures. A plausibility head is fine-tuned to identify local texture degradations, while preserving natural defocus and sensor noise as positive cases. On the features of this backbone, we compute a standard distributional distance between a reference set of real images and a set of generated ones. We apply TRIG to several generative models and observe closer agreement with human realness ratings than baseline metrics.

</details>


### [84] [ExeWorldBench: Benchmarking Code-Based Executable 3D World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15166)
*Xinge Peng,Zhen Fang,Ruibing Wang,Yongxuan Lv,Haodong Li,Guohui Zhang,Xiaoxiao Ma,Yiting Lu,Lin Chen,Zehui Che,Feng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ExeWorldBench，首个通过代码化3D世界构建来综合评估"世界理解"与"可执行实现"能力的基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体正从复现世界外观转向构建可执行世界。这需要"可执行世界建模"，整合世界理解与可执行实现两大能力，但现有基准仅碎片化评估物理推理、视觉保真或行为正确性，缺乏对二者整合的评估。

Method: 构建 ExeWorldBench，包含三个递进难度任务：机制可执行性（从视觉观测实现单一机制）、组合可执行性（含空间、机械、因果依赖的多阶段系统）、意图驱动可执行世界合成（从高层目标与物理需求构建可执行世界）。

Result: 在前沿模型上的大量实验揭示了可执行世界建模存在显著局限。

Conclusion: 该基准为发展更具物理基础与因果一致性的世界构建系统提供了洞见。

Abstract: Coding agents are increasingly evolving beyond reproducing what a world looks like toward constructing executable worlds that capture how a world works. This shift calls for executable world modeling, which integrates two core capabilities: world understanding, reasoning about the underlying structural, mechanical, and causal principles of a world, and executable realization, translating that understanding into a code-based system whose behavior emerges from physical interactions. However, existing benchmarks provide only fragmented evaluations centered on physical reasoning, visual fidelity, or behavioral correctness, leaving the integration of these capabilities insufficiently assessed. To bridge this gap, we introduce ExeWorldBench, to our knowledge the first benchmark to comprehensively evaluate world understanding and executable realization through code-based 3D world construction. ExeWorldBench comprises three progressively challenging tasks: Mechanism Executability, which evaluates the realization of individual mechanisms from visual observations; Compositional Executability, which extends to multi-stage systems with spatial, mechanical, and causal dependencies; and Intent-Driven Executable World Synthesis, which requires constructing executable worlds from high-level goals and physical requirements. Extensive experiments on frontier models reveal substantial limitations in executable world modeling, offering insights into the development of more physically grounded and causally coherent world construction systems.

</details>


### [85] [GeoBridge: Evolving Transferable Decoder Topologies for Geometric Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15280)
*Rui Wang,Haoyu Zhang,Ran Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoBridge提出进化框架，搜索可迁移的解码器拓扑，一次搜索可用于深度估计与高斯重建等多个几何视觉任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度估计与高斯重建都需要多尺度特征处理，但二者接口与训练目标不同；仅搜索基础配置无法改变变换的组织结构，解码器内部计算能否一次优化并复用仍是结构设计问题。

Method: 1) 通过残差、串行、并行的可配置基元组合，使阶段内部拓扑可搜索，保形组合保持固定多尺度骨架接口；2) 用功能树编码支持阶段感知交叉与分层变异，以梯度敏感度对齐代理免训练评估候选，并用多目标进化平衡代理与计算、参数开销；3) 在目标系统中以任务特定预测接口实例化并训练选中拓扑。

Result: 单次源搜索约12 GPU小时；GeoBridgeNet-A3在相同编码器和训练协议下于NYU Depth V2和KITTI上取得四个适配解码器基线中最佳深度精度；同一拓扑在RealEstate10K高斯新视角合成上达27.11 dB PSNR，其精炼表示还支持LERF开放词汇分割。

Conclusion: 结果表明解码器拓扑可作为可复用设计对象，在所评估的几何视觉系统中跨任务迁移有效。

Abstract: Depth estimation and Gaussian reconstruction share a need for multi-scale feature processing, yet use different prediction interfaces and training objectives. Whether a decoder's internal computation can be optimized once and reused across these systems remains a structural design question. Search over primitive configurations alone leaves the organization of transformations fixed. To address this gap, we propose GeoBridge, an evolutionary framework for transferable decoder topologies. First, GeoBridge makes stage-internal topology searchable through residual, sequential, and parallel composition of configurable primitives. Shape-preserving composition retains the stage interfaces of a fixed multi-scale scaffold. Second, a functional-tree encoding supports stage-aware crossover and hierarchical mutation, while a gradient sensitivity-alignment proxy evaluates candidates without training. Multi-objective evolution balances this proxy with computation and parameter cost. Third, selected topologies are instantiated and trained in target systems with task-specific prediction interfaces. A single source search takes approximately 12 GPU hours. GeoBridgeNet-A3 achieves the best depth accuracy among four adapted decoder baselines under a common encoder and training protocol on NYU Depth V2 and KITTI. The same topology reaches 27.11 dB PSNR in Gaussian novel-view synthesis on RealEstate10K, and its refined representations support open-vocabulary segmentation on LERF. These results support decoder topology as a reusable design object across the evaluated geometric vision systems. Code and pretrained weights are available at https://github.com/EMI-Group/GeoBridge-NAS.

</details>


### [86] [Protein Crystallization Segmentation and Classification Using Subordinate Color Channel in Fluorescence Microscopy Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15286)
*Truong X. Tran,Marc L. Pusey,Ramazan S. Aygun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于发射光谱次属颜色（长波长）的百分位强度分割方法，用于痕量荧光标记（TFL'd）蛋白质结晶图像检测。相比主颜色通道，该方法大幅降低误分类率并提升准确率，达到约94%准确率，且经两种新蛋白湿实验验证，误分类率低于1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质晶体检测的准确性对高通量自动化系统至关重要。痕量荧光标记虽能突出晶体，但荧光染料的反射与发射并非总来自晶体区域，因此仅分析发射光谱峰值波长往往效果不佳，需要更可靠的检测依据。

Method: 提出基于次属颜色（对应发射光谱峰值波长更长波段）百分位强度的分割方法，用于TFL'd蛋白质结晶试验图像。并与使用主颜色通道的方法进行对比评估。

Result: 相比主颜色通道，次属颜色通道方法将可能先导物或晶体被误分为非晶体的误分类率从9.71%降至2.02%（依分类器而定）；分类器准确率提升1.77%–5.53%。方法达到约94%准确率，且误分类率低于1%。在Con A和AbIPPase两种蛋白的新湿实验中也验证误分类率低于1%，具有泛化性。

Conclusion: 使用次属颜色通道更有助于TFL'd蛋白质试验图像分类，可显著提升晶体检测准确率并降低误分类，具备实际应用与泛化潜力。

Abstract: The accuracy of detecting protein crystals for fluorescence microscopy images is very critical for high throughput and automated systems. Although the trace fluorescent labeling method could highlight protein crystals, reflection and emission from the fluorescence dye is not always due to crystal regions. Therefore, the analysis of the peak wavelength in the emission spectra of a fluorophore may not always yield effective results. In this paper, we show that using the subordinate color intensity corresponding to longer wavelengths than the peak wavelength of the emission spectra could improve the accuracy of protein crystal detection. Hence, we have built a segmentation method based on the percentile intensity of the subordinate color for trace fluorescently labeled (TFL'd) protein crystallization trial images. Compared to using the dominant color channel, our segmentation method on subordinate color channel was able to reduce the misclassification rate of likely-leads or crystals as non-crystals by the percentage of from 9.71% to 2.02% depending on the classifier. Similarly, the accuracy of classifiers were increased by the percentage of from 1.77% to 5.53%. Our method reached around 94% accuracy while keeping misclassification of likely-leads and crystals as non-crystals below 1%. Moreover, to evaluate the generalizability of our method, we have conducted new wet lab experiments on two proteins, Concanavalin A (Con A) and Ab inorganic pyrophosphate (AbIPPase), and the misclassification rate was below 1%. Our experiments show that using the subordinate channel may be more helpful for TFL'd protein trial image classification.

</details>


### [87] [ASM-6D: Fast Certified Pose-Shape Inference with Complete Correspondence Flow Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15287)
*Ho Jin Choi,Minku Kim,Nadia Figueroa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ASM-6D 通过将生成式完整几何预测与显式的类别级位姿-形状推断耦合，利用共享对应关系实现高效的类别级 6D 位姿与形状估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类别级 6D 位姿与形状估计需在无精确实例模型的情况下，从部分观测中恢复物体位姿和几何。现有方法通常需要单独的对应关系分配阶段，效率较低。

Method: 给定 RGB-D 图像，ASM-6D 生成多个完整 3D 对应假设，其点身份与类别级 Active Shape Model (ASM) 共享（含被遮挡点）。对每个假设直接拟合旋转、平移、度量尺度和实例形状，无需单独对应分配。通过解析消除平移和形状系数，将拟合简化为九变量的缩放旋转优化问题，支持批量实时评估。

Result: 在 ROPE 和 HouseCat6D 基准上达到最先进性能。消融实验表明生成式对应预测和主动形状变化的重要性。该表示还支持形状补全、形状相关位姿歧义、跟踪和机器人抓取，频率为 6-38 Hz。

Conclusion: ASM-6D 通过共享对应关系耦合生成式几何预测与显式位姿-形状推断，实现了高效、实时的类别级 6D 位姿与形状估计，并具备多种扩展应用能力。

Abstract: Category-level 6D pose and shape estimation requires recovering an object's pose and geometry from partial observations without an exact instance model. We present ASM-6D, which couples generative complete-geometry prediction with explicit category-level pose-shape inference through shared correspondences. Given an RGB-D image, ASM-6D generates alternative complete 3D correspondence hypotheses whose point identities are shared with a category-level Active Shape Model (ASM), including occluded points. Each hypothesis is fitted directly for rotation, translation, metric scale, and instance shape without a separate correspondence-assignment stage. We analytically eliminate translation and shape coefficients, reducing fitting to a nine-variable scaled-rotation optimization problem that enables efficient batched evaluation of multiple hypotheses in real time. ASM-6D achieves state-of-the-art performance on ROPE and HouseCat6D benchmarks. Ablations demonstrate the importance of generative correspondence prediction and active shape variation. The representation also supports shape completion, shape-dependent pose ambiguity, tracking, and robotic grasping at 6-38 Hz, depending on the inference budget.

</details>


### [88] [HGA-Quant: Heterogeneity-Aware Quantization for AutoRegressive Visual Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15297)
*Xinmeng Liu,Xianglong Yan,Kaicheng Yang,Weisheng Dong,Yulun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个免训练的PTQ框架，包含T-BGR和CAPS，用于ARVG模型的低比特量化，在保持图像质量的同时降低计算与内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ARVG模型图像生成质量高，但计算和内存需求大，难以部署在边缘设备。现有PTQ方法在低比特量化下图像质量严重下降。

Method: 提出免训练PTQ框架，含两个组件：1) T-BGR，引入尾部感知重建目标校准权重量化器，重视分布尾部系数，保留细粒度信息且不增加参数或高精度分支；2) CAPS，联合考虑不同生成阶段的重要性和计算成本，进行由粗到细的激活精度调度，在给定比特预算下实现高效混合精度分配。

Result: 在多个ARVG骨干网络上实验表明，该框架在激进低比特量化下持续提升鲁棒性。在Infinity上，以有效W4A5.6激活精度实现接近全精度的图像生成质量，而现有方法在W4A6下仍有明显质量下降或不稳定表现。

Conclusion: 该免训练PTQ框架能有效降低ARVG模型量化误差，在低比特下保持高质量图像生成，优于现有方法。

Abstract: Recently, AutoRegressive Visual Generation (ARVG) models have emerged as a competitive paradigm for high-quality image synthesis. However, their substantial computational and memory demands make them difficult to deploy on resource-constrained edge devices. Post-Training Quantization (PTQ) provides a promising solution to reduce the computational and memory costs of ARVG models, yet existing methods often suffer from substantial image quality degradation under low-bit quantization. In this paper, we propose a training-free PTQ framework for ARVG, consisting of Tail-Aware Bounded-init Grid Refinement (T-BGR) and Coarse-to-Fine Activation Precision Scheduling (CAPS). T-BGR introduces a tail-aware reconstruction objective for weight quantizer calibration, assigning greater importance to distribution-tail coefficients and preserving fine-grained information without introducing additional parameters or high-precision branches. CAPS performs coarse-to-fine activation precision scheduling by jointly considering the importance and computational cost of different generation stages, enabling efficient mixed-precision allocation under a given bit budget. Extensive experiments on multiple ARVG backbones demonstrate that our framework consistently improves robustness under aggressive low-bit quantization. Notably, on Infinity, our framework achieves near full-precision image generation quality at an effective W4A5.6 activation precision, while existing methods at W4A6 still exhibit noticeable quality degradation or inconsistent performance. Code is available at https://github.com/lxm-lc/HGA-Quant.

</details>


### [89] [Channel Match: Calibrating Shape Latents for Few-Step 3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15321)
*Di Yang,Zhihao Li,Yanhai Xiong,Yufei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种几步蒸馏框架用于原生3D生成，结合连续自一致性训练、自定义JVP FlashAttention核和解析速度回归目标，并揭示采样导致潜分布漂移破坏PBR纹理合成的问题，提出免训练的自适应逐通道潜校准方法Channel Match。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原生3D生成器依赖flow-based transformer建模结构化3D潜变量，但高分辨率生成因迭代采样代价高昂。此外，作者发现一个先前被忽视的失败模式：少步生成引入的潜分布漂移虽保持几何正确，却破坏下游PBR纹理合成。

Method: 提出针对稀疏结构化3D潜变量的少步蒸馏框架，包含连续自一致性训练、自定义JVP FlashAttention核、解析速度回归目标，以及自动平衡损失的自适应损失加权策略。同时提出Channel Match——一种免训练、自适应、逐通道的潜校准方法。

Result: 在TRELLIS.2的1024^3分辨率设置上验证，两步模型实现4.5倍几何采样加速，几何质量与多步教师相当；Channel Match进一步提升渲染质量。

Conclusion: 该工作加速了原生高分辨率3D生成，并通过恢复几何与外观生成之间的潜接口，解决了少步采样导致的纹理伪影问题。

Abstract: Recent native 3D generators achieve high-quality asset creation by modeling structured 3D latents with flow-based transformers, but their high-resolution generation remains computationally expensive due to costly iterative sampling. In this work, we introduce a few-step distillation framework for native 3D generation, enabled by continuous self-consistency training with a custom JVP FlashAttention kernel and an analytic velocity regression objective for sparse structured 3D latents. To stabilize joint optimization, we further introduce an adaptive loss weighting strategy that automatically balances them without manual tuning. Beyond acceleration, we reveal a previously overlooked failure mode of few-step native 3D generation: sampling-induced latent distribution drift preserves geometric correctness but disrupts downstream PBR texture synthesis, causing texture artifacts on geometrically valid assets. To address this issue, we propose Channel Match, a training-free adaptive per-channel latent calibration method that restores the latent distribution without modifying either generator. We validate our approach on TRELLIS.2 at the $1024^3$ resolution setting. Our two-step model achieves $4.5\times$ faster geometry sampling while maintaining geometry quality comparable to the many-step teacher, and Channel Match further improves rendering quality by recovering the latent interface between geometry and appearance generation.

</details>


### [90] [TempoKV: Predictive KV Cache Quantization for Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15324)
*Jiahui Hu,Weile Mo,Peiliang Cai,Jiacheng Liu,Jinkui Ren,Xiantao Zhang,Xiaobing Tu,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种免训练的预测式KV缓存量化框架，用于自回归视频扩散模型，通过分别预测key和value并仅存储低精度残差，实现最高6.71倍压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散模型支持流式长视频生成，但KV缓存快速增长带来显著显存开销。现有KV量化方法主要面向LLM，迁移到视频模型效果差，因为视频激活统计不同且具有强时空冗余。

Method: 观察到视频KV缓存随时间高度可预测且存在不对称性：key可由时间历史预测，value主要依赖当前key和短历史value。据此分别按各自依赖进行预测，仅存储低精度残差；预测器轻量，通过少量BF16样本离线线性回归校准一次，之后跨提示和生成过程固定不变。

Result: 在Self-Forcing、Causal Forcing和LongCat-Video-13B上，方法在相当或更高压缩率下生成质量持续优于现有KV缓存量化方法，最高达6.71倍压缩，取得最佳质量-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 提出的免训练预测式KV量化框架有效利用视频KV缓存的时间可预测性与不对称性，显著降低显存开销并保持生成质量。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) video diffusion models enable streaming generation of long videos but suffer from significant memory overhead due to the rapidly growing KV cache. Existing KV cache quantization methods are primarily designed for LLMs and transfer poorly to video models, where activations exhibit different statistics and strong spatiotemporal redundancy. We observe that the video KV cache is largely predictable over time, with a clear asymmetry. Keys are highly predictable from their temporal history, while values depend primarily on the current key and need a short history of preceding values. Based on these observations, we propose \textbf{\method}, a training-free predictive KV cache quantization framework for AR video generation. \method predicts keys and values according to their distinct dependencies and stores only the residuals at low precision. Both predictors are lightweight, calibrated once offline by linear regression on a small set of BF16 samples, and remain fixed across prompts and generation runs. Experiments on Self-Forcing, Causal Forcing, and LongCat-Video-13B show that \method consistently achieves higher generation quality than existing KV cache quantization methods at comparable or higher compression ratios, reaching up to $6.71\times$ KV cache compression with the best overall quality--efficiency tradeoff. Our code will be released on GitHub.

</details>


### [91] [BaT-SAM: Introducing Inductive Bias into SAM via a Mixture of Convolutional Experts for Brain Tumor Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15330)
*Sumiaya Jannat,Md Mahedi Hasan,Md Shamimul Islam,Md Nur Hossain*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BaT-SAM，一种参数高效的自适应框架，实现基于SAM的全自动脑肿瘤分割，仅需微调不到5M参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MRI脑肿瘤分割对诊断和治疗至关重要，但人工勾画耗时费力；现有方法要么泛化性差，要么依赖推理时的用户提示。

Method: 通过Conv-LoRA向SAM冻结的ViT编码器注入局部归纳偏置，图像特征由稀疏门控的多尺度卷积分支专家混合处理；并引入轻量级自提示框回归器，通过计划采样与适配器联合优化，从图像嵌入直接预测肿瘤位置，去除对用户提示的依赖。

Result: 在BRISC2025基准上达到0.832的加权平均IoU，超越现有最优方法；跨数据集泛化良好，Figshare上Dice为0.702，LGG MRI分割数据集上Dice为0.784。

Conclusion: BaT-SAM以极少参数实现全自动且泛化性强的脑肿瘤分割，兼具高效性与实用性。

Abstract: Accurate brain tumor segmentation from magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) is essential for diagnosis and treatment planning; however, manual delineation is labor-intensive and time-consuming. Existing approaches are either task-specific with limited generalization or rely on user-provided prompts during inference. We present BaT-SAM, a parameter-efficient adaptation framework that enables fully automatic brain tumor segmentation with the Segment Anything Model (SAM). BaT-SAM injects local inductive bias into SAM's frozen Vision Transformer (ViT) encoder via Conv-LoRA, where image features are processed by a sparsely gated mixture of multi-scale convolutional experts. This design compensates for the limited translation-equivariant inductive bias of vanilla ViTs while fine-tuning fewer than 5M parameters (less than 1% of the full model). To remove the need for user prompts, we further introduce a lightweight self-prompting box regressor that is jointly optimized with the adapter using scheduled sampling and predicts tumor locations directly from image embeddings. On the BRISC2025 benchmark, BaT-SAM achieves a weighted mean IoU of 0.832 while outperforming state-of-the-art benchmarks. It further demonstrates strong cross-dataset generalization, achieving Dice scores of 0.702 on Figshare and 0.784 on the LGG MRI Segmentation dataset.

</details>


### [92] [StimFlow: Synthesizing Brain Activity from Images via Stimulus-conditioned Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15341)
*Bui Van Hung,Dang Thanh Vu,Hoang-Son Vo,Mai Duc Minh Huy,Mai Tien Dung,Soo-Hyung Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StimFlow是一个直接在体素空间用条件流匹配合成fMRI响应的生成式编码器，结合DINOv2结构特征与CLIP语义特征，单样本即可媲美需平均五次采样的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: fMRI响应跨试次存在固有变异性，适合生成式建模。但既有生成式编码器多在压缩潜空间中操作，且主要依赖语义特征，难以为早期视觉皮层提供低层线索。

Method: 提出StimFlow，使用带线性路径的条件流匹配直接在原生体素空间合成fMRI，使训练与推理分布对齐，可用单样本生成；并融合DINOv2结构特征与CLIP语义特征以匹配视觉层级。

Result: 单样本下达到每体素Pearson相关0.371，媲美平均五次采样的基线；体素空间生成支持无需解码器的ROI分析与概念定位；仅用一小时扫描数据即可恢复超过一半的全数据性能。

Conclusion: 直接在体素空间的生成式建模能有效应对fMRI变异性，DINOv2与CLIP特征互补，并支持高效的跨被试适应。

Abstract: The inherent variability of fMRI responses across trials encourages a generative view of the stimulus-to-response mapping. Prior generative encoders often operate in compressed latent spaces and condition predominantly on semantic features, which provide limited low-level cues for early visual cortex. We propose StimFlow, which synthesizes fMRI directly in native voxel space using conditional flow matching with a linear path. This design aligns training and inference distributions, enabling competitive performance with a single sample and reducing reliance on averaging. To match the visual hierarchy, StimFlow combines structural DINOv2 features with semantic CLIP features, which prove complementary along the cortical processing stream. With a single sample, StimFlow achieves a per-voxel Pearson correlation of 0.371, matching baselines that average five draws. Generation in voxel space allows direct ROI analysis and concept localization without a decoder. Furthermore, it recovers over half of full-data performance from just one hour of scans, supporting efficient cross-subject adaptation.

</details>


### [93] [Across Viewpoints and Time: Trajectory-Generalizable Physical Attacks on World Models and Video Understanding Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15343)
*Song Xia,Meiwen Ding,Chenqi Kong,Wenhan Yang,Xudong Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TARA方法，研究单一物理对抗纹理在动态视角轨迹下对世界模型和视频理解模型的攻击效果，并构建可复现测试平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型和视频理解模型日益部署于动态视觉环境，但其对物理对抗威胁的鲁棒性尚未充分探索；且不同视频语义与轨迹导致梯度对齐困难，攻击难以跨场景迁移。

Method: 提出轨迹感知的对齐与细化攻击（TARA），包含轨迹感知优化以捕捉纹理几何变化；轨迹感知对比对齐使多样视频-轨迹实例的表征趋近共享对抗区域；轨迹感知任务细化将表征转化为任务级预测错误。并构建几何跟踪pip用于EPIC-KITCHENS-100和Something-Something V2。

Result: 在两大基准及真实打印-重录视频上验证，单一可打印对抗纹理能在物理条件下持续干扰世界模型与视频理解模型。

Conclusion: 单一物理对抗纹理可持久破坏世界模型和视频理解模型，揭示根本性安全挑战；所建基准与真实数据构成可复现测试平台，供未来评估使用。

Abstract: World models and video understanding models are increasingly deployed in dynamic visual environments, yet their robustness to physical adversarial threats remains largely underexplored. We study whether a single physical adversarial texture can mislead the victim model across different real-world scenarios where observing viewpoints vary along uncontrolled object--camera trajectories. We identify that heterogeneous video semantics and trajectories induce poorly aligned gradients for optimizing the adversarial texture. To address that, we propose the Trajectory-aware adversarial Alignment and Refinement Attack (TARA), which incorporates trajectory-aware optimization to capture the evolving geometric variations of a printed texture under real-world scenarios. To improve cross-instance optimization compatibility, TARA incorporates trajectory-aware contrastive alignment to encourage attacked representations from diverse video--trajectory instances to approach a shared adversarial region and then performs trajectory-aware task refinement to translate the aligned representation into task-level prediction errors. For reproducible evaluation, we develop a geometry-grounded tracking pipeline that provides physically plausible adversarial regions with temporally consistent trajectories on EPIC-KITCHENS-100 and Something-Something V2. We further collect real-world print-and-recapture videos to validate attack effectiveness under physical deployment conditions. Together, the annotated benchmarks and real-world captured data form a reproducible testbed for future evaluation. Notably, our results reveal that a single printable adversarial texture can persistently disrupt world models and video understanding models under physical conditions, highlighting the fundamental security challenge.

</details>


### [94] [Video-Based Sexual Harassment Detection Using Fine-Tuned Video Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15354)
*Jubayer- Al- Mahmud,Md Shamimul Islam,Md Nur Hossain*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文扩展了性骚扰视频数据集SHV至600个短视频，并微调三种视频Transformer模型进行二分类检测，TimeSformer取得最高92.27%的测试准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 性骚扰是普遍的社会挑战，许多事件因证据不足和干预延迟而未被举报。实时智能监控系统可辅助执法并起到威慑作用，但视频骚扰检测面临服装、光照、遮挡、低分辨率和视角多样等挑战。由于缺乏大规模标注视频数据集，该问题研究较少。

Method: 将性骚扰视频SHV数据集扩展至600个来自YouTube的短视频，按一致标注指南分为骚扰与非骚扰两类；微调三种基于Kinetics-400预训练的最先进视频Transformer模型（VideoMAE、ViViT、TimeSformer）进行二分类骚扰检测。

Result: 三种模型均取得有竞争力的分类性能，TimeSformer在扩展SHV测试集上获得最高准确率92.27%，优于VideoMAE的91.71%和ViViT的91.16%。分析发现性能受数据集规模、视频质量、帧采样策略和模型架构显著影响。

Conclusion: 该工作对现代视频Transformer在性骚扰检测这一敏感现实任务上进行了全面评估，并为后续研究提供了基准参考。

Abstract: Sexual harassment represents a pervasive societal challenge, with a substantial proportion of incidents remaining unreported due to insufficient evidence and delayed intervention mechanisms. Smart surveillance systems capable of detecting such incidents in real-time could assist law enforcement and serve as a deterrent. However, video-based harassment detection poses significant challenges, including variations in clothing, illumina- tion, partial occlusions, low resolution, and diverse camera view- points. Despite its social importance, this problem has received limited research attention, primarily due to the scarcity of large- scale annotated video datasets. To address this gap, we extend the Sexual Harassment Video (SHV) dataset to 600 short video clips sourced from YouTube and annotated into harassment and non-harassment categories using consistent labeling guidelines. We fine-tune three state-of-the-art video transformer models, VideoMAE, ViViT, and TimeSformer, pretrained on Kinetics- 400, for binary harassment classification. Experimental results show that all three models achieve competitive classification performance, with TimeSformer attaining the highest test accu- racy of 92.27%, outperforming VideoMAE 91.71% and ViViT 91.16% on the extended SHV test set. Our analysis reveals that performance is notably influenced by dataset size, video quality, frame sampling strategy, and model architecture. This work presents a comprehensive evaluation of modern video transformers for this sensitive real-world task.

</details>


### [95] [UniForge3D: Joint 3D Reconstruction and Generation in a Shared Anchor-Guided Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15357)
*Wenjue Chen,Wenyue Chen,Heliang Zheng,Rongfei Jia,Yuan Liu,Meng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 UniForge3D，在共享锚点引导空间中联合建模重建与生成，解决坐标不匹配与视图选择问题，在 Toys4K 和 GSO 上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏、无位姿图像生成完整 3D 资产需在保留观测几何的同时补全未见区域。联合重建与生成可兼顾两者：重建恢复输入中观测到的几何，生成提供学习到的形状先验。但相机相对重建与规范空间生成之间存在坐标不匹配，难以直接进行几何交互；且对每个 3D 位置无差别地使用所有视图条件，会混合有用几何证据与无关图像内容，影响局部细节恢复。

Method: 1) 在共享的锚点引导空间联合建模重建与生成，该空间遵循锚点视图方位并保持物体直立，从而消除坐标不匹配；2) 两个分支双向交换特征，预测逐视图点图与完整稀疏体素结构；3) 将点图预测以生成的体素结构为条件，建立像素到体素的对应关系，并为每个体素选择信息量最大的输入视图；4) 用所选视图的特征引导精细形状生成，以保留观测到的几何细节。

Result: 在 Toys4K 和 GSO 数据集上取得外观与几何方面的最优（state-of-the-art）结果，生成的完整 3D 资产具有更高保真度。

Conclusion: UniForge3D 通过统一的锚点引导空间与双向特征交互，有效融合重建与生成，并通过视图相关性选择提升局部细节，实现高质量完整 3D 资产生成。

Abstract: Generating complete 3D assets from sparse, unposed images requires preserving observed geometry while completing unseen regions. Joint reconstruction and generation offers a way to address both goals: reconstruction recovers the geometry observed in the input images, while generation contributes learned shape priors. However, combining camera-relative reconstruction with canonical-space generation introduces a coordinate mismatch that makes direct geometric interaction more difficult. Moreover, conditioning each 3D location on all views without accounting for their relevance may mix useful geometric evidence with unrelated image content, hindering local detail recovery. We present UniForge3D, which jointly models reconstruction and generation in a shared anchor-guided space, resolving their coordinate mismatch. This space follows the anchor view's azimuth while keeping the object upright. The two branches exchange features bidirectionally to predict per-view point maps and a complete sparse voxel structure. We further condition point-map prediction on the generated structure to establish pixel-to-voxel correspondences and select the most informative input view for each voxel. Features from the selected views then guide detailed shape generation, helping preserve observed geometric details. Our method achieves state-of-the-art appearance and geometry results on Toys4K and GSO, generating complete 3D assets with improved fidelity. Project webpage: https://xishuxishu.github.io/uniforge3d-project/

</details>


### [96] [Genesis: Visual Augmentation Primitives for Multimodal Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15565)
*Fan Yang,Lifeng Chen,Yipu Wang,Han Wang,Feilong Chen,Yidong Ma,Zhiyang Chen,Chaoyang Zhao,Lingxiang Wu,Hanqi Jiang,Yibo Chen,Xu Tang,Yao Hu,Ming Tang,Xiaojie Jin,Xiaolong Zheng,Jinqiao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VAP数据增强流水线与多模态CoT强化学习，让每步推理锚定视觉证据，并构建UNREAL基准，4B模型超越8B基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型在STEM基准接近人类水平，但在复杂真实场景下能力退化。原因是链式推理几乎局限于语言空间，很少将每步锚定于视觉证据，而根源在于训练数据缺乏此类样本。

Method: 提出Visual Augmentation Primitives(VAP)训练数据增强流水线：用先进视觉生成模型合成多样逼真训练样本及对应推理文本，对种子图像施加基本算子生成变体，并在生成过程中构建连贯的多模态推理链。进一步提出基于rubric的多模态CoT强化学习，从逻辑有效性、完整性、一致性评估中间步骤，兼顾文本与视觉推理。并构建UNREAL基准评估可控难度超真实场景的视觉推理。

Result: 基于该框架的4B模型超越8B基线，且扩展到32B时性能持续提升，验证了视觉推理的扩展律。

Conclusion: 通过数据增强与多模态强化学习将推理锚定视觉证据，可有效提升复杂真实场景下的视觉推理能力并展现良好扩展性。

Abstract: Recent multimodal large language models achieve promising results on visual reasoning tasks, approaching human-level performance on existing STEM benchmarks. However, these capabilities degrade under complex real-world scenes. We identify that chain-of-thought reasoning remains largely confined to the linguistic space, rarely anchoring each step in visual evidence. The main cause is the absence of such data during training. In this work, we introduce a training data augmentation pipeline, \textbf{Visual Augmentation Primitives} (VAP), which leverages state-of-the-art vision generative models to synthesize diverse photorealistic training samples and corresponding reasoning text. VAP introduces some basic operators and applies them to a seed image and gets a variant. It also constructs coherent multimodal reasoning chains throughout the generation process. Such training data force models to perceive visual content and perform logical reasoning according to them at every step. We further introduce multimodal chain-of-thought rubric-based reinforcement learning to enhance this. It rewards not only text reasoning but also visual reasoning by scoring intermediate reasoning steps for logical validity, completeness, and consistency. Moreover, we construct the UNREAL benchmark to systematically evaluate visual reasoning on hyperrealistic scenes across controllable difficulty levels. Experiments show that a 4B model built on this framework surpasses 8B baselines, with performance improving consistently as the model scales to 32B, validating the scaling law for visual reasoning.

</details>


### [97] [Sparse All-Layer Connector for Domain Generalised Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15660)
*Ruoyu Guo,Xin Kun Lin,Maurice Pagnucco,Yang Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Sparse All-Layer Connector (SALC)，打破基础模型融合中固定对齐层级的假设，通过自适应稀疏选择跨模型信息层提升域泛化语义分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DGSS多基础模型融合方法通常只在深度对齐层进行跨模型融合，隐含假设同一深度层间的信息最有价值。但不同预训练目标的基础模型具有不同的表示层级结构，且深度方向上的域敏感性各异，因此深度对齐融合并非最优。

Method: 提出SALC，使每一层可访问另一模型的先前层信息；随深度增加可选层数增多，采用自适应稀疏选择有用层，并引入candidate dropout正则化以增强稀疏性并促进探索多样化层。

Result: 在四种基础模型组合和三种DGSS评估设置下，所提方法比传统深度对齐融合具有更强的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 跨模型融合不应局限于深度对齐层，自适应稀疏的全层连接设计能更有效利用不同基础模型的互补表示，提升域泛化语义分割。

Abstract: Domain generalised semantic segmentation (DGSS) has recently benefited from combining multiple foundation models, such as vision-language and vision foundation models, to leverage their diverse representations for generalisation. Existing multi-model approaches share a common design choice: cross-model fusion is restricted to depth-aligned layers, implicitly assuming that mutually beneficial information resides at the same depth across models. However, foundation models pretrained under different objectives may develop distinct representation hierarchies, making the optimality of depth-aligned fusion questionable. Moreover, we observe that the foundation models exhibit different depth-wise domain sensitivity. Motivated by these observations, we propose a Sparse All-Layer Connector (SALC) that enables each layer to access information from preceding layers of another model. As the number of accessible layers grows with depth, dense aggregation may hurt generalisation. SALC therefore learns to adaptively select a sparse subset of informative layers. We further introduce a candidate dropout regularisation that strengthens sparsity and encourages SALC to explore diverse layers, leading to more robust selection. Across four foundation model combinations and three DGSS evaluation settings, the proposed design demonstrates stronger generalisation than conventional depth-aligned fusion. The code is available at https://github.com/RuoyuGuo/SALC.

</details>


### [98] [A Persian Vision-Language-Action Dataset and Benchmark for Ambiguity- and Infeasibility-Aware Instruction-to-Plan Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15666)
*Abdollah Pourasghari,Saeedeh Sadat Sadidpour,Mohammad Roustaei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 首个波斯语视觉-语言-动作数据集（258实例，230张真实照片），配合消融实验与新的PGOS-FA评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅面向英语、模拟或视频环境，且假设指令清晰可执行，缺乏真实场景下含歧义与不可行性的波斯语资源。

Method: 构建含参考计划、歧义/不可行标签、消歧问答对及自动思维链的波斯语数据集；消融检索增强、思维链提示与智能体消歧步骤；提出PGOS-FA指标（增加步骤顺序敏感性、过度生成惩罚及波斯语适配嵌入）。

Result: 36组配对统计检验表明检索增强效果显著且稳健，更细粒度改进方向一致但不显著；LLM-judge指标效力近零而PGOS-FA有效；79个真实输出的盲测显示PGOS-FA与人类判断的相关性至少不逊于LLM-judge；开权重与闭源规划器的差距主要源于生成而非能力；最佳配置优于复现的外部基线。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了首个波斯语视觉-语言-动作数据集与新评估指标，验证了检索增强的关键作用及PGOS-FA的优越性，数据与代码将按需开放。

Abstract: Converting natural-language instructions into executable action plans conditioned on a scene image is a core capability for embodied human-robot interaction, but existing benchmarks target English, simulated or video-grounded environments, and largely assume instructions are clear and executable. We introduce the first Persian vision-language-action dataset for this task: 258 instances over 230 real household/office photographs, annotated with reference plans, deliberate ambiguity and infeasibility labels, disambiguating question-answer pairs, and an auto-generated chain-of-thought rationale for training. Using this resource, we ablate retrieval-augmentation, chain-of-thought prompting, and an agentic disambiguation step, evaluated with an LLM-as-judge protocol and a new metric, PGOS-FA, extending prior work (PG2S) with step-order sensitivity, an over-generation penalty, and Persian-adapted embeddings. Paired statistical testing across 36 comparisons shows retrieval-augmentation is a large, robustly significant effect, while finer-grained additions are directionally consistent but not statistically distinguishable from noise at this scale, and the LLM-judge metric has near-zero power where PGOS-FA succeeds. A blind human-correlation study on 79 real outputs shows PGOS-FA tracks human judgment at least as closely as the LLM-judge. Testing generalization beyond the ablation's single backbone, we find that most of the gap between an open-weight and a substantially stronger closed planner is attributable to open-weight model generation rather than to the closed model's capability specifically, and that our best configuration significantly outperforms an independently reproduced external baseline despite a weaker backbone. The dataset, PGOS-FA implementation, and evaluation code will be made available upon reasonable request.

</details>


### [99] [ProtoPur: Prototype-Purity Pseudo-Labeling for Weakly Supervised Instance Learning in Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15669)
*Chen Shu,Chengyang Zhang,Boyu Fu,Ting Yin,Wenchuan Zhang,Jie Chen,Yuhao Yi,Hong Bu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ProtoPur：一种基于原型纯度的伪标签方法，利用冻结基础模型特征在弱监督病理全切片图像上实现实例级学习，无需密集实例标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱包级标签在计算病理中易得，而全切片图像的可靠实例级标注昂贵且模糊。现有弱监督MIL虽在包级分类表现好，但基于注意力分数、模型预测、负包或空间细化的实例监督可能无法识别类特异性局部证据。

Method: ProtoPur在由冻结基础模型特征构建的原型区域上估计类平衡的局部纯度，并将伪标签分配给与目标类别强关联的高纯度区域。以"纯度优先"设计区分类特异性证据与负性或共享形态，覆盖率仅用于保证足够的伪标签分配。并给出PAC风格分析，将实例分类器风险分解为均匀收敛项与ProtoPur特有的伪标签噪声项（含选中区域失配和覆盖残差）。

Result: 在三个公开全切片数据集和两种病理基础编码器上，ProtoPur在多种MIL骨干上一致提升实例级性能，同时保持竞争的包级性能。两个公开数据集上的伪标签质量对比表明，原型纯度选择提供了更强的实例监督。

Conclusion: 基础模型特征含有可利用的实例级监督信号，ProtoPur能够在仅用弱包标签、无需密集实例标注的情况下揭示并使用这些信号。

Abstract: Weak bag-level labels are widely available in computational pathology, whereas reliable instance-level annotations for whole-slide images remain expensive and ambiguous. Weakly supervised multiple instance learning (MIL) achieves strong bag-level classification, but instance-level supervision from attention scores, model predictions, negative bags, or spatial refinement may fail to identify class-specific local evidence. We propose ProtoPur, a prototype-purity pseudo-labeling method for weakly supervised instance-level learning in pathology whole-slide images. ProtoPur estimates class-balanced local purity over prototype regions constructed from frozen foundation-model features, and assigns pseudo-labels to high-purity regions strongly associated with a target class. This purity-first design separates class-specific evidence from negative or shared morphology, with coverage used only to ensure sufficient pseudo-label assignment. We provide a PAC-style analysis showing that the risk of an instance classifier trained with ProtoPur pseudo-labels can be decomposed into a standard uniform-convergence term and a ProtoPur-specific pseudo-label noise term, comprising selected-region mismatch and a coverage residual. Experiments on three public whole-slide image datasets with two pathology foundation encoders show that ProtoPur consistently improves instance-level performance across multiple MIL backbones while maintaining competitive bag-level performance. Pseudo-label quality comparisons on two public datasets further demonstrate that prototype-purity selection provides stronger instance supervision. These results suggest that the foundation-model features contain exploitable instance-level supervision signals, and ProtoPur is a way to reveal and use these signals from weak bag labels without requiring dense instance annotations.

</details>


### [100] [Rethinking Human Intent-to-CAD: Parametric CAD Model Generation via Cooperative Multi-Task Alignment and Spatial-Aware Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15681)
*Qingwang Zhang,Jiahao Li,Xiangdong Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HiCAD，将多层人类意图（手绘草图+文本）直接映射为可执行参数化CAD代码，并构建首个大规模对齐数据集，通过两阶段框架实现高保真CAD建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念设计阶段的人类意图表达不完整且非结构化（手绘草图、文本），从意图到参数化CAD建模仍具挑战，且不假设已有目标CAD模型。

Method: 构建首个对齐手绘草图、文本描述与参数化CAD代码的大规模数据集HiCAD；提出两阶段框架：协同多任务对齐（弥合异构输入表征差距）与空间感知强化学习（保证几何与拓扑一致性）。

Result: 大量实验表明该方法在多个任务上显著优于现有基线，验证了将异构人类意图转化为高保真参数化CAD模型的有效性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 重新思考意图到CAD的流程，无需预先存在目标CAD模型即可直接映射人类意图为可执行代码，为概念设计阶段的CAD建模提供统一方案。

Abstract: Parametric Computer-Aided-Design (CAD) modeling from human intent remains challenging, particularly during the conceptual design stage, where design goals are expressed through incomplete and unstructured modalities (e.g., hand-drawn sketches and textual descriptions). In this work, we rethink the human intent-to-CAD pipeline and propose a unified method that directly maps multi-level human intents to executable codes, without assuming the prior existence of target CAD models. To support our study, we construct HiCAD, the first large-scale dataset aligning hand-drawn sketches, textual descriptions, and parametric CAD codes. Based on this, we introduce HiCAD, a two-stage framework comprising Cooperative Multi-Task Alignment to bridge the representational gap between heterogeneous inputs, and Spatial-Aware Reinforcement Learning to enforce geometric and topological consistency. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms existing baselines across multiple tasks, validating its effectiveness and robustness in transforming heterogeneous human intents into high-fidelity parametric CAD models. Our project page: https://zqwlearning.github.io/HiCAD.

</details>


### [101] [PhySimCode: A Benchmark and Evaluation Method for Physics Video to Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15722)
*Souraja Kundu,Aditya Gupta,Joel Julin,Yizhou Zhao,Liuyue Xie,Laszlo A. Jeni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PhySimCode基准，要求模型从原始视频推断物理规律、恢复参数并生成可运行模拟代码，揭示MLLM存在巨大的"视频到模拟"鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM在视频理解和代码生成上表现优异，但能否从视频中恢复支配物理规律尚不清楚。这对将视频转为可执行模拟、为机器人提供结构化物理监督至关重要。现有视频-物理基准只评估合理性、属性估计或未来状态预测，无法判断模型能否将视觉动力学翻译为可运行模拟代码。

Method: 构建PhySimCode基准，含160.6K程序化生成样本，覆盖162种物理现象（SciPy解析模拟与PyBullet渲染的刚体、铰接体、流体、接触密集场景）。每样本包含模拟代码、物理参数、渲染视频及结构化思维链（观察、定律、ODE、数值方法、验证）。评估10个主流闭源/开源MLLM在物理定律正确性、模拟等价性、代码可执行性和参数恢复上的表现。

Result: 模型能可靠陈述物理定律（4.27-4.93/5），但从视频推断仅得1.51-2.47/5；最佳系统Claude Sonnet 4.6在±20%误差内仅恢复7.7%的真实参数，暴露显著的视频到模拟鸿沟。评估集含2,430个视频，已开源。

Conclusion: 现有MLLM虽掌握物理定律知识，却难以从视频中实际推断并转化为可运行模拟，视频到模拟能力仍是重大缺口；PhySimCode为相关研究提供了标准化评测平台。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are good at video understanding and code generation, but it remains unclear whether they can recover the physical laws underlying videos, a capability essential for converting videos into executable simulations and for providing structured physical supervision in robotics. Captioning motion video does not imply that a model has inferred its governing dynamics, estimated quantitative parameters, or can regenerate the observed scene from code. Existing video-physics benchmarks evaluate plausibility, property estimation, or future-state prediction, but offer limited insight into whether models can translate visual dynamics into runnable simulation code. We introduce PhySimCode, a benchmark for physics video-to-code generation: given only a raw video, a model must infer the relevant physics, recover parameter values, and generate runnable Python that reproduces the input video. The dataset contains 160.6K procedurally generated samples spanning 162 physics phenomena across analytic SciPy simulations and PyBullet-rendered rigid-body, articulated, fluid, and contact-rich scenes; each sample bundles the simulation code, physical parameters, rendered video, and a structured chain-of-thought covering observations, laws, ODEs, numerical methods, and validation checks. We evaluate ten state-of-the-art closed- and open-source MLLMs along physical-law correctness, simulation equivalence, code executability, and parameter recovery. Models reliably state physics laws (4.27-4.93/5) yet score only 1.51-2.47/5 on inferring them from video, and the best system (Claude Sonnet 4.6) recovers just 7.7% of ground-truth parameters within $\pm 20\%$, exposing a substantial video-to-simulation gap. The 2,430-video evaluation set is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/SourajaKundu123/PhySimCode and the evaluation code at https://github.com/SourajaKundu/PhySimCode

</details>


### [102] [Visually Legible but Adversarial: Watermark-Constrained Attacks on End-to-End VLMs for Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15728)
*Qingzhe Han,Fan Dang,Shengqi Chen,Xinyi Sun,Haoyu Xiong,Lingkun Li,Di Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AR-AWM，一种模型感知的白盒对抗水印攻击，通过在文档页面上优化空间分布的“水印文字”像素，大幅削弱端到端视觉语言模型的文档理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可见水印常表示文档所有权与机密性，但也可能成为对抗信号载体；本文探索将水印形状区域作为攻击面，破坏文档VLM的视觉表示。

Method: AR-AWM构建小型空间分布的水印文字支撑区域，仅优化其像素；攻击非目标且与问题无关，生成时不使用问题、答案、解析提示、语言模型梯度或输出。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-VL-7B上，覆盖7.98%页面像素的水印支撑将DocVQA EM从0.899降至0.150，ANLS从0.939降至0.212；未优化或随机扰动仍保持ANLS约0.93，布局匹配矩形ANLS为0.276，说明对抗优化是主因、文字形状有增益。OmniDocBench各指标均下降；InternVL2.5-8B白盒下ANLS从0.922降至0.046，跨模型迁移弱。

Conclusion: 在水印形状支撑上进行白盒对抗优化可显著破坏文档VLM理解，表明常规水印形状区域是该设定下的对抗攻击面，且效果强依赖模型编码器。

Abstract: Visible watermarks convey document ownership, confidentiality, and status, but can also carry adversarial signals. We introduce Auto-Regional Adversarial Watermarking (AR-AWM), a model-aware white-box attack on end-to-end visual language models (VLMs) for document understanding. AR-AWM constructs a small, spatially distributed watermark-text support and optimizes only its pixels to disrupt visual representations. The attack is untargeted and question-agnostic: generation uses no questions, answers, parsing prompts, language-model gradients, or language-model outputs. Each attacked page is reused across downstream queries. On Qwen2.5-VL-7B, a watermark support covering 7.98% of page pixels under the reporting threshold reduces DocVQA exact match from 0.899 to 0.150 and average normalized Levenshtein similarity (ANLS) from 0.939 to 0.212. Selected qualitative cases retain visually legible source fields. The same support without optimization and with matched random perturbations retains ANLS of 0.932 and 0.933, respectively, identifying adversarial optimization as the primary cause of degradation. Layout-matched optimized rectangles yield ANLS of 0.276, showing an incremental benefit from text-shaped geometry. AR-AWM also degrades all reported text, formula, reading-order, and table metrics on OmniDocBench. Separate white-box optimization on InternVL2.5-8B reduces ANLS from 0.922 to 0.046, while weak cross-model transfer reveals strong encoder specificity. These results identify conventional watermark-shaped supports as an adversarial surface for document VLMs in the evaluated white-box setting.luated white-box setting.

</details>


### [103] [ScenePlacer: Reformulating Scene-Level Object Pose Estimation as Visibility Field Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15734)
*Ken Deng,Jiarui Liu,Guibiao Liao,Kunming Luo,Mochu Xiang,Hongyu Yan,Yebin Liu,Shenghua Gao,Yizhou Yu,Ping Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ScenePlacer：将物体生成与姿态估计解耦，通过对物体空间的可见性场学习实现场景级位姿估计，无需场景级姿态标注，在3D-FUTURE上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有场景生成方法要么直接在场景坐标中生成物体，损害其标准空间先验；要么在形状生成过程中从完整生成形状回归位姿，该问题病态，对尺度歧义和部分观测敏感。

Method: 将配准重构为条件可见性场学习：给定重建的部分观测点云与生成网格，预测物体空间定义的可见性场，再通过将部分观测对齐到网格可见部分来估计刚体变换。采用预训练3D形状VAE为骨干，保持其网格先验并以单独编码的观测为条件；引入可见性感知、半径约束的监督，仅用物体级网格与渲染视图合成训练对，无需场景级位姿标注。

Result: 在3D-FUTURE上，真实几何与单目深度估计下均持续优于现有放置与位姿精化基线。生成物体时，估计深度下Chamfer距离0.0077、F-score 92.40；真实几何下提升至0.0023和98.27。

Conclusion: ScenePlacer通过解耦生成与位姿估计、以可见性场学习替代病态回归，实现了鲁棒且无需场景级标注的场景级物体放置。

Abstract: We present ScenePlacer, a framework for scene-level pose estimation of objects generated by state-of-the-art native shape generation models, placing generated 3D objects into scenes given a partial geometric observation without modifying the underlying generative pipeline. Existing scene generation approaches either generate objects directly in scene coordinates, compromising their canonical-space priors, or regress poses from complete generated shapes in the shape generation process - the latter being a poorly conditioned problem that is sensitive to scale ambiguity and partial observations. ScenePlacer decouples object generation from pose estimation by reformulating alignment as conditional visibility-field learning. Given an observed partial point cloud from reconstruction and a generated mesh, it predicts a visibility field defined in object space, then estimates the rigid transformation by aligning the partial observation to visible parts on the mesh. We propose a pretrained 3D shape VAE as our backbone, preserving its mesh prior while conditioning it on a separately encoded observation. We further introduce visibility-aware, radius-constrained supervision and synthesize training pairs using only object-level meshes and rendered views, eliminating the need for scene-level pose annotations. On 3D-FUTURE, ScenePlacer consistently outperforms existing placement and pose-refinement baselines under both ground-truth and monocular depth estimation. With generated objects, it achieves a Chamfer distance of 0.0077 and an F-score of 92.40 under estimated depth and improves to 0.0023 and 98.27, respectively, under ground-truth geometry.

</details>


### [104] [RSAJEV: Training-Free Abstaining Prototype Memory for Eliminating Silent Hallucination in JEV-Based Remote Sensing Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15742)
*Man Chen,Antai Chen,Shuai Xu,Chengjie Gu,Huaping Zhou,Kelei Sun,Tao Wu,Yongjie Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 揭示MLLM遥感场景分类中字母token绑定读出的词汇外失效（"silent hallucination"），提出免训练RSAJEV检索式原型记忆读出，恢复校准与开集能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM场景分类依赖字母选项（letter token）打分读出，在训练词表内高效（UCM 97.71% OA），但跨数据集泛化极不稳定（7.35%–96.40%），且开集置信度反向（AUROC 0.38），将未见类判得比已见类更自信，即"silent hallucination"，缺乏可靠弃权通道。

Method: 保留原slot表示，改用免训练弃权原型记忆RSAJEV：每类缓存K张参考图的slot表示作原型，分类变为最近邻检索，并用收缩-Mahalanobis几何校正精化；机制上类比RAG但作用于判别决策，是首个面向MLLM slot表示的检索式读出。

Result: 16参考/类、零梯度更新下，UCM跨数据集门控达89.43% OA，AID达96.90%；两个数据集开集评估ID-ness得分AUROC 0.91–0.99，四种开集设置均超过符号置信度（0.38–0.96），恢复可校准弃权通道。

Conclusion: 字母绑定读出存在词表外脆弱性，检索式原型读出可零成本修复并支持类别增删（缓存矩阵离线重打分），为MLLM判别决策提供更可靠的读出范式。

Abstract: Scene classification of remote sensing imagery with multimodal large language models (MLLMs) is formulated as a single-forward-pass decision problem: candidate class names are lettered options in the prompt, a registered answer slot is conditioned on the prompt, and classification is read out by scoring letter tokens with the language-modeling head. We show that although this letter binding is highly effective within its training vocabulary (97.71% OA on UCM), it behaves erratically out of vocabulary: zero-adaptation accuracy across four cross-dataset directions fluctuates between 7.35% and 96.40%, and under out-of-vocabulary weights its confidence fails inversely on open-set evaluation, assigning higher confidence to unseen than to seen classes (AUROC 0.38), a failure we term silent hallucination. We retain the slot representation and replace the readout with RSAJEV, a training-free abstaining prototype memory that performs retrieval-based prototype matching: K reference images per class are cached as prototypes via their slot representations under the same prompt, and classification becomes nearest-neighbor retrieval over the prototypes, refined by a shrinkage-Mahalanobis geometric correction. This readout is mechanistically isomorphic to retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) but operates on discriminative decisions; it is the first retrieval-based readout for MLLM slot representations. The retrieval readout reaches 89.43% on the UCM cross-dataset gate with 16 references per class and zero gradient updates, and 96.90% on AID. Its ID-ness scores attain 0.91-0.99 AUROC on open-set evaluation over two datasets, exceeding symbolic confidence (0.38-0.96) in all four open-set settings, restoring a calibratable abstention channel and supporting class addition/removal at zero extra inference cost via offline rescoring of the cached matrix.

</details>


### [105] [SpikeOcc: Towards Resilient Multimodal 3D Occupancy Perception via Spiking Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15745)
*Yiding Sun,Zijie Xu,Hongqiang Lin,Jiajun Cao,Dongxu Zhang,Kai Tang,Jihua Zhu,Wenxuan Liu,Zhaofei Yu,Tiejun Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出SpikeOcc，用脉冲神经网络（SNN）结合Spiking Mixture-of-Experts（SMoE）和Sensor Availability Learning（SAL），提升多模态3D占据预测在传感器缺失下的鲁棒性，同时保持竞争性的完整传感器精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态3D占据预测方法多通过复杂融合追求更高精度，但对传感器缺失时的鲁棒性关注不足。作者希望通过SNN构建更具弹性的多模态感知系统，但脉冲神经元的离散性导致优化困难。

Method: 提出SpikeOcc，包含两个互补组件：1）Spiking Mixture-of-Experts（SMoE），将RGB、LiDAR及联合融合组织为专门通路，通过有界残差保留原始多模态特征，实现全宽度单模态表示；2）Sensor Availability Learning（SAL），采用分阶段课程学习与显式路由监督，逐步提升严重感知条件下的鲁棒性。

Result: 实验表明，SpikeOcc在保持竞争性完整传感器精度的同时，显著提高了传感器失效时的性能下限。SAL对传统模型也带来明显增益。

Conclusion: SpikeOcc展示了神经形态计算在更可靠、更节能的自动驾驶中的潜力，并提示SAL具有更广泛的多模态弹性学习价值。

Abstract: Multimodal 3D occupancy prediction underpins safe autonomous driving through comprehensive scene understanding. However, existing methods have largely pursued higher accuracy through sophisticated fusion, leaving resilience to missing sensors underexplored.
