In this paper, we investigate spiking neural networks (SNNs) as a means of building more resilient multimodal perception. Still, the discrete nature of spiking neurons makes optimization challenging. To overcome this difficulty, SpikeOcc is introduced with two complementary components, a Spiking Mixture-of-Experts (SMoE) and Sensor Availability Learning (SAL). Specifically, SMoE organizes RGB, LiDAR, and joint fusion into specialized pathways, enabling full-width unimodal representations while retaining the original multimodal features via a bounded residual. Beyond sensor dropout, SAL employs a staged curriculum with explicit routing supervision, progressively improving robustness under severe sensing conditions. Remarkably, SAL also yields notable gains for conventional models, highlighting its broader potential for resilient multimodal learning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SpikeOcc maintains competitive full-sensor accuracy while markedly raising the performance floor under sensor outages. SpikeOcc illustrates the promise of neuromorphic computing, pointing toward more reliable and energy-efficient autonomous driving.

</details>


### [106] [Frozen History: When Temporal Losses Stop Accumulation in Learned Real-Time Supersampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15752)
*Hao Yu,Guoying Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示：在精确配准下，时间一致性损失会抑制累积（网络在静态内容上复制历史），并识别出四类掩盖该效应的配准错误；修正配准后，小幅时间权重配合归一化累积器能显著提升QRISP超采样指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习式实时超采样沿运动矢量累积抖动低分辨率样本，常用时间一致性损失减少闪烁。但作者发现该损失在配准准确时会抑制累积：静态内容上网络几乎零成本复制其warped历史，导致模型"冻结"而退化解。相机抖动（运动矢量抖动）会抬高此成本从而掩盖问题。

Method: 识别并分析四类可被训练掩盖的配准错误：携带抖动的运动矢量、两个分辨率网格共用一个运动场、抖动深度、未配准的skip路径。用无模型oracle验证正确配准的效果，并进行受控训练实验，跨三个网络族、两个尺度验证，同时用归一化样本累积器与小时间权重。

Result: 无模型oracle显示正确配准使静态像素冻结成本降低两个数量级。受控训练显示配准反转了时间权重对PSNR的影响：精确配准下权重增大会降低像素方差但损失精度（权重0.25时静态帧损失0.7-0.9 dB），且在未抖动STSS数据上也降低精度。小时间权重+归一化累积器下，普通配准网络在×2/×3/×4放大到1080p时将QRISP最优PSNR提升0.34-0.62 dB；×2时静态像素标准差仅为DLSS 2的四分之一；无需重训练在Arm抖动Bistro捕获上超过经典时间上采样≥4.4 dB；最精确×4模型在H200上每1080p帧2.03 ms。

Conclusion: 时间一致性损失与配准质量密切相关：错误配准会掩盖累积抑制问题，修正配准后可获得更优的准确率、稳定性与感知质量。提出方法在多个尺度与数据集上超越现有最优QRISP，并达到实时性能。

Abstract: Learned real-time supersampling accumulates jittered low-resolution samples along motion vectors. Many methods use a temporal consistency loss to reduce flicker. We show that with accurate registration, this loss can suppress accumulation: on static content, the network copies its warped history at almost no temporal cost. Camera jitter in motion vectors raises this cost and can hide the effect. We identify four registration errors that training can conceal: jitter-carrying motion vectors, one motion field for both resolution lattices, jittered depth, and an unregistered skip path. A model-free oracle shows that correct registration makes freezing two orders of magnitude cheaper on static pixels. Controlled training shows that registration reverses the temporal weight's effect on PSNR. Under exact registration, increasing the weight reduces pixel variance at the expense of accuracy and perceived quality, with a 0.7 to 0.9 dB loss on static frames at weight 0.25. This reversal recurs across three network families and two scales; the loss also reduces accuracy on unjittered STSS data. With a small temporal weight and a normalised sample accumulator, a plain registered network improves the best published QRISP PSNR by 0.34 to 0.62 dB at $\times$2, $\times$3 and $\times$4 upscaling to 1080p. At $\times$2, its static pixel standard deviation is one quarter that of DLSS 2. Without retraining, it outperforms classical temporal upsampling by at least 4.4 dB on Arm's jittered Bistro capture. Our most accurate $\times$4 model runs in 2.03 ms per 1080p frame on an H200. Code: https://github.com/CV-AC/Frozen_history

</details>


### [107] [SlotAVS: Object-Centric Audio-Visual Segmentation via Bi-Modal Slot Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15764)
*Arnav Sharma,Anand Mishra,Renu Rameshan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SlotAVS 提出以竞争性双模态 slot attention 为核心的 object-centric 框架，将 AVS 从全局跨模态融合转向显式竞争绑定，在 AVSBench 上取得 S4 88.6% mIoU 与 MS3 71.7% mIoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多源音频-视觉分割中，并发声源常在共享视觉表示里纠缠，导致掩码合并或专业化不稳定；现有对齐导向的 transformer 架构难以分离不同声学实体。

Method: 1) 由时间音频序列初始化离散 object slots；2) 对空间视觉 token 施加严格 slot-wise 竞争以实现竞争性双模态 slot attention 与显式绑定；3) 引入匈牙利匹配，用空间与边界重叠最优对齐 slots 与真值对象；4) 采用数值稳定、置信度感知的掩码融合机制聚合多源预测。

Result: 在 AVSBench 上，Single-Source (S4) 达 88.6% mIoU，Multi-Source (MS3) 达 71.7% mIoU。

Conclusion: 显式竞争绑定对复杂音视频场景理解至关重要，可在不单纯依赖基础模型能力的前提下有效利用其先验。

Abstract: Audio-Visual Segmentation (AVS) requires predicting pixel-wise masks of sounding objects by grounding acoustic signals in visual space. In multi-source scenes, concurrent sound sources often remain entangled within shared visual representations, leading to merged masks or unstable specialization when using standard alignment-focused transformer architectures. To address this, we present SlotAVS, an object-centric framework driven by competitive bi-modal slot attention. While robust foundation models provide rich semantic priors, SlotAVS shifts the paradigm from global cross-modal fusion to explicit competitive binding. By initializing discrete object slots from temporal audio sequences and enforcing strict slot-wise competition over spatial visual tokens, our method structurally mitigates the blending of distinct acoustic entities. Further, to stabilize instance-level learning, we introduce a Hungarian matching strategy that optimally aligns slots with ground-truth objects using spatial and boundary overlap. Coupled with a numerically stable, confidence-aware mask fusion mechanism, SlotAVS robustly aggregates multi-source predictions. Extensive experiments on the AVSBench dataset demonstrate the efficacy of our approach, achieving 88.6% mIoU on the Single-Source (S4) split and 71.7% mIoU on the Multi-Source (MS3) split. These results show that explicit competitive binding is critical for complex audio-visual scene understanding and effectively leveraging foundation-model priors without relying solely on their capacity.

</details>


### [108] [Data-Centric Recursive Self-Improvement for Navigation via Policy-Synchronized Language-Action Dual Bootstrapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15782)
*Songze Li,Haoming Gu,Ziyang Gong,Zhitong Wang,Gengze Zhou,Zun Wang,Yuhan Zhu,Tianxiang Jiang,Juanzi Li,Qi Wu,Weinan Zhang,Yu Qiao,Wenwei Zhang,Yi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RSI-Nav是一种数据中心式的递归自改进框架，通过策略同步的语言-动作双自举，统一导航与指令生成，实现导航性能与指令生成能力的协同提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言导航需要高质量语言监督，但人工标注成本高。现有数据合成方法通常将导航与指令生成分离，或用特定组件耦合两任务，且多数用固定模型一次性标注全部轨迹后才更新，导致生成的监督滞后于不断演化的策略。

Method: 提出RSI-Nav框架：在共享模型内统一视觉-语言到动作（指令跟随）和视觉-动作到语言（指令生成）。先经监督微调建立基础能力，再以小块（shard）方式逐步处理无标注轨迹——当前模型生成指令、执行评估目标完成度与路径效率、保留合格样本，并在处理下一块前立即更新自身，从而保持监督与演化策略对齐。同时引入空间理解数据以增强空间推理。

Result: 在R2R-CE上达到68.6% SR，在RxR-CE上达到72.4% SR，同时递归演化后指令生成效用更强，空间理解也略有提升。

Conclusion: RSI-Nav通过策略同步的递归自改进与语言-动作双自举，有效解决了监督滞后问题，在导航、指令生成与空间理解三方面均取得良好效果。

Abstract: Vision-language navigation requires agents to follow natural-language instructions to reach target locations, making high-quality language supervision essential. Since human annotation is costly, prior work has explored scalable data synthesis methods. However, existing methods typically either use separate models for navigation and instruction generation or couple the two tasks through task-specific components; most also annotate the full trajectory corpus with a fixed model before updating, which can cause generated supervision to lag behind the evolving policy. Therefore, we introduce RSI-Nav, a data-centric recursive self-improvement framework for navigation via policy-synchronized language-action dual bootstrapping, which unifies vision-language-to-action instruction following and vision-action-to-language instruction generation within a shared model. Specifically, after supervised fine-tuning establishes fundamental capabilities, RSI-Nav progressively processes unlabeled trajectories in small shards. The current model generates instructions for these trajectories, executes them to evaluate goal completion and path efficiency, retains qualified samples, and immediately updates itself before processing the next shard. This shard-wise update keeps newly generated supervision aligned with the evolving policy. We also incorporate spatial understanding data, since stronger spatial reasoning benefits navigation. Extensive experiments show that RSI-Nav reaches 68.6% SR on R2R-CE and 72.4% SR on RxR-CE, while providing stronger instruction-generation utility and modestly improved spatial understanding after recursive evolution.

</details>


### [109] [Reference-Efficient Multimodal Bitstream-Corrupted Video Recovery via Depth-Conditioned Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15806)
*Tianyi Liu,Jiaojiao Wang,Ye Lu,Kejun Wu,Chengtao Cai,Zhengguo Li,Chia-Wen Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出多模态比特流损坏视频恢复(MBSCVR)框架,利用RGB与深度信息,通过可靠性感知的多模态选择器和深度条件参考自适应(DCRA),用单个非局部参考实现更高质量、更低资源的视频恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受损视频比特流恢复对可靠多媒体通信与存储很重要。现有方法依赖局部残差和非局部RGB参考,但两者解码后可能都不可靠,且增加参考会提升接收端计算和内存开销。

Method: 提出MBSCVR框架:结合传输前的深度几何信息辅助重建。包含可靠性感知多模态选择器,在参考编码前融合有效外观与深度兼容性;并提出深度条件参考自适应(DCRA),将选中的RGB证据引入特征补全与内容生成,同时用帧对齐深度条件化补全查询。每个恢复窗口仅用一个选中的非局部RGB参考。

Result: 在完整BSCV基准(oracle masks)上,YouTube-VOS达32.30 dB、DAVIS达28.93 dB,分别超过全参考B2SCVR 0.27和0.33 dB,且SSIM均提升。接收端前向测量显示延迟降低36.8%,峰值内存降低40.8%。

Conclusion: 紧凑的几何引导与自适应外观处理能改善多媒体恢复的质量-资源权衡。

Abstract: Recovering visually faithful content from damaged video bitstreams is important for reliable multimedia communication and storage. Existing methods exploit local residuals and non-local RGB references of the input video sequence, but both sources can become unreliable after decoding, while processing additional references increases receiver computation and memory. This paper studies multimodal bitstream-corrupted video recovery (MBSCVR) using RGB and depth information, where pre-transmission depth-based geometry aids reconstruction when the received appearance is corrupted. We propose a novel MBSCVR framework that selects and effectively utilizes a single selected non-local RGB reference per recovery window to achieve higher performance. It integrates a reliability-aware multimodal selector that combines valid appearance with depth compatibility before reference encoding. Depth-Conditioned Reference Adaptation (DCRA) introduces selected RGB evidence into feature completion and content generation, while frame-aligned depth conditions completion queries. On the complete BSCV benchmark with oracle masks, MBSCVR uses one selected reference to achieve 32.30 dB on YouTube-VOS and 28.93 dB on DAVIS, surpassing full-reference B2SCVR by 0.27 and 0.33 dB and improving SSIM on both subsets. Matched receiver-forward measurements show 36.8\% lower latency and 40.8\% lower peak memory. These results demonstrate how compact geometric guidance and adapted appearance processing improve the quality--resource trade-off of multimedia recovery.

</details>


### [110] [Totem: Object-Centric 3D Memory for World-Consistent Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15807)
*Zichen Wan,Tianyu Huang,Fan Li,Kai Wang,Hongzhi Zhang,Wangmeng Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Totem：面向世界一致性视频生成的物体中心3D记忆框架，通过持久3D记忆与双流3D条件架构，在大视角变化下保持物体一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大视角变化下生成连贯视频仍是难题：现有方法难以维持单个物体的稳定表征，导致物体一致性差、空间布局紊乱，难以构建统一的世界模型。

Method: 1) 将每个选定物体表示为对齐到共享世界坐标系的持久3D记忆，并赋予独立状态；2) 设计双流3D条件架构，结合物体记忆与场景几何进行目标视角合成；3) 用物体记忆以3D结构信息约束模型注意力，从而保持物体表面与相对空间布局。

Result: 在世界生成与物体中心基准的广泛实验表明，在挑战性相机轨迹下，Totem生成的视频呈现更物体一致的3D世界，并支持多种应用。

Conclusion: 以物体为中心、对齐共享世界帧的3D记忆是提升大视角变化下世界一致性视频生成的有效途径，兼顾一致性与多任务适用性。

Abstract: Generating coherent videos grounded in a unified underlying world remains a major challenge, particularly under large viewpoint changes, where existing methods often struggle to maintain stable representations of individual objects. To this end, we introduce Totem, an object-centric 3D memory framework for world-consistent video generation. Totem first represents each selected object as a persistent 3D memory aligned to a shared world frame, assigning it a dedicated state. Based on these memories, we design a dual-stream 3D conditioning architecture that combines object memories with scene geometry for target-view synthesis. Moreover, these object memories constrain the model's attention with 3D structural information, thereby preserving object surfaces and relative spatial layouts. Extensive experiments on world-generation and object-centric benchmarks demonstrate that Totem generates videos depicting a more object-consistent 3D world under challenging camera trajectories, while supporting versatile applications.

</details>


### [111] [Adaptive Look, Flexible Thought: Learning How and How Long to Think in Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15810)
*Quanlin Chen,Xiuwei Chen,Kun Xiang,Zehua Ma,Chunjin Ye,Boqiang Guo,Xianming Liu,Xiaodan Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出L2T统一推理框架，将视觉感知与文本推理纳入共享自回归空间，联合优化"看"与"想"的形式和深度，在保持竞争性能的同时实现1.7-3.6倍吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型的快慢推理范式主要调控文本推理长度，而外部工具或视觉token带来的视觉计算仅被视为辅助组件，导致视觉感知与文本推理无法联合优化。

Method: L2T首先将工具产生的视觉状态离散化为视觉token，使视觉感知与文本推理进入共享的自回归空间；在此基础上引入模态感知路由奖励来选择合适推理模式，并采用难度感知的快慢策略根据任务复杂度自适应调整推理深度。

Result: 在通用感知与推理密集型任务上验证了有效性；相比显式工具调用方法，保持竞争性能的同时吞吐量提升1.7-3.6倍。

Conclusion: 通过统一视觉与文本计算空间并联合优化计算形式与深度，L2T有效解决了视觉与文本推理分离的问题，兼顾性能与效率。

Abstract: The introduction of long chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning and external tools has substantially improved the performance of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) on general and reasoning tasks. However, existing fast-slow reasoning paradigms mainly regulate textual reasoning length, while visual computation introduced by external tools or visual tokens is often treated as an auxiliary component. This separation prevents MLLMs from jointly optimizing visual perception and textual reasoning. To address this issue, we propose L2T (adaptive Look, flexible Thought), a unified reasoning framework that jointly optimizes the form and depth of computation. L2T first discretizes tool-produced visual states into visual tokens, bringing visual perception and textual reasoning into a shared autoregressive space. Based on this unified representation, L2T introduces a modality-aware routing reward to select appropriate reasoning modes and a difficulty-aware fast-slow strategy to adapt reasoning depth according to task complexity. Experiments on general perception and reasoning-intensive tasks demonstrate the effectiveness of L2T. Compared with explicit tool-calling approaches, L2T maintains competitive performance while achieving 1.7--3.6 times higher throughput.

</details>


### [112] [ChangeT$^3$: Evaluating Vision Test-Time Training for Remote Sensing Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15813)
*Sharukshan Niranjan,Oshadha Samarakoon,Nichula Wasalathilaka,Roshan Godaliyadda,Parakrama Ekanayake,Vijitha Herath*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ChangeT³是一种朴素Siamese双时相变化检测模型，将ViT³-Tiny编码器与ChangeFormer多尺度解码器结合，通过独立的快权重实现图像条件测试时训练，在LEVIR-CD+上取得80.61 F1，但未实现零样本迁移且速度较慢。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双时相遥感变化检测需要兼顾空间细节与上下文信息。视觉测试时训练可替代显式token-to-token注意力，为检验图像条件内部更新提供一个无需额外时间交互模块的简洁设置。

Method: 采用朴素Siamese配置：共享参数的ViT³-Tiny分层编码器（保留全局SwiGLU与局部自适应深度卷积分支），两条图像流独立计算快权重；解码器在四个分辨率上融合成对特征并自粗到细聚合，输出二值预测。与ChangeFormer在相同解码器、训练配方和预算下从零训练于LEVIR-CD+（三随机种子，按该数据集标准协议在测试集上选检查点）。

Result: ChangeT³达F1 80.61±0.90，ChangeFormer为78.84±0.21；禁用内部更新的同一编码器为78.63±0.20。两模型均无法零样本迁移至SYSU-CD、DSIFN-CD或WHU-CD；在小输入下ChangeT³虽参数量与运算量相近但速度更慢。

Conclusion: 图像条件快权重更新带来的性能提升有限且不稳定（方差较大），并伴随速度代价与零样本泛化缺失，其实际价值仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Bitemporal remote sensing change detection requires representations that preserve spatial detail while capturing contextual information relevant to changes between acquisitions. Visual test-time training offers an alternative to explicit token-to-token attention by adapting a compact inner model to each image. This letter describes ChangeT$^3$, a vanilla Siamese configuration combining the existing hierarchical ViT$^3$-Tiny encoder with the multiscale ChangeFormer decoder. The two image streams share learned encoder parameters, while their fast weights are computed independently from their respective spatial features. Each encoder block retains the inherited global simplified SwiGLU and local adaptive depthwise-convolution branches. Temporal comparison is performed by the decoder through learned fusion of paired features at four resolutions, followed by coarse-to-fine aggregation and binary prediction. This separation provides a direct setting for examining image-conditioned inner updates without introducing an additional temporal interaction module. Trained from scratch on LEVIR-CD+ with the same decoder, recipe, and budget as a ChangeFormer control, ChangeT$^3$ reaches an F1 of 80.61$\pm$0.90 against 78.84$\pm$0.21 for ChangeFormer (three seeds each); as in the standard protocol for this dataset, checkpoints are selected on the test split. Trained with both inner updates disabled, the same encoder reaches 78.63$\pm$0.20. Neither model transfers zero-shot to SYSU-CD, DSIFN-CD, or WHU-CD, and ChangeT$^3$ is the slower of the two at small inputs despite similar parameter and operation counts.

</details>


### [113] [Mobility Helps Learning: Unsupervised Model Adaptation for Object Recognition via Movement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15846)
*Yanan Ma,Yihang Tao,Zhengru Fang,Senkang Hu,Xianhao Chen,Yuguang Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MoCaFe，利用移动智能体在运动中观测同一对象产生的预测方差进行无监督测试时适应，显著提升物体识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TTA方法将测试数据视为独立快照，忽略了移动智能体在不同距离和视角下对同一对象预测质量变化这一丰富监督信号。

Method: 先做mobility-calibrated伪标签过滤，用逆方差权重融合跨视角预测得到可靠伪标签，并指导难样本选择；进一步提出p-MoCaFe，用公开数据为样本赋权，得到有界偏差的干净损失估计器。

Result: 在nuScenes、KITTI及具身智能体数据集上，MoCaFe显著优于SOTA TTA方法，准确率提升最多约20%。

Conclusion: MoCaFe是模型无关、超参不敏感的范式，利用运动诱导的预测方差实现有效无监督适应。

Abstract: Movable agents, such as autonomous vehicles and robots, should be able to autonomously adapt their pre-trained object recognition models to new environments without human annotations. While existing test-time adaptation (TTA) methods address label-free model adaptation, they typically treat test data as independent snapshots, without constructively exploiting a rich, unique source of supervision: the significant variance in prediction quality as an agent observes the same object from different distances and viewpoints. To capitalize on this, we propose \texttt{MoCaFe}, a model-agnostic and hyperparameter-insensitive paradigm that leverages motion-induced prediction variance for unsupervised adaptation. Specifically, we first develop mobility-calibrated filtering of pseudo-labels, which constructs reliable pseudo-labels by fusing cross-view predictions using inverse-variance weights. The filtered pseudo-labels then guide the selection of ``hard samples'' for effective learning. To further suppress pseudo-label corruption, we introduce \texttt{p-MoCaFe}, an optional extension that uses a public dataset to assign weighting factors to data samples, yielding an estimator of the clean loss with bounded bias. Extensive experiments on autonomous driving (nuScenes, KITTI) and embodied-agent datasets show that \texttt{MoCaFe} significantly outperforms state-of-the-art TTA schemes, achieving up to approximately 20% accuracy gain.

</details>


### [114] [Sharper Frames, Worse Rooms: Viewpoint Coverage Dominates Image Quality in Feed-Forward 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15854)
*Alibek Dadajonov,Hikmatillo Umarhojiyev*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在小视图预算下，选帧时追求图像清晰度反而有害；视角覆盖度比单帧质量更重要，且推理时的不确定性信号无法揭示该上游失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈多视图重建受固定视图预算限制，选帧成为关键上游决策。研究检验了传统偏好"清晰帧"在该设定下是否合理。

Method: 对九个手持房间扫描各采样200帧，比较多种选帧策略；分析图像质量与相机运动的耦合；开展2×2析因实验独立操纵视角分布与清晰度；测试推理时的多视图一致性分数与VLM置信度。

Result: 选最清晰16帧最差（房间尺寸误差48%），均匀时序采样仅3%。清晰帧集中于慢速运动区，导致近192°（最差超300°）未观测。视角分布主效应约为质量的三倍；模糊但分布好的选帧在9个中8个胜过清晰但聚集的。优势随预算增大、角度覆盖饱和而减小。多视图一致性分数随重建误差上升，VLM置信度与其空间估计误差无关。

Conclusion: 在有限视图预算下，视角覆盖比单帧图像质量更是关键约束，且现有推理期不确定性信号均无法揭示这一上游失败。

Abstract: Grounded spatial reasoning about indoor scenes rests on a geometric reconstruction that the multimodal system does not itself produce. Feed-forward multi-view reconstruction models operate under a fixed view budget, making frame selection a critical upstream decision. We show that under this setting the conventional preference for sharp frames can be actively harmful. Across nine handheld room scans, selecting the sharpest 16 of 200 candidate frames is the worst strategy we test, reaching 48\% room dimension error where uniform temporal sampling reaches 3\%. We trace this failure to a coupling between image quality and camera motion: sharp frames tend to occur during slow motion and therefore concentrate the selection in a narrow angular region, leaving $192^\circ$ of the room unobserved in the case we illustrate and over $300^\circ$ in the worst cells. A $2\times2$ factorial experiment that independently manipulates viewpoint spread and per-frame sharpness shows that spread has roughly three times the main effect of quality, and that blurry but well spread selections outperform sharp but clustered selections in 8 of 9 captures. The advantage also diminishes as the frame budget increases and angular coverage saturates, consistent with this mechanism. Finally, we ask whether the failure is detectable at inference: the reconstruction model's multi-view consistency score increases with reconstruction error, while a vision-language model given the same frames reports confidence unrelated to its own spatial estimation error. Under small view budgets, viewpoint coverage is a more important constraint than per-frame image quality, and neither uncertainty signal available at inference reveals this upstream failure.

</details>


### [115] [Learning Multimodal Sensor Fusion with JEPA Representations for Breathing Activity and Pose Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15891)
*Yunxiang Luo,Constantino Álvarez Casado,Xiaoting Wu,Nhi Nguyen,Le Nguyen,Manuel Lage Cañellas,Miguel Bordallo López*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估将冻结的V-JEPA2特征迁移到RGB、深度和雷达异构传感器上，通过统一融合模型在所有非空传感器子集上分类呼吸活动与姿态，并系统性考察子集监督、池化能力和表示迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测性视频表示本身无法说明多模态系统应如何处理缺失传感器，因此需要在异构传感器和多种缺失子集条件下进行受控评估。

Method: 将冻结的V-JEPA2特征迁移到RGB视频、深度视频和雷达时间序列；采用共享融合模型，包含模态查询、固定传感器槽位和显式可用性标记，对7个非空传感器子集进行分类；在OMuSense-23的39名受试者上，用5个受试者分组折和3个随机种子评估子集监督、池化容量和表示迁移。

Result: 在匹配下游参数量的情况下，最终模型在7个子集上平均达到记录级活动宏F1 75.23%±0.60；每个批次监督所有子集相较匹配的查询池化控制提升1.923个百分点（条件95%受试者bootstrap区间[0.647, 3.209]）；平衡监督下查询池化相较匹配均值池化无明显优势；雷达探针支持超越池化小波像素的迁移；64-token推理近似保持平均活动性能；Consensus-JEPA损失相较精确控制无测量收益。

Conclusion: 冻结V-JEPA2特征可有效迁移到异构多模态传感器，且通过显式可用性与全子集监督能提升缺失传感器场景下的稳健性；但查询池化未显示明显优势，Consensus-JEPA损失亦未带来收益。

Abstract: Predictive video representations do not by themselves specify how a multimodal system should handle missing sensors. We transfer frozen V-JEPA2 features to a set of heterogeneous sensors comprised by RGB video, depth video, and radar time series. A shared fusion model uses modality queries, fixed sensor slots, and explicit availability to classify breathing activity and pose from all seven non-empty sensor subsets. The contribution is a controlled evaluation of subset supervision, pooling capacity, and representation transfer on 39 OMuSense-23 subjects with five subject-group folds and three seeds. With matched downstream parameter counts, the final model reaches recording-level activity macro-F1 of $75.23%\pm0.60$ averaged across seven subsets. Supervising all subsets in each batch improves the matched query-pooling control by 1.923 percentage points (conditional 95% subject-bootstrap interval [0.647, 3.209]), while query pooling shows no clear advantage over matched mean pooling under balanced supervision. Radar probes support transfer beyond pooled wavelet pixels, 64-token inference approximately preserves mean activity performance, and the tested Consensus-JEPA loss adds no measured benefit against its exact control.

</details>


### [116] [Gen Through the Lens: Camera-Controllable Video Generation via Cross-Coordinate Conditioning Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15900)
*Shuai Li,Jinxu Xiang,Xinyuan Li,Hanlei Guo,Xiyang Tan,Alan Zhao,Xiaotong Zhao,Ran Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Gen Through the Lens 框架，通过融合相机、世界、物体三个坐标空间的控制信号，实现可控且物理一致的图像到视频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有方法将相机运动视为抽象条件或仅依赖单一坐标系，缺乏对相机运动、焦点与构图精确且物理一致的控制。

Method: 显式分解并融合三个坐标空间的控制信号：相机坐标（内参）、世界坐标（空间几何提示）、物体坐标（目标主体的3D包围盒），并通过跨坐标条件融合统一异构线索。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在多种相机操作下生成可控且视觉一致的结果，在真实感与可编辑性上优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 多坐标空间的条件融合能有效提升图像到视频生成中相机控制的精确性与一致性。

Abstract: We present Gen Through the Lens, a camera-controllable image-to-video generation framework that synthesizes videos from a given reference image while enabling realistic and physically consistent camera operations. Unlike previous models that treat camera motion as abstract conditioning or rely on a single coordinate system, our approach explicitly decomposes and fuses control signals across three coordinate spaces: the camera coordinate (modeled by intrinsic parameters), the world coordinate (represented by spatial geometry hints), and the object coordinate (described by 3D bounding boxes of target subjects). Through cross-coordinate conditioning fusion, these heterogeneous cues are unified to guide the generative process, enabling precise control of camera motion, focus, and framing while preserving temporal and spatial coherence. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method produces controllable and visually consistent results across diverse camera operations, outperforming existing approaches in both realism and editability.

</details>


### [117] [VIEWCRAFT: Evolving What Multimodal Agents See and How They Read It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15915)
*Binxiao Xu,Ruichuan An,Bocheng Zou,Weihua Du,Xinyu Wei,Hangyu Guo,YiFan Zhang,Renye Yan,Chengqi Duan,Hang Hua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ViewCraft 通过联合进化可执行的证据界面与其阅读程序，提升多模态智能体的可复用技能，无需更新模型权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可复用技能可在不更新模型权重的情况下改进多模态智能体，但其效果依赖于所呈现的证据及读取证据的过程，二者应协同设计。

Method: 联合进化证据界面（选择、变换、组织观察）与阅读程序（解释视图）；利用失败轨迹指导修订，通过重放与配对评估保留有效改动，并用界面投影从合格的被拒联合提案中恢复视觉编辑。

Result: 在八个基准上平均提升任务分数 6.1 分，每个基准均有增益；在相同输入 token 预算下优势保持；在全部五个多源任务中，学习到的界面比初始技能少用 8%-15% 的输入 token；学到的技能可跨执行器迁移并在所有迁移评估中优于直接执行。

Conclusion: 证据构建与阅读应作为耦合的可复用技能，ViewCraft 验证了其有效性与迁移性。

Abstract: Reusable skills improve multimodal agents without updating model weights, but their effectiveness depends on both the evidence presented and the procedure used to read it. We introduce ViewCraft, which jointly evolves an executable evidence interface and its reading procedure. The interface selects, transforms, and organizes observations; the reader interprets the resulting views. Failure traces guide revisions, replay and paired evaluation retain useful changes, and interface projection recovers visual edits from eligible rejected joint proposals. Across eight benchmarks, ViewCraft improves over direct execution by an average of $6.1$ points in task score, with gains on every benchmark. The advantage persists at a matched input-token budget. On all five multi-source tasks, the learned interface uses 8%-15% fewer input tokens than Initial skill. The learned skills also transfer across executors and outperform direct execution in every evaluated transfer, supporting evidence construction and reading as a coupled, reusable skill.

</details>


### [118] [Frequency-Step Cache: Separating Synchronization and Repair for Efficient Autoregressive Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15922)
*Kerry Xu,Param Misra,Zachary Stenglein,Athri Karunamurthy,Fanjiang Ye,Yuke Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FSC方法，解耦缓存同步与修复两个决策，在自回归视频生成中提升速度-质量权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频生成需反复前向传播大型扩散Transformer，开销大。现有免训练缓存方法将"何时精确同步缓存"与"同步间修复多少计算"两个决策耦合，限制了保真度。

Method: FSC分离同步与修复：在稠密到稀疏轨迹上调度精确全计算锚点；用基于频率漂移与token年龄的轻量潜在风险信号控制锚点间修复，支持整步回放、选择性token重算或完全修复。

Result: SkyReels-V2上FSC-Speed达2.36x加速（胜过MotionCache 2.275x），SSIM+0.019、PSNR+0.5dB；匹配速度下FSC-Quality提升SSIM 0.031、PSNR 1.0dB、降低LPIPS 0.024；无调参迁移至14B模型2.12x vs 1.79x。分离优势在FramePack、Matrix-Game上同样成立。

Conclusion: 将缓存同步与修复解耦并用反应性信号统一控制会降低保真度；FSC在多个自回归视频骨干网络上均改善速度-质量权衡，并具备跨规模迁移能力。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) video generation enables long-horizon synthesis but requires repeated forward passes through large diffusion transformers. Training-free caching reduces this cost by reusing intermediate computation, yet existing controllers often couple two decisions: when to synchronize the cache exactly and how much computation to repair between synchronizations. We introduce Frequency-Step Cache (FSC), which separates these roles. FSC schedules exact full-computation anchors on a dense-to-sparse trajectory, while a lightweight latent-space risk based on frequency-resolved drift and token age controls inter-anchor repair through whole-step replay, selective token recomputation, or full repair. On SkyReels-V2, whole-step reuse provides most of the acceleration, while selective token computation is most effective as fidelity repair. A controlled ablation shows that using one reactive signal for both synchronization and repair reduces fidelity at matched latency, with the same separation advantage observed on FramePack and Matrix-Game. Across multiple autoregressive video backbones, FSC improves speed-quality trade-offs. On SkyReels-V2, FSC-Speed reaches 2.36x speedup versus MotionCache's 2.275x while improving SSIM by 0.019 and PSNR by 0.5 dB. At MotionCache-matched speed, FSC-Quality improves SSIM by 0.031 and PSNR by 1.0 dB and lowers LPIPS by 0.024. Without retuning, FSC-Quality transfers to SkyReels-V2 14B at 2.12x speedup versus 1.79x for MotionCache.

</details>


### [119] [When Trackers Fail: VLM-Guided Verification and Recovery for Robust Video Object Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15929)
*Valay Bundele,Susmit Agrawal,Mehran Hosseinzadeh,The Nam Nguyen,Hendrik P. A. Lensch*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Dual-Agent是一个故障感知跟踪框架，通过显式验证与恢复机制补充掩码传播，提升跟踪鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于SAM等基础模型的跟踪器通过记忆传播掩码性能强，但在遮挡、重现或干扰下，错误预测会进入跟踪循环并随时间传播，导致持续的身份漂移。现有方法仅改进记忆和时序建模，缺乏显式识别或纠正故障的机制。

Method: 提出Dual-Agent框架：一个视觉-语言验证agent基于时空上下文推理判断跟踪器是否仍在跟随目标；检测到故障后，一个纠正agent利用时序上下文恢复目标再继续跟踪。纠正agent在真实SAM3故障上训练，并加入身份切换和干扰物扰动。

Result: 在LVOS-v2和SecVOS上分别将SAM3提升1.2和2.7 J&F。无需重训练即可迁移到SAM2.1、SeC、Cutie，以及LaSOT-ext、TNL2K、VOT-LT2022等长期视频目标跟踪基准。

Conclusion: 显式推理跟踪何时失败以及如何恢复，为鲁棒跟踪提供了补充传播改进之外的路径。

Abstract: Recent trackers built on foundation models such as the Segment Anything Model (SAM) achieve strong performance by propagating object masks through memory. However, this strength is also a key source of failure: under occlusion, reappearance, or distractors, an incorrect prediction can enter the tracking loop and be propagated over time, resulting in persistent identity drift. Existing approaches seek to prevent such failures through improved memory and temporal modeling, but once a failure occurs, the tracker has no explicit mechanism to recognize or correct it. We address this gap with Dual-Agent, a failure-aware tracking framework that complements propagation with explicit verification and recovery. We address this gap with Dual-Agent, a failure-aware tracking framework that complements propagation with explicit verification and recovery. A vision-language verification agent first reasons over spatio-temporal context to determine whether the tracker is still following the target. When a failure is detected, a correction agent uses temporal context to recover the target before tracking continues. The correction agent is trained on real SAM3 failures, augmented with targeted identity-switch and distractor perturbations that expose it to challenging ambiguities during recovery. On LVOS-v2 and SecVOS, Dual-Agent improves SAM3 by 1.2 and 2.7 $J\&F$, respectively. Importantly, the proposed framework transfers without retraining to SAM2.1, SeC, and Cutie, as well as to long-term video object tracking benchmarks including LaSOT-ext, TNL2K, and VOT-LT2022. Our results show that explicitly reasoning about when tracking fails and how to recover provides a complementary path to robust tracking beyond improving propagation alone. Project page: valaybundele.github.io/Dual-Agent.

</details>


### [120] [Meaningful Moments: A Corpus of VLM-Derived Temporal Importance Labels for Video Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16009)
*Cameron Keith,SouYoung Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Meaningful Moments (MM)语料库，包含约458万个VLM生成的片段重要性分数，覆盖622个动作类别，验证VLM能否大规模识别有意义时刻并提升动作识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频中并非每一时刻对识别动作都同等重要，少数决定性时刻往往承载关键证据。但现有视频数据集很少提供大规模、动作条件下的片段重要性标注，因此需要探究VLM能否大规模识别有意义的时刻，以及这些标签能否改善动作识别。

Method: 构建MM语料库，包含约458万条VLM生成的逐片段重要性分数，覆盖近50万个类别标注视频（SSv2、K400、Diving-48，共622个动作类别）。冻结动作分类器，仅改变时间采样方式。采用alpha参数化的重要性导向选择与重要性反转对照进行对比，以区分VLM信号带来的增益与变密度采样本身带来的增益。同时进行直接评分与贪心移除的对比，以及四VLM跨预言机研究。

Result: 在三个数据集上VLM重要性均有信息量：硬切端点下保留VLM认为重要的片段优于保留其认定的填充片段。Diving-48上重要性导向快进可保持全视频识别性能，而反转对照下降约30个百分点；K400上硬切选择比均匀采样高1.45个百分点，阈值选择下提升至2.05个百分点；SSv2上标签有信号但未能恢复全视频性能。直接评分在噪声范围内匹配贪心移除，且预言机调用约少25倍，四VLM研究显示一致的下游趋势。

Conclusion: VLM可在大规模上识别有意义的时刻，其重要性标签能有效提升动作识别且效果依赖类别结构。作者以CC-BY-4.0发布重要性标签与评估协议（SSv2类别字符串保留CC-BY-NC-4.0）。

Abstract: Not every moment in a video is equally useful for recognizing an action. A few decisive moments often carry the evidence: the instant an object moves, the peak of a dive, or one object placed on another. Existing video datasets rarely provide large-scale, action-conditioned labels for which short segments carry that evidence. This work asks whether vision-language models can identify meaningful moments at scale and whether those labels improve action recognition.We introduce Meaningful Moments (MM), a corpus of about 4.58 million VLM-generated per-segment importance scores for nearly 500,000 class-labeled videos spanning Something-Something v2 (SSv2), Kinetics-400 (K400), and Diving-48 across 622 action classes. We freeze the action classifier and vary only temporal sampling. An alpha-parameterized comparison of importance-led selection against an importance-inverted control separates gains from the VLM signal from gains caused by variable-density sampling alone. Across all three datasets, VLM-derived importance is informative: at the hard-cut endpoint, keeping VLM-important segments outperforms keeping VLM-designated filler. Usefulness depends on class structure. On Diving-48, importance-led fast-forwarding preserves full-video recognition while the inverted control collapses by roughly 30 percentage points. On K400, hard-cut selection beats uniform sampling by 1.45 percentage points, rising to 2.05 under threshold selection. On SSv2, the labels carry signal but do not recover full-video performance. Direct scoring matches greedy removal within its noise floor at roughly 25 times fewer oracle calls, and a four-VLM cross-oracle study shows consistent downstream trends. We release the importance labels and evaluation protocol under CC-BY-4.0; SSv2 class strings remain under CC-BY-NC-4.0.

</details>


### [121] [Removing the Compute Bottleneck for Event-based Visual Place Recognition with Spiking Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16022)
*Somayeh Hussaini,Tobias Fischer,Michael Milford*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于稀疏脉冲神经网络的事件视觉位置识别管线，在普通CPU上实现数十微秒级查询，利用STDP学习双峰权重分布并大幅剪枝，兼顾召回率与低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经形态感知与计算本应带来低延迟和高能效，但现有事件视觉位置识别（VPR）管线在稀疏传感器下游重新引入密集的类帧计算，丧失了延迟优势。目标是在普通CPU上以数十微秒完成事件VPR查询。

Method: 训练一个两层脉冲神经网络（SNN），采用加性STDP，使权重矩阵趋向强双峰分布，便于激进幅度剪枝。推理时只读取查询中活跃像素对应的剪枝权重；由于存活权重幅度近似均匀，每个神经元的得分退化为匹配突触的整数计数而非加权求和，计算量小到可常驻CPU片上缓存。

Result: 在三个事件VPR基准上，稀疏SNN召回率超过现有事件SNN方法LENS，同时每查询延迟降低5.5–6.2倍至数十微秒；召回率与稠密版本及SAD基线相当，但读取权重减少10倍。还刻画了召回率与延迟随活跃像素数和参考地点数的变化规律。

Conclusion: 事件输入的稀疏性和学习权重的稀疏性均可直接在传统处理器上被利用以实现低延迟推理。

Abstract: Neuromorphic sensing and computing promise low latency and high energy efficiency for robotics, yet most event-based visual place recognition (VPR) pipelines reintroduce dense, frame-like computation downstream of an inherently sparse sensor, forfeiting the sensor's latency advantage. In this paper, we address the problem of resolving an event-based VPR query in tens of microseconds on a commodity CPU. We train a two-layer spiking neural network (SNN) with additive spike-timing-dependent plasticity (STDP), which drives the learned weight matrix towards a strongly bimodal distribution, amenable to aggressive magnitude pruning. At inference, our pipeline exploits this sparsity by reading only the pruned weights corresponding to the query's active pixels. As surviving weights share a near-uniform magnitude, each neuron's score reduces to an integer count of matching synapses instead of a weighted sum, lowering per-query latency. This computation is small enough to remain resident in a CPU's fast on-chip cache. On three event-based VPR benchmarks, our sparse SNN exceeds the recall of LENS, an existing event-based SNN, while reducing per-query latency by 5.5-6.2x to tens of microseconds. It matches the recall of a dense variant of our network and of the sum-of-absolute-differences (SAD) baseline, while reading 10x fewer weights than the dense variant. We further characterise how recall and latency scale with the number of active pixels and reference places. These results suggest that the sparsity of both event-based input and learned weights can already be exploited for low-latency inference on conventional processors.

</details>


### [122] [From Failure Prediction to Component Selection in Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16058)
*Minhui Kang,Yiyao Zhang,Peizhe Long,Jiafeng Qi,Hongyu Jin,Jiayun Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个以决策为中心的评价框架，连接"分数预测什么、能从现有字段学到什么、排名带来什么"三个问题，用于评估语义分割中组件失败预测器的实际选择价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在语义分割中，组件失败预测器用于决定哪些预测应被信任、保留或检查，但仅凭预测性能无法完全反映最终选择结果的价值，因此需要一种面向决策的评价方式。

Method: 构建决策中心评价框架，围绕三个方面：可用分数的预测能力、其现有输入字段可学习的信息、以及其排序带来的实际选择效果；在 Cityscapes 和 ACDC 上对三个分割系统进行实验，比较适配后的 MetaSeg、预测熵、直接概率排序，并研究图像嵌套源实验、复用完整字段集、以及将固定预测对映射到条件对数损失风险的效果。

Result: 在 90% 保留率下，适配 MetaSeg 在全部十二组"架构-人群"比较中相较预测熵降低了保留失败，但未相对直接概率排序建立失败优势；图像嵌套源实验显示，在三个系统中复用完整字段集均能提升两种学习器下的预测与保留损失表现；原始 OCRNet 探针提升预测但选择增益未定；固定预测对映射到条件对数损失风险产生混合选择效果。

Conclusion: 该框架区分了拟合分数的性能、其输入可获取的价值及其使用后果，为决定是否采用额外分数、重学现有字段或修改选择规则提供实用依据，并能解释组件级可靠性方法的收益与失败。

Abstract: Reliable scene understanding requires deciding which predictions to trust, retain, or inspect. In semantic segmentation, component failure predictors support these decisions, but their predictive performance does not fully describe the value of the resulting selection. We develop a decision-centered evaluation framework that connects three questions: what an available score predicts, what can be learned from its existing fields, and what its ranking delivers. Across three segmentation systems on Cityscapes and ACDC at 90% retention, adapted MetaSeg reduces retained failure relative to forecast entropy in all twelve architecture-population comparisons, but does not establish a failure advantage over direct probability ranking. Image-nested source experiments reveal a different opportunity: reusing the full field set improves predictive and retained-loss performance under both learners in all three systems, while the raw OCRNet probe improves prediction with unresolved selective gains. Mapping a fixed prediction pair to conditional log-loss risk produces mixed selection effects, which we examine through the components exchanged by the two rankings. These findings distinguish the performance of a fitted score from the value obtainable from its inputs and the consequences of its use. The framework provides a practical basis for deciding whether to adopt an additional score, relearn existing fields, or revise a selection rule, and for explaining the gains and failures of component-level reliability methods.

</details>


### [123] [PIGEONS: Incremental Long-Video Captioning with Persistent Identity Gallery and Evolving Overarching Narrative Summary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16066)
*Xinlong Chen,Weihong Lin,Yuanxing Zhang,Xinyu Liu,Yue Ding,Bozhou Li,Bohan Zeng,Yang Shi,Qiang Liu,Pengfei Wan,Liang Wang,Tieniu Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PIGEONS 通过持久身份库与演化式总览摘要实现长视频增量式详细描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频描述方法主要针对短视频，扩展到长视频受限于上下文窗口。简单的分段描述后拼接会导致主体指代不一致和长程叙事碎片化。

Method: 提出 PIGEONS 模型，包含持久身份库（预先扫描全视频构建关键主体及其视觉参考）和演化式总览叙事摘要（逐片段描述时紧凑维护更新长程上下文）。采用三阶段训练策略，结合有监督微调与强化学习。

Result: 大量实验表明 PIGEONS 显著提升长视频描述性能，在准确性、完整性和主体指代一致性上优于现有方法，同时在短视频描述上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: PIGEONS 通过身份库和演化摘要有效缓解长视频描述中的指代不一致与叙事碎片化问题。

Abstract: Detailed video captioning has become an essential semantic interface for multi-modal understanding and generation. While existing methods mainly target short videos, extending them to long videos remains challenging, primarily due to limited context windows of current video understanding models. A naive solution is to adopt segment-wise captioning followed by concatenation, yet this paradigm often suffers from inconsistent subject references and fragmented long-range narratives. To mitigate these issues, we introduce PIGEONS, an incremental long-video captioning model built upon a Persistent Identity Gallery and an Evolving Overarching Narrative Summary. Before caption generation, PIGEONS scans the entire video to construct an identity gallery that captures key subjects along with their visual references, thereby providing a stable referential anchor. The model then performs clip-by-clip captioning conditioned on the persistent gallery and an evolving overarching narrative summary, which compactly maintains and updates essential long-range contextual information after each captioning step. To enable these capabilities, we develop a three-stage training strategy that integrates supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PIGEONS markedly improves long-video captioning performance, achieving superior accuracy, completeness, and subject referential consistency over existing methods, while maintaining competitive performance on short-video captioning.

</details>


### [124] [Regime-Aware Router Learning for Multi-Domain Medical Image Generation with Diffusion Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16082)
*Yaoshuai Ma,Wei Yao,Yongxiang Liu,Feifan Wang,Da Chang,Pengxiang Xu,Shixun Zhang,Yonghong Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究在医学多域扩散模型中如何学习路由专家组合，比较仅用服务器数据的Server-Only与利用去噪损失证据的Anchor-Delta(AD)策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构医学图像数据需要领域专用的扩散专家，但仅靠专家专业化无法决定采样时如何组合其预测；当服务器图像有限且可能不具代表性时，学习全局路由器尤其困难。

Method: 针对固定专家池研究两种路由学习策略：Server-Only从服务器图像及组件标签学习依赖输入和时间步的专家分配；Anchor-Delta(AD)用相同图像和冻结专家，从去噪损失获取额外证据，将证据与当前路由器分配结合进行蒸馏，并用代理门控控制后续精化步骤。

Result: AD训练的路由器在异构四域设置中生成指标优于Server-Only；另一项服务器数据研究显示其在留出路由上优于修正路由对比方法；配对评估表明推理时证据混合的效果因基准而异。

Conclusion: 服务器数据可用性与专家性能是多域扩散模型路由器学习中两个不同且需分别考虑的要素。

Abstract: Heterogeneous medical image data motivate domain-specialized diffusion experts, but expert specialization alone does not determine how their predictions should be combined during sampling. Learning a global router is particularly challenging when the server has limited, potentially unrepresentative images. We study two router-learning strategies for a fixed expert pool. Server-Only learns input- and timestep-dependent expert assignments from server images with component labels. Anchor-Delta (AD) uses the same images and frozen experts to obtain additional evidence from denoising losses. It distills targets that combine this evidence with the current router's assignments, while a proxy gate controls a subsequent refinement step. Component labels describe data origin; expert losses measure empirical prediction performance. We evaluate these distinct signals through routing analyses and paired medical image generation. An AD-trained router improves generation metrics over Server-Only in a heterogeneous four-domain setting, and a separate server-data study shows gains in held-out routing over a corrected-routing comparator. Additional paired evaluations show that the effect of inference-time evidence mixing varies across benchmarks. These findings establish server-data availability and expert performance as distinct considerations in learning routers for multi-domain diffusion models.

</details>


### [125] [Elephant in the Fridge: Constant-Memory Frame Packing for Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16118)
*Shuo Gao,Chenhao Zheng,Zhongzheng Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FramePack-U是一个无需训练、无需微调的框架，将任意长度视频压缩为固定大小的视觉表示，以恒定内存成本处理超长视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM逐帧编码导致token序列随视频长度线性增长，内存和延迟开销巨大；token削减和关键帧选择仅缩短序列但O(n)瓶颈仍在。

Method: 受生成领域FramePack启发，提出内容感知压缩：1) 依据帧与文本提示的语义相似度评分并做多样性驱动的排序；2) 将选中帧以不同分辨率打包进固定长度上下文窗口，使预算集中在指令相关且视觉多样的帧上。

Result: 在LongVideoBench、MLVU、Video-MME上，跨多种模型族、参数规模和帧预算，持续优于全分辨率基线及现有token削减/关键帧选择方法。Qwen3.5-35B-A3B、128帧预算下，相比官方模型分别提升+12.9%、+10.6%、+9.7%。

Conclusion: FramePack-U以极简、免训练方式实现恒定内存的任意长视频理解，为VLM长视频处理提供了可插拔的高效解决方案。

Abstract: Hour-long videos, streaming feeds, and movie-length content are pushing vision-language models (VLMs) past their breaking point -- the standard recipe of encoding each frame into visual tokens yields sequences that grow linearly with video length, causing prohibitive memory consumption and inference latency. Existing approaches, such as token reduction and keyframe selection, shorten the token sequence but leave the O(n) scaling intact, failing to resolve the fundamental bottleneck. We propose FramePack-U, a strikingly simple, training-free frame packing framework that compresses arbitrarily long videos into a fixed size visual representation at constant memory cost. FramePack-U requires no fine-tuning or architectural modification -- it plugs directly into off-the-shelf VLMs at inference time. Inspired by FramePack from the generation literature, we propose a content-aware compression strategy for visual understanding under a fixed token budget. Specifically, FramePack-U (1) scores each frame by its semantic similarity to the text prompt and ranks frames in a diversity-driven manner, and (2) packs the selected frames at varying resolutions into a fixed-length context window. This concentrates the memory budget on frames that are both instruction-relevant and visually diverse. While FramePack-U can in theory pack arbitrarily many frames into the fixed budget, we identify an empirical instantiation that generalizes robustly across settings. Extensive experiments on LongVideoBench, MLVU, and Video-MME show that FramePack-U consistently outperforms full-resolution baselines and prior token reduction and keyframe selection methods across diverse model families, parameter scales, and frame budgets. Notably, on Qwen3.5-35B-A3B with a 128-frame budget, FramePack-U yields gains of +12.9\%, +10.6\%, and +9.7\% over the official model on the three benchmarks, respectively.

</details>


### [126] [Geometry-Guided Semantic Reconstruction for 3D Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16122)
*Yuanhao Su,Shaofeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出掩码语义重建任务与几何调制语义注入，将2D语义蒸馏进3D编码器，在ScanNetV2/200上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D实例分割需兼具几何结构与语义判别力。纯3D编码器具空间结构但缺外观语义；直接拼接提升2D先验虽带语义，却无法保证其与3D结构对齐。

Method: 将2D到3D语义迁移建模为掩码语义重建：遮盖部分背景提升的2D先验，训练3D编码器恢复缺失语义，显式监督蒸馏。并提出几何调制语义注入，用原始3D几何在稀疏编码前调制提升的2D先验，使几何显式控制语义注入并缓解2D语义与3D几何特征间的分布差距。

Result: 在两个基线框架上，于ScanNetV2与ScanNet200的全数据及少样本设置下均达到SOTA。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 验证了掩码语义重建与几何调制语义注入能有效实现2D-to-3D语义迁移，提升3D实例分割性能。

Abstract: 3D instance segmentation requires representations that are both geometrically structured and semantically discriminative. Pure 3D encoders model spatial structure well but often lack strong appearance semantics, while directly lifting 2D priors into 3D by concatenation injects rich semantics without ensuring that they align well with the underlying 3D structure. In this paper, we address this problem by formulating 2D-to-3D semantic transfer as a \textbf{masked semantic reconstruction} task. Specifically, we mask a subset of background lifted 2D priors and train the 3D encoder to recover the missing semantic priors, providing explicit supervision for distilling rich 2D semantics into the 3D backbone. We further propose \textbf{geometry-modulated semantic injection}, which uses raw 3D geometry to modulate lifted 2D priors before sparse encoding, so that geometry explicitly controls semantic injection and alleviates the distribution gap between 2D semantic features and 3D geometric features. Experiments on two baseline frameworks demonstrate that the proposed method achieves state-of-the-art results in both full-data and few-shot settings on ScanNetV2 and ScanNet200. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/yharoldsu0627/Geometry_Guided_Semantic_Reconstruction

</details>


### [127] [A Concept-informed Interpretable Artificial Intelligence Framework for Breast Ultrasound Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16128)
*Shengzhou Zhong,Ziying Wang,Liang Li,Lihua Yan,Shuochun Chen,Yudie Zou,Jifan Zhang,Wenhao Li,Huiling Zhang,Jiayin Lu,Ziting Xu,Qianjin Feng,Zhenwei Shi,Yan Chen,Yingjia Li,Yu Zhang,Zhenyuan Ning*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出IBUS，一个整合BI-RADS结构化描述的乳腺超声AI决策支持系统，基于20个独立数据集、9941名患者训练，在14个测试集上验证其泛化性、可解释性及临床辅助价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有乳腺超声AI多依赖图像驱动预测，未充分结合BI-RADS知识与放射科医生的中间评估流程，导致可解释性差、与临床实践脱节。

Method: 构建迄今最大的多中心乳腺超声图像数据集，配对结构化BI-RADS描述符（9941名患者、20个数据集）；开发IBUS系统，将BI-RADS描述符纳入预测流程，实现细粒度风险分层，并通过交叉核对预测BI-RADS分级与恶性概率识别内部不一致病例；开展8名放射科医生的读者研究。

Result: 在14个测试集上，IBUS跨不同临床中心和成像条件保持一致的泛化性能；BI-RADS描述符支持细粒度风险分层；交叉核对可识别需额外临床复核的内部不一致预测；读者研究中，IBUS辅助提升了初级与高级读者的诊断敏感性，并有望引导放射科医生针对性修正风险分层。

Conclusion: IBUS作为一种第二阶段决策支持系统，通过提供额外风险估计与可解释证据，辅助放射科医生处理诊断困难病例，具有临床应用潜力。演示视频见 https://github.com/zsz1997/IBUS 。

Abstract: Most existing artificial intelligence systems for breast ultrasound rely primarily on image-driven prediction, with limited incorporation of the knowledge from the Breast Imaging Reporting and Data System (BI-RADS) and the intermediate assessment processes underlying radiologists' workflow, thereby constraining their interpretability and alignment with clinical practice. We curated, to our knowledge, the largest multicenter collection of breast ultrasound images paired with structured BI-RADS descriptors, comprising 9,941 patients across 20 independent datasets. Across 14 testing datasets, IBUS demonstrated consistent generalization performance across different clinical centers and imaging conditions. Incorporating BI-RADS descriptors enabled fine-grained risk stratification, while cross-checking predicted BI-RADS grades against malignancy probabilities identified internally discordant predictions that may warrant additional clinical review. In a reader study involving eight radiologists, IBUS assistance increased diagnostic sensitivity among both junior and senior readers, with the potential of guiding radiologists to make targeted revision in risk stratification. Overall, IBUS exhibits its potential as a second-stage decision-support system that complements radiologists by providing additional risk estimates and interpretable evidence for diagnostically challenging cases. The demo video of IBUS platform is publicly available at https://github.com/zsz1997/IBUS.

</details>


### [128] [ProMoDD: Dataset Distillation for 3D Human Motion via Prototype-Conditioned Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16134)
*Yijin Wei,Yawen Zou,Wenqi Cai,Guang Li,Hangli Ge,Katsuya Hotta,Chunzhi Gu,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ProMoDD，用类原型条件化运动扩散模型并做预算感知混合选择，实现骨架动作识别的数据集蒸馏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 骨架动作识别的DD需保留判别性运动变化且不破坏关节几何与时间连续性。直接优化关节点会违反约束，而单纯生成合理运动也不保证训练有用。

Method: 以类级时序识别特征原型条件化运动扩散模型，并针对运动数据定制扩散训练以建模骨架构型及其时间演化。再用预算感知混合选择构建紧凑集：代表性真实序列覆盖密集区域，生成候选补充稀疏区域。

Result: 在NTU RGB+D 60单人动作的5个难度分层子集上，紧预算下增益最大：Full49三类每类3序列时top-1达40.32%，比class-wise k-means高4.57个百分点。同一蒸馏集可无需重生成直接训练ST-GCN、MS-G3D、CTR-GCN。

Conclusion: 结合原型引导生成与特征空间选择，平衡真实运动可靠性与合成多样性，在紧预算下显著提升骨架动作识别蒸馏效果并具跨模型迁移性。

Abstract: Dataset distillation (DD) aims to retain the training utility of a large dataset in a much smaller set of examples. For skeleton-based action recognition, this requires capturing discriminative motion variation without disrupting the geometry and temporal continuity of articulated bodies. Directly optimizing joint coordinates can violate these constraints, while plausible motion generation alone does not ensure useful training examples. We propose Prototype-Conditioned Motion Dataset Distillation (ProMoDD), which conditions a motion diffusion model on class-wise prototypes of temporally resolved recognition features. We further tailor diffusion training to motion data, enabling the generator to model both skeletal configurations and their temporal evolution. We then construct each compact training set through budget-aware hybrid selection: representative real sequences cover densely populated regions of the pooled recognition space, while selected generated candidates complement them in less-populated regions. This couples prototype-guided generation with feature-space selection, balancing the reliability of observed motions with the variation of synthesis under a fixed per-class sequence budget. Across five difficulty-stratified subsets of the single-person classes in NTU RGB+D 60, ProMoDD achieves its largest gains under tight distillation budgets, reaching 40.32% top-1 accuracy on Full49 with three sequences per class, 4.57 percentage points above class-wise k-means. These gains reflect the complete framework. The same distilled sets train ST-GCN, MS-G3D, and CTR-GCN without regeneration.

</details>


### [129] [All-St3R SLAM: Real-time RGB Dynamic SLAM with Pixel-wise Motion Masking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16143)
*Yewon Kim,Paul Hongsuck Seo,Sang Chul Ahn*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个仅依赖RGB输入的实时动态SLAM框架，通过DUSt3R的ego-motion预测与All-Tracker点跟踪差异，在像素级精确移除真正运动的物体，约10 FPS，优于现有0.5-3 FPS的稠密动态SLAM方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态环境下的SLAM具有重要实用价值，但现有动态SLAM方法存在过度移除（因对象级掩码连同暂时静止部分一起剔除）和高计算成本的问题，且常依赖预定义标签或分割模型。

Method: 结合由ego-motion（基于DUSt3R预测）得到的像素运动与All-Tracker的点跟踪结果，通过分析两种运动来源之间的差异，仅在像素级移除实际运动的像素，不使用预定义标签或分割模型。通过每3帧子采样实现约10 FPS实时性能。

Result: 在标准30 FPS视频上约10 FPS运行，实现实时性能；仅用RGB输入，显著优于现有最先进的稠密动态SLAM方法（0.5-3 FPS）。

Conclusion: 该像素级运动移除策略避免了对象级掩码的过度移除，使暂时静止的区域仍可用于跟踪，为动态环境提供了一种高实用性、仅需RGB的实时SLAM方案。

Abstract: SLAM (Simultaneous Localization and Mapping) is a core technology in autonomous systems that enables them to estimate their own position and build a map of unknown environments simultaneously. SLAM systems in dynamic environments are of great practical importance. However, existing dynamic SLAM approaches still have several limitations like over-removal and high computational costs. To address these limitations, we propose a real-time RGB Dynamic SLAM framework that precisely removes moving objects at the pixel level without relying on predefined labels or segmentation models. Our system combines pixel motion predicted by ego-motion using DUSt3R with point tracking from All-Tracker, removing only actually moving pixels by analyzing discrepancies between the two motion sources. Unlike object-level masking methods, our approach eliminates only currently moving regions, allowing temporarily static parts to be utilized for tracking. Operating at approximately 10 FPS, the system achieves real-time performance on standard 30 FPS video streams by subsampling every 3 frames. Relying solely on RGB inputs, this significantly improves upon state-of-the-art dense dynamic SLAM approaches (0.5-3 FPS), presenting a highly practical solution.

</details>


### [130] [Unravel: Recovering an Interactive 3D Scene from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16146)
*Hui Shan,Keyang Luo,Binjia Zhou,Ming Li,Kai Li,Xin Wang,Zhenyang Li,Zesong Yang,Weikai Chen,Runze Zhang,Xin Wang,Yanwei Fu,Zhen Chen,Xiangru Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Unravel：无需训练的框架，将单张图像转为可交互3D场景，每个物体都是独立可移动资产。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成器要么产生无法移动的纠缠几何体，要么一次前馈预测所有物体，错误无法检测或修复。

Method: 先拆后装：用智能体框架逐层剥离图像，以点云为主要模态恢复空间关系。VLM规划可见物体，图像编辑器移除并修复遮挡区域，每次编辑经校验后纳入重建；按逆序重建场景使支撑物先放置；将观测点视为物体尺寸下界；最后物理沉降去除悬浮与穿插，并经智能体审计修正错误。

Result: 在多样室内场景实验中，Unravel比现有方法恢复更多物体、放置更忠实、场景更符合物理规律。

Conclusion: 通过"先分解再重建"的智能体流程及物理校验，Unravel实现了从单图构建独立可移动物体的高质量3D场景。

Abstract: We present Unravel, a training-free framework that turns a single image into an interactive 3D scene, where every object is an independent and movable asset. Existing generators either produce one entangled geometry in which no object can be moved, or predict all objects in one feed-forward pass, where a wrong guess cannot be detected or repaired. Unravel instead takes a different approach: it first takes the scene apart, and then puts it back together. Starting from the input image, an agentic harness that iteratively peels the image layer by layer, and adopts point cloud as major modality for spatial relationship recovery. Specifically, a vision-language model plans which objects are fully visible, an image editor removes them and restores the occluded regions. Every edit is verified before being incorporated into the reconstruction. This process gradually turns occluded parts of the scene into direct observation, allowing objects to be reconstructed when they are best exposed rather than hallucinated from the original image alone. The scene is then rebuilt in reverse removal order, so that the supporting objects are naturally placed before the objects they support. Since a single-view image only reveals part of an object, we treat the observed points as a lower bound on its size: an asset may grow to cover its points, but can not shrink away from them. Finally, we physically settle the reconstructed scene to remove floating objects and interpenetrations, followed by an agentic audit that corrects remaining placement errors. Extensive experiments on diverse indoor scenes show that Unravel recovers more objects, places them more faithfully, and produces scenes that are more physically-plausible than existing methods.

</details>


### [131] [Esports World Model Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16166)
*Weiyu Ma,Zeyuan Gong,Xuhui Liu,Wenxuan Zhang,Jian Ding,Xinyu Cui,Jian Zhao,Mohamed Elhoseiny*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Esports World Model Dataset (EWM)，涵盖 StarCraft II、Dota 2、Counter-Strike 2，共 3,053 场比赛、约 4,880 玩家视角小时，通过回放时钟对齐视频、动作、状态与事件，为多玩家交互式世界模型研究提供数据支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式世界模型需刻画多个参与者如何共同改变共享环境、同时各自只观测到部分信息。竞技游戏是这一挑战的典型场景：玩家既合作又竞争，而战争迷雾、团队视野与镜头位置决定各自可见内容。现有数据难以把个体观测与动作同共享状态、事件关联起来，因此需要新数据集。

Method: 构建 EWM 数据集：收集三类游戏的人类对局（3,053 场比赛，约 4,880 玩家视角小时，含 CS2 扩展预估时长）。包含 4,628 个完整 SC2 视角、303 场 Dota 2 比赛的十个视角、CS2 每回合最多十个视角。通过回放时钟对齐，将渲染视频与游戏原生动作、结构化状态及事件关联。三款游戏提供互补观测条件：私有迷雾、团队共享视野、第一人称视角。SC2 中玩家专属实体记录区分实时观测与记忆快照，并跨对立视角链接共同观测实体。

Result: 数据集实现了个体观测、动作与共享状态、事件之间的时间对齐与跨视角关联，尤其能区分“合法信息差异”与“对共享状态的分歧”，覆盖三种互补观测条件。

Conclusion: EWM 连接人类决策、共享动态与玩家专属信息，支持面向多人共同控制的世界模型及由人类对局学习的队友/对手交互环境研究。

Abstract: Interactive world models must capture how multiple participants jointly change a shared environment while observing different parts of it. Competitive games make this challenge concrete: players cooperate and compete, while fog of war, team vision and camera placement determine what each can see. Modeling these interactions requires data that connect individual observations and actions to shared state and events. We introduce the Esports World Model Dataset (EWM), a collection of human gameplay spanning StarCraft II, Dota 2 and Counter-Strike 2, with 3,053 games and maps and approximately 4,880 player-view hours, including estimated hours for the ongoing CS2 extension. It includes 4,628 completed StarCraft II views, all ten perspectives for 303 Dota 2 matches, and up to ten views per round in CS2. Across these views, replay-clock alignment connects rendered video with game-native actions, structured state and events from the same match. The three games expose complementary observation conditions: private fog of war, shared team vision and first-person viewpoints. In StarCraft II, player-specific entity records further distinguish live observations from remembered snapshots and link co-observed entities across opposing views. Together with temporal alignment, these records allow researchers to distinguish legitimate information differences from disagreement about shared state. By connecting human decisions, shared dynamics and player-specific information, EWM supports research toward jointly controlled multiplayer world models and interactive environments populated by teammates and opponents learned from human play.

</details>


### [132] [Geometric Parrots: Rule Absence vs. Rule Fragility in Spatial and Physical Reasoning for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16170)
*Bilal Jawaid,Hamza Hanif,Muhammad Maaz Uddin,Anas Zafar,Inad Alqurashi,Aizan Zafar,Osama Farooqui,Sheheryar Khan,Ferhat Sadak,Shaina Raza,Rizwan Qureshi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GRIP：一个程序化生成的几何/物理推理基准，通过双环评估区分模型"规则缺失"与"规则脆弱"两类失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM能答对推理题却行为不一致，遇到自信但错误的用户断言时会放弃正确答案；传统单轮准确率无法区分"屈服"与"有益纠正"。

Method: 提出GRIP基准（34个程序化推理域、10万图像、60万问题，覆盖几何/空间/视觉/物理推理，答案由场景状态精确推导）。并提出双环评估：闭环用五步组合问题阶梯定位推理失败点；开环在受控挑战下（同伴模型回答、无依据的用户断言）重新获取答案。

Result: 因真值精确已知，答案变化可标记为纠正（错→对）或屈服（对→错），从而分离出两种失败模式：规则缺失与规则脆弱。

Conclusion: GRIP为评估VLM推理的稳健性提供了可控、有精确真值的基准，能揭示单轮准确率掩盖的行为不一致问题。数据集与详情已公开。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can answer many geometric and physical reasoning questions correctly, yet still behave inconsistently in interaction, for example, abandoning a correct answer when confronted with a confident but incorrect user assertion. Standard single-turn accuracy cannot distinguish such capitulations from beneficial corrections because it scores each response in isolation. We introduce GRIP, a controlled benchmark with 34 procedurally generated reasoning domains, 100,000 images, and 600,000 questions spanning geometric, spatial, visual, and physical reasoning. Each scene is generated by deterministic code, and every answer is derived directly from the underlying scene state, providing exact ground truth without human annotation. On GRIP, we propose Dual-Loop Evaluation. A closed loop probes each image with a five-step compositional question ladder to localize where reasoning fails. An open loop then re-elicits the model's answer under controlled challenges, including peer-model responses and unsupported user assertions. Because ground truth is known exactly, answer changes can be signed as corrections (wrong-to-right) or capitulations (right-to-wrong), separating two failure modes: rule absence and rule fragility. The dataset is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/bilaljawaid980/GRIP-Benchmark, with further details at https://bilaljawaid980.github.io/grip-benchmark/.

</details>


### [133] [EventLens: Event-Structure Reinforcement Learning for Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16172)
*Guowen Zhang,Boshen Xu,Zihao Yue,Ziheng Wang,Xiaokun Liu,Xin Tao,Wenyu Qin,Pengfei Wan,Qin Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EventLens，一个以视觉为中心的强化学习框架，通过事件结构恢复（分割、进展判别、多事件关系推理）训练MLLM，并用多教师在线蒸馏整合策略，提升时序理解且保持通用视频QA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型在视频问答上表现强，但难以恢复视频的时序结构，常合并相邻事件、错过单事件进展或推断不合理的事件关系。作者认为根源在于当前后训练目标与时序推理不匹配：字幕式监督让模型依赖语言捷径而非视觉时序grounding。

Method: EventLens：以视觉为中心的强化学习框架，不重建字幕，而是在受控扰动（改时序粒度、反转进展、打乱事件顺序）下恢复时序结构。基于三层时序本体构造三类可验证任务族——事件分割、进展判别、多事件关系推理，均为确定性奖励，可用任务专用GRPO训练而无需人工偏好标签或奖励模型；再用多教师在线策略蒸馏（MT-OPD）将各专用策略整合为统一模型。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL骨干上，时序grounding和事件敏感推理基准一致提升，同时保持通用视频QA性能。学习曲线显示EventLens以显著更低的训练成本达到与直接混合RL优化相当的下游迁移效果。

Conclusion: 通过事件结构恢复的强化学习可有效弥补MLLM时序理解短板，确定性奖励与多教师蒸馏提供了低成本、无需偏好标注的训练路径，兼顾专门能力与通用性能。

Abstract: Recent multimodal large language models (MLLMs) achieve strong results on video question answering, yet often fail to recover the temporal structure that makes a video coherent. They may merge neighboring events, miss single-event progression, or infer implausible relations across events. We study structured temporal understanding as the ability to recover event structure from visual evidence, including event boundaries, single-event progression, and multi-event relations. We argue that this gap arises from a mismatch between current post-training objectives and temporal reasoning, where caption-style supervision allows models to rely on language shortcuts rather than visual temporal grounding. To address this, we propose EventLens, a vision-centric reinforcement learning framework that learns video understanding through event-structure recovery. Instead of reconstructing captions, EventLens trains models to recover temporal structure under controlled perturbations, such as altered temporal granularity, reversed progression, and shuffled event order. We instantiate this framework with a three-level temporal ontology and derive three verifiable task families: event segmentation, progression discrimination, and multi-event relation reasoning. These tasks admit deterministic rewards, enabling task-specialized GRPO training without human preference labels or learned reward models. We further introduce multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MT-OPD) to consolidate specialized policies into a unified model. Experiments on Qwen3-VL backbones show consistent improvements on temporal grounding and event-sensitive reasoning benchmarks, while preserving general video-QA performance. Learning curves further demonstrate that EventLens achieves comparable downstream transfer with significantly reduced training cost compared to direct mixed RL optimization.

</details>


### [134] [EgoAlign: Situated 3D Understanding without Situation Descriptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16176)
*Yiyang Li,Tianyi Ma,Zehong Wang,Yanfang Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EgoAlign，通过以智能体为中心的坐标系和自中心对齐预训练，让3D语言模型直接从数值位姿建立空间上下文，无需文本情境描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D问答方法依赖自然语言情境描述来传递智能体位姿，但真实场景中这类描述可能缺失或成本高。作者发现随机打乱数值位姿对性能影响很小，说明模型对数值位姿的功能依赖有限。

Method: EgoAlign以智能体为中心的坐标系表示场景，并设计自中心对齐阶段，包含四个互补任务：物体类别识别、自中心方向预测、度量距离估计、空间关系预测；先对齐再针对下游任务微调。

Result: 在SQA3D、Real-3DQA、MSQA上取得对比方法中最优性能且保持高效；移除文本情境描述后性能相当或更好，而打乱数值位姿则导致显著下降。

Conclusion: EgoAlign能仅凭数值位姿建立智能体中心的空间上下文，无需文本情境描述，证明其有效利用了数值位姿。

Abstract: Situated 3D Question Answering requires models to interpret a 3D scene relative to an agent's pose. Existing methods commonly convey that pose through a natural-language situation description, which may be unavailable or costly to obtain in real-world deployments. While some methods additionally incorporate numerical pose, we find that randomly shuffling the agent pose causes little performance degradation, indicating limited functional dependence on the numerical pose. To address this gap, we introduce EgoAlign, a simple 3D language model that represents a scene in an agent-centric coordinate frame, together with a dedicated egocentric alignment stage comprising four complementary tasks: object category recognition, egocentric direction prediction, metric distance estimation, and spatial relation prediction. This stage teaches the model to consistently interpret scene geometry relative to the agent's pose before task-specific fine-tuning. EgoAlign achieves the strongest performance on SQA3D, Real-3DQA, and MSQA among compared methods while remaining highly efficient. More importantly, removing textual situation descriptions yields comparable or better downstream performance, whereas shuffling EgoAlign's numerical pose causes substantial degradation. These results show that EgoAlign establishes agent-centric spatial context directly from numerical pose without requiring textual situation descriptions.

</details>


### [135] [DIGS: Distribution-Informed Gaussian Splatting for Training-Free Open-Vocabulary 3D Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16177)
*Zhifan Zeng,Wenbo Xiao,Arcot Sowmya,Changming Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DIGS是一个免训练的开放词汇3D高斯分割框架，通过解耦实例发现与语言命名，利用分布感知表示和双向状态机，在保持清晰边界的同时实现高效3D查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇3D高斯分割方法将语义特征绑定到每个高斯，导致边界高斯被迫做出硬决定，降低检索精度并产生不稳定的掩码边界。作者希望解耦实例发现与语言命名来解决这一问题。

Method: DIGS包含两个阶段：类别无关映射和多模态编码。在高斯层面，采用分布感知表示，通过temporated Dirichlet规则维护每个primitive的显式top-M标签分布，保留边界处的多峰分布，配合late-argmax渲染器恢复清晰边界。双向状态机将这些分布聚合为持久的类别无关实例本体。实例级编码阶段融合每个实例孤立3D足迹的CLIP视觉嵌入与LLM生成属性描述的CLIP文本嵌入，以区分视觉相似的实例。

Result: 在单张A100上，DIGS对LERF-OVS的映射和实例编码耗时1.0-3.2分钟/场景，原生3D查询仅5.4ms。在LERF-OVS上达到71.95%平均mIoU，优于免训练的SFS和需训练的LaGa基线；在LERF-Mask和3D-OVS上也取得最高平均mIoU，并在Replica上超过Gaga和ObjectGS。

Conclusion: DIGS通过解耦实例发现与命名、维护多峰标签分布，实现了免训练、高效且边界清晰的开放词汇3D高斯分割，在多个基准上达到领先性能。代码已开源。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary 3D Gaussian segmentation typically binds semantic features to the per-Gaussian state via per-scene gradient training (1-4 h) or the more recent closed-form distillation. Both commit each primitive to a single feature; therefore, boundary Gaussians spanning multiple 2D instances are forced into a hard decision that degrades retrieval accuracy and yields unstable mask boundaries at render time. We present DIGS, a training-free framework with two stages, class-agnostic mapping and multi-modal encoding, that decouples instance discovery from linguistic naming. At the Gaussian level, our distribution-informed representation maintains an explicit top-$M$ label distribution per primitive, updated by a tempered Dirichlet rule; preservation of multi-peaked label distributions at boundary primitives allows a late-argmax renderer to recover clean instance boundaries. A bidirectional state machine aggregates these distributions into a persistent class-agnostic instance ontology. The instance-level encoding stage then fuses the CLIP visual embedding of each instance's isolated 3D footprint with the CLIP-text embedding of an LLM-generated attribute description, disambiguating visually similar instances. On a single A100, DIGS completes mapping and instance encoding on LERF-OVS in 1.0-3.2 min per scene, depending on whether LLM fusion is enabled, and serves native 3D queries in 5.4 ms. It reaches 71.95% mean mIoU on LERF-OVS, outperforming both the training-free SFS and training-based LaGa baselines. DIGS also achieves the highest mean mIoU on LERF-Mask and 3D-OVS and surpasses Gaga and ObjectGS on Replica under their respective evaluation protocols. Code is available at https://github.com/ZephyrZos/DIGS.

</details>


### [136] [VGGA: Geometry-Grounded Autoencoder for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16204)
*Chaofan Gan,Yian Zhao,Shijie Li,Tieyuan Chen,Zicheng Zhao,Mehrtash Harandi,Weiyao Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出几何感知视频自编码器VGGA，通过引入深度、运动对应和投影变换三类几何约束，学习更有结构的潜空间，在保持重建质量的同时显著提升视频生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频自编码器主要针对像素级重建优化，对学习结构化潜表示提供的监督有限。作者认为视频不应仅被当作待重建信号，而应视为具有明确几何属性的动态3D世界的2D投影。

Method: 提出VGGA，将几何约束显式融入重建过程，沿三个互补几何维度对表示进行约束：结构深度、运动对应、投影变换。这些目标统一在一个Video Geometry Decoder中，采用共享的时空上采样主干，联合优化几何学习与RGB重建。

Result: 在几何中心领域和人体运动领域均持续提升视频生成质量，RealEstate10K上gFVD降低28.1%，Taichi-HD上降低37.7%，同时保持相当的重建质量。

Conclusion: 几何约束的表示学习能产生更有效的视频生成潜空间，证明将视频作为3D世界投影进行建模优于纯像素重建优化的方法。

Abstract: Autoencoders are the core component of latent video generative models, compressing high-dimensional videos into compact latent spaces for downstream generation. However, existing video autoencoders are primarily optimized for pixel-level reconstruction, providing limited supervision for learning structured latent representations. We argue that videos should not be treated merely as signals to be reconstructed, but rather as 2D projections of dynamic 3D worlds that exhibit explicit and well-structured geometric properties. Building on this insight, we propose \textbf{V}ideo \textbf{G}eometry \textbf{G}rounded \textbf{A}utoencoder (\textbf{VGGA}), a geometry-aware video autoencoder that explicitly incorporates geometric constraints into the reconstruction process. Specifically, VGGA grounds the learned representations along three complementary geometric dimensions: \emph{Structural Depth}, \emph{Motion Correspondence}, and \emph{Projective Warping}. These objectives are unified within a Video Geometry Decoder, which employs a shared spatio-temporal upsampling trunk to jointly optimize geometric learning and RGB reconstruction. Through these geometry-grounded objectives, VGGA encourages the latent representations to capture the underlying 3D structure and motion of dynamic scenes. VGGA consistently improves video generation across both geometry-centric and human-motion domains, reducing gFVD by 28.1\% on RealEstate10K and 37.7\% on Taichi-HD. Notably, these substantial generation improvements are achieved while maintaining comparable reconstruction quality, demonstrating that geometry-grounded representation learning yields a more effective latent space for video generation.

</details>


### [137] [MP-MNCA: Morphology-Prior Masked Neighbor Cross-Attention for Efficient Spatial Domain Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16212)
*Sidharthan Sivacoumar Chitra,Sanjeev Manivannan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MP-MNCA：一种形态先验掩码邻居交叉注意力框架，用于空间转录组空间域识别，收敛更快且质量不降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间域方法通常只在训练末期比较，忽略了收敛效率的差异，难以全面评估方法优劣。

Method: 提出MP-MNCA框架，保留全部3000维高变基因表示，通过掩码邻居交叉注意力在空间邻居间选择性传递分子信息，并将组织学相似度作为连续注意力先验，配合自监督掩码邻居对比目标训练。

Result: 在12个DLPFC切片上，MP-MNCA平均峰值refined ARI为0.546（基线STAIG为0.549），平均refined NMI最高为0.657（基线0.640）；在ARI上12切片中7个优于STAIG，NMI上11个优于STAIG，且收敛所需epoch显著更少。

Conclusion: MP-MNCA在保持空间域质量的同时显著加快收敛，证明收敛效率应作为空间域方法的重要评估维度。

Abstract: Spatial transcriptomics jointly captures molecular state, tissue morphology, and spatial organization, yet spatial-domain methods are typically compared only at late training checkpoints, obscuring differences in convergence efficiency. We introduce MP-MNCA, a morphology-prior masked neighbor cross-attention framework that preserves the full 3,000 dimensional highly-variable-gene representation while selectively routing molecular information across spatial neighbors, with histological similarity incorporated as a continuous attention prior under a self-supervised masked neighbor-contrastive objective. Across 12 heterogeneous human dorsolateral prefrontal cortex (DLPFC) sections, MP-MNCA is evaluated against established spatial-domain baselines using both convergence trajectories and epoch-wise spatial-domain visualizations. Our results show that MP-MNCA (i) reaches coherent spatial-domain structure in substantially fewer training epochs, as reflected quantitatively by convergence curves and qualitatively by progressive spatial grids, and (ii) maintains strong clustering quality, achieving a mean peak refined ARI of 0.546 versus 0.549 for the strongest baseline (STAIG), while attaining the highest mean refined NMI of 0.657 versus 0.640. Moreover, MP-MNCA outperforms STAIG on 7 of 12 sections in ARI and 11 of 12 in NMI, demonstrating faster convergence without sacrificing spatial-domain quality.

</details>


### [138] [Look Back to Move Forward: Learning to Revisit for Agentic Compositional Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16224)
*Zixu Wang,Xiaoran Zhao,Yahui Liu,Feng-ao Wang,Junhao Xiao,You Shu,Bing Liu,Yiting Liu,Songlei Jian,Zhiliang Tian,Dongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GenRevisit智能体框架，将动作选择扩展为"继续状态+动作"联合选择，并引入NACA奖励分配机制，在组合式图像生成任务上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体式文生图方法通过迭代生成与编辑改进组合式生成，但固定以"最新图像"作为继续精炼的起点。由于迭代编辑并非单调改进，当最新图像已退化时，智能体仍会从劣质图像继续优化，导致性能受限。

Method: 提出GenRevisit框架，让智能体利用交互历史调整编辑指令，同时从候选图像中选择要编辑的对象，联合决定"从哪里继续"和"如何精炼"。训练采用SFT加智能体强化学习；针对GRPO中终局奖励对所有动作赋予相同轨迹级优势的粗粒度问题，提出归一化动作信用分配（NACA），依据每个动作对降低剩余目标的贡献重新分配正优势。

Result: 在GenEval2上，使用Qwen-Image-2512和Z-Image-Turbo分别取得84.3和74.4的GM分数，相较同生成器的最强基线提升12.7和10.9分。在GenEval、GenEval++和GenEval2上均达到基线中的SOTA。

Conclusion: 联合选择继续状态与动作能有效缓解迭代编辑非单调带来的退化问题，NACA提升信用分配精度，GenRevisit在多个组合式生成基准上实现领先性能。

Abstract: Text-to-image models have made remarkable progress in visual fidelity and instruction following, yet complex compositional generation remains challenging. Existing agentic approaches address this limitation through iterative generation and editing, adapting their actions based on interaction history. However, they typically fix the continuation state (i.e., the image from which refinement proceeds) to the latest image. Since iterative editing is not necessarily monotonic, the agent may continue refining from the latest image even when it has already degraded. In this work, we introduce GenRevisit, an agentic framework that extends action selection to joint continuation-state and action selection. The agent can not only use interaction history to adjust editing instructions but also select an image candidate to edit, jointly deciding where to continue from and how to refine. We train GenRevisit with SFT followed by agentic reinforcement learning. To address the coarse credit assignment of final outcome reward in GRPO, which assigns the same trajectory-level advantage to all actions, we further propose Normalized Action Credit Assignment (NACA) to redistribute positive advantage according to each action's contribution to reducing the remaining objective. On GenEval2, GenRevisit achieves GM scores of 84.3 and 74.4 with Qwen-Image-2512 and Z-Image-Turbo, surpassing the strongest baselines using the same generators by 12.7 and 10.9 points respectively. GenRevisit achieves state-of-the-art performance on GenEval, GenEval++, and GenEval2 among baselines.

</details>


### [139] [Anomaly-Guided Observation and Two-Level Semantic Feedback for Training-Free Video Anomaly Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16324)
*Hangil Park,Seungheon Kim,Tae-Kyun Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无需训练的语义反馈框架，用VAD引导的主动观察生成聚焦异常的描述，再反哺时序检测，提升视频异常检测与理解性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免训练VAD方法虽利用更广的时序与语义上下文，但通用上下文未必能凸显异常事件，也未能明确视频级语义应如何修正局部异常分数。

Method: 基于上游异常分数曲线进行推理式迭代选帧：高分区域作为观察种子，结合异常分数与VLM相关性评估引导时序探索；用冻结的视频语言模型从所选帧生成一句开放式异常描述；通过视频级反馈（抑制/保持/锐化分数分布并选择性视觉复核）和局部反馈（检索事件相关窗口，经目标局部视觉验证后再提升分数）返回，模型权重全冻结且反馈仅应用一次。

Result: 在UCF-Crime和XD-Violence的HIVAU视频摘要评估中，VAD引导观察在GPT评分的合理性、细节与一致性上优于CLIP引导；VAD检测上语义反馈将上游检测器从84.36%提至86.25% AUC（UCF-Crime），从68.07%提至72.58% AP（XD-Violence），最终AUC达91.74%。

Conclusion: VAD引导的主动观察能生成更聚焦异常的语义描述，其反馈可有效细化时序检测，在免训练设定下显著提升VAD与VAU表现。

Abstract: Training-free language-based video anomaly detection (VAD) uses frozen vision--language models (VLMs) and large language models (LLMs) to localize abnormal events without task-specific model-weight optimization. Existing approaches increasingly exploit broader temporal and semantic context, yet generic context does not necessarily foreground abnormal events or specify how video-level semantics should modify local anomaly scores. We introduce a training-free semantic feedback framework that uses VAD-guided active observation for video anomaly understanding (VAU) and returns the resulting anomaly-focused description to refine temporal detection. Starting from an upstream anomaly-score curve, we adapt reasoning-based iterative frame selection: high-score regions seed the observations, and anomaly scores together with VLM relevance assessments guide subsequent temporal exploration. A frozen video-language model generates a single open-ended anomaly description from the selected frames.
