The description is returned through two complementary feedback levels: video-level feedback determines whether the score distribution should be suppressed, preserved, or sharpened, with selective visual rechecking, while local feedback retrieves event-related windows and promotes scores only after target-local visual verification. All model weights remain frozen, and feedback is applied once without regenerating the description. On the HIVAU video-summary evaluations of UCF-Crime and XD-Violence, VAD-guided observation improves GPT-rated reasonability, detail, and consistency over the matched CLIP-guided variant. For VAD, semantic feedback improves the re-evaluated upstream detector from 84.36% to 86.25% AUC on UCF-Crime and from 68.07% to 72.58% AP on XD-Violence, where the final AUC is 91.74%.

</details>


### [140] [Explicitly Aligned Multi-View Breast Cancer Risk Prediction from Variable-Length Screening Histories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16329)
*Solveig Thrun,Zijun Sun,Suaiba A. Salahuddin,Kristoffer Wickstrøm,Elisabeth Wetzer,Stine Hansen,Robert Jenssen,Michael Kampffmeyer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FlexLMV-Net，一个乳腺级纵向多视角框架，联合建模CC和MLO视角并支持可变数量的历史检查，实现显式纵向对齐与跨视角时间融合；同时发布BCRBench基准，在EMBED和CSAW-CC上一致优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的乳腺癌风险预测对风险分层和个性化筛查至关重要。利用纵向检查的乳腺组织变化和CC/MLO互补视角信息可提升预测。但现有方法：1）多依赖隐式特征对齐，缺乏显式空间一致性；2）即使有显式对齐也各视角独立建模，无法利用CC与MLO互补信息；3）显式方法仅限单个历史检查。

Method: 提出FlexLMV-Net：乳腺级纵向多视角框架，跨筛查检查联合建模CC与MLO视角，支持可变数量历史检查。显式对齐纵向特征图，并通过跨视角时间融合与双流注意力模块整合互补视角。另发布BCRBench开源基准，统一预处理、训练、推理与评估协议。

Result: 在EMBED和CSAW-CC数据集上，FlexLMV-Net在乳腺密度、癌症亚型及人口统计子群上一致优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 显式纵向对齐、多视角建模以及灵活使用可用筛查历史带来显著收益。BCRBench为单/多时间点风险预测模型提供公平可复现的比较基准。

Abstract: Accurate breast cancer risk prediction from screening mammography is essential for improved risk stratification and personalized screening. It can benefit from both temporal breast tissue changes across longitudinal examinations and complementary anatomical information from craniocaudal (CC) and mediolateral oblique (MLO) views. Leveraging longitudinal information requires identifying corresponding tissue regions across examinations, yet most approaches rely on implicit feature alignment without explicitly enforcing spatial consistency. Recent work has demonstrated the benefits of explicit temporal alignment; however, existing methods model each view independently, preventing them from exploiting complementary CC and MLO information. Furthermore, explicit approaches have been limited to a single prior examination. To fill these gaps, we propose (github.com/sot176/FlexLMV-Net), a breast-wise longitudinal multi-view framework that jointly models CC and MLO views across screening examinations while accommodating a variable number of prior examinations. FlexLMV-Net explicitly aligns longitudinal feature maps and integrates complementary views through cross-view temporal fusion and a dual-stream attention block. Evaluated on EMBED and CSAW-CC, FlexLMV-Net consistently outperforms existing approaches across breast density, cancer subtypes, and demographic subgroups, highlighting the benefits of explicit longitudinal alignment, multi-view modeling, and flexible use of available screening history. We further introduce (github.com/sot176/BCRBench), an open-source benchmark that unifies state-of-the-art single- and multi-timepoint breast cancer risk prediction models under standardized preprocessing, training, inference, and evaluation protocols, enabling fair and reproducible comparison of existing methods.

</details>


### [141] [BAT3R: Robust Online 3D Reconstruction with Bayesian Adaptive State Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16330)
*Lijian Li,Yuanpeng He,Yudian Zheng,Chung-ju Huang,Linyu Li,Dongming Jin,Wenpin Jiao,Zhi Jin,Chi-Man Pun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BAT3R是一个免训练的贝叶斯自适应框架，通过SIG和BASU两个模块缓解流式3D重建中的误差累积问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈Transformer在3D视觉中表现优异，但二次复杂度使其难以处理长视频。流式重建虽以恒定内存顺序处理帧，但现有循环架构未考虑观测的内在可靠性和历史状态的累积置信度，导致长序列中严重误差累积与渐进退化。

Method: 提出BAT3R免训练贝叶斯自适应框架，包含两个协同模块：State Innovation Gating (SIG) 作为分布正则项，将状态演化锚定于几何初始化以抑制表征漂移；Bayesian-guided Adaptive State Updating (BASU) 将状态更新重构为卡尔曼滤波框架下的递归贝叶斯推断，通过信息论增益建模观测的时间可靠性、通过交叉注意力图建模空间相关性，实现非对称时空状态融合——保护已巩固的历史区域、积极纳入新颖场景内容。

Result: 在7-Scenes、TUM、ScanNet、KITTI等标准基准上，BAT3R持续优于最先进的在线3D重建方法，在长序列和复杂场景中增益最为显著。

Conclusion: 通过引入贝叶斯自适应机制显式建模观测可靠性与历史状态置信度，BAT3R有效缓解了流式3D重建中的误差累积，且无需训练即可即插即用，在长序列复杂场景中表现突出。

Abstract: Feed-forward transformer models have driven remarkable progress in 3D vision,yet their quadratic complexity renders them impractical for long video sequences. Streaming 3D reconstruction addresses this bottleneck by processing frames sequen?tially with constant memory. However, existing recurrent architectures inevitably suffer from progressive degradation over long sequences, as they fail to account for the intrinsic reliability of observations and the accumulated confidence of the historical state, leading to severe error accumulation. To address these issues, we introduce BAT3R, a training-free Bayesian adaptation framework comprising two synergistic modules: State Innovation Gating (SIG) and Bayesian-guided Adaptive State Updating (BASU). SIG acts as a distributional regularizer that anchors state evolution to its geometric initialization, effectively arresting representational drift during long-term inference. Building on this, BASU reformulates state update as a recursive Bayesian inference process within a Kalman filtering framework. By jointly modeling the temporal reliability of observations via information-theoretic gain and spatial relevance via cross-attention maps, BASU achieves an asymmetric spatio-temporal state fusion: well-consolidated historical regions are protected from redundant updates, while genuinely novel scene content is aggressively incor?porated. Extensive experiments on standard benchmarks (7-Scenes, TUM, ScanNet, KITTI, etc.) demonstrate that BAT3R consistently outperforms state-of-the-art online 3D reconstruction methods, with the most significant gains observed on long sequences and complex scenes.

</details>


### [142] [WorldRoPE: Probabilistic World-Centric Rotary Embedding for Multi-View Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16337)
*Mijeong Kim,Kyujin Lim,Bohyung Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorldRoPE 提出一种以世界坐标系为中心的概率化旋转位置编码，用体积高斯原语建模多视图 patch 的 3D 支撑，实现跨视图对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视图 Transformer 中，patch 坐标在各图像内独立定义，同一 3D 点在多视图下坐标不一致，无法建立跨视图对应关系。

Method: 每层将 patch token 反投影为共享世界坐标系下的体积高斯原语，表征多假设深度、深度不确定性与 patch 视锥空间范围；将 key 支撑投影到 query 视图得到出现分布，并以闭式解在该分布上积分旋转算子，按混合权重组合各原语中心旋转并加入协方差衰减。当支撑坍缩为点时退化为确定性 RoPE。

Result: 在新视图合成、深度估计和 3D 几何重建任务上验证了有效性。

Conclusion: WorldRoPE 通过几何引导的跨视图注意力与特征-几何迭代精化，为多视图 Transformer 提供了有效的位置编码方案，无需栅格化或特征 warping。

Abstract: Rotary Positional Embedding (RoPE) is widely used to encode relative positions in Transformers. In multi-view settings, however, patch coordinates are defined independently in each image: the same 3D point can therefore have different coordinates across views, making them insufficient for establishing cross-view correspondences. We introduce WorldRoPE, a probabilistic world-centric RoPE for multi-view attention. At each layer, WorldRoPE back-projects each patch token into a set of volumetric Gaussian primitives in a shared world frame. These primitives represent multiple plausible 3D supports for a token, jointly capturing alternative depth hypotheses, depth uncertainty, and the finite spatial extent of its patch frustum. When a key token's support is projected into a query view, it induces a distribution over where that key may appear in the query image. WorldRoPE integrates the rotary operator over this distribution in closed form, combining rotations at the projected primitive centers according to their mixture weights with covariance-dependent attenuation. This makes attention less sensitive to uncertain key locations in the query view, without rasterizing or warping token features. The formulation reduces to deterministic RoPE when the support collapses to a point. Across layers, the predicted geometry guides cross-view attention, while the updated features refine the geometry used at the next layer. Experiments on novel view synthesis, depth estimation, and 3D geometry reconstruction demonstrate the effectiveness of WorldRoPE for multi-view Transformers.

</details>


### [143] [SyncReward: Human-Aligned Synchronization Reward for Audio-Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16338)
*Kaisi Guan,Ying Ba,Wenjing Wang,Xihua Wang,Chen Li,Jing Lyu,Ruihau Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对音视频生成中的音视频同步难题，构建了数据、基准与奖励模型，提出SyncReward以提升同步质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成语音与声音常与视觉事件在时间上错位，而缺乏人工标注数据、可靠基准和同步感知评估模型是关键瓶颈。

Method: 引入含80K专业同步评分的SyncReward-Data-80K数据集（覆盖Speech/Onset/Instrument/Ambient四类）；构建1K片段的SyncReward-Bench基准；提出SyncReward奖励模型，结合真实片段语义-时间对比预训练与生成片段的人工监督。

Result: 现有专用同步指标和全模态模型与人类判断存在显著偏差；SyncReward作为评估指标与人类评分一致性最高，作为奖励可通过测试时best-of-N选择和强化学习后训练提升同步性。

Conclusion: SyncReward在数据、基准和奖励建模三方面系统推进音视频同步问题，显著提升同步评估与生成质量，并将开源数据、基准、模型与代码。

Abstract: Recent years have witnessed rapid progress in joint audio--visual generation. However, audio--visual synchronization remains a challenge, as generated speech and sound often temporally misalign with visual events. A key bottleneck is the lack of human-annotated data, reliable evaluation benchmarks, and synchronization-aware evaluation models for generated audio--visual outputs. To address these limitations, we conduct a comprehensive study spanning data, benchmarking, and reward modeling. We first introduce SyncReward-Data-80K, a dataset containing 80K professional synchronization ratings for clips from eight audio--visual generators. A taxonomy-guided construction pipeline covers four sound-event families: Speech, Onset, Instrument, and Ambient. We further construct \textbf{SyncReward-Bench}, a held-out benchmark of 1K clips, each independently rated by multiple professionals. Using this benchmark, we systematically evaluate specialized synchronization metrics and omni-modal models, revealing their substantial misalignment with human judgments. Finally, we develop SyncReward, a reward model that combines semantic--temporal contrastive pretraining on real clips with human supervision on generated clips. As an evaluation metric, SyncReward achieves the strongest agreement with human ratings among the evaluated methods. As a reward, it improves synchronization through inference-time best-of-$N$ selection and reinforcement learning based post-training. We will release the data, benchmark, model, and code at https://github.com/WeChatCV/SyncReward.

</details>


### [144] [VideoSlate: Stateful Commit Control for Temporally Grounded Agentic Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16357)
*Shuo Zheng,Yuanjie Dang,Peng Chen,Jianjun Xiang,Ronghua Liang,Mohammed Bennamoun,Qiuhong Ke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VideoSlate，一个以共享证据为核心的状态化提交控制框架，用于智能体视频推理中的时序证据选择与修订。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体视频推理需跨阶段收集和评估视频证据并选择相关片段。新候选和观察会触发对当前片段的重新评估，而修订既可能纠正定位错误，也可能丢弃已有有用证据。因此系统需要将证据组织与时序状态控制相连接，让累积信息共同参与修订评估，同时用显式采纳决策控制状态更新。

Method: VideoSlate围绕共享证据构建。使用类型化证据黑板连接候选片段、来源、构造与验证器观察；通过 incumbent-relative certifier 结合候选、当前片段及相关提案评估修订支持度；控制器基于认证结果授权修订或保留当前片段，将证据累积与状态更新分离，并在黑板上记录每个决策及其证据。

Result: 在视频接地与接地问答基准上的广泛评估表明，VideoSlate可通过选择性修订改进时序定位。

Conclusion: VideoSlate通过证据黑板与提交控制机制，有效协调证据累积与状态更新，提升视频推理中的时序定位表现。

Abstract: Agentic video reasoning systems gather and assess video evidence across stages, selecting relevant spans as visual evidence for answering questions. New candidates and observations prompt reassessment of the current span, but revisions can correct localization errors or discard useful evidence already present in the adopted span. Systems therefore need to connect evidence organization across stages with temporal state control, allowing accumulated information to jointly inform revision assessment while explicit adoption decisions govern how assessment outcomes affect downstream visual evidence. We introduce VideoSlate, a stateful commit-control framework built around shared evidence. A typed evidence blackboard links candidate spans, provenance, construction, and verifier observations, enabling an incumbent-relative certifier to assess revision support from the candidate, current span, and related proposals. Based on certification, the controller authorizes a revision or retains the current span, separating evidence accumulation from state updates and recording each decision and its evidence on the blackboard. Extensive evaluations across video grounding and grounded question-answering benchmarks show that VideoSlate can improve temporal localization through selective revision.

</details>


### [145] [From Sound to Sense: An Anatomy of Semantic Supervision for Speech-Driven Visual Grounding in Remote Sensing Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16364)
*Zhongyu Pang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SemEcho框架，将冻结Whisper语音通路接入VLTVG定位主机，用同主机文本作上限，剖析三种语义注入通道，并发布Audio-DIOR-R/Comp数据集，发现直连范式仅在识别器弱时优于ASR级联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感语音视觉定位(RSTalker等直连范式)绕过ASR，但其"学到了什么语言"未被度量；需一个可公平对比语音与文本、量化语义注入效果的评测工具。

Method: 将冻结Whisper语音路径嫁接到VLTVG定位主机，使语音与文本共享解码器，配对文本(由DIOR-RSVG合成)作为训练期教师及同主机文本上限；用线性语义探针解剖特征模仿蒸馏、词级CTC监督、两阶段ASR预训练三种语义注入通道；发布Audio-DIOR-R(80,973训练片段,10音色×K=3,Qwen3-TTS加sox噪声λ∈[0,0.4])与Audio-DIOR-Comp(2×2声学-语义协议下留出17个属性-类别组合的新划分)。

Result: 直连是否胜过领域训练ASR级联取决于识别器质量：对弱域内级联(CTC 0.53)，直连以9.0 Acc@0.5点胜出，转录错误集中于空间关系词；而对强域内级联(CTC 0.029)，达到文本上限(80.8 vs 81.0)，甚至超过最强直连模型8.8点。

Conclusion: 反ASR的前提仅在识别器较弱时成立；识别器质量是关键决定因素，强识别器下ASR级联明显优于直连范式。

Abstract: Speech-driven visual grounding in remote sensing (RSVG) removes the typing bottleneck of text-based interaction and is intended to preserve prosodic intent, enabling hands-free target acquisition in time-critical missions. The direct-audio paradigm, mapping raw speech straight to a bounding box and bypassing automatic speech recognition (ASR), was recently adopted by RSTalker, but what such grounders actually learn about language remains unmeasured. SemEcho grafts a frozen Whisper speech pathway onto a VLTVG grounding host so that speech and text share one decoder, making the paired text, free by construction since speech corpora in this task are synthesized from DIOR-RSVG expressions, a training-time teacher and a fair, same-host text upper bound. On this instrument we dissect three semantic injection channels (feature-mimicry distillation, word-level CTC supervision, and two-stage ASR pretraining) with linear semantic probes, and release Audio-DIOR-R (80,973 training clips; 10 timbres x K=3; Qwen3-TTS synthesis with online sox noise lambda in [0, 0.4]) and Audio-DIOR-Comp, a re-split holding out 17 attribute-category combinations under a 2x2 acoustic-semantic protocol. Our anatomy yields four findings. (i) Whether direct alignment beats a domain-trained ASR cascade is decided by recognizer quality: against a weak in-domain cascade (CTC 0.53) direct wins by 9.0 Acc@0.5 points with transcription errors concentrated on spatial-relation words, but a strong in-domain cascade (CTC 0.029) reaches the text upper bound (80.8 vs. 81.0) and beats even the strongest direct model by 8.8 points; the anti-ASR premise holds only against weak recognizers.

</details>


### [146] [Throwing4: Phase-Aware Pose Modeling for Throw-Distance Prediction in Elite Athletics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16383)
*Bikash Kumar Badatya,Kartike Tiwari,Jyotirmoy Amin,Ravi Hegde*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过显式对齐投掷技术阶段，可提升基于姿态的投掷距离预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精英投掷项目具备结构化的动作序列与客观连续的投掷距离，适合开展面向成绩的运动分析；但广播条件下姿态建模中，是否能利用显式阶段对齐提升预测仍不明确。

Method: 构建 Throwing4 数据集（500 段精英投掷片段，涵盖标枪、铁饼、链球、铅球，含二维姿态序列、帧级阶段标注与官方距离标签）；将距离预测建模为回归问题，对比全序列建模与阶段条件建模（把每次投掷分为四个技术阶段，并融合各阶段运动表征），在多种图基姿态编码器上验证。

Result: 在多个图基姿态编码器上，引入阶段结构后预测误差一致降低，说明阶段对齐表征比将整体动作视为无序序列更稳定。

Conclusion: 显式生物力学阶段信息是精英投掷姿态成绩建模的有效归纳先验；该数据集与协议可支持细粒度技术分析、可解释运动分析与广播视频鲁棒建模等后续研究。

Abstract: Elite athletics throwing provides a structured biomechanical action sequence together with an objective continuous outcome, namely throw distance, making it a suitable setting for performance-oriented sports analytics. In this work, we investigate whether explicit phase alignment of pose dynamics improves throw-distance prediction under realistic broadcast conditions. To support this study, we curate Throwing4, a dataset of 500 elite throwing clips spanning javelin, discus, hammer, and shot put, with corresponding two-dimensional pose sequences, frame-level phase annotations, and official distance labels. We formulate throw-distance prediction from broadcast-derived pose as a regression problem and compare full-sequence modeling against a phase-conditioned approach that partitions each attempt into four technique stages and fuses phase-specific motion representations. Experiments across multiple graph-based pose encoders show a consistent reduction in prediction error when phase structure is incorporated, indicating that phase-aligned modeling provides a more stable representation than treating the entire motion as an unstructured sequence. These findings suggest that explicit biomechanical phase information is a useful inductive prior for pose-based performance modeling in elite athletics throwing. Beyond score prediction, the dataset and protocol provide a basis for future work on fine-grained technique analysis, interpretable sports analytics, and robust modeling from broadcast video.

</details>


### [147] [Recovering from Pose-Selection Failures in Repeated Visual Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16409)
*Jaehyun Kim,Wonseok Kang,Tae-Wan Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HPR方法，利用过去定位输出来重新选择位姿候选，解决重复结构导致的错误位姿选择问题，在多个配置中提升定位召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重复结构会让不准确的位姿候选获得比正确候选更多的内点支持，导致视觉定位系统错误地选择它。需要一个方法区分彼此分离的当前查询候选。

Method: HPR使用最多五个过去定位输出的粗略位置预测来区分候选。最佳拟合预测提出一个替代候选；仅当所有良好拟合的预测都更接近该替代候选而非定位器给出的位姿时，HPR才接受它。无需里程计，返回所选候选不变，仅用于选择而非位姿细化。

Result: 在HLoc、MASt3R位姿生成和JIST检索的全部12个主要配置中，使用一个开发集阈值，HPR在5米和10度内提升召回率，丢失成功查询少于0.4%。在NAVER Gangnam B1上，HPR纠正97个HLoc失败，丢失6个成功；三个HLoc测试集在0.25米和2度内均有增益。HLoc候选中位运行时间低于1毫秒。

Conclusion: HPR能有效利用历史定位信息重新选择位姿候选，显著提升定位召回率且代价极低，但在定位频率较低时恢复效果减弱。

Abstract: Repetitive structures can give an incorrect pose candidate more inlier support than an accurate one, causing visual localization to select it. History-Guided Pose Reselection (HPR) uses coarse position forecasts from up to five past localization outputs to distinguish well-separated current-query candidates. The best-fitting forecast proposes an alternative; HPR accepts it only if every well-fitting forecast is much closer to it than to the localizer's pose. HPR needs no odometry and returns the selected candidate unchanged, using prediction for selection rather than pose refinement. With one development-set threshold, HPR improves recall within 5 meters and 10 degrees in all 12 main configurations of Hierarchical Localization (HLoc), MASt3R-based pose generation, and JIST-based retrieval, losing successes on fewer than 0.4 percent of queries. On NAVER Gangnam B1, HPR corrects 97 HLoc failures and loses six successes; gains persist within 0.25 meters and 2 degrees on all three HLoc test sets. Median HPR runtime on HLoc candidates is below one millisecond, excluding pose generation. Recovery weakens under less frequent localization.

</details>


### [148] [Not All Challenges Are Equal: Evaluating Interactive Liveness Detection Against Real-Time Face Swaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16417)
*Anton Firc,David Drtil,Martin Perešíni,Milan Šalko,Vojtěch Staněk,Kamil Malinka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文通过108次真人会话评估随机手势挑战对实时换脸攻击（DeepFaceLive、FaceFusion）的检测能力，发现动态手势窗口的AUC（0.988）显著优于静态校准段（0.878），且响应时间可作为额外信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程身份验证假设摄像头流展示的是真人，但实时换脸工具可在验证者接收前替换人脸。被动深度伪造检测器泛化性差，而交互式挑战的手势选择缺乏证据支撑，不知哪些手势能有效暴露当前攻击。

Method: 在108次真实会话中，以6名受试者、24个被冒充身份，通过虚拟摄像头注入DeepFaceLive与FaceFusion两种换脸工具。使用冻结的UCF空间检测器，对比静态校准段与随机主动手势窗口的检测表现，并分析端到端验证响应时间。

Result: 静态校准段会话级AUC为0.878；主动手势窗口均值达0.988。姿态与组合手势点估计最高，表情无明显提升。配遮挡掩码的FaceFusion在手部遮挡下得分低于静态基线。端到端响应时间可区分攻击与会话，会话级AUC为0.732。

Conclusion: 挑战手势集应针对当前攻击配置进行测试，响应时间可作为部署特定的额外检测信号。

Abstract: Remote identity verification assumes that a camera stream shows a live person, yet public real-time face-swap tools can replace that person before the stream reaches the verifier. Passive deepfake detectors generalize poorly to new generators, while interactive challenges are often selected without evidence of which gestures expose current attacks. We evaluate randomized gesture challenges in 108 live sessions with six subjects, 24 impersonated identities, and two face-swap tools, DeepFaceLive and FaceFusion, injected through a virtual camera. The static calibration segment reaches a session-level AUC of 0.878 with a frozen UCF spatial detector, while the mean across the active-gesture windows reaches 0.988. Pose and combined gestures have the highest point estimates, whereas expressions show no clear improvement. Hand occlusion against FaceFusion, configured with an occlusion mask, scores below the static baseline. The end-to-end time required to satisfy the validator also distinguishes attacks from genuine sessions, achieving a session-level AUC of 0.732, although this signal includes synthesis and transport delays. These results show that challenge sets should be tested against current attack configurations and that response time can provide an additional, deployment-specific signal.

</details>


### [149] [VisExMEM: Visual Evidence-Based Explainable Multimodal Evaluation Metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16441)
*Subhadeep Roy,Christoph Leiter,Steffen Eger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 VisExMEM，一种无参考图像-文本评估器：将字幕分解为原子声明、定位视觉证据、逐条验证，并聚合为全局分数，解释与决策共享证据；同时提出 AGITA-Bench 基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像-文本评估多依赖全局分数，难以看出哪些语义细节被图像支持；可解释指标虽给出理由，但支撑理由的视觉证据仍难验证。

Method: 无参考评估器 VisExMEM：分解字幕为原子声明→定位视觉证据→验证每条声明→平均得全局分；解释复用同一证据与决策。训练 Qwen3.5 的 4B/9B 模型，用 100K 样本做多任务 SFT、反事实精炼与 evidence-aware GDPO。

Result: 八个基准上，VisExMEM-9B 在开源评估器中取得最高描述性综合分，超 VQAScore 11.7 分，与 GPT-5.6 Sol 具竞争力。AGITA-Bench 含 600 人工标注图文对与 8.7K 原子声明。未调优模型上，分解式提示比整体式提示提升综合分 3.6–5.1 分。人工评估显示其解释优于已有可解释指标，接近 GPT-5.6 Sol。

Conclusion: VisExMEM 通过声明级证据定位实现可验证的可解释评估，兼顾全局评分与细粒度解释，性能优异，并推动可解释评估基准建设。

Abstract: Current image-text evaluation often relies on a global score, which gives limited insight into which semantic details are supported by the image. Explainable metrics provide rationales, but the visual evidence supporting those rationales can still be difficult to verify. We introduce VisExMEM, a reference-free evaluator that decomposes captions into atomic claims, localizes visual evidence, verifies each claim, and averages claim-level support into a global score. Explanations use the same evidence and decisions. We train Qwen3.5-based 4B and 9B models on 100K samples with multi-task SFT, counterfactual refinement, and evidence-aware GDPO. Across eight benchmarks, VisExMEM-9B achieves the highest descriptive composite among open-source evaluators in our comparison, exceeding VQAScore by 11.7 points while remaining competitive with GPT-5.6 Sol. We also introduce AGITA-Bench, containing 600 human-annotated image-text pairs and 8.7K atomic claims for global and claim-level evaluation. For untuned Qwen3.5 and GPT-5.6 Sol controls, decomposed prompting raises the same composite by 3.6-5.1 points relative to holistic prompting. Human evaluation shows that VisExMEM explanations outperform prior explainable metrics and approach GPT-5.6 Sol.

</details>


### [150] [Pixels or Code? Benchmarking Instruction-Guided Visual Artifact Operation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16444)
*Shun Guo,Shuizhou Shi,Boao Wang,Wei Xiong,Siyu Sun,Jiangtong Li,Jie Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出视觉工件操作(VAO)任务范式与VAO-Bench基准，覆盖1573个任务，对比像素编辑、代码生成与智能体三种范式的直接编辑、依赖更新与内容保持能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图表、技术图、网页与文档等视觉工件支撑科研与工程流程，其编辑需直接修改、依赖感知更新、保持无关内容并满足领域约束，但现有基准仅覆盖部分问题（如图表的级联更新），缺乏跨视觉域、跨范式、统一协议的评测。

Method: 提出VAO任务定义；构建VAO-Bench，含9类工件、3个难度层级共1573个人工筛选任务；采用可执行工件与确定性渲染追踪参考变换，并标注直接编辑、依赖更新与保持约束；在仅图像输入协议下，对像素空间编辑器、代码生成多模态系统与工具使用智能体进行统一评测，指标含直接编辑准确率、依赖感知更新、内容保持、领域有效性与视觉质量。

Result: 可执行代码类系统更擅长依赖更新；像素空间编辑器执行更快、视觉质量更强；显式声明依赖对两类范式均有提升，说明隐式更新推断是核心瓶颈。

Conclusion: VAO-Bench为跨生成范式的可靠视觉工件编辑提供了可复现的评测基础。

Abstract: Visual artifacts such as charts, technical diagrams, webpages, and document pages underpin scientific, commercial, and engineering workflows. Editing these artifacts requires direct modification, dependency-aware updates, preservation of unaffected content, and compliance with domain constraints. We define this capability as Visual Artifact Operation (VAO). Prior benchmarks test parts of this problem, including cascading updates in charts. We study direct edits, dependent updates, and preservation across visual domains and pixel, code, and agent paradigms under one image-only protocol. We introduce Visual Artifact Operation Bench (VAO-Bench), comprising 1,573 manually screened tasks across nine artifact categories and three difficulty levels. VAO-Bench uses executable artifacts and deterministic rendering to trace reference transformations and annotate direct edits, dependent updates, and preservation constraints. All systems receive the same source image and instruction; we evaluate pixel-space editors, code-generating multimodal systems, and tool-using agents on direct-edit accuracy, dependency-aware update, content preservation, domain validity, and visual quality. Experiments show that executable-code systems better support dependent updates, while pixel-space editors offer faster execution and strong visual quality. Explicitly stating dependencies improves both paradigms, identifying implicit-update inference as a central bottleneck. VAO-Bench provides a reproducible foundation for dependable visual artifact editing across generation paradigms.

</details>


### [151] [Tikhonov-Stabilized Bezier Representation Forecasting for Training-free Diffusion Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16448)
*Lei Zhu,Mujie Lin,Ruochong Zheng,Guangyi Wang,Hao Li,Peng Jin,Chang Liu,Jie Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 BeziCast，一种无需训练的扩散模型加速框架，利用低阶 Bezier 轨迹预测输出近端去噪表示，实现 FLUX.1 最高 4.79×、HunyuanVideo 最高 4.11× 加速且质量损失更小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型（尤其 Diffusion Transformer）推理昂贵。现有 cache-then-forecast 方法多依赖 Taylor 有限差分外推，在较长缓存间隔下不稳定，且通常预测局部模块输出，误差会沿后续去噪块累积。

Method: BeziCast 用低阶 Bezier 轨迹预测输出近端去噪表示；通过 Tikhonov 稳定化拟合估计 Bezier 控制点，避免显式高阶导数估计并解耦轨迹容量与缓存查询密度；推导等价预测权重，并引入凸包启发的护栏机制，检测高风险预测并软性拉向保守的单纯形投影估计。

Result: 在多种先进图像与视频扩散模型上验证有效：FLUX.1 加速最高 4.79×，HunyuanVideo 最高 4.11×，生成质量显著优于竞争加速基线。

Conclusion: BeziCast 是无需训练、部署友好的扩散加速方案，通过稳定的 Bezier 轨迹预测与护栏机制，在保持生成质量的同时实现显著加速。

Abstract: Diffusion models, particularly Diffusion Transformers, achieve strong image and video generation quality but remain expensive at inference time due to repeated denoiser evaluations. Feature caching offers a deployment-friendly acceleration strategy by reusing or predicting intermediate representations without retraining the generator or modifying the sampler. However, existing cache-then-forecast methods often rely on Taylor-style finite-difference extrapolation, which can become unstable over longer cache intervals, and typically predict local module outputs whose errors may accumulate through subsequent denoiser blocks. We propose \textbf{BeziCast}, a training-free diffusion acceleration framework that forecasts output-proximal denoising representations using low-order B'ezier trajectories. BeziCast estimates B'ezier control points via Tikhonov-stabilized fitting, providing a smooth, capacity-controlled temporal parameterization that avoids explicit high-order derivative estimation and decouples trajectory capacity from cache-query density. To moderate aggressive extrapolation, we further derive equivalent forecasting weights and introduce a convex-hull-inspired guardrail that detects high-risk predictions and softly pulls them toward a conservative simplex-projected estimate. Extensive evaluations across advanced image and video diffusion models demonstrate the effectiveness of BeziCast. In particular, BeziCast accelerates FLUX.1 by up to (4.79\times) and HunyuanVideo by up to (4.11\times), while preserving substantially better generation quality than competing acceleration baselines.

</details>


### [152] [Not Every Cue Is Evidence: Cue Supportability for Forensic Distillation in Face Forgery Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16452)
*Binjia Zhou,Dawei Luo,Shuai Chen,Feng Xu,Haoyuan Li,Jia wen Seow,Jiachi Wang,Jie Lei,Zunlei Feng,Yijun Bei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 可解释人脸伪造检测中，学生模型难以感知细粒度伪造线索，导致"答案对、证据错"(CAWE)失败。作者提出双重可支持性审计，在蒸馏前过滤特权线索，提升解释忠实度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练线索源自伪造生成流程，但输入图像质量与分辨率有限，学生模型难以观察细粒度痕迹；以特权监督提供此类线索会加剧问题，使学生表达其无法感知的证据，导致模板化、过度泛化的表述与CAWE失败。

Method: 提出双重可支持性审计，在蒸馏前过滤特权线索：每条候选线索需同时满足(1)可由剩余特权上下文预测，(2)与当前图像中高归因视觉区域存在因果关联；仅同时满足者获得显著蒸馏权重，防止无支持细节泄漏给学生。

Result: 在八个基准上，相比现有范式，方法在保持或提升检测准确率的同时显著提高解释忠实度。

Conclusion: 通过双重可支持性审计过滤特权线索，可有效缓解CAWE失败，在可解释人脸伪造检测中兼顾检测性能与解释忠实度。

Abstract: Explainable face forgery detection requires not only correct judgments but also forensic evidence supported by the input image. However, training cues are typically derived from the forgery generation pipeline. Since images available to the model are of limited quality and resolution, it is difficult to observe the fine-grained traces these cues refer to. Training methods that further supply such cues as privileged supervision exacerbate the problem, as the student is taught to articulate evidence it cannot perceive. The student consequently resorts to templated or over-generalized expressions, giving rise to the correct-answer, wrong-evidence (CAWE) failure. We propose a dual supportability audit that filters privileged cues before distillation. Each candidate cue must be predictable from the remaining privileged context and causally tied to high-attribution visual regions in the current image. Only cues satisfying both conditions receive substantial distillation weight, preventing unsupported details from leaking into the student. Experiments across eight benchmarks show that our method maintains or improves detection accuracy while significantly raising explanation faithfulness compared to existing paradigms.

</details>


### [153] [GHOST: Geometry-Constrained Hybrid Diffusion for Open-Vocabulary 3D Scene Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16455)
*Changqun Feng,Wangxiandi Yin,Xin Hu,Ruiting Dai,Feiyang Yang,Jielei Wang,Tao He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GHOST：一种几何约束的混合扩散框架，用于开放词汇3D场景图生成，通过几何-约束-谓词去噪流程提升空间关系预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇方法在空间谓词上表现差，因为语言相似性反映的是语义共现而非显式几何关系。

Method: 联合建模连续节点几何与离散边谓词，采用几何-约束-谓词去噪流程；引入语言约束归纳，将谓词嵌入映射为可微几何约束以指导布局优化和谓词重加权；设计几何-拓扑协同去噪器，通过噪声自适应交互和方向感知建模耦合节点与边推理。

Result: 在3DSSG上取得闭集和开放词汇设置的SOTA，空间谓词较RelationField提升超17个点。

Conclusion: GHOST通过显式几何约束有效解决开放词汇3D场景图空间关系预测难题。

Abstract: 3D scene graph generation requires understanding both object semantics and metric spatial relations. However, existing open-vocabulary methods often struggle with spatial predicates because language similarity reflects semantic co-occurrence rather than explicit geometry. We propose GHOST, a Geometry-constrained Hybrid diffusion framework for Open-vocabulary 3D Scene graph generaTion. GHOST jointly models continuous node geometry and discrete edge predicates, following a geometry-to-constraint-to-predicate denoising process to ensure geometrically consistent relation prediction. It introduces language-grounded constraint induction, which maps predicate embeddings to differentiable geometric constraints that guide both layout refinement and predicate reweighting. A Geometry-Topology Collaborative Denoiser further couples node and edge reasoning through noise-adaptive interaction and orientation-aware modeling. Experiments on 3DSSG demonstrate state-of-the-art performance in both closed-set and open-vocabulary settings, including over 17-point gains over RelationField on spatial predicates.

</details>


### [154] [Teaching Vision-Language Models to Decline: Support Completion Against Mirage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16465)
*Alfaj Uddin Ahmed,Md. Shaown Miah,Md Arid Hasan,Syed Ishtiaque Ahmed,Sayeed Shafayet Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出"海市蜃楼"(mirage)失败：视觉语言模型会对从未见过的图像给出答案，且答案可能正确但无证据支撑。作者将成因定位于交叉熵训练目标，并提出四条件训练协议TIDE，使模型学会在证据不足时"拒答"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型对未见图像也会作答，且外部检查者看不到输入时无法分辨答案是否有依据。交叉熵只按答案与图像的条件互信息区分"读图"与"忽略图"的模型，且没有任何训练样本奖励"拒答"，因此该失败无法被现有目标消除。

Method: 提出支持补全(Support Completion)协议TIDE：每个训练问题同时配以空图、同域错误图、域外图，并给出"应拒答"的目标；理论证明在TIDE下任何图像盲预测器每例至少次优1.04 nats，最优解恰在反事实样本上拒答而保持对有效图像的行为，且任何有限模型的"答/拒"决策错误下界由其特征对有效与无效证据的可分性决定。实验中在Qwen2.5-VL-7B上以冻结留出协议、双评审员评测其三条件前身。

Result: 三条件前身将无效证据的海市蜃楼率从0.759降至0.097，空图率从0.700降至0.003，误拒代价为0.126；无标签grounding反使该率升至0.859；似然优势对比仅降至0.718（因其空图项无梯度）；在支持补全后施加grounding则重新引发海市蜃楼。四条件协议在三个基座模型上的实验（含随机种子与组件消融）仍在进行，尚未报告。

Conclusion: 海市蜃楼源于训练目标缺乏"拒答"激励，支持补全TIDE在理论上保证图像盲预测器次优、在最优处正确拒答，并在初步实验中大幅压低无效证据与空图上的幻觉率，同时暴露了grounding与似然对比方法的局限；完整四条件结果待后续报告。

Abstract: Vision-language models answer questions about images they were never shown, a failure called mirage. Such an answer can be correct without being justified by the evidence, and the difference matters wherever a model's output is checked by someone who did not see its input. We locate the failure in the training objective: cross-entropy separates a model that reads the image from one that ignores it by only the conditional mutual information between answer and image, and no training example rewards declining. We formalise support completion, TIDE, which also shows each training question with a blank image, a same-domain wrong image and an out-of-domain image, with targets that decline, and prove that under TIDE every image-blind predictor is suboptimal by at least 1.04 nats per example, that the optimum declines exactly on the counterfactual kinds while keeping its behaviour on valid images, and that for any finite model the error of its answer-or-decline decision, declining on valid input plus answering on invalid input, is bounded below by how well its representation separates valid from invalid evidence. On Qwen2.5-VL-7B under a frozen held-out protocol with two judges, the three-condition predecessor of TIDE reduced the invalid-evidence mirage rate from 0.759 to 0.097 and the blank-image rate from 0.700 to 0.003 at a false-abstention cost of 0.126; label-free grounding raised the rate to 0.859, a likelihood-advantage contrast moved it only to 0.718 because its empty-image term carries no gradient, and grounding applied after support completion re-opened the mirage. The four-condition protocol on three base models, with seeds and component ablations, is in progress and not reported here.

</details>


### [155] [Auditing Observation-Quality Shortcuts in the Evaluation of Person-Centered Motion for Video Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16471)
*Chaehyun Kim,Changwon Lim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出观察质量感知的评估框架，揭示视频异常检测中基于运动的判别可能源于人体检测、姿态估计与跟踪的观察质量捷径，而非真实的任务相关运动信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VAD中的"偏离正常模式"未必对应安全关键事件。作者聚焦以人为中心的紧急事件与暴力，怀疑运动异常判别实际反映的是观察质量差异（来自检测、姿态、跟踪），而非任务相关运动信息。

Method: 构建观察质量感知评估框架，包含质量诊断、质量平衡、运动正交化与稳健性分析；在UCF-Crime以人为中心类别上，用仅质量特征与多线索运动特征对比，并采用基数匹配将11个质量变量控制在预设边际平衡阈值下，在LR、RF、RBF-SVM上评估。

Result: 原始队列中仅质量特征的判别力达到或超过多线索运动，暴露强观察质量捷径；匹配后多线索运动稳定优于仅质量特征，ROC-AUC提升0.046-0.141。但非线性仅质量预测仍高于随机，说明是超出已测量边际质量的额外判别，而非无质量依赖或因果性运动理解。

Conclusion: 高VAD性能不能直接解读为异常运动建模能力，必须先审计人体观察流水线的可靠性。本研究提供了系统化协议，用于评估观察质量捷径并检验运动判别能否经受检测、姿态估计与跟踪可靠性的显式控制。

Abstract: Video anomaly detection (VAD) identifies deviations from normal patterns, but such deviations do not necessarily correspond to safety-critical events. We focus on human-centered emergencies and violence and investigate whether motion-based anomaly discrimination reflects task-relevant motion information or observation-quality differences arising from person detection, pose estimation, and tracking. We introduce an observation-quality-aware evaluation framework that combines quality diagnostics, quality balancing, motion orthogonalization, and robustness analyses. On human-centered categories of UCF-Crime, quality-only features match or exceed the discrimination of multi-cue motion in the original cohort, revealing a strong observation-quality shortcut. After cardinality matching brings all 11 measured quality variables below a prespecified marginal balance threshold, multi-cue motion consistently outperforms quality-only features across Logistic Regression, Random Forest, and RBF-SVM, yielding ROC-AUC gains of 0.046-0.141. Because nonlinear quality-only prediction remains above chance after matching, we interpret this result as additional discrimination beyond measured marginal quality rather than as evidence of quality-free or causal motion understanding. These findings show that high VAD performance should not be interpreted as evidence of abnormal-motion modeling without auditing the reliability of the person-observation pipeline. Our study provides a systematic protocol for evaluating observation-quality shortcuts and for assessing whether motion-based discrimination survives explicit control of detection, pose-estimation, and tracking reliability.

</details>


### [156] [MiMaAlign: Hierarchical Micro-Macro Alignment for Physically Plausible Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16482)
*Yansong Liu,Litong Gong,Weijie Li,Tiezheng Ge,Ming Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MiMaAlign分层微观-宏观对齐框架，将动力学与事件常识知识迁移至视频扩散模型不同抽象深度，提升物理合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: T2V扩散模型生成视觉保真度高，但仍存在不连续动态和事件交互不合理等物理不可行现象。现有表示对齐方法仅迁移通用视频特征或短程运动线索，将物理合理性视为单一层次问题。

Method: 分层对齐框架：微观层用Neural ODE学习的连续时间运动表示约束扩散特征；宏观层用指令提示的VLM提供物体交互与事件语义表示。通过仅训练用的辅助目标，将两个教师的互补监督分别对齐到较浅和较深的生成器特征。

Result: 在CogVideoX-2B上，VideoPhy基准的Physical Commonsense分数相对基线最高提升21.0%，在VideoPhy2基准上也有显著增益，同时在VBench上保持通用视频质量，并获得盲测用户对比支持。

Conclusion: 分层微观-宏观对齐能有效将互补的物理先验内化到扩散模型中，显著提升生成视频的物理合理性而不损害通用质量。

Abstract: Text-to-video (T2V) diffusion models have made remarkable progress in generating high-fidelity visuals, yet their outputs still often exhibit physically implausible phenomena, including discontinuous dynamics and implausible event interactions. Existing representation alignment methods mainly transfer generic video features or short-range motion cues, treating physical plausibility as a single-level representation problem. To address this issue, we propose MiMaAlign, a hierarchical micro-macro alignment framework that transfers complementary dynamics-oriented and event-oriented knowledge into different abstraction depths of a pretrained video diffusion model. Specifically, micro-level continuous dynamics alignment imposes constraints by aligning diffusion features with a continuous-time motion representation learned by a Neural Ordinary Differential Equation (Neural ODE), while macro-level event-commonsense alignment provides representations of object interactions and event semantics through an instruction-prompted vision-language model (VLM). With training-only auxiliary objectives, we align the complementary supervision from the two teachers with shallower and deeper generator features, respectively, enabling the generator to internalize the corresponding physical priors. Empirical evaluations show that, on CogVideoX-2B, MiMaAlign achieves a relative improvement of up to 21.0% in the Physical Commonsense (PC) score on the VideoPhy benchmark over the baseline, and also delivers substantial gains on the highly challenging VideoPhy2 benchmark, while maintaining general video quality on VBench and being supported by blind user comparisons.

</details>


### [157] [CineRecon: Benchmarking Frontier Models for Agentic Cinematic Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16577)
*Jialei Wang,Zehan Wang,XiaoYang Wu,Ziang Zhang,Siyu Chen,Jiaer Huang,Yujia Zhang,Tianyu Pang,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CineRecon基准，用于评估agent从参考视频重建可编辑动态3D场景的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可编辑动态3D场景重建是实现可控视频生成的重要路径，但缺乏评估agent该能力的基准。

Method: 构建含50个参考视频和360个标注片段的基准，涵盖电影、实景、游戏；在Proxy、Simplified、Detailed三种表示设置下评估，用checklist和视觉语言judge对比时间对齐帧的11类属性（相机、静态、动态一致性）。

Result: 评估12个模型（含各自agent框架），三个维度均出现失败；即使在Proxy设置下主体运动和交互仍难复现。

Conclusion: 现有agent在重建可编辑动态3D场景方面能力不足，主体运动与交互是主要瓶颈。

Abstract: Reconstructing reference videos as editable dynamic 3D scenes is an important path toward controllable video generation for filmmaking: these scenes provide modifiable, renderable visual proxies that enable explicit control over scene layout, subject actions, and camera behavior. To evaluate agents' ability to reconstruct editable dynamic 3D scenes from dynamic reference videos, we introduce CineRecon, a benchmark comprising 50 reference videos and 360 annotated evaluation clips. The reference videos span film, real-world footage, and games. Given a complete video, an agent uses tools to construct, animate, render, and refine its reconstruction. We evaluate this task under three representation settings: Proxy, Simplified, and Detailed. These settings increase geometric and appearance requirements while retaining the same requirements for spatial relations, camera behavior, and subject dynamics. This design tests whether agents can reproduce the events in a video when detailed appearance is unnecessary, and how performance changes as representation requirements increase. For evaluation, we construct checklists that associate observable properties and events with specific entities and times. A vision-language judge compares timestamp-aligned reference and rendered frames across 11 categories, grouped into camera, static, and dynamic consistency dimensions. We evaluate 12 models using their corresponding agent harnesses and find failures across all three dimensions. Subject motions and interactions remain difficult to reproduce even under the Proxy setting.

</details>


### [158] [The Midas Touch for Metric Depth: Interpretable Metricization for Generalizable Depth Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16749)
*Mengtan Zhang,Yu Ma,Shiqi Jiang,Yi Feng,Wei Ye,Xieyuanli Chen,Hanli Wang,Qijun Chen,Rui Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MTDv2是一个混合式度量深度感知框架，结合显式深度度量化和数据驱动精化，解决单目相对深度估计的尺度模糊与局部尺度不一致问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型提升了单目相对深度估计的泛化性，但恢复准确的度量深度仍受尺度模糊和局部尺度不一致困扰。现有方法（全局仿射变换或数据驱动深度补全）分别无法适应空间变化的相对-度量关系、且对稀疏观测分布敏感。

Method: 提出MTDv2，融合显式度量化和数据驱动精化。显式部分进行分段仿射变换恢复空间变化的度量尺度（保持相对深度几何结构），再做逐像素精化，沿几何变化累积最小的路径传播度量观测；数据驱动部分将单路径传播扩展为可学习的多路径公式，同时保持可解释性。

Result: 在16个公开数据集上的大量实验表明，MTDv2在多种稀疏观测来源和不同深度估计范式下均具备稳健且可泛化的深度尺度恢复能力，并广泛应用于下游3D感知任务。

Conclusion: MTDv2同时解决空间变化尺度适应性和稀疏观测敏感性问题，兼具泛化性与可解释性，代码将于发表后公开。

Abstract: Recent foundation models have drastically improved the generalization of monocular relative depth estimation, yet recovering accurate metric depth from their estimates for depth perception tasks remains challenging due to scale ambiguity and local metric-scale inconsistency. Existing approaches recover metric depth from sparse observations using either explicit global affine transformation algorithms or implicit data-driven depth completion networks. The former cannot accommodate spatially varying relative-to-metric relationships, while the latter are sensitive to the distribution of sparse observations and may not fully exploit generalizable relative depth priors. To simultaneously address these issues, this study extends our previous work MTD and proposes MTDv2, a hybrid metric depth perception framework that combines explicit depth metricization with data-driven metric depth refinement. The explicit depth metricization process performs segment-wise affine transformations to recover spatially varying metric scales while preserving the geometric structure of relative depth, followed by pixel-wise depth refinement that propagates metric observations along paths with minimal accumulated geometric variation. The data-driven depth refinement process subsequently extends the explicit single-path depth propagation to a learnable multi-path formulation while preserving its interpretability. Extensive experiments conducted on 16 public datasets demonstrate the robust and generalizable depth scale recovery capability of MTDv2 across diverse sources of sparse observations and different depth estimation paradigms, as well as its broad applicability to downstream 3D perception tasks. Code will be publicly available at https://mias.group/MTD upon publication.

</details>


### [159] [Image Tokenizers Should Be Trained Between Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16759)
*Ole Beißwenger,Jan-Niklas Dihlmann,Hendrik Lensch*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 InterpTok，通过微调 MAETok 使其能解码图像码之间的混合，从而改善生成图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成器产生的码往往偏离训练码，落在码空间中间区域，而现有方法仅靠加噪声增强解码器鲁棒性，与真实情况不符。

Method: 对 MAETok 进行微调，用判别器判断解码出的混合码是否与两个源图一样自然，并加入混合比例损失保持两码的混合比例。

Result: 保持 MAETok 重建质量，插值质量提升三倍以上；匹配扩散 Transformer 的 FID 从 11.61 降至 8.48，1/8 采样步数下达 8.11，训练步数减半即达原结果。

Conclusion: 图像码之间的空间应被显式监督而非忽略。

Abstract: Latent diffusion models generate images as codes of an image tokenizer, whose decoder turns them into the pixel image. Recent tokenizers improve how well these codes reconstruct an image and how easily a generator learns them. Yet a generator does not reproduce the codes of training images, and when a generated code lands outside what the decoder expects, the decoded image contains imperfections. Current methods make the decoder more robust to generated codes by adding noise during training. However, generated codes lie between the training codes and resemble their mixture more than one of them plus noise. To improve generated images, decoders should therefore be trained on codes in the space between training samples. We propose InterpTok, which fine-tunes MAETok to decode mixtures of image codes into natural images. Since no target image exists between two training samples, a discriminator judges whether each decoded mixture looks as natural as its two sources. A mixing ratio loss requires the decoded mixture to keep the mixing ratio of the two codes. Together, they prevent collapse to a trivial solution and yield a sharp and natural composition of both images. InterpTok keeps MAETok's reconstruction quality and improves interpolation quality more than threefold, the best of all tokenizers in a recent benchmark. Beyond interpolation, InterpTok improves the generation FID of a matched diffusion transformer from 11.61 to 8.48 without guidance, and even reaches 8.11 at an eighth of the sampling steps, where the generator on MAETok's codes worsens. The generator trained on our tokenizer also reaches the final result of the one trained on MAETok at half the training steps. Our findings show that the space between image codes can and should be explicitly supervised rather than ignored.

</details>


### [160] [Towards Complementary Keypoint Detection via Mixture of Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16762)
*Xiaoyong Lu,Guobao Xiao,Dong Liang,Songlin Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Mixture of Detectors (MoD)，通过自适应组合异构检测器实现互补的关键点检测，提升密集匹配中的关键点分布与几何性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检测器在不同场景结构和关键点预算下各有所长，依赖单一检测器往往导致次优的关键点分布。

Method: MoD 先将异构检测器蒸馏到统一模型，实现单次前向高效预测多样检测图；随后将检测器选择建模为逐块、预算条件的决策过程，由轻量路由器为每个局部区域分配合适的检测器；路由器用下游匹配质量推导的任务驱动奖励训练，同时兼顾关键点数量与精度。

Result: 在 MegaDepth、ScanNet 和 HPatches 上的大量实验表明，MoD 在多种关键点预算下持续优于强基线。

Conclusion: MoD 有效改善了关键点分布和下游几何性能，验证了自适应组合异构检测器的有效性。

Abstract: Keypoint detection plays a central role in geometric vision pipelines. Existing detectors exhibit complementary strengths across different scene structures and keypoint budgets, and reliance on a single detector often results in suboptimal keypoint distributions. Motivated by this observation, we propose \textbf{Mixture of Detectors (MoD)} for dense matching pipelines, a unified framework for complementary keypoint detection through the adaptive composition of heterogeneous detectors. MoD first distills heterogeneous detectors into a unified model, allowing diverse detection maps to be predicted efficiently within a single forward pass. Detector selection is subsequently formulated as a patch-wise, budget-conditioned decision process, in which a lightweight router assigns the most suitable detector to each local region. To avoid heuristic supervision, the router is trained with task-driven rewards derived from downstream matching quality, jointly capturing both keypoint quantity and precision. Extensive experiments on MegaDepth, ScanNet, and HPatches demonstrate that MoD consistently outperforms strong baselines across a wide range of keypoint budgets, demonstrating its effectiveness in improving keypoint distribution and downstream geometric performance.

</details>


### [161] [InfoVis: Unified Slide Image Generation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16784)
*Jiahao Wang,Xiyan Jiang,Chenyuan Wu,Haoqiang Zhang,Xin Luo,Yueze Wang,Defu Lian,Bo Zhang,Jiajun Zhang,Zheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出幻灯片生成/编辑四个任务、构建超大规模数据引擎与VisSlideBench基准，训练统一9B模型InfoVis，性能领先开源模型并接近Nano-Banana-2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 幻灯片生成与编辑需同时具备文本渲染、指令跟随、视觉指向、美学设计与精确编辑能力，但现有工作仅覆盖部分能力，且缺乏大规模幻灯片原生监督与诊断性评测。

Method: 定义四个以幻灯片为中心的任务；构建可扩展数据引擎（13M+持续预训练记录、1.9M监督微调记录）；提出VisSlideBench（1,527个人工筛选实例，多维度评测）；基于FLUX.2-klein-base-9B训练统一9B模型InfoVis，并进行幻灯片特定后训练。

Result: VisSlideBench上InfoVis在全部11项指标领先所有评测开源模型，总分7.94（Nano-Banana-2为7.99）；后训练较基座整体提升1.6分，内容忠实度提升最大；与专有模型差距在内容忠实度最小、美学质量最大；文档转幻灯片流程在SlidesGen-Bench上获第二高美学分。

Conclusion: 构建了数据、基准与模型一体化资源，验证了幻灯片特定后训练的有效性，并识别出美学质量为主要短板；将开源模型、代码、数据与基准。

Abstract: Generating and editing presentation slides requires models to combine accurate text rendering, instruction following, visual grounding, aesthetic design, and precise editing within one canvas. Existing work covers only subsets of these capabilities and lacks large-scale slide-native supervision and diagnostic evaluation. We formulate four slide-centric tasks and build a scalable data engine with over 13M continual-pretraining records and 1.9M supervised fine-tuning records. We also introduce VisSlideBench, a benchmark of 1,527 manually screened instances that covers all four tasks with task-specific, multidimensional evaluation. Its automatic rankings agree with blinded, dimension-wise human rankings. Using these resources, we train InfoVis, a unified 9B slide generation and editing model. On VisSlideBench, InfoVis leads every evaluated open-source model on all eleven metrics and scores 7.94 overall against 7.99 for Nano-Banana-2. Starting from FLUX.2-klein-base-9B, slide-specific post-training adds 1.6 points overall, with the largest generation gains in content faithfulness; the remaining gap to proprietary models is smallest in content faithfulness and largest in aesthetic quality. Built on InfoVis, our document-to-slide workflow achieves the second-highest Aesthetics score on the external SlidesGen-Bench. We will release the model, code, data, and benchmark.

</details>


### [162] [ViewStruct: View-Grounded Articulation and Connectivity Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16793)
*Wenjing Bian,Ranran Huang,Andreas Geiger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 ViewStruct，一个从 RGB 图像（可选多视图/视频）联合预测物体连通性与关节结构的框架，无需运动观测或逐实例优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 与陌生物体交互需理解其部件如何连接与运动，但现有方法通常依赖类别规范朝向、观测到的关节运动或逐实例优化，难以泛化。

Method: 输入带部件分割与深度的 RGB 图像（可辅以图像或视频）；适配预训练视觉-语言模型，预测刚体分组、连接关系和关节参数；采用视图锚定表示，将预测关联到观测区域，并在重力对齐的视图相对坐标系中表达关节几何；无需类别规范朝向。

Result: 在结构预测上优于评测基线，并在仅用合成数据训练的情况下，迁移到未见类别、资产来源和真实人类交互视频；模拟操作实验证明预测关节可用于生成机器人运动轨迹。

Conclusion: ViewStruct 提供快速、免运动观测的通用连通性与关节预测，具备跨域泛化能力并可用于机器人操作。

Abstract: Interacting with unfamiliar objects requires understanding how their parts connect and move. We introduce ViewStruct, a framework for jointly predicting connectivity and articulation in objects and scenes. Given an RGB image with aligned part segmentation and depth, optionally supplemented by images or video, ViewStruct predicts rigid-body groupings, attachments, and joint parameters. Its view-grounded representation links predictions to observed regions and expresses joint geometry in a gravity-aligned, view-relative frame, without requiring category-specific canonical orientations. A pretrained vision--language model is adapted to predict structure in seconds, without requiring observed articulation motion or per-instance optimisation. Experiments demonstrate improved structural prediction over evaluated baselines and transfer to unseen categories, asset sources, and real-world human-interaction videos, despite task-specific training exclusively on synthetic data. Simulated manipulation experiments further demonstrate the utility of predicted joints for generating robot motion trajectories.

</details>


### [163] [X-Ragent: Tool-Augmented Reinforcement Learning for Evidence-Grounded Chest X-Ray Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16811)
*Yi Wang,Zhenlong Yuan,Yuchan Guo,Maolei Huang,Zijian Song,Dapeng Zhang,Rongbin Tan,Jiyuan Wang,Zhiyuan Wang,Fangfang Lin,Shuhan Song,Huawei Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: X-Ragent：面向胸片推理的工具增强强化学习后训练框架，解耦证据寻求与有效性门控的定量测量，在三个CXR基准上达到最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胸片解读需结合视觉发现与任务相关证据，而非单纯视觉识别。现有工具增强医疗智能体缺乏显式机制判断何时需要辅助证据，也缺乏防止无效定量证据进入诊断推理的门控机制。

Method: 提出X-Ragent后训练框架：解耦"学习式辅助证据寻求"与"有效性门控的定量测量获取"。构建Rad-CoT（7.7K样本），将因果有序的工具增强轨迹与报告式诊断推理结合，用于Qwen3-VL-8B的智能体SFT初始化；再用GRPO优化，奖励函数采用准确率门控，仅对正确轨迹施加测量一致性与工具成本的过程级项。

Result: 在三个CXR基准上取得最强结果，在完全留出的ChestAgentBench上提升8.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过选择性寻求证据并仅接纳有效测量，X-Ragent支持更忠实、更具证据依据的胸片推理。

Abstract: Chest X-ray (CXR) interpretation often requires integrating visual findings with task-specific evidence rather than relying on direct visual recognition alone. While tool-augmented medical agents can supply such evidence via external modules, they often lack explicit mechanisms for learning when auxiliary evidence is needed and for preventing inapplicable quantitative evidence from entering diagnostic reasoning. To address this, we propose X-Ragent, a post-training framework for tool-augmented reinforcement learning that decouples learned auxiliary-evidence seeking from validity-gated quantitative measurement acquisition for evidence-grounded CXR reasoning. We construct Rad-CoT, a 7.7K-sample corpus combining causally ordered tool-augmented trajectories with report-style diagnostic reasoning, and use it to initialize Qwen3-VL-8B via agentic supervised fine-tuning (SFT). We then optimize the policy with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) using an accuracy-gated reward that applies process-level terms for measurement consistency and auxiliary-tool cost only to correct trajectories, preventing plausible process behavior from compensating for an incorrect diagnosis. Across three CXR benchmarks, X-Ragent achieves the strongest results, including an 8.6-point improvement on the fully held-out ChestAgentBench. By seeking evidence selectively and admitting only valid measurements, X-Ragent supports more faithful and evidence-grounded CXR reasoning.

</details>


### [164] [Carve VFM: Carving Specialists out of Generalists, Automatic Task-Specific Compression of Vision Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16813)
*Lotfi Abdelkrim Mecharbat,Abdelrahman Elsayed,Ibrahim Almakky,Martin Takáč,Mohammad Yaqub*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Carve VFM，通过联合决定子网络选择与恢复，从视觉基础模型中直接"雕刻"出紧凑密集编码器，无需重训练即可高效压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉基础模型（VFM）推理成本高，难以部署。微调不缩小模型，蒸馏需昂贵教师引导训练，结构化剪枝虽直接但压缩激进时现有方法因将子网络选择与恢复视为独立轴而失效：跨层容量需按任务敏感性分配而非均匀，层内移除的影响还取决于剩余权重能否恢复丢失贡献。

Method: Carve VFM 在给定 VFM、校准集和参数预算下：(i) 跨层搜索任务特定的容量分配；(ii) 用自适应、恢复感知的适应度准则指导搜索；(iii) 通过迭代闭式低秩校正重建被移除的行为并合并进存活权重。

Result: 在 DINOv3 与 MetaCLIP-2 的四个模型、十二个基准上，Carve VFM 在八个骨干-预算配置中的七个达到所评估密集结构化剪枝方法中的最高平均精度；进一步微调后超越最强边缘模型（88.1% vs. 85.8%），并降低计算与内存，GPU 吞吐量提升 1.3-2.0 倍。

Conclusion: 所需紧凑模型本就蕴含于 VFM 之中，只需将其"雕刻"出来；该方法可在单 GPU 上几分钟内生成密集编码器而无需骨干重训练。

Abstract: Vision foundation models (VFMs) provide transferable visual representations, but their inference cost limits deployment in many settings. Fine-tuning specializes a VFM without shrinking it; distillation shrinks it, but through expensive teacher-guided training. Structured pruning offers a more direct route by extracting a compact subnetwork from the VFM. However, as compression gets aggressive, existing methods fail because they treat subnetwork selection and recovery as separate axes. Across layers, capacity must follow task sensitivity rather than spread uniformly. Within a layer, a removal cannot be judged by importance alone; its impact also depends on how well the lost contribution can be recovered from what remains. Selection and recovery must therefore be decided jointly. We introduce Carve VFM, which resolves this interdependence. Given a VFM, a calibration set, and a parameter budget, it (i) searches for task-specific capacity allocations across layers; (ii) guides the search via an adaptive recovery-aware fitness criterion; and (iii) reconstructs removed behavior through iterative closed-form low-rank corrections merged into the surviving weights. The result is a dense encoder produced in minutes on a single GPU without backbone retraining. Across twelve benchmarks and four models from DINOv3 and MetaCLIP-2, Carve VFM achieves the highest reported mean accuracy in seven of eight backbone-budget configurations among the evaluated dense structured-pruning methods. Further fine-tuning surpasses the strongest evaluated edge model (88.1% vs. 85.8%), and carved models cut compute and memory while delivering 1.3-2.0x higher GPU throughput than the uncompressed one. These results show that the compact model you need is inside the VFM; it only has to be carved out.

</details>


### [165] [Growing to See: Efficient Pretraining of Self-Supervised Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16817)
*Amir Mohammad Karimi Mamaghan,Ferdinand Kapl,Andrea Dittadi,Karl Henrik Johansson,Kaitlin Maile,Johannes von Oswald,Stefan Bauer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究自监督视觉Transformer中的渐进式深度增长，发现其提升下游性能并减少训练FLOPs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 渐进式深度增长在LLM中重新受到关注，但其如何影响自监督视觉Transformer的表示质量和计算模式尚不清楚。

Method: 在MAE和DINOv3两种自监督家族中系统研究渐进式深度增长，分析其对下游性能、中间表示和内部计算的影响。

Result: 在全局和密集任务上，增长提升下游性能，训练FLOPs减少13.9–24.8%，ImageNet线性探测精度最高提升3.36个百分点；重塑内部计算模式，MAE出现全局与局部注意力的反复交替，DINOv3出现更早的局部到全局注意力进程。

Conclusion: 渐进式深度增长是一种有效且高效的视觉预训练策略，能用更少计算提升全局与密集视觉任务性能，并加深了对视觉计算的理解。

Abstract: Progressive depth growth builds large networks by starting with shallow models and inserting layers during training. Recent advances in large language models have renewed interest in this longstanding approach, demonstrating benefits for training efficiency and reasoning alongside changes in internal computations. However, how growing affects representation quality and computational patterns in self-supervised vision transformers remains unclear. In this work, we systematically investigate progressive depth growth in two popular self-supervised families, namely masked autoencoders (MAE) and DINOv3, and analyze how growing affects downstream performance, intermediate representations, and internal computations. Across global and dense tasks, we show that growing improves downstream performance while reducing training FLOPs by 13.9--24.8%, with ImageNet linear-probe accuracy gains of up to 3.36 percentage points. Growing also improves downstream performance at intermediate layers and reshapes internal computational patterns, introducing recurring alternations between global and localized attention in MAE and earlier local-to-global attention progression in self-distilled DINOv3. Together, these findings establish progressive depth growth as an effective and efficient pretraining strategy for improving downstream performance across global and dense visual tasks with less computation, and advance our understanding of how growing shapes visual computation.

</details>


### [166] [A Multi-Task Deep Learning Framework for Pose-Invariant Face Recognition and Facial Landmark Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16849)
*Ali Ashkeyev,Zukhra Abdiakhmetova*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多任务深度学习框架，在统一架构中联合进行姿态不变人脸识别与五点关键点定位，基于CPLFW改造的多任务数据集，验证准确率81.58%，关键点MSE为96.38。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大姿态变化会导致面部外观改变和判别性特征可见度下降，使人脸识别性能下降。作者希望通过联合身份识别与面部结构学习，缓解跨姿态条件下的识别退化问题。

Method: 将原始CPLFW数据集改造为多任务数据集，为图像关联身份标签和五点关键点标注。框架包含两个共享同一面部输入的任务分支：基于ResNet50的身份识别分支，以及基于EfficientNetV2B0并引入空间注意力的关键点定位分支。两任务通过加权多任务损失联合优化，学习语义与空间互补的人脸表征。

Result: 在改造后的CPLFW多任务数据集上，验证集识别准确率达81.58%，关键点定位均方误差为96.38。

Conclusion: 在单一优化框架中结合身份分类与关键点回归，是学习跨姿态条件下鲁棒人脸表征的有效方法；多任务学习与注意力特征精炼在无约束人脸分析中具有潜力。

Abstract: Face recognition systems often experience performance degradation under large pose variations due to changes in facial appearance and visibility of discriminative features. This study presents a multi-task deep learning framework that jointly addresses pose-invariant face recognition and facial landmark localization within a unified architecture.
  To enable simultaneous optimization of identity recognition and facial structure learning, the original Cross-Pose Labeled Faces in the Wild (CPLFW) dataset was adapted into a multi-task learning dataset by associating facial images with identity labels and five-point landmark annotations. The proposed framework consists of two task-specific branches operating on a common facial input: a ResNet50-based branch for identity recognition and an EfficientNetV2B0-based branch with spatial attention for facial landmark localization. The two objectives are jointly optimized using a weighted multi-task loss, allowing the model to learn complementary semantic and spatial facial representations.
  The proposed framework was evaluated on the prepared CPLFW-based multi-task dataset. Unlike the original CPLFW benchmark, which focuses primarily on identity recognition, the proposed dataset adaptation incorporates five-point facial landmark annotations to enable joint recognition and localization learning. Experimental results demonstrate that the model achieves a validation recognition accuracy of 81.58% and a facial landmark localization mean squared error of 96.38. The results indicate that combining identity classification and landmark regression within a single optimization framework provides an effective approach for learning robust facial representations under cross-pose conditions
  The proposed method demonstrates the potential of multi-task learning and attention-based feature refinement for unconstrained facial analysis.

</details>


### [167] [Towards Cost-Efficient Video Dataset Construction with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16857)
*Mohammed Shabot,Jonathan Adams,Jan Van den Stock,Joost Vennekens*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出在下载候选视频前用大语言模型(LLM)基于元数据预筛选视频的过滤步骤，以降低视频分类数据集构建中的存储、时间和标注成本，并与关键词启发式基线及人工标注进行对比评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频数据集构建方法需要先下载候选视频再做相关性判断，导致存储、时间和标注成本高昂，亟需一种在下载前进行预筛选以节省成本的手段。

Method: 利用LLM基于视频元数据进行预筛选；以四名独立标注者的人工多数投票为参考标准，通过Gwet's AC1评估标注一致性；与两种关键词启发式无LLM基线对比；采用Leave-One-Rater-Out(LOO)和Add-One-Rater-In(AORI)机制评估LLM作为虚拟标注者的表现；并进行成本对比。

Result: LLM预筛选与严格的人工多数投票进行了对比评估，并与两种关键词启发式基线进行性能比较；通过LOO和AORI验证了LLM作为虚拟标注者的表现，同时报告了成本对比结果。

Conclusion: LLM可基于元数据在下载前有效预筛选视频，作为虚拟标注者具备可行性，能显著节省数据集构建成本；实现已开源以保证可复现性。

Abstract: Research on video classification models has witnessed significant growth recently, accompanied by a significant increase in the demand for datasets to train such models. Existing video dataset curation approaches share a common bottleneck: candidate videos must be downloaded before any relevance judgment is made, which results in storage, time and annotation costs. In this work, we present a filtering step, using Large Language Models (LLMs) to pre-screen videos based on their metadata, before actually downloading these videos, thereby saving on these costs. We evaluate the LLMs against a strict human majority vote derived from four independent annotators and validate the reliability of the annotations through inter-annotator agreement analysis (Gwet's AC1). We compare the LLM pre-filter against two no-LLM baselines, namely two keyword heuristics. Via a Leave-One-Rater-Out (LOO) mechanism and an Add-One-Rater-In (AORI), we evaluate the performance of the LLMs as virtual annotators. Finally, we report cost comparisons relative to existing approaches. To ensure reproducability, we made our implementation available on github.

</details>


### [168] [Vision Models Capture Complementary Representations in Children That Converge in Adults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16860)
*Domenic Bersch,Timothy Schaumlöffel,Michela Proietti,Antonia Franaszek-Traczewska,Hannah Elisabeth Zwad,Siying Xie,Marlena Baldauf,Teresa Sylvester,Christina Maria Schätz,Bert Turtleton,Moritz Köster,Stefanie Höhl,Radoslaw Cichy,Gemma Roig*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究用时间分辨表征相似性分析比较了CLIP（图文对齐）与纯视觉模型在8岁、12岁儿童及成人EEG上的大脑对齐，发现CLIP在儿童期即显著对齐、纯视觉模型随发育增强，两者捕获互补信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图文对齐模型与纯视觉模型同大脑表征的对齐关系如何随发育变化尚不清楚。研究旨在揭示这两类模型在视觉表征发展中的相对贡献及时间进程。

Method: 对8岁、12岁儿童与成人的EEG数据应用时间分辨表征相似性分析，比较CLIP与纯视觉模型的模型-大脑对齐，并进行残差分析及语义结构（类别、生命性）与低层图像属性的对比。

Result: CLIP在儿童期即显示显著的模型-大脑对齐；纯视觉模型在发育后期对齐更强。残差分析显示互补结构，从8岁起CLIP在与架构匹配的纯视觉模型之外仍保持对齐。模型-大脑对齐很大程度反映语义结构，EEG对类别与生命性的表征随成年增强，而与低层图像属性的对齐在各年龄段均较弱。

Conclusion: 图文对齐模型与纯视觉模型捕获发展中的视觉表征的互补方面，可为旨在随时间学习与适应的AI系统提供有用约束。

Abstract: How the relationship between image-text aligned and vision-only models changes across development remains unknown. We applied time-resolved representational similarity analysis to EEG data from 8-year-olds, 12-year-olds, and adults. CLIP showed significant model-brain alignment already in children, whereas vision-only models aligned more strongly later in development. Residual analyses revealed complementary structure, with CLIP retaining alignment beyond an architecture-matched vision-only model from age 8. Much of the model-brain alignment reflected semantic structure, with EEG representations of category and animacy strengthening toward adulthood, while alignment with low-level image properties remained weak across age. Together, these findings suggest that image-text aligned and vision-only models capture complementary aspects of developing visual representations and may provide useful constraints for AI systems that aim to learn and adapt over time.

</details>


### [169] [From Volpiano to Pixels: 30,000 Lines of OMR Ground Truth for Medieval Chant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16862)
*Robert Klugseder*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文利用Cantus Database的Volpiano转写，构建了中世纪圣咏手稿图像-符号配对数据集，并训练OMR模型识别乐谱符号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中世纪圣咏的光学音乐识别（OMR）缺少跨记谱体系的图像-符号训练数据，阻碍模型开发。

Method: 构建流水线：定位谱表、推断谱号并将已知音高映射到谱表位置；用六个检测器系列处理不同版面与记谱；启发式失败处用两个小型U-Net学习谱线和谱号字母；从零训练CRNN。

Result: 生成30,567个图像-目标对（11次导出、10个来源、339个诗节片段），目标序列含1,952,143个token。CRNN在3,086测试行上符号错误率9.6%，旧模型为45.3%；三次留一法在排除手稿上错误率21.5%-31.0%；模型辅助对齐额外恢复2,686行。

Conclusion: 该数据集与流水线可支持跨记谱体系的圣咏OMR；但抽样审核错误定义不一，尚缺统一语料审计，跨手稿泛化仍是挑战。

Abstract: Optical music recognition (OMR) for medieval chant lacks training data linking manuscript images to musical symbols across notational families. Using existing Volpiano transcriptions from the Cantus Database, we created 30,567 image-target pairs from ten sources in eleven exports, including 339 verse fragments. Their target sequences contain 1,952,143 tokens. The pipeline locates staff systems, infers clefs and maps known pitches to staff positions. Six detector families handle the different layouts and notations. Where heuristics failed, two small U-Nets learned staff lines and clef letters from manual annotations. Reviews of sampled export lines found zero to four pitch or clef errors, but used different error definitions and do not provide a uniform audit of the corpus. A CRNN trained from scratch achieved a symbol error rate of 9.6 % on 3,086 test lines. The earlier model, trained mainly on Austrian sources of the 15th and 16th centuries, scored 45.3 %. In three separate training runs, each excluding one manuscript, error rates on the excluded manuscript were 21.5-31.0 %. Model-assisted alignment recovered a further 2,686 lines.

</details>


### [170] [EviStress: Evidence Stress Learning for Generalizable Face Forgery Detection with Textual Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16879)
*Jiuyao Jing,Xuanyu Yi,Yu Zheng,Chunlei Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EviStress两阶段框架，通过针对性扰动高依赖视觉证据和指令微调，实现可泛化的人脸伪造检测及文本解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web环境下的伪造媒体来自不断演变的生成流程并经过多样处理，检测器需在未见过的伪造类型和视觉变化下保持可靠。作者观察到LoRA适配的CLIP检测器存在patch级决策依赖不均的问题，少数视觉patch承载了过多类别相关证据，导致鲁棒性不足。

Method: 两阶段框架。第一阶段ETPP：从全局、关系和语义线索估计patch依赖，分配正相关扰动概率，在训练中压力测试高依赖视觉证据；并用DVP约束干净与扰动表示间的残差以保留鉴别信息。第二阶段IEIT：将适配的CLIP编码器作为LLaVA视觉塔，进行参数高效多模态指令微调，直接从伪造敏感表示进行真实性预测和文本解释，无需外部检测器。

Result: 实验表明跨数据集、跨方法泛化能力和损坏鲁棒性均有提升，多模态模型可同时输出真实性预测和人类可读的解释。

Conclusion: EviStress通过针对性压力测试高依赖证据并迁移至多模态大模型，有效提升了人脸伪造检测的泛化性和鲁棒性，并兼具可解释性。

Abstract: The rapid spread of face forgeries on the Web challenges the credibility of visual content. In practical Web environments, manipulated media originate from evolving generation pipelines and undergo diverse processing during dissemination, making reliable authenticity assessment under unseen manipulations and visual changes a critical challenge. We observe that a standard LoRA-adapted CLIP detector exhibits uneven patch-level decision dependence, concen trating a disproportionate share of class-specific relevance on a small fraction of visual patches. To improve robustness to variations in such highly dependent evidence, we propose EviStress, a two-stage framework for generalizable face forgery detection with textual explanations. In the first stage, Evidence-Targeted Patch Perturbation (ETPP) estimates patch dependence from global, relational, and semantic cues and assigns positively correlated perturbation probabilities, explicitly stress-testing highly dependent visual evidence during training. We further introduce Discriminative Variation Preservation (DVP), which constrains the residual between clean and perturbed representations to retain authenticity discriminative information. In the second stage, Intrinsic Evidence Instruction Tuning (IEIT) transfers the adapted CLIP encoder into LLaVA as its vision tower and performs parameter-efficient multimodal instruction tuning, enabling authenticity prediction and textual explanation directly from the learned forgery-sensitive representation without an external detector. Experiments demonstrate improved cross-dataset and cross-method generalization and corruption robustness, with the multimodal model providing authenticity predictions and human-readable explanations.

</details>


### [171] [Barycentric Guidance: Turning Foundation Image Editors into Continuous Affective Controllers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16892)
*Harvey Mannering,Zhiwu Huang,Adam Prügel-Bennett*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Barycentric Guidance，一种免训练的框架，将预训练基础图像编辑器转化为VA空间上的连续情感控制器，无需微调、额外数据或模型修改，在精度、覆盖度和多样性上显著优于基线，并可跨领域泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于VA的图像编辑器多为训练式且领域特定，仅局限于训练中见过的VA区域，跨数据集与领域时表现脆弱；而先进的基础图像编辑器虽能产生高质量编辑，却缺乏连续情感控制能力，因文本编码器中的非线性情感表示使提示插值校准不良，导致准确且连续的VA控制不可靠。

Method: Barycentric Guidance将任意目标VA点映射为基于单纯形的引导权重，从而利用预训练基础图像编辑器的能力实现VA可控性，涵盖三方面：准确命中VA状态、广泛覆盖VA平面、连续控制。整个过程免训练，不需微调、额外数据或模型改动。

Result: 在三个人脸数据集上，相比最强基线，VA目标精度提升25-29%，VA平面覆盖扩大28-50%，表情多样性增加29-49%。

Conclusion: 该免训练方法利用基础模型内在的多功能性，突破了以往领域特定方法的局限，可在单一框架内泛化到动物、艺术作品和复杂场景，而不仅限于人脸。

Abstract: Emotions are expressed on a continuum and are naturally described in the valence-arousal (VA) space. Existing VA-based image editors are typically training-based and domain-specific, limiting them to VA regions seen during training and making them brittle across datasets and domains. Meanwhile, despite producing high-quality edits, state-of-the-art foundation image editors are not continuous affective controllers: non-linear emotion representations in text encoders makes prompt interpolation poorly calibrated, so accurate and continuous VA control remains unreliable. To fill this gap, we introduce Barycentric Guidance, a training-free framework that turns pretrained foundation image editors into continuous affective controllers over VA, requiring no finetuning, extra data, or model changes. Our method maps any target VA point to simplex-based guidance weights, enabling VA controllability in three aspects: (i) accurate targeting of VA states, (ii) broad coverage across the VA plane, and (iii) continuous control. Across three face datasets, we improve VA target accuracy by 25-29%, expand VA-plane coverage by 28-50%, and increase expression diversity by 29-49%, over the strongest baseline. Unlike prior domain-specific methods, our training-free approach exploits the foundation model's inherent versatility, enabling generalization beyond human faces to animals, artworks, and complex scenes within a single framework. Code is available at https://github.com/harveymannering/BarycentricGuidance.

</details>


### [172] [Mining Cross-Modal Perception Gaps in Autonomous Driving: When Camera and LiDAR Disagree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16906)
*Hongsen Cao,Mona Jaber,Shanxin Yuan,Ahmed Sayed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文挖掘40万目标的多模态检测数据库(SFFA分析)，揭示现有3D融合设计中三条假设不成立（模态偏好按目标类型、相机主导区间在10-30m、失效因素非加性、模态支配随方向变化），据此构建"模态可靠性图"注入四种预训练检测器，在微调<4%参数、2-3 GPU时内提升最高1.3 mAP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态3D检测默认相机与LiDAR大致互补，但实际每个位置信任哪种模态由学习权重决定，这些权重既无法刻画也无法审计。作者希望把"可靠性"从权重中提取出来，作为一个部署前可检验、可作为安全依据的量。

Method: 1) 对每个真值目标标注其被哪些模态检出，构建nuScenes/KITTI/Waymo共0.4M目标数据库；2) 用Spatially Stratified Feature Attribution (SSFA)，按距离分区对Shapley归因做条件化，观察被全局平均掩盖的结构；3) 将挖掘出的结构编译为Modality Reliability Map，通过轻量注意力层注入四种预训练融合检测器。

Result: 发现三条假设不成立：模态偏好边界跟随目标本身（交通锥仅靠相机可见的概率几乎为卡车六倍，16.8% vs 2.9%，与距离无关）；纯相机检测在10-30m（通常让给LiDAR的区间）达到峰值并向两侧衰减；失效驱动因素非加性，距离-密度交互项单独超过视角主效应；模态支配方向非平稳，车后目标比车前更依赖LiDAR。注入可靠性图后，四种检测器mAP最高提升1.3，微调参数<4%，耗时2-3 GPU小时。

Conclusion: 研究将可靠性从模型权重中解耦，使其成为部署前可审查的量，为多模态感知的安全度量提供了基础。

Abstract: Multi-modal fusion for 3D object detection assumes that cameras and LiDAR are broadly complementary. In practice, how much each modality is trusted at a given location is decided by learned weights that are neither characterized nor auditable. We label every ground-truth object by which modalities detected it, then mine the resulting 0.4M-object database across nuScenes, KITTI, and Waymo. Because the patterns shift with distance from the ego vehicle, we read the mined model through Spatially Stratified Feature Attribution (SSFA), which conditions Shapley attribution on distance zones and exposes structure that global attribution averages away. Three assumptions built into the current fusion design do not hold. The modality-preference boundary tracks the object itself: a traffic cone is nearly six times as likely as a truck to be seen by the camera alone (16.8% vs. 2.9%), regardless of range. Camera-only detections peak at 10-30 m, inside the band usually ceded to LiDAR, and fall away on either side. The drivers of failure are not additive either: the distance - density interaction alone outweighs the entire main effect of viewing direction. We further find that modality dominance is directionally non-stationary, with objects behind the vehicle being markedly more LiDAR-dominant than those in front. We compile the mined structure into a Modality Reliability Map and inject it into four pretrained fusion detectors through a lightweight attention layer. This improves performance by up to 1.3 mean Average Precision (mAP) while fine-tuning under 4% of their parameters in 2-3 GPU-hours. Our work lifts reliability out of the weights and treats it as a quantity that can be examined before deployment. That is what a safety measure for multi-modal perception must rest on.

</details>


### [173] [ConDenseDrive: Knowledge-Dense, Token-Sparse Visual Encoder for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16908)
*Shengzhen Li,Yongkang Li,Hao Gao,Chenxi Wu,Yajing Sun,Lu Hou,Wenyu Liu,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ConDenseDrive，一种知识密集且token稀疏的视觉编码器，通过三阶段框架提升端到端自动驾驶的表示质量与推理效率，在NAVSIM和HUGSIM上取得领先结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶系统通常用通用图像预训练初始化视觉编码器，并密集处理所有视角与帧的patch token，导致表示质量受限且推理效率低下，难以在严格延迟约束下兼顾场景理解与轨迹规划。

Method: 三阶段框架：1）从三个视觉基础模型蒸馏视觉、语义、几何知识，使编码器"知识密集"；2）将蒸馏编码器适配到多帧端到端驾驶，并把时空上下文整合进register token用于规划；3）用register引导的token压缩使编码器"token稀疏"，减少冗余patch计算。

Result: 实现2.0倍加速的同时提升规划性能；NAVSIM v1达94.5 PDMS，NAVSIM v2达90.8 EPDMS，HUGSIM零样本评测达37.4 HD-Score。

Conclusion: 实验与定性分析表明，视觉编码器设计是实现可靠且高效端到端自动驾驶的有效途径。

Abstract: End-to-end driving requires rich spatiotemporal representations to support scene understanding and trajectory planning under strict latency constraints. However, most systems initialize their visual encoders from generic image pretraining and densely process all patch tokens for each camera view and input frame, limiting both representation quality and inference efficiency. To address these challenges, we present ConDenseDrive, a knowledge-dense and token-sparse visual encoder for end-to-end autonomous driving. Our three-stage framework first makes the encoder knowledge-dense by distilling complementary visual, semantic, and geometric knowledge from three vision foundation models. It then adapts the distilled encoder to multi-frame end-to-end driving and integrates spatiotemporal context into register tokens for planning. Finally, it makes the encoder token-sparse by applying register-guided token compression, reducing redundant patch computation and yielding a 2.0$\times$ speedup while improving planning performance. ConDenseDrive achieves 94.5 PDMS on NAVSIM v1 and 90.8 EPDMS on NAVSIM v2, and reaches 37.4 HD-Score in zero-shot evaluation on HUGSIM. Extensive experiments and qualitative analyses demonstrate that visual encoder design provides an effective route to reliable and efficient end-to-end driving.

</details>


### [174] [FOCUS: A Subspecialty Foundation Model for Intraoperative Central Nervous System Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16914)
*Xing Liu,Yifan Guo,Hongtai Lv,Lei Lou,Yuyi Tan,Jiang Du,Xijia Zhang,Ming Zhang,Chao Pang,Minglei Li,Wei Liu,Xiaobo Zhu,Song Zhang,Rong Yu,Hanlin Shu,Wenzhuo Xu,Tao Jiang,Cheng Lu,Jie Tian,Qing Chang,Zhenyu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FOCUS是一个基于27,930张冰冻切片的CNS专科病理基础模型，在诊断和生物标志物预测任务中表现领先，辅助初级病理医生接近或超越高级医生水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 术中冰冻切片诊断CNS病变对手术决策至关重要，但受伪影、诊断复杂性和时间限制的挑战。现有病理基础模型对CNS冰冻组织暴露有限，缺乏针对该场景的专用模型。

Method: 构建FOCUS基础模型，在27,930张富集CNS组织的冰冻切片上预训练；开展11项内部诊断任务和12项生物标志物预测任务，并将10项诊断任务组织为WHO对齐的诊断层级；单独评估淋巴瘤检测能力，并由6位神经病理医生进行读者评估。

Result: 诊断与生物标志物预测任务平均AUC分别为0.969和0.877，在时间队列和外部队列中保持领先。读者评估中，FOCUS辅助使初级读者接近或超越未辅助高级读者；初级读者淋巴瘤敏感度从81.6%提升至94.7%。

Conclusion: FOCUS可提升诊断准确性和颗粒度，辅助淋巴瘤相关切除决策并提供临时分子信息，展现出为术中CNS病理提供稳健、可临床落地支持的潜力。

Abstract: Intraoperative frozen-section diagnosis of central nervous system (CNS) lesions guides surgical decisions but is challenged by artefacts, diagnostic complexity and time constraints. Most pathology foundation models have limited exposure to CNS frozen tissue. Here we present FOCUS, a subspecialty foundation model pretrained on 27,930 frozen-sections enriched for CNS tissue. FOCUS led 11 internal diagnostic and 12 biomarker-prediction tasks, with mean AUCs of 0.969 and 0.877, respectively, retaining leading performance in temporal and external cohorts.
