To provide more comprehensive diagnostic information, we further organized ten diagnostic tasks into a WHO-aligned diagnostic hierarchy and separately evaluated FOCUS for lymphoma detection, a critical task for intraoperative surgical decision-making. In reader assessments by six neuropathologists, FOCUS assistance enabled junior readers to approach or exceed unassisted senior readers in hierarchical diagnosis and raised junior readers' lymphoma sensitivity from 81.6% to 94.7%. FOCUS could improve diagnostic accuracy and granularity, inform lymphoma-related resection decisions and provide provisional molecular information. Such capabilities highlight the potential of FOCUS to provide robust, clinically actionable support for intraoperative CNS pathology.

</details>


### [175] [UniMetric: Universal Metric Scale Recovery for Visual Geometry Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16945)
*Lei Yang,Yuner Zhang,Ziyang Liu,Ziying Song,Kai WU,Yiyang Gao,Jiangtao Su,Yiming Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 UniMetric，一种无需训练、无需额外传感器的通用度量尺度恢复框架，利用熟悉物体的尺寸先验联合约束几何基础模型重建的共享尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态基础模型推动具身推理发展，但用互联网视频做具身预训练需可靠的物理尺度标注；现有视觉几何基础模型虽能恢复场景结构和相机运动，却缺乏可靠的度量尺度。

Method: 核心洞察是熟悉物体的不确定尺寸先验可联合约束重建的共享尺度。方法分三步：Metric Prior Acquisition 用视觉语言模型获取物体高度区间；Semantic-Geometric Alignment 沿支撑面法线测量各帧重建物体高度，将先验转为尺度区间；Global Scale Recovery 通过凸目标协调区间，重叠时得共同可行尺度，否则得唯一折中值。序列级尺度统一应用于重建点和相机平移。

Result: 真实 VLM 先验的控制实验表明，组合观测可在精确相对物体高度下提升尺度精度。提出 MetricBench，涵盖十二个具身、驾驶和路侧子集及传感器真值。在九种几何基础模型上，UniMetric 将平均深度 Abs Rel、重建 Chamfer 距离、轨迹误差分别降低 54.2%、83.8%、65.6%，在套件层面全面改进所有模型的三个任务。

Conclusion: UniMetric 为几何基础模型提供通用、免训练、无需额外传感器或任务特定监督的度量尺度恢复方案，并开源代码与 MetricBench 数据。

Abstract: Recent advances in multimodal foundation models, exemplified by GPT-6 Astra, raise the prospect of general-purpose embodied reasoning and planning. Using Internet videos for embodied pretraining calls for reliable physical-scale annotations. Yet visual geometry foundation models often recover scene structure and camera motion without reliable metric scale. We show that uncertain size priors of familiar objects can jointly constrain a reconstruction's shared scale. Building on this insight, we present UniMetric, a universal and training-free metric scale recovery framework for geometry foundation models, without additional sensors or task-specific metric supervision. Specifically, Metric Prior Acquisition elicits object height intervals from a vision-language model. Semantic-Geometric Alignment measures reconstructed object heights along the estimated support-surface normal in each frame and converts the priors into scale intervals. Global Scale Recovery reconciles these intervals through a convex objective, yielding a jointly feasible scale when they overlap and a unique compromise otherwise. The estimated sequence-level scale is applied uniformly to reconstructed points and camera translations. Controlled experiments with real VLM priors show that combining observations improves scale accuracy with exact relative object heights. To evaluate metric accuracy on GFM reconstructions without ground-truth scale alignment, we introduce MetricBench, covering twelve embodied, driving, and roadside subsets with sensor-derived ground truth. Across nine geometry foundation models, UniMetric reduces mean depth Abs Rel, reconstruction Chamfer distance, and trajectory error by 54.2%, 83.8%, and 65.6%, respectively, improving every model on all three tasks at the suite level. We release the UniMetric code and MetricBench data.

</details>


### [176] [DDVR-Net: Degradation-Aware Decoupling and Visibility-Guided Restoration for Historical Document Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16956)
*Jiu Sha,Yu Weng,Mengxiao Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DDVR-Net，一种退化感知解耦与可见性引导的历史文档识别框架，并构建藏、蒙、维三语低资源基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实历史文档存在墨水侵蚀、结构损坏等严重退化，导致字符可见性混杂。传统OCR在证据受限时表现差，而上下文语言模型可能过度纠正视觉上清晰的字符。

Method: DDVR-Net在特征层面解耦字形结构与物理损坏，预测局部可见性以估计字符可靠性，并通过可见性引导门选择性激活上下文解码器。同时构建涵盖藏、蒙、维三种文字、含18.7万文档对的低资源多语言基准，结合大规模合成数据与渐进式训练及真实标注。

Result: 在三种语言和多种阅读顺序上，DDVR-Net持续优于现有方法，并提供有效框架与基准。

Conclusion: DDVR-Net为低资源历史文档识别提供了有效方案，缓解了合成到真实的差距，并公开代码与基准。

Abstract: Historical document analysis is crucial for cultural heritage preservation and low-resource language digitization. However, real historical documents often suffer from severe degradation, such as ink erosion and structural damage, causing mixed-visibility character sequences. Conventional OCR systems struggle in such evidence-limited scenarios, while contextual language models may over-correct visually clear characters. To address these challenges, we propose DDVR-Net, a degradation-aware decoupling and visibility-guided restoration framework for historical document recognition. DDVR-Net decouples intrinsic glyph structures from physical corruption at the feature level and predicts local visibility to estimate character reliability. A contextual decoder is selectively activated through a visibility-guided gate, restoring degraded characters while preserving clear visual recognition. Furthermore, we construct a low-resource multilingual benchmark covering Tibetan, Mongolian, and Uyghur scripts with 187000 document pairs, combining large-scale synthetic data and progressive training with real-world annotations to reduce the synthetic-to-real gap. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DDVR-Net consistently outperforms existing methods across three languages and diverse reading orders, providing an effective framework and benchmark for low-resource historical document recognition. Code and Benchmark: https://github.com/Shajiu/DDVR-Net

</details>


### [177] [Proactive Physical AI through Continuous Observation and Timely Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16967)
*Zhenhua Yang,Jiafeng Liang,Xingyu Zhou,Yi Jiang,Xiayu Cao,Zui Chen,Luqi Wang,Haiyang Sun,Yufei Xiang,Chang Li,Rongchuan Mu,Bowen Chi,Zihe Tong,Mingyu Li,Huijie Ma,Yiqiao Yang,Baoqi Ren,Zhiming Ma,Zhihao Zhu,Jiahao Ji,Zhuoyao Wang,Qianyu Wang,Xiangyu Liu,Wang Zixiang,Shunliang Zhang,Ming Liu,Xiaocheng Feng,Duyu Tang,Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出"主动物理AI(Proactive Physical AI)"这一新问题，即系统无需显式任务指令，持续观察物理世界、识别需介入的情境并适时行动。作者开发了端到端系统ProAgent，包含主动时机模型StreamingJev和响应模型，并通过自动化数据管线将视频转化为监督信号。实验表明其在介入时机和减少误触发上优于基线，真机实验验证了其识别并转化为物理行动的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Physical AI通常围绕显式指定的任务展开（给定目标或指令再感知、推理、行动），但现实场景中许多重要任务是在世界状态持续变化中自然涌现的，早于人类明确表达。因此需要一个能主动观察世界、判断何时介入并采取行动的互补框架。

Method: 提出ProAgent端到端系统，由两部分组成：(1) StreamingJev——Jev风格的主动时机模型，判断是否需要介入及何时介入；(2) Response Model——生成面向人的响应或调用设备能力进行物理执行。同时构建自动化数据管线，将视频转化为主动时机的监督信号以支持大规模学习。

Result: 在显式目标和固定角色两种设定下均展现强主动性能，相比源主干基线具有更准确的介入时机和更少的误触发；真机评估进一步证明ProAgent能识别涌现需求并转化为恰当的物理动作。

Conclusion: 主动物理AI是Physical AI的重要补充方向，ProAgent通过时机判断与响应生成的组合以及可扩展的数据管线，验证了系统主动识别需求并执行的可行性。

Abstract: Physical AI is commonly formulated around an explicitly specified task: given a goal or instruction, a system perceives, reasons, and acts. In many real-world settings, however, important tasks emerge from continuously changing world states before a person explicitly formulates them. We study this complementary problem as Proactive Physical AI, where an AI system continuously observes the physical world, identifies situations that warrant intervention, and initiates appropriate actions at the right time. We develop ProAgent, an end-to-end system built around two components: a Jev-style proactive timing model (dubbed StreamingJev) that determines whether and when intervention is needed, and a Response Model that generates responses for people or invokes available device capabilities for physical execution. To support learning at scale, we develop an automated data pipeline that converts videos into supervision for proactive timing. Experiments demonstrate strong proactive performance under both explicit objectives and standing roles, combining more accurate intervention timing with fewer false triggers than the source-backbone baseline. Real-robot evaluations further demonstrate ProAgent's ability to identify emerging needs and translate them into appropriate physical actions.

</details>


### [178] [The Value of Being Wrong: Self-Mined Visual In-Context Learning from Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16977)
*Shu Zhao,Tan Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SMILE提出利用VLM自身预测错误作为反事实演示，构建模型特定的困难负样本池，通过预测条件检索在测试时改进视觉上下文学习，兼顾闭集分类和开放式VQA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实选择方法虽能提升视觉ICL效果，但依赖属性提取、caption工程、图像匹配等多阶段流程，复杂且耗时。作者观察到模型自身预测错误天然编码了其失败模式，可作为反事实演示。

Method: 离线记录目标VLM自身错误，构建模型特定的困难负样本池；测试时将模型预测作为方向信号，在语义嵌入空间中检索当前查询最可能重演的历史错误，实现预测条件化的演示选择，无需任务特定设计。

Result: 在四个基准和四个VLM上，SMILE相比SOTA基线平均任务得分提升+3.09分，同时延迟降低5倍。

Conclusion: SMILE以更简单统一的方式改进视觉ICL，验证了利用模型自身错误作为反事实演示的有效性，兼具性能提升与效率优势。

Abstract: Visual in-context learning (ICL) enables vision-language models (VLMs) to adapt at test time via demonstration examples, but its effectiveness depends critically on the examples selected. Recent counterfactual selection methods improve ICL by constructing demonstrations that expose how individual attribute changes affect the answer. However, they require multi-stage pipelines involving attribute extraction, caption engineering, and composed image matching. We propose a simpler alternative grounded in a key observation: a model's own prediction errors are natural counterfactual demonstrations that directly encode its failure modes. Our method, SMILE (Self-Mined Visual In-Context Learning from Errors), builds a model-specific pool of hard negatives offline by recording the target VLM's own mistakes. At test time, the prediction serves as a direction signal that surfaces, in the semantic embedding space, the past mistakes most likely to recur for the current query. This prediction-conditioned identification provides a unified mechanism for both closed-set classification and open-ended visual question answering without task-specific design. Across four benchmarks and four VLMs, SMILE delivers an average task-score gain of +3.09 points over the SOTA baseline at 5x lower latency. Project page: https://tree-shu-zhao.github.io/SMILE/

</details>


### [179] [AUGUstus: Autonomous Driving VLA Model with Unified Generation and Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16985)
*Yuqi Ye,Caigao Jiang,Zhuonan Wang,Congyuan Pei,Chao Qu,Changhao Peng,Junhong Lin,Qize Yu,Zetian Song,Guangzhao Fu,Chongruo Wu,Shiyu Huang,Shuchang Zhou,Honggou Yang,Wei Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AUGUstus，将扩散式动作生成与自回归CoT理解统一于一个VLA模型，实现低延迟、高性能自动驾驶部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型存在两大局限：一是退化为视觉-动作模型，直接生成动作但未充分利用VLM理解，导致不安全、难解释；二是自回归方法顺序生成CoT和动作，理解充分但延迟高，难以部署。

Method: 将扩散式动作生成与自回归CoT理解统一在一个模型中，理解仅服务于生成；引入联合CoT监督以在共享表示中保留VLM空间理解；采用AUGUstus-mode先动作后CoT顺序，使CoT在训练中监督动作而推理仅需动作输出；提出AUGUstus-GRPO统一强化学习算法联合优化动作与CoT。

Result: NAVSIMv1达94.6 PDMS，NAVSIMv2达90.7 EPDMS，无scorer模块下的SOTA规划；nuScenes上平均L2误差0.27m、平均碰撞率0.05%，均为对比方法中最佳；在512块H800 GPU上训练约2天，内部1000km数据集评测并初步实车部署，实现12Hz运行。

Conclusion: AUGUstus在保持低延迟的同时充分利用VLM理解，兼顾安全性与性能，具备实际部署可行性，为自动驾驶VLA提供统一生成与理解的有效方案。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models show strong potential for end-to-end autonomous driving by leveraging world knowledge and understanding to excel in long-tail scenarios. However, current VLAs have two limitations: 1) Degeneration into vision-action (VA) models. For low latency, regression/flow-based methods directly generate actions but underuse VLM understanding, causing unsafe, unexplainable actions. 2) Autoregressive high latency. Autoregressive methods sequentially generate chain-of-thought (CoT) and action tokens, fully leveraging VLM understanding, but high latency hinders deployment. To enable low-latency deployment while using CoT to assist action generation, we propose AUGUstus, an Autonomous-driving VLA model with Unified Generation and Understanding. We unify diffusion-based action generation and autoregressive CoT understanding in one model, where understanding only serves generation. Joint CoT supervision preserves VLM spatial understanding in shared representation for safer, more accurate trajectories. AUGUstus-mode orders action generation before CoT, so CoT supervises reliable actions during training while inference needs only action output, enabling low latency. AUGUstus-GRPO, a unified GRPO algorithm, jointly optimizes action and CoT, improving action performance. Experiments show AUGUstus achieves 94.6 PDMS on NAVSIMv1 and 90.7 EPDMS on NAVSIMv2, SOTA planning without scorer module. On nuScenes, it achieves average L2 error 0.27 m and average collision rate 0.05%, best among compared methods. To validate AUGUstus practicality, we train on in-house data with 512 NVIDIA H800 GPUs for ~2 days, evaluate on an internal 1,000 km dataset, and preliminarily deploy on a real vehicle, achieving 12 Hz operation. Project page: https://augustus-ad.github.io

</details>


### [180] [Self-Supervised Learning of Semantically Consistent Visual Features via a Simple 3D Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16988)
*Sebastian Day,Oisin Mac Aodha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种自监督方法，通过对比学习和全局数据集级分布匹配学习拓扑复杂类别的特征，提升语义对应质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DINOv2、Stable Diffusion等视觉模型提取的特征虽能实现高质量语义对应，但存在局限，如无法区分左右对称或重复部件，导致对应估计不准确。

Method: 不依赖昂贵的实例级分析-合成流程，也不使用监督数据（关键点对或相机位姿）或限制性拓扑假设（如将物体建模为3D球体）。采用自监督对比学习与全局数据集级分布匹配学习类别的粗略形状与外观，并结合额外几何损失，复现真实图像视觉特征的全局与局部统计。

Result: 在多个具挑战性的物体类别上进行定量实验，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该自监督方法能以简单先验学习拓扑复杂类别的特征，克服现有特征在对称性与重复部件上的局限，改善语义对应。

Abstract: Visual features extracted from vision models like DINOv2 or Stable Diffusion enable high quality semantic correspondence between images. However, such features exhibit documented limitations that hinder accurate correspondence estimation such as the inability to disambiguate the left from right for categories exhibiting symmetries or repeated parts. Previous attempts to improve these features rely on using supervised data such as annotated keypoint pairs or camera pose information, or make restrictive topological assumptions, such as modelling the object of interest as a 3D sphere. We introduce a new self-supervised approach that allows us to learn features for topologically complex object categories using a simple prior. Instead of relying on an expensive instance-level analysis by synthesis pipeline, our approach makes use of contrastive learning and distribution matching at the global dataset-level to learn the coarse shape and appearance of a category. Combined with additional geometric losses, this enables us to replicate the global and local statistics of visual features which match those that are extracted from real images. Quantitative results are presented across several challenging object categories, demonstrating the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [181] [MaskSense: Confronting the Visual Exploration Trap in Masked Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17009)
*Yawen Shao,Jie Xiao,Kai Zhu,Yu Liu,Hongchen Luo,Xueyang Fu,Yang Cao,Wei Zhai,Zheng-Jun Zha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MaskSense是一个针对掩码生成模型(MGM)的强化学习框架，解决"视觉探索陷阱"问题，在采样和优化两阶段提升生成多样性与对齐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将GRPO直接用于MGMs存在根本冲突：MGM基于置信度的采样本质是剥削式解码，与GRPO所需的探索多样性相悖，导致"视觉探索陷阱"——牺牲被试多样性探索去贪婪解决低不确定性背景，使探索空间过早坍缩。

Method: 提出MaskSense框架，含两部分：1)语义锚定路由采样机制，利用语义先验保持高熵主体token探索，并通过探索-剥削路由增大组内奖励方差以获得更具区分度的优势估计；2)全局熵转移锚定策略，识别最关键的解码步骤并集中梯度更新。

Result: 在多个文生图基准上显著提升：GenEval准确率从54%升至86%，HPS从28.89升至36.39，达到SOTA水平。

Conclusion: MaskSense有效应对视觉探索陷阱，在采样与优化两阶段增强MGMs的RL对齐，取得显著且领先的性能提升。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has shown strong potential for aligning generative models with human intent, but adapting RL to masked generative models (MGMs) remains largely underexplored. Existing approaches that directly adapt Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to MGMs leave a foundational conflict unaddressed: MGMs' exploitative decoding nature is inherently at odds with the exploratory diversity that GRPO presupposes. In this work, we reveal a structural decoding bias termed the visual exploration trap: confidence-based sampling trades the exploration of diverse subject realizations for the greedy resolution of low-uncertainty background regions, causing premature collapse of the generative exploration space and starving GRPO of the rollout diversity it depends on. To this end, we propose MaskSense, a novel RL framework for MGMs that confronts the visual exploration trap at both the sampling and optimization stages. Specifically, we introduce a semantic anchored routing sampling mechanism that leverages semantic priors to preserve high-entropy subject token exploration while amplifying intra-group reward variance through exploratory-exploitative routing to yield more discriminative advantage estimates. Furthermore, we design a global entropy transition anchoring strategy to identify the most consequential decoding steps and concentrate gradient updates on them. Extensive experiments on multiple text-to-image benchmarks demonstrate that MaskSense substantially improves upon the base model, with GenEval accuracy improving from 54% to 86% and HPS from 28.89 to 36.39, achieving state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [182] [DIME: Scaling Facial Representation Learning via Differential Masked Autoencoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17015)
*Hao Yu,Haoyu Chen,Hui Wei,Yan Jiang,Nicu Sebe,Guoying Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DIME是一种差分掩码自编码器，通过对同一身份图像对进行差分重建并施加显式差分方向监督(EDDS)，解决细粒度人脸分析的扩展瓶颈，在2000万高清人脸图像上预训练后，多项人脸任务超越专业方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩大数据和模型容量的方法在视觉表征学习中取得进展，但传统预训练任务在细粒度人脸分析上收益递减。作者将其归因于扩展瓶颈：这些任务对每张图像的外观建模，其信号被身份、姿态和光照主导，因此同一身份内部细微的面部差异对训练信号贡献甚微。

Method: DIME基于一个简单观察：同一人的两张图像共享身份特定的外观，因此它们的差异能暴露面部变化。方法在身份匹配的图像对上重建两张被掩码的图像，通过显式差分方向监督(EDDS)目标，使重建差异的方向与真实差异对齐；同时通过source isolation使每个重建仅依赖自身图像。共享外观在差分中被抵消，且EDDS忽略幅度，因此细微面部变化与大的姿态或光照变化同等重要。仅需身份标签构建预训练对，无需密集标注，可学到具有部件级语义的密集人脸表征。

Result: 在由现有数据集整合的2000万高清人脸图像上预训练。AU检测性能随预训练数据从600万增至2000万持续提升，BP4D F1提升5.9分，而MAE在同一子集上仅提升0.5分。DIME在AU检测、AU强度估计、跨域防伪、关键点定位、头部姿态估计、人脸解析和人脸生成上均超越专业人脸方法。

Conclusion: DIME通过差分建模身份匹配图像对的差异，突破传统预训练任务在细粒度人脸分析上的扩展瓶颈，无需密集标注即可学习密集人脸表征，展现出优于专业方法的多任务泛化能力。

Abstract: Scaling up data and model capacity has driven substantial progress in visual representation learning. However, conventional pretext tasks exhibit diminishing returns on fine-grained facial analysis. We attribute this to a scaling bottleneck: these tasks model each image's appearance and are dominated by identity, pose, and illumination, so subtle within-identity facial differences contribute little to the training signal. We introduce DIME, a differential masked autoencoder for large-scale facial representation learning. DIME builds on a simple observation: two images of the same person share identity-specific appearance, so their difference exposes facial changes. DIME reconstructs both masked images in an identity-matched pair and, through an Explicit Differential Direction Supervision (EDDS) objective, aligns the direction of their reconstructed difference with the true difference, while source isolation makes each reconstruction depend only on its own image. Shared appearance cancels in this difference, and because EDDS ignores magnitude, subtle facial changes count as much as large pose or illumination changes. DIME uses identity labels only to form pretraining pairs, requires no dense annotations, and learns dense facial representations with emergent part-level semantics. We pretrain DIME on 20 million high-resolution facial images consolidated from existing face datasets. DIME's AU detection performance continues to improve as pretraining data scales from 6M to 20M images, with its BP4D F1 increasing by 5.9 points, while MAE gains only 0.5 points on the same subsets. DIME also outperforms specialized facial methods on AU detection, AU intensity estimation, cross-domain anti-spoofing, landmark localization, head pose estimation, face parsing, and face generation.

</details>


### [183] [PerturbBridge: Direct Phenotypic Supervision for Cellular Perturbation Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17066)
*Yucheng Yang,Xiaoxiao Sun,Huangqingbo Sun,Elaine Sui,Emma Lundberg,Serena Yeung-Levy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 PerturbBridge，一种随机 data-to-data 桥模型，在训练中直接监督扰动特异性表型，联合优化桥回归与可微生物响应损失，在多个显微基准上超越 CellFlux。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚拟细胞模型应能捕捉特定扰动诱发的表型，但基于图像的模型通常只按匹配真实图像分布来训练和评估，未显式优先考虑扰动特异的生物响应。近期方法通过强化学习后训练或推理时引导引入生物信号，引出核心问题：生物响应信息应在生成流程何处进入。

Method: 提出 PerturbBridge，一种随机 data-to-data 桥模型，在训练中对预测的扰动图像直接监督扰动特异表型。联合优化桥回归与可微生物响应损失，生物梯度通过冻结的图像编码器传播。并引入基于下游分类与检索的扰动响应评估。

Result: 在三个显微基准上，生物保真度、图像质量与采样效率均超越 CellFlux。BBBC021 上机制分类分别提升 5.66 与 7.5 个百分点（形态特征与学习视觉特征）。JUMP 与 AllenCell 上检索 Hit@10 与 Hit@1 分别提升 1.77 与 7.15 个百分点。BBBC021 与 JUMP 上以少 80% 的模型评估次数取得更低 FID。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在训练中直接监督扰动响应可提升表型保真度，同时实现高效的细胞图像生成。

Abstract: Virtual cell models should capture phenotypes induced by specific perturbations. However, image-based models are typically trained and evaluated to match real-image distributions, which alone does not explicitly prioritize perturbation-specific biological responses. Recent approaches add biological signals through reinforcement-learning post-training or inference-time steering, raising a central question: where should biological response information enter the generative pipeline? We introduce PerturbBridge, a stochastic data-to-data bridge model that directly supervises perturbation-specific phenotypes in predicted perturbed images during training. PerturbBridge jointly optimizes bridge regression and differentiable biological response losses, with biological gradients passing through a frozen image encoder. We further introduce perturbation-response evaluations based on downstream classification and retrieval. Across three microscopy benchmarks for perturbation profiling and subcellular imaging, PerturbBridge surpasses CellFlux in biological fidelity, image quality, and sampling efficiency. On BBBC021, mechanism-of-action classification improves by 5.66 and 7.5 percentage points with morphological and learned visual features, respectively. On JUMP and AllenCell, retrieval Hit@10 and Hit@1 improve by 1.77 and 7.15 percentage points, respectively. On BBBC021 and JUMP, PerturbBridge achieves lower FID with 80% fewer model evaluations than CellFlux. Overall, these results show that directly supervising perturbation responses during training improves phenotypic fidelity while enabling efficient cellular image generation. Code is available at https://github.com/yyc1215lll-creator/PerturbBridge.

</details>


### [184] [A unified three-branch deep learning framework for automated detection and counting of wildlife in aerial imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17067)
*Zeyu Xu,Tiejun Wang,Andrew Skidmore,Megan Claase,Harry Wells,Isla Duporge,Ramiro D. Crego,Richard Lamprey*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出统一三支深度学习框架（WildSeg、WildMatch、WildDet），集成航拍条带分割、图像匹配去重和动物检测，用于自动化航空野生动物调查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化航拍野生动物调查需要可靠的动物检测、一致的观测条带划定，以及相邻图像间重复覆盖的校正。作者希望用统一框架同时解决这三项任务。

Method: 框架包含三个模块：WildSeg结合杆状物分割与几何精化，从机载标记中划分计数条带；WildMatch用师生蒸馏学习稠密图像对应关系，在不依赖地理定位元数据的情况下识别重叠区域；WildDet结合双尺度检测、特征融合、密度监督和空图门控实现动物检测与计数。

Result: 在南苏丹大尼罗河迁徙景观的四种羚羊斜向航拍图像上评估：WildSeg实现可靠杆状物提取；WildMatch以更轻量更快的模型接近教师级精度；两模块在缩放图像上保持高精度。WildDet在四种物种上均提升检测性能，相比YOLO11将基于检测的计数MAE、RMSE和平均偏差分别降低21.0%、4.6%和27.8%。

Conclusion: 通过将空间调查约束与动物检测相连接，该框架为大规模航拍图像集的野生动物监测与种群调查提供了实用且计算高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Automating aerial wildlife surveys requires reliable animal detection, consistent delineation of observation strips, and correction for repeated coverage between successive images. We present a unified three-branch deep learning framework that integrates these tasks through WildSeg, WildMatch and WildDet. WildSeg combines rod segmentation with geometric refinement to delineate counting strips from aircraft-mounted markers. WildMatch uses teacher-student distillation to learn dense image correspondences and identify overlapping survey areas without relying on geolocation metadata. WildDet combines dual-scale detection with feature fusion, density supervision and empty-image gating to support animal detection and counting. We evaluated the framework using oblique aerial imagery of four antelope species from the Great Nile Migration Landscape in South Sudan. WildSeg achieved reliable rod extraction, while WildMatch approached teacher-level accuracy with a substantially lighter and faster model. Both modules maintained high accuracy on resized images, improving processing efficiency. WildDet improved detection performance across all four species and reduced detection-based count MAE, RMSE and mean bias by 21.0%, 4.6% and 27.8%, respectively, relative to YOLO11. By connecting spatial survey constraints with animal detection, the framework provides a practical and computationally efficient approach to processing large aerial image collections for wildlife monitoring and population surveys.

</details>


### [185] [Rethinking LLM-Guided Visual Question Answering: Does Deeper Knowledge Integration Really Help?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17079)
*Aneerban Saha,Vijay Kumar,Sameer Madhav D.,Priyam Srivastava,Dhruv Bansal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探究LLM知识引导在知识型VQA中对视觉语言架构的集成深度，发现在数据稀缺场景下浅层后期融合优于深层注意力注入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识型VQA需要超越图像内容的常识与事实推理，虽已有工作用LLM作知识源，但引导应多深地集成进视觉语言架构尚不清楚。

Method: 用Llama 3.3 70B生成结构化推理引导、视觉焦点概念、干扰元素和外部知识，在冻结ViT+FLAN-T5框架下于OK-VQA上对比两种策略：决策级后期融合与注意力矩阵注入，并提出LLM数据增强（问题改写）扩大训练数据十倍。

Result: 浅层后期融合效果最佳（39.4%精确匹配，比无引导基线高10.0），超过深层注意力修改（31.8%）。

Conclusion: 集成深度必须与数据规模（数据制度）相匹配，深层集成未必更优。

Abstract: Knowledge-based Visual Question Answering (VQA) requires reasoning that extends beyond visible image content, demanding external commonsense and factual knowledge. While recent work leverages Large Language Models (LLMs) as knowledge sources, the question of how deeply such guidance should be integrated into a vision language architecture remains underexplored. In this work, we generate structured reasoning guidance, visual focus concepts, distractor elements and external knowledge using Llama~3.3~70B and systematically compare two integration strategies within a unified frozen ViT + FLAN-T5 framework on the OK-VQA benchmark: decision level late fusion and attention-matrix injection. Contrary to the prevailing assumption that deeper integration is superior, we find that shallow late fusion achieves the strongest results (39.4% exact match, +10.0 over the no guidance baseline), outperforming deep attention modification (31.8%) under data scarce fine-tuning. We further contribute an LLM based augmentation pipeline that expands training data more than tenfold via question paraphrasing. Our findings indicate that integration depth must be matched to the data regime.

</details>


### [186] [Scaling Unified Multimodal Models to Long-Horizon Interleaved Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17080)
*Ziqiao Peng,Yutao Cui,Wang Lin,Zhong-Yu Li,Qingwen Gu,Tianhui Song,Chengyou Jia,Guozhen Zhang,Xuerui Qiu,Qixun Wang,Miles Yang,Zhao Zhong,Yonglong Tian,Hongyan Liu,Jun He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个原生训练的统一多模态大模型及视觉记忆框架，将交错图文生成扩展到更长的自回归轨迹，并引入LongMIG-Bench评测基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型仅在短轨迹或简化视觉内容上展示，生成的图像持续累积在上下文中，无关或不完美的视觉状态会干扰后续合成，而早期建立的字符和场景在长时间间隔后仍需恢复。

Method: 原生训练于交错多模态数据的大型UMM，具备图像合成、文字渲染、复杂构图和多模态规划能力；引入视觉记忆框架：维护持久视觉参考、选择性检索场景相关历史状态同时保留完整语义历史、使用少步自rollout使模型适应不完美的生成历史。

Result: 在长时程文本到多图及图像到多图生成任务上实现高保真长时程交错生成，具有强字符与场景一致性。

Conclusion: 视觉记忆能够将原生交错生成扩展到显著更长的轨迹，证明了该框架的有效性。

Abstract: Unified multimodal models (UMMs) provide an interface for interleaved text-image generation, where generated visual states can directly inform subsequent reasoning and synthesis. However, existing systems are still primarily demonstrated on short trajectories or simplified visual content. We develop a large UMM natively trained on interleaved multimodal data, with strong capabilities in image synthesis, text rendering, complex composition, and multimodal planning, and study how to extend these native capabilities to substantially longer autoregressive trajectories. The main challenge is that generated images continuously accumulate in context: irrelevant or imperfect visual states interfere with future synthesis, while previously established characters and scenes must still be recovered after long intervals. We introduce a visual-memory framework that maintains persistent visual references, selectively retrieves scene-relevant historical states while preserving the complete semantic history, and uses few-step self-rollout to adapt the model to imperfect generated histories. We further introduce LongMIG-Bench for evaluating long-horizon text-to-multi-image and image-to-multi-image generation. Our model achieves high-fidelity long-horizon interleaved generation with strong character and scene consistency, demonstrating how visual memory can scale native interleaved generation to substantially longer trajectories.

</details>


### [187] [Contrastive learning for video reuse detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17098)
*Tristan J. B. Cann,Arjun Biswas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于孪生网络和对比学习的框架，用于从视频中提取关键帧并识别其可能的源素材，实现高效的视频内容复用检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体内容"混剪"盛行，加上针对特定用途定制的素材库不断发展，使得识别内容复用（尤其是视频中引用他人素材）的方法有广泛需求。传统版权视角下，使用其他来源的片段会损害权利方利益，因此需要能在大规模视频数据集中高效识别素材来源的方法。

Method: 构建基于孪生网络（Siamese network）的框架，并采用对比学习（contrastive learning）进行微调。该方法首先将复杂视频数据压缩为关键视觉集合（通常小于全集10%的规模），随后在这些缩减后的集合上高效识别组成视频的源素材。

Result: 在由素材库汇编的视频集上进行测试，验证了方法在识别组成视频来源方面的有效性，平均准确率超过0.95。

Conclusion: 该框架为大规模视频数据集分析作出两项贡献：一是可将视频数据大幅缩减为关键视觉集合（小于10%），二是能在缩减集上高效且有效地识别源素材，为内容复用检测提供了实用方案。

Abstract: The incorporation of footage from other sources has long been a concern from a copyright perspective, as such usage can harm the interests of rights holders. Recent trends in the ``remixing'' of social media content and the development of bespoke stock libraries for specialist causes mean that methods to identify content reuse will have a range of applications. In this paper, we develop a framework based on a Siamese network fine tuned using contrastive learning to identify potential sources for key frames in a given video. This framework makes two contributions to analysis of large video datasets. Firstly, we demonstrate its utility at reducing complex video data to a set of key visuals typically less than 10\% of the size of the full set. Secondly, we show that these reduced sets allow for efficient and effective identification of component footage. We test this method over a set of videos compiled from a stock footage library and demonstrate its effectiveness for constituent video identification with average accuracy greater than 0.95.

</details>


### [188] [EgoProbe: Benchmarking Long-Range and Exact Memory in Streaming Egocentric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17128)
*Haokai Pang,Yuqian Fu,Yifei Wang,Yujiao Shen,Tianwen Qian,Mohamed Elhoseiny,Hongbo Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出EgoProbe基准，从"记忆多远（长程保持）"和"记忆多准（干扰下事件特定回忆）"两个维度评估流式第一人称视频的记忆能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一人称视频是穿戴式AI助手的自然基础，但流式系统需实时处理观测且查询可能指向遥远的过去。现有工作只关注能否跨长时间回忆（记住"多远"），却忽视了重复出现的物体、动作和场景带来的挑战——模型必须记住哪个细节属于哪个事件（记住"多准"）。

Method: 构建EgoProbe基准，基于长时第一人称视频，用事件锚定的数据引擎扩展回忆间隔、识别相关事件、构造事件锚定干扰项，并经人工验证。包含两个子集：EgoProbe-Base（无中间相关事件的长程回忆）和EgoProbe-Echo（含相似中间事件与锚定干扰项），后者采用粗到细多轮探测：第一轮测粗粒度事件回忆，第二轮测能否检索指定事件的目标细节。

Result: 在多种模型上的广泛评估显示，长程回忆存在显著局限，在相关经历中恢复目标事件细节的挑战更大。即使模型正确回答粗粒度问题，也常无法回忆指定事件的具体细节。

Conclusion: EgoProbe揭示了这一此前未被充分探索的失败模式，为推进流式第一人称视频理解中的长程且精确记忆提供了测试平台。

Abstract: Egocentric video provides a continuous first-person record of everyday activities, making it a natural foundation for wearable AI assistants. Unlike offline video understanding, streaming systems must process observations as they arrive, while queries may refer to experiences from the distant past. Recent work has begun to explore whether information can be recalled across long temporal gaps. Yet remembering how far is only part of the challenge: recurring objects, actions, and locations also require models to remember exactly which detail belongs to which event. We introduce EgoProbe, a benchmark that evaluates streaming egocentric memory along these two complementary dimensions: long-range retention, i.e., how far, and event-specific recall under interference, i.e., how exact. Built on long-form egocentric videos, EgoProbe uses an event-grounded data engine to extend recall intervals, identify related events, and construct event-grounded distractors, followed by human verification. It contains two subsets: EgoProbe-Base, which evaluates long-range recall without intervening related events, and EgoProbe-Echo, which challenges models with similar intervening events and event-grounded distractors. EgoProbe-Echo adopts coarse-to-fine multi-turn probing: the first turn evaluates coarse-grained event recall, while the second tests whether the model can retrieve the target-specific detail associated with the designated event. Extensive evaluation across diverse models reveals substantial limitations in long-range recall, with further challenges in recovering target-event details among related experiences. Even when models correctly answer coarse-grained questions, they often fail to recall the specific details of the designated event. EgoProbe exposes this underexplored failure mode and provides a testbed for advancing long-range and exact memory in streaming egocentric video understanding.

</details>


### [189] [AReA: Adaptive Receptive-Field Attention for Efficient High-Resolution Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17227)
*Javad Rajabi,Koorosh Roohi,David B. Lindell,Babak Taati*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练注意力机制AReA，让感受野随去噪从粗到细演化，解决DiT超分辨率生成的结构失真与细节退化问题，兼顾速度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散Transformer生成超出训练分辨率的图像困难：token数随分辨率快速增长、全注意力开销大，且输出常有结构失真和细节退化。作者指出统一的全注意力是这两个问题的共同根源：它带来二次成本，并使模型接触更多token和超出训练范围的相对距离，导致分布外问题。

Method: 观察到注意力有效感受野在去噪中逐渐收窄（早期全局成结构、后期局部精细节）。据此提出AReA：早期在降采样后的key/value上做跨尺度粗注意力以捕获全局上下文；后期在逐步缩小的窗口内做局部注意力。无需额外参数或训练。

Result: 在各目标分辨率和不同模型上，AReA一致优于当前最优的免训练基线，同时更快、更高效。

Conclusion: 通过让感受野随去噪进程从粗到细演化，免训练即可有效实现DiT的超训练分辨率生成，兼顾质量与效率。

Abstract: Generating images beyond the training resolution of Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) remains challenging, as the number of tokens grows rapidly with resolution, full attention becomes expensive, and outputs often suffer from structural distortions and degraded detail. We identify uniform full attention as the common source of both problems. Attending to all tokens at every step causes quadratic cost and exposes the model to more tokens and relative distances beyond the training range, pushing the model out of distribution. We observe that attention's effective receptive field narrows during denoising, from global in early steps that form structure to local in later steps that refine details. We propose AReA, a training-free attention mechanism whose receptive field evolves from coarse to fine, following the denoising progression. In early steps, AReA applies cross-scale coarse attention over downsampled keys and values, capturing global context, while in later steps it applies local attention within progressively shrinking windows. Without additional parameters or training, experiments show that AReA consistently outperforms state-of-the-art training-free baselines across target resolutions and different models, while also being faster and more efficient.

</details>


### [190] [CrossPortrait: Cross-Species Portrait Animation via Adversarial Motion Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17532)
*Gezhong Pan,Zhe Li,Yunfan Tao,Yongheng Zhao,Shiqi Zhou,Juyong Zhang,Xiaosong Jia,Yanyong Zhang,Shuquan Ye,Shaofeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CrossPortrait框架，实现跨物种肖像动画，支持人-动物、动物-人及跨物种动物动画，解决驱动物种泄漏问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨物种肖像动画因物种间面部结构差异巨大而困难。现有方法在动物视频上微调人类肖像动画模型，但常出现驱动物种泄漏问题。

Method: 将肖像运动分解为刚性头部姿态与表情关键点；提出物种路由表情编码器，将物种特定的LoRA融入统一表情编码器以兼顾物种特异性与跨物种表情对齐；并引入表情级对抗域适应（Ex-ADA），抑制表情潜变量中的驱动物种信息。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法实现了表达力强、身份保持的跨物种肖像动画，并优于其他最先进方法。

Conclusion: CrossPortrait通过物种路由编码与对抗域适应，有效缓解物种泄漏，在多种跨物种场景下实现高质量肖像动画。

Abstract: Cross-species portrait animation, which aims to animate a source portrait of species A using a driving video of species B, is particularly challenging due to substantial anatomical differences in facial structures across species. Previous works typically fine-tune human portrait animation models on animal video datasets but often suffer from driving-species leakage. To address this, we propose CrossPortrait, a novel framework for expressive cross-species portrait animation, supporting diverse scenarios including human-to-animal, animal-to-human and cross-species animal-to-animal (e.g. cat-to-dog) animation. Specifically, we decompose the portrait motion into rigid head poses and expression keypoints. To accommodate species-specific facial structures while facilitating cross-species expression alignment, we introduce a species-routed expression encoder that incorporates species-specific LoRAs into a unified expression encoder. Furthermore, we introduce expression-level adversarial domain adaptation (Ex-ADA) to suppress driving-species-specific information in the expression latents, thereby mitigating species leakage and promoting a shared expression space across species. Extensive experiments show that our method enables expressive and identity-preserving portrait animation across diverse species and outperforms other state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [191] [FlashVSR+: Efficient Video Super-Resolution via Hybrid Attention and Reward-Yield Distribution Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17552)
*Ruchang Yao,Shi Guo,Junhao Zhuang,Mingde Yao,Xin Cai,Jiahao Zhan,Bin Fu,Xinyuan Chen,Yu Qiao,Tianfan Xue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlashVSR+通过混合注意力和Ry-DMD蒸馏提升视频超分的高分辨率效率与偏好对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VSR蒸馏模型高分辨率推理成本高，且难与人类偏好对齐，需同时改进架构与训练策略。

Method: 架构上引入基于局部性的混合注意力并保留稀疏全局交互；训练上提出奖励-产出分布匹配蒸馏Ry-DMD，用奖励加权教师rollout将黑盒奖励融入分布匹配，无需反传奖励模型。

Result: DiT提速达2倍、峰值内存降低45%，单张24GB RTX 4090实现无分块4K VSR；恢复质量更强、高分辨率效率显著提升、用户偏好一致改善，Ry-DMD还可泛化到T2I生成。

Conclusion: FlashVSR+在下游任务中兼顾高效高分辨率推理与偏好对齐，并开放代码、模型与训练数据。

Abstract: Recent video super-resolution (VSR) models achieve efficient one-step generation through diffusion distillation. However, high-resolution inference remains costly, and distilled models are difficult to align with human preferences. These challenges call for improvements in both model architecture and training strategy. We present \textbf{FlashVSR+} to address both limitations. On the architecture side, we introduce hybrid attention based on the strong locality observed in pretrained VSR models, while retaining sparse global interaction for long-range consistency. It achieves up to $2\times$ DiT speedup and 45\% lower peak memory, enabling spatially untiled 4K VSR on a single 24GB RTX 4090. On the training side, we propose Reward-Yield Distribution Matching Distillation (Ry-DMD) for preference-aligned one-step generation. Ry-DMD uses reward-weighted teacher rollouts to incorporate black-box rewards into distribution matching, without backpropagating through the reward model. We further show that Ry-DMD generalizes beyond VSR to T2I generation. Together, FlashVSR+ delivers stronger restoration quality and substantially improves high-resolution efficiency, with consistent gains in user preference. We release our code, models, and training data at \href{https://www.flashvsr.tech}{\textcolor{FlashBlue}{\textbf{www.flashvsr.tech}}}.

</details>


### [192] [Benchmarking Self-Supervised 3D Medical Vision Foundation Models and Transferability Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17573)
*Jiaqi Tang,Yu Wang,Yang Liu,Jing Tao,Fandong Zhang,Zhou Zhen,Xiahai Zhuang,Qingchao Chen,Yining Yang,Yizhou Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对23个3D医学基础模型在14个分割、分类和回归任务上进行统一基准评测，并提出InsightRank方法，用任务适配的冻结特征统计和架构感知成对比较，在微调前对候选模型排序以降低选择成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D医学基础模型的自监督预训练评测使用不同数据集、指标和微调协议，模型架构、预训练目标和数据也各异，导致已发表结果缺乏直接可比性，难以指导新任务的模型选择。直接比较适配性能需对候选模型在标注目标数据上微调，计算成本高昂。

Method: 在相同设置下评测23个候选模型，覆盖跨解剖结构和模态的14个分割、分类与回归任务；将多种可迁移性估计器与微调后的真实排名进行对比；提出InsightRank，使用任务适配的冻结特征统计和架构感知的成对比较，在微调前对候选模型排序。

Result: 没有候选模型在所有分割任务或所有分类回归任务中均进入前三，卷积编码器与同目标训练的Transformer在不同任务上互有胜负。InsightRank在23模型池上的平均Kendall tau-b为分割0.431、分类回归0.352；分割Top3在9个任务中有4个包含任务最优模型，图像级Top3在5个任务中均未包含。三模型候选短名单使7个分割任务的提取加训练总耗时减少85.8%。

Conclusion: 该基准为模型选择提供了统一参照，InsightRank提供了一种低成本优先微调候选的方式，但短名单性能因目标类型而异。

Abstract: Self-supervised pretraining has expanded the pool of 3D medical foundation models, but published evaluations use different datasets, endpoints and fine-tuning protocols. Models also differ in architecture, pretraining objective and data, while downstream targets range from voxel-level masks to patient-level diagnoses and outcomes. As a result, published findings provide little directly comparable evidence for choosing a model for a new task. Direct comparison of adaptation performance requires fine-tuning candidates on labelled target data, at substantial computational cost. We benchmark 23 candidates across the same 14 segmentation, classification and regression tasks spanning multiple anatomies and modalities. No candidate ranked among the top three across all segmentation tasks or across all classification and regression tasks; a convolutional encoder could outperform a transformer trained with the same objective on one task but underperform it on another. We benchmark transferability estimators against the fine-tuned rankings and find that their agreement varies across dense segmentation, classification and regression. InsightRank uses task-appropriate frozen-feature statistics and architecture-aware pairwise comparisons to rank candidates before fine-tuning. Across the 23-model pool, mean Kendall tau-b was 0.431 for segmentation and 0.352 for classification and regression. The segmentation Top 3 contained a task winner on four of nine tasks; the image-level Top 3 contained none on the five tasks, despite better full-ranking agreement than the matched conventional estimators. A three-model shortlist reduced recorded extraction-plus-training job time by 85.8% across seven segmentation tasks. Together, the benchmark provides a common reference for model selection and InsightRank offers a lower-cost way to prioritize fine-tuning, with shortlist performance that differs by target type.

</details>


### [193] [Physical-Field-Bridged Material Parameter Inference from Multi-View Deformation Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17591)
*Linrui Shou,Jie Li,Yu Liu,Tao Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出物理场桥接的材料推断框架，用稠密物理响应将多视角RGB形变视频与材料类型分类及连续参数回归连接，单次前向即完成推断，大幅提升速度与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从视觉观测估计可用于仿真的材料参数对可形变物体操作至关重要，但材料属性只能通过物理响应间接体现，反物理方法需要逐场景重建、在线调优或仿真在环优化，成本高。作者希望减少对表观或类别捷径的依赖。

Method: 提出物理场桥接的材料推断：单次前向框架，预测力/接触掩码、投影运动流场和投影应力响应图，再以这些物理场为条件进行材料类型分类与连续参数回归。并构建DeformFieldBench——基于3DGS与MPM的学习环境，含5,000次仿真、100种形状、5种加载模式和稠密像素对齐物理监督。

Result: 四个连续参数中位数误差均为最佳；应力热点召回率从0.0762提升至0.5121；未见物体同仿真器重放达0.8676 SSIM/36.82 dB PSNR。GSO协议匹配评估下推断与未来状态预测约30秒，逐场景优化需15–120分钟。五个真实序列上有可测量的重放一致性。

Conclusion: 稠密物理场是快速材料推断的有效桥梁，但跨仿真器与真实世界测试仍暴露领域差距和可辨识性缺口。

Abstract: Estimating simulation-ready material parameters from visual observations is crucial for deformable-object manipulation but challenging because material properties are only indirectly revealed through physical responses. We introduce physical-field-bridged material inference, a single-pass framework that uses dense physical responses to connect multi-view RGB deformation videos with constitutive-type classification and continuous parameter regression. Unlike inverse-physics methods requiring per-scene reconstruction, online tuning, or simulation-in-the-loop optimization, our model predicts force/contact masks, projected motion-flow fields, and projected stress-response maps, then conditions material inference on these fields to reduce reliance on appearance or category shortcuts. We construct DeformFieldBench, a 3DGS MPM learning environment comprising 5,000 simulations across 100 object shapes and five mechanically informative loading modes, with dense pixel-aligned physical supervision. Our method achieves the best median errors on all four continuous parameters, improves stress-hotspot recall from 0.0762 to 0.5121, and attains 0.8676 SSIM/36.82 dB PSNR in same-simulator replay on unseen objects. Under a protocol-matched GSO evaluation, inference and future-state prediction take approximately 30 seconds, compared with 15-120 minutes for per-scene optimization. The method also yields measurable replay agreement on five real-world sequences. These results support dense physical fields as an effective bridge for fast material inference, while cross-simulator and real-world tests reveal remaining domain and identifiability gaps.

</details>


### [194] [Tanawpamana: Benchmarking Vision-Language Models on Philippine Cultural Heritage Sites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17596)
*Ivan Yuri De Leon,Hiroki Ouchi,Sakriani Sakti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出Tanawpamana数据集与基准，用于评估视觉语言模型对菲律宾文化遗产地的识别与知识能力。评估七个模型发现：细粒度视觉区分比粗粒度识别更困难，且从图像推断的地理知识弱于从文本推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型日益用于识别与描述地点，但其对特定文化（尤其菲律宾等代表性不足地区）的识别与知识能力缺乏理解。

Method: 构建Tanawpamana数据集（基于菲律宾国家文化艺术委员会Talapamana名录的遗产地图像及元数据），并提出包含4,046个任务的Tanawpamana基准，用于评估模型对遗产地的识别与知识。

Result: 评估七个视觉语言模型发现：模型在相似遗产地的细粒度视觉区分上比粗粒度识别表现更差；从图像推断地理知识的能力弱于从文本推断。

Conclusion: 视觉语言模型在代表性不足文化区域的遗产识别与地理知识上仍存在明显不足，尤其细粒度判别与图像地理推断方面。

Abstract: Vision-language models are increasingly used to identify and describe places, yet their recognition and knowledge of specific cultures remain poorly understood, particularly in underrepresented regions such as the Philippines. This study introduces the Tanawpamana Dataset, a repository of Philippine heritage site images with metadata based on the Talapamana registry of the National Commission for Culture and the Arts of the Philippines. We also introduce the Tanawpamana Benchmark, comprising 4,046 tasks designed to evaluate models' recognition and knowledge of heritage sites. Evaluating seven vision-language models, we find that they struggle more with fine-grained visual discrimination among similar heritage sites than with coarse recognition, and that their geographic knowledge is weaker when inferred from image inputs than from text.

</details>


### [195] [Morphology-aware deep learning predicts colorectal cancer survival from whole-slide images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17603)
*Hikmat Khan,Metin N. Gurcan,Anil Parwani,Wendy L. Frankel,Wei Chen,Muhammad Khalid Khan Niazi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MorphContrast，一种形态学感知的对比多实例学习框架，利用组织形态标签作为结构监督进行结直肠癌五年生存预测，结合门控注意力池化与三个辅助目标，在多个队列上展示了分布式的预后判别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从H&E全切片图像进行五年生存预测有助于风险分层，但该任务为弱监督，且携带预后信息的组织形态学特征尚未被充分刻画，因此需要能利用组织形态结构信息的方法。

Method: 采用回顾性二次分析，数据包括TCGA-COAD/READ（n=562，形态学分析）、Alliance/CALGB 89803（n=424，模型开发）和SurGen（n=218，外部评估）。框架MorphContrast结合门控注意力池化与三个辅助目标：补丁级形态标签定义患者内对比、结局一致性/不一致性在匹配形态内定义患者间对比、多样性正则化防止表征坍缩；并将结局不一致患者的形态匹配补丁作为难负样本。

Result: 形态学特异性模型显示弱但分布式的预后判别：脂肪组织AUC 0.622，间质0.604，与肿瘤上皮（0.601-0.605）相当。在Alliance/CALGB 89803上，单模型AUC 0.642，事后集成池化折外AUC达0.70，风险组HR为3.79（95%CI：2.55-5.63）。外部SurGen结肠癌队列（n=218）单模型与集成AUC分别为0.669和0.678。

Conclusion: 结果表明组织形态学可作为结构监督用于结直肠癌预后中结局感知的表征学习。

Abstract: Background: Five-year survival prediction from hematoxylin-and-eosin whole-slide images could support risk stratification, but the task is weakly supervised, and the tissue morphologies carrying prognostic information remain incompletely characterized.
  Methods: We conducted a retrospective secondary analysis using TCGA-COAD/READ (n=562) for morphology-specific analysis, Alliance/ CALGB 89803 (n=424) for model development, and SurGen (n=218) for external evaluation. We propose MorphContrast, a morphology-aware contrastive multiple-instance learning framework that uses tissue labels as structural supervision for five-year survival prediction. MorphContrast combines gated-attention pooling with three auxiliary objectives: patch-level morphology labels define within-patient contrasts, outcome concordance or discordance defines cross-patient contrasts within matched morphologies, and diversity regularization discourages representational collapse. Morphology-matched patches from patients with discordant outcomes serve as hard negatives, encouraging prognostic discrimination within comparable histologic contexts.
  Results: Morphology-specific models show weak but distributed prognostic discrimination: adipose tissue achieves an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) of 0.622, stroma achieves 0.604, and both perform comparably to tumor epithelium (0.601-0.605). On Alliance/CALGB 89803, the single model achieves an AUC of 0.642, while the post hoc ensemble reaches a pooled out-of-fold AUC of 0.70 and a risk-group hazard ratio of 3.79 (95% confidence interval: 2.55-5.63). On the external SurGen colon cancer cohort (n=218), the single model and ensemble achieve AUCs of 0.669 and 0.678, respectively.
  Conclusions: These findings support tissue morphology as structural supervision for outcome-aware representation learning in colorectal cancer prognosis.

</details>


### [196] [PLEDGE: Progressive Lossy Error-Bounded Compression of 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17605)
*Mingze Xia,Yuxiao Li,Baixi Sun,Wenbo Li,Pu Jiao,Sheng Di,Franck Cappello,Hanqi Guo,Xin Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PLEDGE 是一个免训练、渐进式、误差有界的 3D 高斯泼溅(3DGS)编解码器，将四种用户声明的容差转换为逐基元的量化边界，并在整个相机域(含未见视角)内保证满足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3DGS 编解码器只报告压缩后的重建质量，用户无法控制单个基元的失真，也缺乏对误差的细粒度控制。

Method: PLEDGE 将四类用户容差(屏幕空间位置、因旋转与缩放引起的足迹形变、视角相关颜色、不透明度)分别映射为逐基元量化边界：位置与不透明度直接约束，足迹形变在光栅化仿射投影模型下约束，颜色在所有观察方向上约束；量化残差被组织为具有显式误差界的渐进比特流。

Result: 在十三个场景上的实验验证了所有声明的各机制误差界，并展现出具竞争力的率失真性能；四种容差带来显著不同的码率开销。

Conclusion: 用户可据此将码率分配到应用最需要的部分，实现灵活可控的 3DGS 压缩。

Abstract: Existing 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) codecs report reconstruction quality after compression, and give users little control over the distortion of individual primitives. We present PLEDGE, a training-free, progressive, error-bounded codec that translates four user-declared tolerances into per-primitive quantization bounds: screen-space position, footprint deformation due to rotation and scale, view-dependent color, and opacity. The bounds apply throughout a declared camera domain, including previously unseen viewpoints. Position and opacity are bounded directly; footprint deformation is bounded under the rasterizer's affine projection model, and color is bounded over all viewing directions. PLEDGE organizes quantized residuals into a progressive bitstream with explicit error bounds for every valid prefix. Experiments across thirteen scenes verify all declared per-mechanism bounds and demonstrate competitive rate-distortion performance. The four tolerances incur substantially different rate costs, so users can spend rate where their application needs it.

</details>


### [197] [Smartphone-Based, Field-Deployed AI Inspection System for Power Distribution Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17620)
*Sampath Balaji Masabathula,Srija Vanapalli,Gangadhar Kanchi,Sreenivasa Rao V. A. N,Vijaya Lalitha,Sasank Chilamkurthy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该工作提出基于智能手机的AI配电设施巡检系统，集成深度学习、单目深度估计与几何分析，可评估杆倾斜、植被净距、变压器对地净距等隐患；在APEPDCL五个馈线试点中发现1222处缺陷、整改84处，并公开PD-Defect数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配电网络地域分散，传统人工巡检难以一致、规模化执行，需要可落地的智能化现场巡检手段。

Method: 面向现场作业的多阶段巡检流程：先做图像质量与拍摄视角校验，再融合深度学习模型、单目深度估计、几何分析与规则处理，评估杆塔倾斜、横担/顶扣倾斜、电压等级、植被净距风险及配电变压器对地净距。

Result: 在APEPDCL五条配电馈线试点部署，识别1222处缺陷并进入整改与验证流程，研究期内记录整改84处；馈线停电记录分析不足以证明可靠性改善；公开PD-Defect多模块现场数据集。

Conclusion: 验证了智能手机AI辅助巡检融入现场运维的可行性，为配电设施缺陷的系统识别、记录、整改跟踪与验证提供机制，但可靠性提升尚需更多证据。

Abstract: Routine inspection of power distribution infrastructure remains challenging for utilities operating geographically dispersed networks, where conventional manual inspection can be difficult to perform consistently and at scale. This work presents a smartphone-based AI inspection system for assessing targeted distribution-infrastructure conditions from images captured by field personnel. The proposed workflow integrates image-quality and viewing-perspective validation with AI-assisted assessment of pole lean, crossarm and top-cleat tilt, voltage level, vegetation-clearance risk, and distribution-transformer (DTR)-ground clearance. The system integrates deep-learning models with monocular depth estimation, geometric analysis, and rule-based processing within a common multi-stage inspection workflow designed for practical field operation. The system was pilot deployed across five selected distribution feeders within the Eastern Power Distribution Company of Andhra Pradesh Ltd. (APEPDCL), where 1,222 defect instances were identified across the assessed categories and subsequently tracked through a rectification and verification workflow, with 84 defect instances recorded as rectified during the study period. Feeder-level interruption records were also examined over the study period; however, the available observations do not provide sufficient evidence to establish a reliability improvement attributable to the proposed system. A curated multi-module field-captured dataset, PD-Defect, is also publicly released to support reproducible research in distribution-infrastructure inspection. The field deployment demonstrates the practical integration of smartphone-based AI-assisted inspection into field inspection and maintenance activities, providing a mechanism for systematic identification, documentation, rectification tracking, and verification of targeted distribution-infrastructure defects.

</details>


### [198] [4LC: 4D Latent Cache for Efficient Conditioning in Dynamic World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17625)
*Jianhui Zhang,Zuyao Lin,Pengxin Xu,Jingdong Wang,Xingyu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出4LC——一种带有复合视觉表征和灵活时空访问接口的4D潜在缓存，并基于它构建4LC-Video统一完成多种视频生成任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态世界建模中，观测到的静态与动态内容应被高效复用以支持不同时间与视角下的重复生成。但现有方法通常需按请求重新进行RGB渲染与编码，效率低，且难以统一支持多任务。

Method: 4LC作为潜在缓存，直接复用静态与动态内容的视觉表征作为生成条件，避免逐请求RGB渲染与重编码；作为复合缓存，将多种视觉编码器（如VAE、DINO、V-JEPA、VGGT）的表征绑定到共享的几何与运动上，形成信息丰富的复合表征；并设计灵活的时空接口，在独立指定的时间与视角读取缓存并组合跨观测读数，提供统一条件格式。基于此构建4LC-Video，通过配置源观测、时空请求与历史复用，支持相机控制、时间控制、新视角合成、场景重访和长期生成。

Result: 实验表明，条件获取更高效，时空可控性强，且单一4LC-Video模型无需特定任务微调即可在多个任务上保持场景一致性。

Conclusion: 4LC通过潜在缓存复用与统一时空接口，实现了高效、可控、一致的动态世界视频生成，且具备良好的多任务泛化能力。

Abstract: For video generation in dynamic world modeling, observed static and dynamic content should be efficiently reused to provide conditions for repeated generation at different times and viewpoints. We introduce 4LC, a 4D latent cache with composite visual representations and a flexible spatiotemporal access interface. As a latent cache, 4LC directly reuses visual representations of static and dynamic content to provide generation conditions, avoiding per-request RGB rendering and re-encoding. As a composite cache, 4LC binds visual representations from multiple visual encoders (e.g., VAE, DINO, V-JEPA, VGGT) to shared geometry and motion, allowing the composite representations to describe the dynamic world with rich information. Besides, we design a flexible spatiotemporal interface, which directly reads the cache at independently specified times and viewpoints and combines readouts across observations, providing a common conditioning format for diverse downstream tasks. Building on 4LC, we develop 4LC-Video to address multiple video generation tasks through different configurations of source observations, spatiotemporal requests, and historical reuse, including camera control, temporal control, novel-view synthesis, scene revisits, and long-term generation. Our experiments show more efficient condition acquisition, effective spatiotemporal controllability, and scene consistency across tasks using a single 4LC-Video model without task-specific fine-tuning.

</details>


### [199] [FrameLite: Decision-Oriented Aesthetic Cropping with a Compact Vision-Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17639)
*Jianze Li,Kai Liu,Ziqing Zhang,Weihang Zhang,Yong Guo,Linghe Kong,Yulun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FrameLite：基于Qwen3.5-0.8B的紧凑图像裁剪模型，通过三阶段训练（空间/构图训练 + QOS + GRO）实现无需搜索的单次坐标预测，性能超越大VLM裁剪器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型虽能为图像裁剪带来语义知识，但规模过大，难以用于轻量级摄影辅助。且观察到小模型能生成比其首次回答更优的裁剪结果。

Method: 基于Qwen3.5-0.8B，采用三阶段训练：1）空间与构图训练建立定位与取景偏好；2）质量引导在线监督（QOS）从当前策略生成的候选中学习最高质量的完整答案；3）贪心参考优化（GRO）利用相对当前贪心答案的正负质量差异进一步优化策略。两个在线阶段均用标注衍生的反馈，推理时只输出一个坐标答案，无需搜索或外部评分器。

Result: 在公开基准上，FrameLite以远小于对比大VLM的骨干网络超越了它们。受控实验证实，选择高质量训练答案比仅在线采样更能提升最终模型。

Conclusion: 小型裁剪模型通过高质量答案选择与在线优化可获得优于大模型的性能，代码和权重将在 https://github.com/JianzeLi-114/FrameLite 发布。

Abstract: Aesthetic image cropping improves composition by balancing subjects, context, and image boundaries. Large vision-language models bring semantic knowledge to this task, but their scale limits lightweight photography assistance. We propose FrameLite, a compact cropping model built on Qwen3.5-0.8B. Our approach is motivated by the observation that a small model can generate better crops than its first answer suggests. We develop three-stage training to turn this potential into stronger direct predictions. Spatial and composition training establishes localization and framing preferences. Quality-Guided Online Supervision (QOS) then learns the highest-quality complete answers among the current policy's generated candidates. Greedy-Referenced Optimization (GRO) further refines the policy using positive and negative quality differences relative to its current greedy answer. Both online stages use annotation-derived feedback, while inference returns one coordinate answer without search or an external scorer. Experiments show that FrameLite outperforms the compared large-VLM croppers across public benchmarks with a substantially smaller backbone. Controlled studies confirm that selecting high-quality training answers improves the final model beyond online sampling alone. We will release our code and model weights at https://github.com/JianzeLi-114/FrameLite .

</details>


### [200] [MemAct: Streaming Spatial Understanding with Memory and Active Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17640)
*Yuxi Wei,Wei Huang,Qirui Chen,Lu Hou,Xiaojuan Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了MemAct框架，将记忆与主动探索结合，实现闭环的流式空间理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体需在增量观测下流式推理，且需闭环：既要回忆过去观测，也要主动获取新视角，而现有流式方法侧重处理新观测，忽略任务驱动的主动探索。

Method: 共享视觉-语言模型增量写入结构化历史记忆，按需检索记录与过往帧，并通过移动与转向获取额外证据；检索证据与动作观测共同驱动后续决策，统一于推理循环。构建约100万训练样本的数据生成流程；结合监督微调与反事实动作优化（利用累积证据增益、动作成本、终局答案质量）。

Result: MemAct-bench含2万主QA对（评估当前观测理解、历史证据使用、主动探索）及2000个Timing问题。实验显示MemAct优于现有空间理解与流式基线，接近前沿多模态模型，同时保持流式推理效率。

Conclusion: 统一记忆与行动的流式空间理解框架可行有效，能提升探索与停止决策，兼具性能与效率。

Abstract: Spatial understanding is essential for embodied agents navigating and interacting with the physical world. As observations arrive incrementally, agents must reason in a streaming manner. Yet streaming understanding must also be closed-loop: task-relevant evidence may require both recalling past observations and actively acquiring new views. Existing streaming approaches largely emphasize processing incoming observations, leaving task-driven active exploration underexplored. We introduce MemAct, a framework for streaming spatial understanding with memory and action. A shared vision--language model incrementally writes structured historical memory, retrieves records and past frames on demand, and acquires additional evidence through movement and turning. Retrieved evidence and action observations inform subsequent decisions, enabling the agent to continue exploring or answer within a unified reasoning loop. We develop the MemAct suite, a data generation pipeline and collection across real and simulated environments, with approximately 1M training examples for spatial reasoning, memory writing, retrieval, and exploration. Its MemAct-bench comprises 20,000 main QA pairs evaluating current-observation understanding, historical evidence use, and active exploration, plus 2,000 separate Timing questions. MemAct combines supervised fine-tuning for memory and interaction with counterfactual action optimization using cumulative evidence gains, action costs, and terminal answer quality to improve exploration and stopping decisions. Experiments show that MemAct outperforms existing spatial understanding and streaming baselines and approaches frontier multimodal models on MemAct-bench while retaining streaming inference efficiency.

</details>


### [201] [Open-o3-Video-v2: Seek, Ground, and Reason with Verifiable Spatio-Temporal Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17656)
*Jiahao Meng,Zhongyuan Bao,Yue Tan,Haiquan Wen,Renrui Zhang,Anna Wang,Jason Li,Yanwei Fu,Yunhai Tong,Ming-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Open-o3-Video-v2 将视频推理建模为统一的智能体式证据锚定过程，主动获取观测并写入轨迹，配合新数据集与 CS-GRPO 强化学习方法，在多类视频基准上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠视频推理需答案正确且提供视觉证据。前作 Open-o3-Video 虽引入时间戳与物体框实现可验证推理，但受限于初始稀疏采样帧，无法主动获取缺失信息。

Method: 将视频推理形式化为统一智能体式证据锚定过程：识别缺失信息、追加观测、锚定支持推理的时刻与物体并写入轨迹。构建 STGR-v2-CoT135K（含52K多轮智能体锚定推理轨迹）和 STGR-v2-RL-13K，并提出 Clue-Seek GRPO（CS-GRPO），追踪工具调用对证据积累的影响，奖励线索覆盖与逐步增益，惩罚过度、重复或无信息检索。

Result: 在 V-STaR 上，Open-o3-Video-v2-4B 达 40.4% mAM 与 56.3% mLGM，较 Qwen3-VL-4B 骨干分别提升 8.5% 和 12.6%，并在通用视频理解、长视频推理和时间锚定基准上取得一致增益。

Conclusion: 主动证据获取与可验证物体级锚定对可靠视频推理具有互补作用。代码与模型已开源。

Abstract: Reliable video reasoning requires models to produce correct answers and expose supporting visual evidence. Open-o3-Video enables verifiable reasoning by embedding timestamps and object-level bounding boxes into reasoning traces, but is restricted to frames from an initial sparse sampler. We introduce Open-o3-Video-v2, which formulates video reasoning as a unified agentic evidencegrounded process. The model identifies missing information, acquires additional observations, grounds the moments and objects supporting its reasoning, and writes this evidence into the trajectory before continuing or answering. To support this behavior, we construct STGR-v2-CoT135K, including 52K multi-turn agentic grounded reasoning trajectories, and STGR-v2-RL-13K for reinforcement learning. We further propose Clue-Seek GRPO (CS-GRPO), which tracks how tool calls change accumulated evidence, rewards clue coverage and step-wise gains, and penalizes excessive, repetitive, or uninformative retrieval. On V-STaR, Open-o3-Video-v2-4B achieves 40.4% mAM and 56.3% mLGM, outperforming its Qwen3-VL-4B backbone by 8.5% and 12.6%, respectively. It also delivers consistent gains across general video understanding, long-video reasoning, and temporal grounding benchmarks. These results show that active evidence acquisition and verifiable object-level grounding are complementary for reliable video reasoning. The code and models are released at https://msalab-pku.github.io/projects/Open-o3-Video-v2.

</details>


### [202] [ScenePercept: Scenario-Conditioned Perceptual Assessment of Real-World User-Generated Content Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17661)
*Shuhan Xia,Yu Chen,Xiang Wang,Shuai Li,Peipie Li,Zekun Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 ScenePercept-Bench 基准（23,100 张 UGC 图像，覆盖 21 大类、105 细粒度场景，15 个属性标注）及 SCPA 训练框架，实现场景感知的图像感知评估并超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动成像与社交平台发展使 UGC 图像激增，但现有感知基准多为任务导向，对不同场景采用统一感知标准，难以刻画真实 UGC 图像的场景多样性与感知复杂性。

Method: 构建 ScenePercept-Bench：21 类 105 场景、23,100 张图，设计分层场景感知标注协议，覆盖视觉质量、美学感知、场景语义共 15 个属性，每图含总体分、属性分与结构化解释理由。并提出 Scenario-Conditioned Perceptual Alignment (SCPA) 框架，联合优化绝对分数预测、偏好排序与跨场景校准。

Result: 大量实验表明 SCPA 在 UGC 图像感知上达到 SOTA，并有效泛化到既有 IQA 和 IAA 基准。

Conclusion: ScenePercept-Bench 与 SCPA 为场景感知的 UGC 图像综合感知评估提供了新基准与有效方法，兼顾通用感知能力与跨场景感知标准适配。

Abstract: The rapid development of mobile imaging and social platforms has led to explosive growth in user-generated content images, making their systematic and comprehensive perceptual assessment increasingly important. Existing image perception benchmarks are typically task-oriented, curating specific scenarios for quality or aesthetic assessment and applying a uniform perceptual criterion across diverse scenarios. This limits their ability to capture the scene diversity and perceptual complexity of real-world UGC images. To address these limitations, we introduce ScenePercept-Bench, a benchmark designed to reflect the distribution of real-world UGC images, comprising 23,100 images across 21 categories and 105 fine-grained scenes. To account for the influence of scene context on human perception, we design a hierarchical, scene-aware annotation protocol, covering visual quality, aesthetic perception, and scene semantics, with 15 attributes in total. Each image is annotated with an overall score, 15 attribute-level scores, and structured explanatory reasoning. Building on this benchmark, we further introduce Scenario-Conditioned Perceptual Alignment, a training framework that tackles the heterogeneity of perceptual criteria across scenarios. SCPA jointly optimizes absolute score prediction, preference ranking, and cross-scenario calibration, preserving the model general perceptual assessment ability while encouraging it to explore and adapt perceptual criteria across different scenarios. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SCPA achieves state-of-the-art performance in UGC image perception and generalizes effectively to established IQA and IAA benchmarks.

</details>


### [203] [Hierarchical Curriculum Learning for Surgical Video Generation: Focusing on Bilateral Axillo-Breast Approach Robotic Thyroidectomy Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17684)
*Youngbin Kong,Seungho Lee,Jae Kyung Shin,Jong-hyuk Ahn,Hyoun-Joong Kong,Kyu Eun Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出结合层次化手术标注与课程迁移学习的手术视频生成框架，在缺乏基准的机器人甲状腺切除术上实现（动作级模型相比任务级 FVD降20.1%、FID降15.7%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真手术视频对教育、仿真与下游AI训练重要，但传统仿真受成本、真实感和场景多样性限制；通用文生视频扩散模型难以适配手术场景，原因包括复杂解剖结构、器械-组织交互、层次化流程及稀缺的标注数据。

Method: 将手术流程分解为三层次（phase/task/action），把预训练文生视频扩散模型从粗到细逐步适配；配合动态采样策略以处理变分辨率、变时长的手术片段；在无现成基准的双侧腋乳入路机器人甲状腺切除术上实例化。

Result: 动作级最终模型相比任务级模型 FVD 降低20.1%、FID 降低15.7%；同等预算消融表明增益来自课程设计而非更多训练；六位临床医生的用户研究对视觉真实感评价高，但指出解剖准确性与临床可靠性仍有差距。

Conclusion: 该框架为数据稀缺手术的AI驱动手术仿真提供了可行路径。

Abstract: High-fidelity surgical video generation is increasingly important for surgical education, simulation, and downstream AI training, as conventional simulation pipelines are limited by cost, realism, and scenario diversity. Adapting generic text-to-video diffusion models to surgical procedures remains difficult, however, due to complex anatomical structures, intricate tool-tissue interactions, the inherently hierarchical nature of surgical workflows, and the scarcity of curated training data outside a few well-studied procedures. We propose a surgical video generation framework that combines hierarchical procedural annotation with curriculum transfer learning. Surgical procedures are decomposed into three levels (phase, task, and action) and a pretrained text-to-video diffusion model is progressively adapted from coarse to fine-grained surgical context, supported by a dynamic sampling strategy that handles variable-resolution and variable-length surgical clips. We instantiate the framework on Bilateral Axillo-Breast Approach robotic thyroidectomy, a procedure for which no curated benchmark currently exists. Relative to the preceding task-level model, the final action-level model reduces FVD by 20.1% and FID by 15.7%, and a matched-budget ablation indicates that these gains arise from the curriculum design rather than from additional training. A user study with six clinicians rates visual realism highly while highlighting remaining gaps in anatomical accuracy and clinical reliability. The proposed framework offers a step toward AI-driven surgical simulation in procedures where curated data are scarce.

</details>


### [204] [SWORD: Scene-Grounded Wasserstein Object-Relation Distance for 3D Scene Graph Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17692)
*U-Chae Jun,Jaeeun Ko,Yoonhyuk Choi,Jiwoo Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SWORD，通过Wasserstein距离在固定对齐的节点与边上构建语义图距离监督，超越精确标签惩罚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D场景图预测用对象/关系分类损失，只鼓励精确标签恢复，不直接优化与真值图的语义接近度；两个错误图可能获得相近惩罚，即使其中一个在对象角色和关系上下文上更接近。直接图距离因需图匹配与编辑代价设计而困难。

Method: 观察到现有监督协议基于固定对象节点索引和边槽，使图比较成为定义良好对齐的图距离问题。提出SWORD：从训练标注经确定性base-to-context顺序构建块归一化的对象与关系ground距离度量（无需外部语义先验），并将其提升为对齐节点边际与null增强边边际上的Wasserstein距离。

Result: 在对齐边际概率空间中保持真值图为零距离唯一解；证明SWORD是对齐边际概率场景图上的严格度量，保持场景图驱动的替换序，控制加性图驱动语义可观测量，并给出上界化解码语义误差的代理损失。

Conclusion: SWORD提供超越精确标签惩罚的语义图距离监督，理论与实验（摘要未含具体数据）表明其有效性与合理性。

Abstract: 3D scene graph prediction is commonly optimized with object-node and relation-edge classification losses. These objectives encourage exact object and relation label recovery, but they do not directly optimize semantic proximity to the ground-truth scene graph: two wrong graphs can receive comparable penalties even when one is much closer in object roles and relation contexts. A direct graph-distance loss is difficult in general, since graph matching and graph edit distance require correspondence search and edit-cost design. Our key observation is that prior supervised 3D scene graph prediction protocols are conducted on fixed object-node indices and supervised edge slots. This can turn a scene graph comparison into a well-defined aligned graph-distance problem. To this end, we propose SWORD, a Scene-Grounded Wasserstein Object-Relation Distance that builds block-normalized object and relation ground distance metrics from training scene graph annotations through a deterministic base-to-context order, without external semantic priors. SWORD lifts these metrics to Wasserstein distances over aligned node and null-augmented edge marginals, providing semantic graph-distance supervision beyond exact-label penalties while preserving the ground-truth graph as the unique zero-distance solution in aligned marginal probability space. We prove that SWORD is a strict metric on aligned marginal probabilistic scene graphs, preserves scene-graph-grounded substitution ordering, controls additive graph-grounded semantic observables, and yields a surrogate upper-bounding decoded semantic error.

</details>


### [205] [A Differentiable 3D Scene Graph Metric via Contextual Hellinger Triplet Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17705)
*U-Chae Jun,Yoonhyuk Choi,Jiwoo Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Contextual Hellinger Triplet Geometry，一种图级别监督目标，将3D场景图预测器的分类输出重新解释为对齐的概率场景图场，度量预测图与真值图之间的语义和结构差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D场景图预测多用局部物体/谓词分类损失，仅做逐槽位标签预测，缺乏图级别的语义距离度量：语义轻微标签错误与结构性关系错误被同等对待，且主语-谓词-宾语三元组未被作为场景级单元比较。

Method: 将分类器输出重塑为对齐的概率场景图场，物体与谓词预测联合定义上下文化的SPO事实；据此构建可微的度量式训练目标，并采用高效因式分解实现，可直接用于现有3D场景图预测架构而无需改动网络设计。

Result: 在3DSSG上实验表明该监督提升了图条件3D场景生成的下游性能；消融研究证实所提图级别项具有互补作用；用户研究显示更好的图描述性、关系合理性与结构保持性。

Conclusion: 图级别监督目标能有效替代/补充局部逐槽位损失，在保持架构兼容的同时提升3D场景图预测的语义与结构质量及下游任务表现。

Abstract: 3D scene graph prediction is commonly supervised with local object and predicate classification losses. Although effective for slot-wise label prediction, this formulation does not provide a graph-level semantic distance between a predicted scene graph and its ground truth: semantically mild label errors and structurally disruptive relational errors can be treated similarly, and subject-predicate-object facts are not compared as scene-level units. We propose Contextual Hellinger Triplet Geometry, a graph-level supervision objective that measures the semantic and structural discrepancy between 3D scene graphs. Our key idea is to reinterpret the conventional classifier outputs of a 3D scene graph predictor as an aligned probabilistic scene graph field, where object and predicate predictions jointly define contextual subject-predicate-object facts. This formulation yields a differentiable metric-based training objective with an efficient factorized implementation and can be applied to existing 3D scene graph prediction architectures without modifying their network design. Experiments on 3DSSG demonstrate that our supervision improves downstream graph-conditioned 3D scene generation, while ablation studies confirm the complementary roles of the proposed graph-level terms and user studies show better graph descriptiveness, relation plausibility, and structural preservation.

</details>


### [206] [From Token Correction to Claim Admission: Training-Free Hallucination Mitigation for Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17706)
*Hyeonggeun Han,Taehyun Cho,Sangwoo Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无需训练的EGVCA框架，通过对比"有图"与"视觉空输入"下主张的似然来判定是否接纳该主张，从而抑制LVLM的物体幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型常出现物体幻觉，生成图像不支持的主张；现有方法多依赖token级分布纠正，需训练或干预生成过程。作者希望用更简单的claim级判定来替代。

Method: EGVCA为无需训练框架：只有当语义主张获得足够的"图像依赖支持"时才接纳。支持度由主张在真实图像输入与视觉空输入下的似然差异衡量。对caption任务，控制语义扩展而不改变token生成；对二元VQA，结合原子视觉支持与图像引起的原生Yes-No margin变化。

Result: 在CHAIR、POPE、MME及三个LVLM骨干上实验，EGVCA显著降低物体幻觉并保持caption质量；在九种骨干-采样设置中有八种取得最高POPE F1，并在三个骨干上均提升原生MME总分。

Conclusion: claim级接纳是一种简单有效的替代token级分布纠正的方案，可显著缓解物体幻觉。

Abstract: Large vision-language models often exhibit object hallucination, generating claims that are not supported by the input image. To mitigate this issue, we propose Evidence-Guided Visual Claim Admission (EGVCA), a training-free framework that admits a semantic claim only when it receives sufficient image-dependent support. EGVCA measures this support by comparing the claim likelihood under the observed image and a visual-null input. For captioning, it controls semantic expansion without altering token generation. For binary visual question answering, it combines atomic visual support with the image-induced change in the model's native Yes--No margin. Experiments on CHAIR, POPE, and MME across three LVLM backbones show that EGVCA substantially reduces object hallucination while preserving caption quality. It achieves the highest POPE F1 in eight of nine backbone--sampling settings and improves the native MME total score on all three backbones. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of claim-level admission as a simple alternative to token-level distribution correction.

</details>


### [207] [DT-LoRA: Reaching Beyond the Tangent Space of Low-Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17730)
*Yangming Zhang,Ling Yi,Datong Li,Jinliang Ding,Xiaojie Wang,Tiecheng Song,Zhaolong Ning*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LoRA因更新被限制在当前低秩因子切空间内、忽略切空间外残差梯度而劣于全量微调。作者提出DT-LoRA，将固定秩预算划分为稳定分支与动态分支以捕获主要残差梯度方向，不增加可训练参数与推理开销，在CV与NLP任务上稳定超越LoRA及其变体，并在开放词汇域泛化语义分割上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA常劣于全量微调。作者从切空间视角解释该差距：因子化更新被约束在当前低秩因子的切空间内，切空间外的梯度分量被忽略。对十种代表性LoRA变体的分析表明，这些残差分量占梯度能量的绝大部分且具有强谱集中性。

Method: 提出Dynamic Tangent LoRA（DT-LoRA）：将固定秩预算划分为稳定分支和动态分支以捕获主要残差梯度方向；证明这些方向是在正交补与秩约束下局部二次模型的最优更新；用免矩阵的随机化SVD恢复方向，无需显式构造残差梯度；通过受控写回保留累积更新并在预算内稳定训练。

Result: 在计算机视觉与自然语言处理任务上一致优于LoRA及其变体；作为通用适配方法，在开放词汇域泛化语义分割任务上超越任务特定方法，取得SOTA；相比LoRA不增加可训练参数或推理成本。

Conclusion: DT-LoRA从切空间残差梯度角度解释了LoRA的性能差距并给出高效解决方案：通过稳定+动态分支捕获主残差方向，兼得通用性与高效性，无额外参数或推理开销，可作为LoRA的即插即用替代。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) often underperforms full fine-tuning. We investigate this performance gap through a tangent-space lens: factorized updates are confined to the tangent space of the current low-rank factors, leaving gradient components outside this space unexploited. An analysis of ten representative LoRA variants reveals that these residual components account for most of the gradient energy and exhibit strong spectral concentration. Motivated by this finding, we propose Dynamic Tangent LoRA (DT-LoRA), which partitions a fixed rank budget into a stable branch and a dynamic branch to capture the principal residual gradient directions. We show that these directions define the optimal update of a local quadratic model under orthogonal-complement and rank constraints. Matrix-free randomized SVD recovers them without materializing the residual gradient, while controlled write-back preserves accumulated updates and stabilizes training within the allocated rank. DT-LoRA adds no trainable parameters or inference cost over LoRA. Experiments across computer vision and natural language processing tasks demonstrate consistent improvements over LoRA and its variants. Moreover, despite being a general-purpose adaptation method, DT-LoRA outperforms task-specific approaches to open-vocabulary domain generalization for semantic segmentation, achieving state-of-the-art results. Code is available at https://github.com/Snipine/DT-LoRA.

</details>


### [208] [AttenIR: Aligning Dense Visual Attention with Mesh Warping for Unsupervised Image Retargeting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17743)
*Yun Zhang,Zhiruo Zhang,Lifei Zhu,Zhe Zhu,Tong Li,Fang-Lue Zhang,Shengjuan Liu,Xinyuan Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AttenIR：一种无监督图像重定向框架，基于内容感知网格变形，将密集视觉注意力与稀疏网格约束耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型图像重定向方法仍难以保持全局结构与内容保真度，且依赖手工特征与优化过程。

Method: 将网格变形建模为顶点位移回归任务；以显著性先验引导，联合优化尺度、结构和几何保持损失；采用由粗到精的投影策略对齐密集视觉注意力先验与稀疏网格约束，结合语义特征实现无监督重定向。

Result: 实验表明该方法与传统方法及最新学习型方法相比具有竞争力甚至更优的性能。

Conclusion: AttenIR在无需人工标注的情况下实现了内容保真与全局结构保持的图像重定向，并实现了像素级内容感知变形。

Abstract: Image retargeting aims to resize images while preserving salient content free from distortion and stretching. Recent learning-based solutions reduce reliance on traditional hand-crafted features and optimization procedures, leading to more efficient and generalizable performance. Nevertheless, existing approaches still struggle to preserve global structure and content fidelity. To address these limitations, we propose AttenIR, an unsupervised image retargeting framework requiring no manual labels, based on content-aware mesh warping, which ef- fectively couples dense visual attention with sparse mesh constraints. We for- mulate the mesh deformation as a vertex displacement regression task. Guided by saliency priors, our method jointly optimizes scale, structure, and geometry preservation losses. By combining dense visual attention with semantic features, it achieves robust, unsupervised retargeting without manual annotations. Our primary contributions include (1) a lightweight unsupervised method for image retargeting that ensures content fidelity and preserves global structure, and (2) a coarse-to-fine projection strategy to align dense visual attention priors with sparse mesh constraints, enabling content-aware deformation at the pixel level. Experiments demonstrate that our method achieves competitive and superior performance compared with both traditional and state-of-the-art learning-based methods.

</details>


### [209] [When, Where, and Why: Hierarchical Causal Explainability for Video Face Understanding with MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17791)
*Anubhav Kataria,Shreya Ghosh,Jingwen Ye,Abhinav Dhall*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 VideoFace-X-LLM 框架，用于可解释的面部视频理解，含 ExplainFace 语料与 H-TCC 因果解释模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型能标注和描述视频，但难以对随时间变化的小面部动作提供忠实解释，缺乏对"何时、何处、为何"的证据性回答。

Method: 框架含两部分：1) ExplainFace，基于 CelebV-HQ 自动构建的弱监督视频-文本语料，经人工评估与双模型审计（矛盾率≤2.3%）验证；2) H-TCC（分层时空因果概念），定位关键时刻（When）、面部区域（Where），并通过定向遮挡验证因果必要性（Why）。

Result: ExplainFace 微调使 appearance Macro F1 从 19.48% 提升至 32.87%（+13.39），动作 +4.38、时序情绪 +6.77、深度伪造检测 F1 +11.84。并通过 pointing lift、face/edge lift、稳定性、Adebayo sanity check 与必要性测试等多维指标评估解释质量。

Conclusion: 该框架在多个任务上带来一致提升，并提供因果性可验证的解释，模型、代码与语料可申请用于研究。
