Abstract: Understanding facial behaviour in video requires answering three fundamental questions: when a key facial expression occurs, where the model attends on the face at that moment, and why that evidence is necessary rather than merely correlated. Today's multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can label and describe video, but they rarely provide faithful explanations, especially for small facial movements that change over time. We present VideoFace-X-LLM, a framework for explainable facial video understanding comprising two key components. First, we introduce ExplainFace, a weakly supervised video-text corpus for facial behaviour understanding, automatically constructed from CelebV-HQ videos and validated through human evaluation and a two-model audit (at most 2.3% contradictions), demonstrating consistent gains across two backbone architectures. Second, H-TCC (Hierarchical Temporal Spatial Causal Concepts), which finds the key temporal moments (When), the facial regions (Where), and verifies its causal necessity (Why) through targeted occlusion. Fine-tuning on ExplainFace raises appearance Macro F1 from 19.48% to 32.87% (+13.39), with gains of +4.38 on actions, +6.77 on temporal emotion, and +11.84 F1 on deepfake detection. We also measure the explanations pointing lift, face/edge lift, stability, an Adebayo sanity check, and a necessity test rather than only showing examples. Our models, code, and corpus are available on request for research use.

</details>


### [210] [MHVF: Training-Free GUI Grounding via Verification-Guided Multi-Hypothesis Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17792)
*Minghua Chen,Xingming Liao,Weiqiang Zhong,Zhuowei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的多假设验证框架MHVF，通过多假设探索与局部视觉验证提升GUI grounding精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集布局和相似控件易使模型定位看似合理但错误的元素，而单预测的局部细化无法探索其他候选目标。

Method: 结合多假设探索与局部视觉验证（单一预训练VL模型）：由多个全屏预测构建空间假设，通过缩放再定位检查区域；在标记局部视图中验证候选与指令一致性，验证分数引导第二轮局部搜索，并对两轮候选排序选择。

Result: 在8个基准、5个通用及GUI专用模型上多数设置均提升。UI-Venus-Ground-7B在Mind2Web和VisualWebBench分别提升4.3和2.1个百分点；Qwen2.5-VL-7B在ScreenSpot-Pro从23.6%提升至53.6%。

Conclusion: MHVF作为免训练框架，有效缓解GUI定位歧义，显著提升多种模型与基准的grounding精度。

Abstract: GUI grounding enables web agents to translate natural-language instructions into actionable interface locations. However, dense layouts and small, visually similar controls can lead models toward plausible but incorrect elements, while refinement around a single prediction may leave alternative targets unexplored. We propose the Multi-Hypothesis Verification Framework (MHVF) for training-free GUI grounding. It combines multi-hypothesis exploration with local visual verification using a single pretrained vision-language model. To explore alternative targets, MHVF constructs spatial hypotheses from multiple full-screen predictions and examines the corresponding regions through zoomed re-localization. To resolve ambiguity among the resulting candidates, it verifies their agreement with the instruction in marked local views. Verification scores then guide a second round of local search and rank candidates retained from both search levels for final selection. Experiments with five general-purpose and GUI-specific models across eight benchmark settings demonstrate improvements in grounding accuracy in most settings. MHVF improves UI-Venus-Ground-7B by 4.3 and 2.1 percentage points on Mind2Web and VisualWebBench, respectively, and raises Qwen2.5-VL-7B's accuracy on ScreenSpot-Pro from 23.6% to 53.6%.

</details>


### [211] [Multimodal Fusion of RGB and Complementary Modalities for Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17800)
*Hu Cao,Yifeng Cheng,Mu He,Mengyu Li,Yinlong Liu,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出双向极性感知跨模态融合模块BPCF，用于RGB-X多模态语义分割，提升特征对齐与融合稳定性，在八个公开数据集上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态语义分割通过引入辅助传感流（热成像、LiDAR、深度、事件、偏振、光场）提升弱光与运动模糊下的鲁棒性，但不同传感器特征不匹配、融合不稳定。

Method: 提出双向极性感知跨模态融合（BPCF）模块，能有效捕捉互补线索并增强特征对齐；在六种模态组合（RGB-热成像、RGB-LiDAR、RGB-深度、RGB-事件、RGB-偏振、RGB-光场）上验证框架。

Result: 在八个公开数据集（MFNet、KITTI-360、DELIVER、DDD17、DSEC、MCubeS、ZJU、UrbanLF）上取得SOTA结果。

Conclusion: BPCF模块有效缓解跨模态特征不匹配与融合不稳定问题，具有广泛模态适用性；代码与训练模型将在接收后公开。

Abstract: Multi-modal semantic segmentation adds an auxiliary sensing stream to RGB images (RGB-X), such as thermal, LiDAR, depth, event, polarization, or light field. This can improve robustness in poor lighting and under motion blur. But different sensors often produce features that do not match well, and fusion can become unstable. To alleviate these issues, we propose a bidirectional polarity-aware cross-modality fusion (BPCF) module that effectively captures complementary cues while enhancing feature alignment. We evaluate the framework on six modality pairings: RGB-Thermal, RGB-LiDAR, RGB-Depth, RGB-Event, RGB-Polarization, and RGB-Light Field. Our model achieve state-of-the-art (SOTA) results on eight public datasets, including MFNet, KITTI-360, DELIVER, DDD17, DSEC, MCubeS, ZJU, and UrbanLF. The codes and trained models will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [212] [SPARCL: Spatially Adaptive Regional Coverage for Active Learning in Chest X-ray Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17808)
*Juho Jung,Seongjae Kang,Mijoo Kim,Sungduk Cho,Yunkyoung Jun,Sunghoon Joo,Yoona Hwang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SPARCL主动学习策略，通过空间自适应区域覆盖平衡不确定性与空间覆盖，解决CXR病变分割中焦点/弥漫病症性能不一致问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胸部X光病变分割的像素级标注成本高昂，主动学习很关键。但现有AL策略忽略了所选样本的空间分布，导致在焦点性（如主动脉钙化）与弥漫性（如结节）病症上性能不一致。

Method: SPARCL动态平衡预测不确定性与累积空间覆盖：采用凹覆盖函数对已标注区域施加递减收益，将标注预算导向欠标注区域；当缺乏空间变化时，用熵门恢复为标准不确定性采样。该覆盖目标为单调子模，保证贪心批选择的近最优性。此外提出Spatial Inconsistency (SI)和Performance Coverage (PC)两个指标量化AL轮次中的解剖公平性。

Result: 在内部钙化队列和公开CXR基准上，SPARCL仅用24%数据达到全监督性能的95%，而最佳基线需29%。

Conclusion: SPARCL确保了稳健的空间可靠性，揭示了聚合指标（如Dice）所掩盖的区域性增益。

Abstract: Pixel-level annotation for chest X-ray (CXR) lesion segmentation is expensive, making active learning (AL) essential. However, existing AL strategies fail to account for the spatial distribution of selected samples, resulting in inconsistent performance across focal (e.g., aortic calcification) and diffuse (e.g., nodules) pathologies. In this paper, we propose SPARCL (Spatially Adaptive Regional Coverage for Active Learning) to dynamically balance predictive uncertainty with cumulative spatial coverage. By employing a concave coverage function, SPARCL imposes diminishing returns on previously annotated regions, naturally directing the annotation budget toward under-labeled areas. Furthermore, an entropy gate reverts the algorithm to standard uncertainty sampling when spatial variation is absent. This coverage objective is monotone submodular, guaranteeing near-optimal greedy batch selection. To address how aggregate metrics like Dice obscure regional failures, we further introduce two metrics---Spatial Inconsistency (SI) and Performance Coverage (PC)---to quantify anatomical fairness across AL rounds. Evaluated on an in-house calcification cohort and public CXR benchmarks, SPARCL reaches 95% of fully supervised performance using only 24% of the data, compared to 29% for the best baseline. Ultimately, SPARCL ensures robust spatial reliability, surfacing regional gains that aggregate metrics miss.

</details>


### [213] [QK-Edit: Revisiting Attention-based Injection in MM-DiT for Image and Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17829)
*Tiancheng Shen,Zilong Huang,Xiangtai Li,Zhijie Lin,Jiyang Liu,Yitong Wang,Jiashi Feng,Ming-Hsuan Yang,Jun Hao Liew*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出QK-Edit，一种无需训练、基于QK注意力操控的框架，用于MM-DiT模型的精确文本引导图像和视频编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MM-DiT中的多模态自注意力将图像与文本token拼接处理，导致传统基于U-Net的注意力操控方法失效，编辑面临挑战。

Method: 利用MM-DiT特有的注意力动态，提出QK-Edit训练-free框架；通过新颖的query-key操控策略，分离并调整关键注意力组件，在提示保真度与结构一致性间取得平衡，并可用推理时特征替换轻松实现。

Result: 支持对象添加、移除、替换、背景/材质/颜色改动、风格转换等多种编辑任务；在FLUX、HunyuanVideo等先进模型上展现出优越编辑性能。

Conclusion: QK-Edit弥合了MM-DiT生成能力与可编辑灵活性之间的差距，为可扩展的多模态内容创作铺平道路。

Abstract: Multimodal Diffusion Transformers (MM-DiTs) have recently emerged as a powerful framework for unified text-vision synthesis, surpassing traditional U-Net architectures in generative tasks. One key innovation lies in its Multimodal Self-Attention (MM-SA) interaction, where image and text tokens are concatenated and processed via self-attention. However, this mechanism poses significant challenges for editing, rendering conventional U-Net-based attention manipulation methods ineffective. To address this limitation, we propose QK-Edit, a training-free framework that exploits the unique attention dynamics of MM-DiTs for precise text-guided image and video editing. By introducing a novel query-key manipulation strategy, our method isolates and adjusts critical attention components to achieve an optimal balance between prompt fidelity and structural consistency. This enables seamless edits across various tasks, including object addition, object removal, object replacement, changing background, changing material, changing color, and style transformation. Notably, it can be easily implemented with feature replacement in inference. QK-Edit demonstrates superior editing performance on state-of-the-art models, such as FLUX and HunyuanVideo, effectively bridging the gap between generative power and editable flexibility in MM-DiTs, and paving the way for scalable multimodal content creation.

</details>


### [214] [What Makes a Good Visual Representation for Spatial Understanding in MLLMs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17844)
*Xiaohu Huang,Yumin Guo,Kai Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究"什么样的视觉表征有助于MLLMs的空间理解"，通过分离"迁移什么"与"如何迁移"，发现表征的3D结构与其帮助程度强相关，且表征形式决定所需迁移机制，并据此蒸馏潜在特征与显式几何，显著提升空间推理表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLMs的视觉预训练数据几乎全是2D，视觉token缺乏内在3D结构信息，限制了其对3D物理世界的推理。尽管已有方法通过迁移外部视觉/几何模型表征来注入结构，但"什么使视觉表征有利于空间理解"这一问题尚未被充分探索。

Method: 将"迁移什么"与"如何迁移"分离：迁移来源包括3D几何模型、自监督图像/视频模型、视觉语言模型、生成模型；迁移形式为潜在特征或显式深度/点图；迁移方式包括蒸馏（作为监督目标）或融合（作为额外输入）。进一步将差异追溯至表征本身的属性，并以跨视角一致性（潜在特征）和深度/重建精度（显式几何）衡量其3D结构。

Result: 表征的3D结构与其帮助程度强相关；表征形式决定所需机制：潜在特征从融合与蒸馏中获益相当，显式几何从融合获益甚微，其蒸馏增益主要依赖几何解码头。据此将潜在特征与显式几何蒸馏进单一MLLM，仅以RGB视频为输入，在静态3D与动态4D空间推理基准上均稳定优于基座模型。

Conclusion: 好的空间理解表征关键在于其内在3D结构，且迁移机制须与表征形式匹配；据此设计的融合蒸馏方案能有效提升MLLM的3D/4D空间推理能力。

Abstract: Spatial understanding is a necessary step in moving multimodal large language models (MLLMs) toward reasoning about the 3D physical world. Their visual pretraining data, however, is almost entirely 2D, so the visual tokens processed by MLLMs often lack inherent 3D structural information. A growing number of methods inject such structure by transferring representations from external vision or geometry models, and collectively establish that such transfer helps. Behind these methods lies a largely underexplored question: what makes a visual representation good for spatial understanding? To answer it, we first separate what is transferred from how it is transferred, and then trace the resulting differences back to properties of the representations themselves. What we transfer comes from 3D geometry models, self-supervised image and video models, vision-language models, and generative models, in the form of latent features or explicit depth and point maps. We transfer it either by distillation, using it as a supervision target, or by fusion, feeding it as an additional input. We find that a representation's 3D structure correlates strongly with how much it helps, measured by cross-view consistency for latent features and by depth and reconstruction accuracy for explicit geometry. We further show that the form of a representation dictates the mechanism it needs. Latent features gain as much from fusion as from distillation; explicit geometry gains little from fusion, and most of its distillation gain depends on a geometry decoding head. Guided by these findings, we distill both latent features and explicit geometry into a single MLLM. Taking only RGB video as input, it consistently improves over its base model on both static 3D and dynamic 4D spatial reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [215] [DVTR-KD: Distilling Vision-Language Models via Intermediate Visual Token Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17853)
*Haolan Wei,Jiahui Wang,Junli Zha,Xinkai Lu,Yunxiao Zou,Chao Zhou,Xiang Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DVTR-KD框架，通过重建教师密集视觉token来解决视觉token压缩学生的知识蒸馏问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM计算需求大，边缘部署受限。知识蒸馏和视觉token压缩是两条效率路线，但token压缩导致学生与教师视觉token数量不匹配，难以直接进行token级知识迁移。

Method: 基于密集视觉token重建的蒸馏框架：从学生压缩token重建教师密集视觉token；在LLM末层及一个对视觉信息读出重要的中间层进行重建；中间层用教师响应预测而非token级KL/MSE监督；辅以注意力读出损失对齐上下文整合方式。

Result: 在多样基准上，DVTR-KD提升了token压缩学生的性能，同时保留视觉token压缩带来的推理效率。

Conclusion: DVTR-KD有效缓解了token数量不匹配问题，兼顾性能与效率，适用于边缘受限部署。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have achieved strong performance on diverse vision-language tasks, but their computational demands limit deployment under constrained budgets, particularly on edge devices. Many works focus on Knowledge Distillation (KD) and try to transfer knowledge from powerful teacher models to students. In addition to reducing model size, visual token compression provides a complementary route to efficiency, but introduces mismatched visual token counts that complicate direct token-wise knowledge transfer. To mitigate this, we propose DVTR-KD, a knowledge distillation framework based on \textbf{D}ense \textbf{V}isual \textbf{T}oken \textbf{R}econstruction, which reconstructs the teacher's dense visual tokens from the student's compressed tokens. We reconstruct visual states after the final LLM layer and at the input to an intermediate layer identified as important for visual-information readout. At the intermediate layer, reconstruction is supervised through the teacher's response predictions rather than by direct token-wise KL or MSE matching. An auxiliary attention-readout loss complements this response-level supervision by aligning how the student and the original teacher integrate contextual information when predicting responses. Evaluations on diverse benchmarks demonstrate that DVTR-KD improves the performance of token-compressed students while retaining the inference efficiency afforded by visual token compression.

</details>


### [216] [Frequency Routing and Identity Conditioning for Thermal-to-Visible Face Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17859)
*Haojie Li,Xinlong Sun,Xiaohaoyang Lei,Kai Cheng,Yiguo Qiao,Xu Yang,Cunjian Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 FreqID-Control，一种参数高效的热红外人脸到可见光人脸翻译框架，按频谱可靠性与语义角色组织热引导信号，兼顾图像保真度与身份保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热红外人脸到可见光（T2V）翻译在夜间生物识别、低光监控与人眼可解释性方面有重要应用。但热图不直接记录可见光外观，且跨光谱线索在不同频率成分与语义角色上的作用差异大；现有空间条件模型用同质适应路径处理这些线索，未显式区分局部结构与全局身份。

Method: FreqID-Control 在保留预训练生成先验的前提下，按频谱可靠性和语义角色组织热引导：一是频率路由低秩适应（frequency-routed low-rank adaptation），为低频结构和高频细节分配不同的变换子空间；二是共享跨光谱身份条件（shared cross-spectral identity conditioning），独立于空间热token提供紧凑的全局身份token。设计依据来自 MCXFace 与 SpeakingFaces 上的受控分析，发现稳定低频对应与边缘定位的细节对应。

Result: 在两个数据集上实验表明图像保真度和生物身份保持均表现良好；MCXFace 上的受控消融证实频率路由与共享身份条件具有互补贡献。

Conclusion: 通过频率路由与共享身份条件解耦局部结构与全局身份，可在参数高效下有效提升 T2V 人脸翻译质量与身份保持能力。

Abstract: Thermal-to-visible (T2V) face translation has important applications in nighttime biometric recognition, low-light surveillance, and human interpretation of thermal observations. The task remains challenging because thermal images do not directly record visible appearance, while the cross-spectral cues useful for reconstruction vary across frequency components and semantic roles. Existing spatially conditioned models tend to process these cues through a homogeneous adaptation path and do not explicitly separate local structure from global identity. We propose FreqID-Control, a parameter-efficient framework that organizes thermal guidance according to spectral reliability and semantic role while preserving the pretrained generative prior. It combines frequency-routed low-rank adaptation, which allocates separate transformation subspaces to low-frequency structure and high-frequency detail, with shared cross-spectral identity conditioning, which supplies compact global identity tokens independently of spatial thermal tokens. A controlled analysis on MCXFace and SpeakingFaces provides the empirical basis for this design, revealing stable low-frequency correspondence and edge-localized detail correspondence. Experiments on both datasets demonstrate strong image fidelity and biometric identity preservation, while controlled ablations on MCXFace confirm the complementary contributions of frequency routing and shared identity conditioning.

</details>


### [217] [MicroCLIP: Connecting Text and Microscopy Images via Contrastive Language--Image Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17866)
*Yanran Ruan,Zhixiang Wei,Shiyao Hong,Xiaoxiao Ma,Yuxuan Gu,Liming Ye,Huaian Chen,Yi Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MicroCLIP：面向荧光显微镜的视觉-语言基础模型，通过三级对齐策略在250万图像-文本对上预训练，实现开放集分类与检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有荧光显微图像理解方法多从封闭集数据学习预定义概念，难以跨任务和生物情境迁移；且多数显微图像缺乏描述性文本与生物学标注，导致图像与生物语义对齐困难。

Method: (1) 构建MicroPool-2M数据集（约250万荧光显微图像-文本对，覆盖多样细胞结构、细胞系与成像条件）；(2) 使用自然语言监督预训练MicroCLIP视觉-语言模型；(3) 三级渐进对齐：类别级（子细胞结构-细胞系组合的标准标签）、知识级（由标签衍生的跨物种/细胞类型生物先验）、细节级（用多模态大模型重标注与校验细粒度视觉特征）。

Result: 在零样本分类与文本-图像检索任务上取得领先性能；可从数千候选中检索具有特定细胞特征的图像；无需额外监督即可泛化到未见生物学概念。

Conclusion: MicroCLIP展示了作为荧光显微镜基础模型的潜力，为开放集、跨情境的生物图像理解提供了可迁移方案。

Abstract: Understanding fluorescence microscopy images is fundamental to automating biological research. However, existing methods typically learn predefined concepts from closed-set data, limiting transfer across tasks and biological contexts. In this work, we introduce MicroCLIP, a vision-language model that learns generalizable cellular and subcellular representations from natural language supervision for open-set image classification and retrieval. To pretrain MicroCLIP, we construct MicroPool-2M, a dataset of approximately 2.5 million fluorescence microscopy image-text pairs spanning diverse cellular structures, cell lines, and imaging conditions. Aligning microscopy images with biological concepts in natural language is challenging because most images lack descriptive captions and biological knowledge annotations. We therefore progressively establish image-text associations at three levels to provide MicroCLIP with richer semantic supervision: (1) category-level alignment links images to standardized labels defined by subcellular structure-cell-line combinations; (2) knowledge-level alignment incorporates biological priors derived from these labels to capture relationships across species and cell types; and (3) detail-level alignment uses a multimodal large language model to recaption and verify image-text pairs at scale, emphasizing fine-grained visual characteristics. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MicroCLIP achieves leading performance in zero-shot classification and text-image retrieval on microscopy images. It retrieves images with specific cellular characteristics from thousands of candidates and generalizes to unseen biological concepts without additional supervision. These results highlight MicroCLIP's potential as a foundation model for fluorescence microscopy.

</details>


### [218] [LoopREG: Representation Supervision Enables Test-Time Recurrent Depth in Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17877)
*Zipeng Wang,Anping Xiong,Jing Zhang,Xiaoya Zhu,Yi Jiang,Yanbing Xiao,Zige Zeng,Zezhou Chen,Xiang Liu,Huan Hu,Ping Chen,Kai Wang,Zhaoxiang Liu,Shiguo Lian,Ye LV,Fang Zhao,Huanlin Gao,Zhengrui Huang,Bo Shen,Yuren You*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LoopREG是一种表征监督的循环扩散Transformer，通过共享块复用并在预训练视觉编码器监督下对齐表征，实现推理时增加计算深度来提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复用Transformer块可在测试时增加扩散模型计算量，但朴素循环会损害超出训练深度的生成质量。

Method: 提出LoopREG，反复应用共享块，并在预训练视觉编码器监督的状态下进行表征对齐；训练时在最终循环访问后进行对齐，推理时可将同一学习算子扩展到更多访问次数。

Result: 在ImageNet 256x256上，LoopREG将SiT-B/2的FID从REG基线的15.08降至4.91（400K步，降低67.41%）；两访问训练checkpoint加一次推理访问，FID从14.71降至5.34且权重不变；在SiT-L/2和SiT-XL/2上持续有效。对照实验显示有用循环跟随表征监督位置，REPA对齐的外推收益较小。

Conclusion: 表征监督的循环是提升扩散生成、通过增加推理深度改善效果的实际可行途径。

Abstract: Reusing Transformer blocks offers a way to increase diffusion-model computation at test time, but naive recurrence can degrade generation beyond its training depth. We introduce LoopREG, a representation-supervised recurrent diffusion Transformer that repeatedly applies a shared block at the state supervised by a pretrained visual encoder. Alignment is applied after the final recurrent visit during training; inference extends the same learned operator to additional visits. On ImageNet 256 x 256, LoopREG reduces SiT-B/2 FID from the local REG baseline's 15.08 to 4.91 at 400K training steps, a 67.41% reduction. Within a checkpoint trained with two visits, one extra inference visit improves FID from 14.71 to 5.34 with unchanged weights. The benefit persists on SiT-L/2 and SiT-XL/2. Controlled interventions show that useful recurrence follows the location of representation supervision, while REPA alignment produces a smaller extrapolation benefit. These results make representation-supervised recurrence a practical route to improving diffusion generation through additional inference depth.

</details>


### [219] [Diagnose-to-Generate: Eliminating Structural Distortions in Flow Models via Patch-Level Grounded Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17882)
*Yifan Li,Lishuai Gao,Tong Zhang,Qilong Huang,Chen Zhao,Zhuoliang Kang,Jie Hu,Yujie Zhong,Fenghe Tang,S Kevin Zhou,Xiaoming Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: D2G将视觉诊断与生成优化分离，通过区域级奖励和轨迹保持采样修正流模型局部结构失真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式图像生成器语义合成强但存在局部结构失真（手、肢体异常）。现有后训练方法用全局标量奖励，空间信用分配弱，可能修正缺陷时破坏已良好区域。

Method: 构建Achilles-600K区域标注数据集，训练ARGUS（监督冷启动+区域感知GRPO）预测异常类别与框；将诊断转为置信加权空间奖励用于Patch级Flow-GRPO；提出TPSS，早期去噪用确定性ODE轨迹锚定，后期引入随机性以限制组内空间漂移。

Result: 在FLUX.1-dev和SD3.5上提升结构保真度，FID与CLIP保持；在HumanRefiner、零样本GPT-4.1和盲测人类评估下均有一致增益。

Conclusion: 区域级诊断奖励与轨迹保持采样可有效修正局部结构缺陷且不损害全局质量，改进可泛化到训练奖励模型之外。

Abstract: Flow-based image generators achieve strong semantic synthesis but still produce localized structural distortions, such as malformed hands and limbs. Existing post-training methods typically optimize global scalar rewards, which provide weak spatial credit assignment and may alter well-formed regions while correcting local defects. We present \textit{Diagnose-to-Generate (D2G)}, a grounded alignment framework that separates visual diagnosis from generative optimization. We construct \textbf{Achilles-600K}, a region-annotated dataset for structural anomalies, and train \textbf{ARGUS} through supervised cold-start and Region-Aware Group Relative Policy Optimization to predict anomaly categories and bounding boxes. These diagnoses are converted into confidence-weighted spatial rewards for our \textbf{Patch-level Flow-GRPO}. We further identify that global stochastic exploration can shift semantic regions across group rollouts, making patch-wise relative advantages spatially inconsistent. Our \textbf{Trajectory-Preserving Stochastic Sampling (TPSS)} anchors early denoising with deterministic ODE trajectories and introduces stochasticity only at later steps, thereby limiting intra-group spatial drift while retaining local exploration. Experiments on FLUX.1-dev and SD3.5 show improved structural fidelity with preserved FID and CLIP scores. Consistent gains under HumanRefiner, zero-shot GPT-4.1, and blinded human evaluation further demonstrate that the improvements extend beyond the training reward model.

</details>


### [220] [Visual Dependence Does Not Imply Answerability: A Systematic Analysis of Answerability Signals in Frozen InstructBLIP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17902)
*Minjun Kim,Hyunmin Song,Changwon Lim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在UNK-VQA子集上用冻结InstructBLIP评估多种可答性信号，发现区分能力普遍很弱（AUROC约0.50-0.54），无稳健的独立代理指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VQA模型即使缺乏足够视觉证据也会给出看似合理的答案；现有基准与弃答机制不能说明：从冻结视觉语言模型中提取的哪些信号，能可靠判断未见图文对是否可答。

Method: 在UNK-VQA分层1万子集上，用冻结InstructBLIP系统评估多类可答性信号：预测不确定性、跨模态对齐、视觉定位、视觉依赖、内部表征与学习探针；并做配对bootstrap、干预敏感度、探针容量/多信号融合/推理感知监督、事后交叉验证及选择性回答与失败模式分析。

Result: 可答性判别普遍较弱，测试AUROC多在0.50-0.54；CLIP对齐点估计最高但不显著优于生成置信度；针对性移除问题相关视觉证据比随机掩码造成更大答案置信度变化，但干预敏感度几乎不能判别可答性；提升探针容量、融合信号、加监督均无稳健提升，选择性回答在低风险标准下可用覆盖有限。

Conclusion: 在冻结InstructBLIP下，视觉依赖、不确定性、语义对齐与静态表征都不是稳健的可答性独立代理；需区分模型对视觉证据的依赖与该证据是否足以支持答案。

Abstract: Visual question answering models often produce plausible responses even when the available visual evidence is insufficient to support a valid answer. Although prior work has introduced benchmarks and abstention mechanisms for unanswerable visual questions, it remains unclear which signals derived from a frozen vision-language model reliably indicate answerability on unseen image-question pairs. We systematically evaluate answerability signals on a stratified 10K subset of UNK-VQA using frozen InstructBLIP, covering predictive uncertainty, cross-modal alignment, visual grounding, visual dependence, internal representations, and learned probes. Answerability discrimination remains weak, with test AUROC largely confined to 0.50-0.54. CLIP alignment yields the highest point estimate but does not significantly outperform generation confidence under paired bootstrap analysis. Targeted removal of question-relevant visual evidence causes substantially larger changes in answer confidence than same-size random masking, yet intervention sensitivity provides essentially no answerability discrimination. Increasing probe capacity, combining multiple signals, and adding reason-aware supervision do not provide robust improvements, while post-freeze cross-validation yields similarly weak discrimination. Selective-answering analysis shows limited usable coverage under low-risk operating criteria. Failure-mode analysis shows that the relative utility of individual signals varies across perturbation mechanisms. Taken together, these results show that, on our UNK-VQA subset with frozen InstructBLIP, visual dependence, uncertainty, semantic alignment, and static representations are not robust standalone proxies for answerability. Our findings emphasize the need to distinguish a model's reliance on visual evidence from whether that evidence is sufficient to support an answer.

</details>


### [221] [DriveWLA: Unifying Future Dynamics and Scene Semantics through World-Language-Action Modeling for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17903)
*Shuzheng Wang,Haoxuan Li,Shengzhen Li,Chenxi Wu,Yajing Sun,Lu Hou,Wenyu Liu,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DriveWLA是一个基于Mixture-of-Transformers的统一World-Language-Action模型，联合建模语义理解、未来场景动态与轨迹生成，结合Flow-GRPO强化学习后训练，在NAVSIM等基准及零样本泛化任务上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型与世界模型各自具备语义理解和未来场景演化能力，但如何有效整合二者用于轨迹生成仍具挑战性。

Method: 提出基于MoT的DriveWLA，含World、Language、Action三个专家；引入Cross-Expert Attention实现跨专家受控信息流并对齐语义与动态表示；采用Flow-GRPO强化学习后训练精炼Action专家。

Result: 在开环与闭环评测中表现优异：NAVSIM v1达92.3 PDMS，NAVSIM v2达90.1 EPDMS；无需微调即可零样本泛化至HUGSIM、nuScenes和Bench2Drive。

Conclusion: 验证了统一World-Language-Action建模的有效性，DriveWLA可作为自动驾驶的通用框架。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) and world models have demonstrated strong capabilities in semantic understanding and future scene evolution for autonomous driving. However, effectively integrating both capabilities for trajectory generation remains challenging. In this work, we propose DriveWLA, a unified World-Language-Action model based on Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) that jointly models semantic understanding, future scene dynamics, and trajectory generation. DriveWLA consists of three experts: a World Expert for future prediction, a Language Expert for semantic understanding, and an Action Expert for trajectory generation. We further introduce Cross-Expert Attention, which enables controlled information flow across experts while aligning semantic and dynamic representations. To improve trajectory generation beyond imitation learning, we apply Flow-GRPO reinforcement learning post-training to refine the Action Expert. Extensive experiments show that DriveWLA achieves superior performance in both open-loop and closed-loop evaluation. DriveWLA achieves 92.3 PDMS on NAVSIM v1 and 90.1 EPDMS on NAVSIM v2, demonstrating strong performance among VLM-based and world-model-based planners. Without specific fine-tuning, DriveWLA further demonstrates strong zero-shot generalization to HUGSIM, nuScenes, and Bench2Drive. These results validate the effectiveness of our unified World-Language-Action modeling, establishing DriveWLA as a generalizable framework for autonomous driving.

</details>


### [222] [LinMorph: Learning to Morph Weights for Efficient Video DiT Linearization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17914)
*Lingyi Long,Congyue Zhao,Xiaoou Li,Yanmin Qian,Tao Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LinMorph将Video DiT线性化重构为可学习的softmax到线性权重变换问题，通过学习跨参数映射实现更好的预训练知识迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后置线性化方法将注意力替换引起的质量损失视为恢复问题，但真正瓶颈在初始化：随机初始化丢弃预训练知识，手工权重复用又依赖僵化不完整的映射。

Method: LinMorph学习一个跨参数软max到线性权重映射，让每个目标Gated DeltaNet参数联合地从预训练query/key/value/output投影中抽取；单一映射跨层共享，通过局部教师对齐学习，生成层特定初始化，再物化为独立GDN模块进行渐进蒸馏与联合恢复；因在权重空间操作，可用短低分辨率片段学习并迁移到大视频尺度。

Result: 在多种预训练Video DiT上，相比随机初始化和启发式权重复用，LinMorph实现更快恢复和更好最终生成质量。

Conclusion: 高效线性化不仅取决于转换后模型如何恢复，更关键取决于恢复前预训练知识如何迁移。

Abstract: Post-hoc linearization is an attractive way to accelerate pretrained Video Diffusion Transformers (Video DiTs), yet existing methods largely treat the quality loss induced by attention replacement as a recovery problem. We show that a more fundamental bottleneck lies upstream: \emph{initialization}. Random initialization discards pretrained knowledge, whereas manual weight reuse transfers it through rigid and incomplete source--target correspondences. We introduce \textbf{LinMorph}, which recasts Video DiT linearization as a learnable softmax-to-linear weight transformation problem. Instead of copying predefined counterparts, LinMorph learns a cross-parameter morphism that lets each target Gated DeltaNet (GDN) parameter draw jointly from pretrained query, key, value, and output projections, including for parameters with no direct softmax counterpart. A single morphism is shared across layers and learned through local teacher alignment, while producing layer-specific initializations from their respective pretrained weights. The resulting weights are then materialized as independent GDN modules for progressive distillation and joint recovery.Since the morphism operates in weight space rather than token space, it can be learned on short, low-resolution clips and directly reused at larger video scales. Across pretrained Video DiTs, LinMorph enables markedly faster recovery and better final generation quality than random initialization and heuristic weight reuse. Results show that efficient linearization depends not only on how a converted model is recovered, but critically on how pretrained knowledge is transferred before recovery. Project page: https://lonelyling.github.io/LinMorph.

</details>


### [223] [CineMem: Attention-Aligned Fine-grained Memory Retrieval for Multi-Shot Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17926)
*Xiaoxue Wu,Xinyu Gao,Fang Yu,Yu Qiao,Xinyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CineMem，一个注意力对齐的细粒度记忆检索框架，结合在线记忆组织与查询自适应检索，用于自回归多镜头视频生成，并引入VLM辅助评测指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频生成依赖记忆检索建模长程依赖，多镜头场景尤其需要兼顾镜头切换的巨大视觉变化与语义连续性。现有方法在帧级检索且所有查询token共享同一历史上下文，忽略了异构信息需求，导致检索冗余与历史记忆利用低效。

Method: 基于注意力分析揭示的内在记忆访问模式，提出在线聚类的KV cache作为记忆组织策略，实现语义感知的记忆分组与更精确检索；并引入查询级检索机制，在固定检索预算下提供更广的历史信息访问，提升长期记忆利用率。

Result: 在有限记忆预算下逼近全记忆访问效果，尤其在重访场景中保持跨镜头切换的长程一致性；同时提出一套任务特定的VLM辅助指标用于评估镜头间一致性，大量实验验证了框架有效性。

Conclusion: CineMem通过细粒度、查询自适应的记忆检索提升了自回归多镜头视频生成的长期一致性与记忆效率。

Abstract: Autoregressive video generation relies on memory retrieval to model long-range dependencies. This capability becomes particularly critical for multi-shot generation, where models must reconcile substantial visual changes across shot transitions with persistent semantic continuity. Existing methods tend to perform memory retrieval at the frame level and share the same historical context across all query tokens, overlooking the heterogeneous information needs and leading to redundant retrieval and inefficient use of historical memory. To bridge this gap, we introduce CineMem, an attention-aligned fine-grained memory retrieval framework that combines online memory organization with query-adaptive retrieval for autoregressive multi-shot video generation. Specifically, guided by intrinsic memory-access patterns revealed through attention analysis, we propose an online-clustered KV cache as a memory organization strategy over all historical tokens, enabling semantic-aware memory grouping and more precise retrieval. In addition, we introduce a query-level retrieval mechanism that provides broader access to historical information under a fixed retrieval budget, thereby improving long-term memory utilization. Together, these designs enable the multi-shot video generator to preserve long-range consistency across shot transitions, particularly in revisitation scenarios, while approximating full-memory access under a constrained memory budget. To facilitate evaluation, we further introduce a suite of task-specific, VLM-assisted metrics for faithful inter-shot consistency assessment. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework.

</details>


### [224] [HierarchiRetina: A Diabetic Retinopathy Screening and Grading Cascade with Heterogeneous Mixture-of-Experts Lesion Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17933)
*Md Asif Uddin,Niloy Farhan,Rafeed Rahman,Md. Golam Rabiul Alam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HierarchiRetina级联系统，包含筛查、分割和分级三个阶段，各阶段输出可检查，并在端到端评估中实现QWK 0.804、DDR六分类准确率85.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化糖网筛查系统多采用单一黑盒网络分级，存在三个缺口：(i)病灶大小和对比度差异大，分割器却对所有病灶用相同模块；(ii)不可分级图像常被排除或当作额外类别，且很少验证病灶是否真正驱动分级；(iii)分级系统仅报告各阶段到达图像的性能，未衡量级联传播误差。

Method: HierarchiRetina三阶段级联：1) HSMoE-AUNet——U-Net的skip、bottleneck和decoder块为异构专家的稀疏混合，专家类型/数量/损失与病灶匹配；SwinHRUNetPP双编码器血管分割器采用视野受限损失。2) LG-DRG将可分级性与序数严重度分离，病灶掩膜通过门控交叉注意力查询图像。3) 端到端评估将筛查漏检计为错误，并追踪每个错误到对应阶段。

Result: Pooled Dice: 微动脉瘤0.184至渗出0.579；血管Dice 0.731。六数据集58,689张测试图端到端QWK 0.804，转诊敏感度0.832、特异度0.947，筛查门导致66%的错误。消融显示分级忽略掩膜（即使交叉注意力门初始开放）。DDR 4,105张测试图六分类准确率85.6%，高于最佳已发表结果83.8%。

Conclusion: 分级式筛查系统在临床部署前应进行端到端评估，并进行阶段归因和掩膜消融；本文的级联设计在多个数据集上取得领先性能。

Abstract: Clinical screening for diabetic retinopathy proceeds in stages, yet most automated systems grade fundus images with a single black-box network. Three gaps remain: (i) lesions differ widely in size and contrast, but segmenters typically apply the same blocks to all; (ii) ungradable images are usually excluded or treated as an extra class, and lesion-aware graders seldom test whether lesions drive the grade; (iii) staged systems often report each stage only on the images reaching it, leaving propagated errors unmeasured. HierarchiRetina, a screening, segmentation and grading cascade with inspectable stage outputs, addresses each gap. First, HSMoE-AUNet is a U-Net whose skip, bottleneck and decoder blocks are sparse mixtures of heterogeneous experts, with lesion-matched types, counts and losses; SwinHRUNetPP, a dual-encoder vessel segmenter, uses a field-of-view-restricted loss. Second, LG-DRG separates gradability from ordinal severity and lets lesion masks query the image through gated cross-attention. Third, an end-to-end evaluation counts screening misses as errors and traces each error to its stage. Pooled Dice ranged from 0.184 (microaneurysms) to 0.579 (exudates); vessel Dice was 0.731. On 58,689 held-out images from six datasets, end-to-end quadratic weighted kappa was 0.804, with referral sensitivity 0.832 at specificity 0.947, and the screening gate caused 66% of errors. In ablations, the grade ignored the masks, even with the cross-attention gate started open. On all 4,105 official DDR test images, six-class accuracy was 85.6%, above the best published six-class result we found (83.8%). Staged screening systems should be evaluated end-to-end, with stage attribution and mask ablation, before clinical deployment.

</details>


### [225] [PUGS: Physics-Guided Uncertainty Modeling for Underwater Dynamic 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17936)
*Tingjun Li,Yuxi Wang,Guoqiang Zhong,Zhaoxiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PUGS提出一种物理引导的不确定性模型用于水下动态3D高斯泼溅，将高斯运动建模为概率分布，通过随机变形优化与自一致性蒸馏实现高质量动态重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态3DGS方法使用确定性变形场，假设每个高斯沿唯一几何轨迹运动，但水下光散射、折射、海雪和低能见度造成图像物理歧义，该假设失效。

Method: 学习水下成像固有的不确定性并作为物理信号；将每个高斯运动参数化为含期望变形与物理不确定性的概率分布；采用局部重参数化的随机变形优化采样轨迹；引入不确定性随机退火与双路径自一致性蒸馏目标，将随机探索转化为确定性重建。

Result: 在水下动态数据集和物理感知基准上取得更优渲染质量、时间一致性与鲁棒性；UVEB上PSNR比最强基线提升1.16 dB，DRUVA上提升3.99 dB。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模水下物理不确定性，PUGS有效克服确定性变形假设的局限，显著提升水下动态场景重建性能。

Abstract: Dynamic 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) provides an efficient framework for dynamic scene reconstruction. Most existing approaches model scene dynamics with deterministic deformation fields, assuming each Gaussian follows a distinct geometric trajectory over time. This assumption breaks down underwater, where light scattering, refraction, marine snow and low visibility create substantial physical ambiguity in captured images. We present PUGS, a physics-guided uncertainty model for underwater dynamic 3DGS. PUGS learns inherent uncertainty induced by underwater image formation and leverages it as a physical signal for dynamic scene modeling. Rather than estimating a single deterministic displacement, it parameterizes each Gaussian's motion as a probability distribution with expected deformation and physical uncertainty. We adopt stochastic deformation optimization to sample Gaussian trajectories via local reparameterization, exploring multiple physically plausible solutions during training. We further propose uncertainty stochastic annealing and a dual-path self-consistency distillation objective, converting stochastic exploration to deterministic reconstruction while preserving temporal coherence. Extensive experiments on underwater dynamic datasets and physics-aware benchmarks demonstrate superior rendering quality, temporal consistency and reconstruction robustness. Our method achieves 1.16 dB PSNR gain over the strongest baseline on UVEB and 3.99 dB on DRUVA.

</details>


### [226] [MERIT: Multimodal Evidence Reasoning over Image Tables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17937)
*Haoyang Jiang,Zhuotong Fan,Mingyang Yi,Ju Fan,Lanqing Hu,Xiao Zan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MERIT，一个在表格图像上进行多模态证据推理的三阶段框架，解决序列化表格丢失信息的问题，通过表格专用编码器适配、监督微调和强化学习提升表格问答与事实核查性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格推理通常假设可直接访问干净的文本表格，但现实中表格常被序列化为文本，导致单元格内容损坏、结构关系扭曲、视觉线索丢失，模型只能在不完整证据上推理。而表格图像保留了这些信息，但利用图像需要准确的感知和基于相关单元格的推理。

Method: MERIT分三阶段：(1)表格专用编码器适配，通过重建、定位和颜色条件监督来增强视觉证据获取；(2)基于适配表示进行监督微调，用验证过的轨迹教模型显式选择证据并推理；(3)强化学习，对证据正确性、计算、最终答案和输出格式分别设置奖励，对中间步骤反馈。序列化表格作为可选辅助输入。

Result: 在表格问答和事实核查任务上评估该方法，分析了视觉输入和各训练组件的贡献，并检验了对具有不同表格结构和视觉呈现的其他数据集的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 视觉证据获取是瓶颈，MERIT通过表格专用编码器适配、证据选择监督和分步强化学习，能有效利用表格图像进行推理，并具备跨不同表格结构数据集的泛化性。

Abstract: Table reasoning with language models typically assumes access to clean textual tables. In practice, tables are often serialized into text, which can corrupt cell contents, distort structural relationships, and discard visual cues, leaving the model to reason over incomplete evidence. In contrast, table images retain this information, but using it requires both accurate perception and reasoning grounded in the relevant cells. To address this, we propose MERIT (Multimodal Evidence Reasoning over Image Tables), a three-stage framework for learning to reason over table images, with serialized tables serving as optional auxiliary inputs (if provided). Our analysis identifies visual evidence acquisition as a bottleneck, motivating table-specific encoder adaptation through reconstruction, localization, and color-conditioned supervision. Building on the adapted representations, supervised fine-tuning teaches explicit evidence selection and reasoning using verified trajectories. Finally, reinforcement learning refines the policy with separate rewards for evidence correctness, calculation, final answers, and output format. By providing feedback on intermediate steps, the framework targets retrieval and computational errors that final-answer supervision alone does not distinguish. We evaluate the approach on table question answering and fact verification, analyze the contributions of visual inputs and training components, and assess generalization to additional datasets with different table structures and visual presentations.

</details>


### [227] [LensErase: A Foundation Model for Erase Anything](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18072)
*Yunuo Chen,Xiuyu Wu,Qinhong Yang,Jinjing Zhao,Yanchen Dong,Sirui Zhang,Dong Chen,Fangyun Wei,Lei Shi,Ji Li,Dongdong Chen,Guo Lu,Yan Lu,Baining Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LensErase是一个通用掩码擦除基础模型，覆盖多种擦除场景，构建了LensErase-100M数据集，并在多个基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: real-world擦除场景多样（对象、文本、水印、视觉效果、多对象、不同尺度与分辨率），现有模型覆盖不足。

Method: 构建101.07M配对数据集LensErase-100M；基于T2I模型Lens预训练；提出Mask-Aware RoPE将掩码信息注入源图像token；SFT+RL（合同引导、区域感知奖励）；推理时用TraceFusion保持编辑区外高保真。

Result: 在6个公开基准及LensErase-Bench上，参考指标和无参考VLM评估均取得显著提升。

Conclusion: LensErase有望成为擦除相关应用与系统的基础模型。

Abstract: We introduce LensErase, a general-purpose mask-based Erase Anything foundation model designed to cover a broad spectrum of real-world erase scenarios. LensErase supports diverse objects, visual text, watermarks, associated visual effects, multiple-object removal, and a wide range of object scales, aspect ratios, and image resolutions. First, to enable such broad coverage, we construct LensErase-100M, a large-scale dataset containing 101.07 million paired source-mask-target examples spanning diverse erase scenarios. Second, we pretrain LensErase from the text-to-image model Lens, adapting its strong generative prior to mask-based erasing. To efficiently incorporate the source image and erase mask, we propose Mask-Aware RoPE, a 3D rotary positional encoding that directly injects mask information into the source-image tokens without introducing a separate mask token sequence. Finally, we refine the model through supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning with contract-guided, region-aware rewards, and apply TraceFusion at inference to preserve high-fidelity content outside the effective edit region. Across six public benchmarks and the proposed LensErase-Bench, LensErase achieves substantial improvements under both reference-based objective metrics and reference-free VLM evaluation. We hope LensErase can serve as a foundational model for erase-related applications and systems.

</details>


### [228] [APT: Feedback-Driven Agentic Post-Training for Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18097)
*Sirui Zhang,Fangyun Wei,Dong Chen,Jinjing Zhao,Yitong Wang,Yunuo Chen,Baining Guo,Zheng-Jun Zha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: APT是一个全自动的文本到图像后训练框架，通过三个智能体协同完成评估集构建、能力诊断与自适应强化学习数据生成，形成递归自改进的反馈闭环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像后训练流程依赖人工整理训练数据和固定的训练分布，难以适应模型不断变化的能力特征，缺乏自动化与自适应能力。

Method: 协调三个智能体：从用户需求构建评估集、诊断模型特定的优势与失败模式、生成强调弱势能力的自适应提示分布强化学习数据；反复执行评估-诊断-更新生成器的循环，并额外设计了适用于无用户指定场景的通用评估工具。

Result: 在多种基础模型、基准测试与生成架构上的实验表明，APT具备模型无关、基准无关、架构无关且完全自动化的特性。

Conclusion: APT通过自适应、全自动的后训练流程提升文本到图像模型，验证了其通用性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Post-training is crucial for improving text-to-image models, yet existing pipelines typically rely on manually curated training data and fixed training distributions. We introduce APT (Agentic Post-Training), a fully automated framework that adaptively post-trains a base model according to its evolving capability profile. APT coordinates three agents to construct evaluation sets from user requirements, diagnose model-specific strengths and failure modes, and generate reinforcement learning data with an adaptive prompt distribution that emphasizes underperforming capabilities. By repeatedly evaluating, diagnosing, and updating the generator, APT lets the current model's behavior shape its next training tasks, forming a feedback loop inspired by recursive self-improvement. For general-purpose settings without user-specified evaluation scenarios, we further develop an evaluation harness that enables broad and diverse evaluation set generation. Experiments across multiple base models, benchmarks, and generation architectures demonstrate that APT is model-agnostic, benchmark-agnostic, architecture-agnostic, and fully automated.

</details>


### [229] [H-DDCM: Hadamard Codebooks for Fast Generative Image and Video Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18125)
*Or Ronai,Vladimir Kulikov,Tomer Michaeli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出H-DDCM，用隐式随机Hadamard字典替代高维高斯码本，实现可扩展、低内存低算力的免训练生成式图像/视频压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于码本的免训练生成式压缩方法（如Turbo-DDCM）虽在超低码率下感知质量强，但依赖稠密高斯码本，内存与计算开销大，难以扩展到大潜空间维度的现代图像/视频生成器，且组合比特流编码在高维下计算沉重。

Method: 用隐式随机Hadamard字典替代高斯码本，借助快速Walsh-Hadamard变换以O(d log d)计算和O(d)内存隐式合成全维字典，无需显式物化；提出无表的字典序编码方案使高维组合编码实用化；并将框架适配到自回归视频流模型，设计流式压缩，周期性将近期重建帧重新编码为新KV-cache前缀以在无额外传输比特下保持时序上下文。

Result: 在图像与视频基准上达到有竞争力的率-失真-感知性能，同时显著降低基于码本的免训练生成式压缩的内存与计算开销；支持单GPU上长时长、接近实时的生成式视频压缩。

Conclusion: H-DDCM通过Hadamard字典与高效编码提升可扩展性，并扩展至流式视频生成压缩，在保持感知质量的同时大幅降低资源开销。

Abstract: Training-free diffusion-based compression methods repurpose the generative priors of a diffusion or a flow model to compress data without retraining them as task-specific codecs. Recent codebook-based approaches such as Turbo-DDCM achieve strong perceptual quality at ultra-low bitrates, but their reliance on dense Gaussian codebooks remains costly in both memory and computation, and scales poorly with the large latent dimensions of modern image and video generators. Their combinatorial bitstream encoding further becomes computationally heavy at this scale. We introduce H-DDCM, a scalable generative compression framework that replaces Gaussian codebooks with implicit randomized Hadamard dictionaries. The fast Walsh-Hadamard transform allows for implicitly synthesizing full-dimensional dictionaries with $O(d \log d)$ compute for projection and reconstruction and $O(d)$ memory, without materializing the full-dimensional dictionary. We further introduce an efficient table-free lexicographic coding scheme that makes combinatorial encoding practical at larger dimensions. Beyond images, we adapt the framework to an autoregressive video flow model and develop a streaming compression scheme that periodically re-encodes recently reconstructed frames into a fresh KV-cache prefix, preserving temporal context across segments at no additional transmitted bits. This enables long-form, close to real-time generative video compression on a single GPU. Across image and video benchmarks, H-DDCM achieves competitive rate-distortion-perception performance while substantially reducing the memory and computational overhead of codebook-based training-free generative compression.

</details>


### [230] [Human-Inspired Asymmetric Collaboration for Dual-Student Semi-Supervised Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18133)
*Huiyi Ren,Ziming Fang,Jiaming Cai,Rui Xu,Haojie Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HAC闭环双学生框架，通过自适应难度校准与几何保持解决SSMIS中强增强难平衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SSMIS中，强增强不足会使双学生同质化，过度增强则破坏解剖证据并破坏协作稳定性，需动态平衡强弱学习。

Method: 1) RDC：将强增强控制建模为窗口级强化学习，利用增强历史、扰动量与训练响应,动态调整增强幅度、操作数量与幅度范围；2) G2PC：用全局体积与成对面积约束保持早期三支几何，防止共享监督导致同质化。

Result: 在LA、Pancreas、BraTS-2019上验证了分割效果及能力感知的难度校准。

Conclusion: 受人类反馈调节教学启发，HAC实现闭环自适应难度校准并保持几何多样性，有效提升半监督医学图像分割。

Abstract: Dual-student learning enriches predictive diversity in SSMIS by introducing a strongly augmented view alongside a weakly augmented view. Insufficient strong augmentation may homogenize the students, whereas excessive augmentation can corrupt anatomical evidence and destabilize collaboration. This challenge echoes feedback-regulated human instruction, where learners receive differentiated tasks and the task difficulty is continually adjusted according to their evolving responses. Inspired by these, we propose Human-Inspired Asymmetric Collaboration (HAC), a closed-loop dual-student framework that maintains weak--strong learning through adaptive difficulty calibration and geometry preservation. Specifically, Reinforcement-guided Difficulty Calibration (RDC) formulates strong-augmentation control as a window-level reinforcement learning process, using augmentation history, realized perturbations, and training responses to dynamically adjust the augmentation magnitude, operation number, and magnitude range. Meanwhile, the Gramian Geometry Preservation Constraint (G2PC) preserves the early-stage three-branch geometry through global volume and pairwise area constraints, preventing shared supervision from homogenizing the two students. Comprehensive evaluations across LA, Pancreas, and BraTS-2019 validate both the segmentation effectiveness and capability-aware difficulty calibration of HAC.

</details>


### [231] [FlowCred: Hierarchical Credit Assignment and Functional Coordination for Joint Text-to-Video Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18142)
*Mingxuan Zhu,Yanwei Huang,Shiyuan Liu,Xinzhi Zhang,Shujie Li,Yuanxing Zhang,Lu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlowCred 提出两级框架实现 T2V 联合后训练，通过分层信用分配与功能协调解耦提示与生成器的贡献，在多基准上稳定提升联合训练基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示优化与生成器后训练联合训练时，二者共享同一终端奖励，导致两个歧义：提示质量与随机生成混淆、两模块产生重叠的局部改进。需要一种机制分离各自贡献。

Method: 两级框架：1) 分层信用分配，用匹配的提示-种子 rollout 网格，在共享随机性下把提示级信用与提示条件下的生成器残差分离；2) 功能协调，在真实生成轨迹的状态上探测替代当前策略的提示，为提示优化提供生成器感知证据，同时选择性衰减已由条件变化支持的生成器更新。

Result: 在多个 T2V 基准上稳定优于匹配的联合训练基线。LTX-2.5 上，T2V-CompBench 从 61.47 提升到 64.46，StoryEval 从 55.58 提升到 58.08，且未增加解码视频或奖励模型调用预算。因子与实现更新分析显示提示塑造与生成器路由的互补贡献，人类评估及跨骨干、跨更新器家族的迁移提供进一步支持。

Conclusion: FlowCred 通过解耦提示与生成器的信用分配和功能协调，有效解决了 T2V 联合后训练中的奖励歧义问题，在保持预算不变的前提下实现一致提升，并具备良好泛化性。

Abstract: Prompt optimization and generator post-training are two complementary ways to improve text-to-video (T2V) generation, and recent systems increasingly train both modules jointly. However, optimizing a prompt policy and a video generator from the same terminal reward creates two ambiguities: prompt quality is confounded with stochastic generation, and the two modules may realize overlapping local improvements. We introduce FlowCred, a two-level framework for joint T2V post-training. First, hierarchical credit assignment uses matched prompt-seed rollout grids to separate prompt-level credit from prompt-conditioned generator residuals under shared randomness. Second, functional coordination probes alternative current-policy prompts at states from actual generation trajectories, providing generator-aware evidence for prompt optimization while selectively attenuating generator updates already supported by conditioning changes. Across multiple T2V benchmarks, FlowCred consistently improves matched joint-training baselines. On LTX-2.5, FlowCred improves a strictly matched joint-training control from 61.47 to 64.46 on T2V-CompBench and from 55.58 to 58.08 on StoryEval, without increasing decoded-video or reward-model-call budgets. Factorial and realized-update analyses show complementary contributions from prompt shaping and generator routing, while human evaluation and transfer across backbones and updater families provide further supporting evidence.

</details>


### [232] [Benchmarking Multimodal Large Language Models for Zero-Shot Human-Object Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18161)
*Oriol Rabasseda,Zenjie Li,Kamal Nasrollahi,Sergio Escalera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究多模态大模型(MLLM)在人物交互(HOI)识别与检测中的零样本能力，提出评测框架与视觉提示策略，并在HICO/HICO-DET/V-COCO上取得最优零样本性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统HOI方法依赖大量标注数据和固定交互分类体系，难以适应动态现实场景；而MLLM具备灵活零样本推理能力，但其在HOI上的潜力尚未被探索。

Method: 提出由外部目标检测器定位实体、零样本多模态推理理解交互的pipeline；构建零样本评测框架（含提示策略统一分类体系，以及将token级logits转为交互分数的方法）；构建新问答标注将标准HOI数据集转为适合MLLM的格式；提出"Objects as Context"和"Black Other Objects"两种视觉提示策略。

Result: 在HICO上达到53.50 mAP，在HICO-DET上达到23.69 mAP，实现最优零样本性能。

Conclusion: 为MLLM在HOI领域提供了有原则的评测框架，为新的模型与数据集开辟方向，推动灵活开放世界交互理解。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated strong zero-shot reasoning across diverse domains, but their potential for Human-Object Interaction (HOI) remains unexplored. Unlike traditional HOI approaches that rely on extensive labeled datasets and fixed interaction taxonomies, MLLMs offer flexible reasoning, making them well-suited for dynamic, real-world scenarios. In this work, we present the first systematic study of MLLMs for HOI recognition and detection within a pipeline that combines an external object detector for entity localization with zero-shot multimodal reasoning for interaction understanding. We introduce a benchmark framework for principled zero-shot evaluation, consisting of (i) a unified taxonomy of zero-shot prompting strategies, and (ii) a methodology that transforms token-level logits into interaction scores, enabling fair comparison with specialized HOI models under standard metrics. To support prompt-based evaluation, we additionally construct new question-answer annotations for standard HOI datasets, transforming them into a format suitable for MLLMs. Building on this, we propose two visual prompting strategies, namely Objects as Context and Black Other Objects, that guide MLLMs to target objects while preserving scene context. Experiments on HICO, HICO-DET, and V-COCO demonstrate state-of-the-art zero-shot performance, achieving 53.50 mAP on HICO and 23.69 mAP on HICO-DET. Overall, our work provides a principled benchmarking framework for MLLMs in HOI, opening the door to new models and datasets and enabling flexible open-world interaction understanding.

</details>


### [233] [VARI: Robust Text-to-Video Generation via Group-Structured Intent Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18189)
*Mingxuan Zhu,Hohei Chan,Shiyuan Liu,Yuanxing Zhang,Lu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VARI将同一视觉意图的多种验证改写形式作为共享监督单元，通过组一致性蒸馏与形式对比Flow-DPO提升文生视频的跨改写可靠性，无需额外推理模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生视频模型对提示词改写的语义实现和质量敏感；提示词改写只改推理输入，传统后训练每次只基于一种提示词形式构造监督，均未显式优化跨等价形式的可靠性。

Method: 构建匹配的形式-种子rollout池，让每个视频与所有形式交叉打分，并用措辞无关的结构化意图审计识别有效实现与硬语义失败。GCD对齐所有验证形式与可互换的有效实现；FC-DPO从审计的正负样本对学习组级偏好，优先同种子不同形式的对比。

Result: 在HunyuanVideo-1.5上，VARI在T2V-CompBench、VBench、StoryEval中取得最高分；在提示词形式扩展上，最弱形式对齐分别比最强提示级基线提升2.45和3.55分，跨形式下降减少30.5%和32.2%，并保持视觉质量。人类评估、自然caption控制、提示增强、多样性分析及迁移到LTX-2.3均支持其鲁棒性与可迁移性。

Conclusion: VARI通过组结构监督显式优化跨等价提示形式的可靠性，在多个基准和模型上提升对齐与跨形式稳定性，且无需额外推理时模块。

Abstract: Modern text-to-video (T2V) models generate visually compelling videos, yet meaning-preserving changes in prompt wording can substantially alter semantic realization and overall quality. Existing prompt rewriting modifies the inference input, whereas conventional post-training constructs supervision from one prompt formulation at a time; neither explicitly optimizes reliability across equivalent forms. We introduce VARI (Verified Alignment over Representations of Intent), a group-structured framework that treats automatically verified formulations of the same visual intent as a shared supervision unit. VARI builds matched form-seed rollout pools, cross-scores each video against all forms, and applies a wording-independent structured intent audit to identify valid realizations and hard semantic failures. Group-Consistent Distillation (GCD) aligns every verified form with interchangeable valid realizations, while Form-Contrastive Flow-DPO (FC-DPO) learns group-level preferences from audited positive-negative pairs, prioritizing same-seed, different-form contrasts. On HunyuanVideo-1.5, VARI attains the highest T2V-CompBench, VBench, and StoryEval scores among the evaluated methods. On prompt-form extensions of T2V-CompBench and VBench, it improves weakest-form alignment over the strongest prompt-level baselines by 2.45 and 3.55 points and reduces cross-form drop by 30.5% and 32.2%, respectively, while preserving visual quality. Human evaluation, natural-caption controls, prompt-enhancement experiments, diversity analyses, and transfer to LTX-2.3 further support the robustness and transferability of these gains, while VARI requires no additional inference-time module.

</details>


### [234] [Closing the Estimation Gap: Training-Free Transductive Inference for Cross-Domain Few-Shot Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18209)
*Changyi Li,Zeyu Yang,Yu Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过oracle分析揭示CD-FSAR的核心瓶颈在于原型与决策边界的估计误差(非表征问题),并提出免训练的直推式推理框架TRACE,在五个数据集上显著提升基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CD-FSAR推理依赖极少support样本估计原型的,导致估计瓶颈。作者用固定表征+ground-truth query标签构造oracle原型与决策边界,发现5-way 1-shot准确率提升7-20个百分点,证明表征蕴含足够判别信息,但稀缺support估计的原型/边界无法捕捉每episode真实类别结构。

Method: 提出免训练直推式推理框架TRACE,不修改backbone,含两阶段:1) CTPR(置信度阈值化直推式原型精化)用高置信query精化support原型;2) TCR(直推式分类器精化)以精化原型初始化轻量per-episode分类器,并通过鼓励对全部query的置信、类别均衡预测进一步适配。

Result: 在HMDB51、UCF101、SSv2、Diving48、RareAct五个数据集,1-shot和5-shot设置下均稳定提升强CD-FSAR基线,HMDB51 1-shot提升超6个百分点,并弥补了大部分oracle估计出的差距。

Conclusion: 在极端support稀缺下,改进测试时估计是表征学习的强有力且通用的补充手段,可在不改变backbone的情况下显著提升CD-FSAR性能。

Abstract: Cross-domain few-shot action recognition (CD-FSAR) aims to recognise unseen action classes in a target domain from a handful of labelled videos. Despite advances in representation learning, CD-FSAR inference still relies on prototypes estimated from extremely limited support examples. To assess this bottleneck, we perform an oracle analysis with the representation fixed. Constructing oracle prototypes and decision boundaries from ground-truth query labels improves 5-way 1-shot accuracy by 7--20 percentage points across five target datasets. This reveals a substantial estimation gap: the representation contains useful discriminative information, but prototypes and boundaries estimated from scarce supports fail to capture each episode's true class structure. Motivated by this, we propose TRACE, a training-free transductive inference framework that improves episode-level estimation without modifying the backbone. TRACE comprises two complementary stages. Confidence-Thresholded Transductive Prototype Refinement (CTPR) refines support-based prototypes using confidently predicted queries. The refined prototypes initialise a lightweight per-episode classifier, which Transductive Classifier Refinement (TCR) further adapts by encouraging confident, class-balanced predictions over all queries. Together, the two stages improve prototype and boundary estimation, enabling better use of information in the unlabelled queries. Across five target datasets (HMDB51, UCF101, SSv2, Diving48, and RareAct) in both 1-shot and 5-shot settings, TRACE consistently improves a strong CD-FSAR baseline, including gains of more than six percentage points on HMDB51 1-shot, while recovering much of the oracle-estimated gap. These results suggest that improving test-time estimation is a powerful, broadly applicable complement to representation learning for CD-FSAR under extreme support scarcity.

</details>


### [235] [Heading Hierarchy Reconstruction for Complex and Long Documents with Level-Wise Multimodal Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18238)
*Qian Chen,Xianyin Zhang,Lifan Guo,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于VLM的文档标题层级重建框架，利用层级多模态记忆增量式重建标题结构，并构建CLDoc-HHRBench基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在文档解析和OCR中表现良好，但下游任务（如RAG）需要文档级结构一致性。由于输入分辨率和上下文长度限制，VLM无法同时处理数十甚至数百页的文档图像，逐页预测缺乏文档级一致性。

Method: 提出VLM框架，使用"层级多模态记忆"增量式重建标题层级。记忆通过锚点选择保留先前处理页面的一小部分，支持灵活的锚点选择策略；本文选择每一层最近的节点，使VLM能观察到各层级标题的视觉、文本和布局模式。

Result: 在CLDoc-HHRBench（250个文档、11,212页、专家验证标注）及三个现有基准上的实验证明了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法能在不超出上下文限制的情况下实现文档级标题层级一致性重建，并提供了新的评测基准。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have been widely adopted for document parsing and optical character recognition (OCR), as they can accurately extract page-level elements. However, downstream applications such as retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) also require coherent document-level structure. Although these models indicate heading levels using different numbers of preceding ``\#'' symbols, page-by-page prediction often lacks document-level consistency. In practice, input-resolution and context-length constraints make it infeasible for a VLM to jointly process all page images in documents containing tens or hundreds of pages. To address this limitation, we propose a VLM-based framework that incrementally reconstructs heading hierarchies with a \emph{level-wise multimodal memory}. The memory retains a compact subset of all previously processed pages, selected by anchors. Our framework supports flexible anchor selection, and in this work we select the most recent node at each level, allowing the VLM to observe the visual, textual, and layout patterns of headings at every level. For comprehensive evaluation, we also introduce the Complex and Long Document Heading Hierarchy Reconstruction Benchmark (CLDoc-HHRBench), comprising 250 documents and 11,212 pages with expert-verified annotations. Experiments on CLDoc-HHRBench and three existing benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of our method.

</details>


### [236] [LiFUSE: Frequency-Spatial Unified Self-Supervised Pretraining for Liver Disease Analysis on Non-contrast CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18243)
*Hao Zhang,Yi Huang,Wei Liu,Yuan Gao,Zeju Li,Xu Han,Yu Shi,Ling Zhang,Ke Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LiFUSE自监督预训练框架，结合频域与空域学习，提升NCCT肝脏疾病分析性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球肝肿瘤与弥漫性肝病负担加重，NCCT因低成本、安全性好被用于机会性筛查，但其组织对比度低、缺乏血流动力学增强、解剖背景复杂，导致肿瘤检测和细粒度诊断困难。体素级重建无法充分利用NCCT中的互补信息，低层强度模式对病理识别信息量不足。

Method: LiFUSE包含频域分支：随机带阻掩码与频谱重建，捕捉病灶与周围组织的细微差异；空域分支：混合粒度掩码体素重建，适应不同尺寸和形态的病灶；跨域一致性方案耦合空间与频率分支，产生更具判别力的肝脏结构与病灶表征。

Result: 在内部队列的三个临床相关任务及公开目标域迁移设置上，LiFUSE 持续优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 统一的频率-空间学习可改善NCCT上的肝病分析，展现大规模筛查潜力。

Abstract: Given the growing global burden of liver tumors and diffuse liver diseases, non-contrast computed tomography (NCCT) is increasingly used for opportunistic screening because of its low cost and favorable safety profile. However, downstream tasks such as tumor detection and fine-grained disease diagnosis remain challenging due to NCCT's inherently low tissue contrast, lack of hemodynamic enhancement, and complex anatomical background. These limitations make subtle, heterogeneous, and multifocal hepatic lesions difficult to identify. Although large-scale self-supervised pretraining can learn robust representations from unlabeled NCCT scans, conventional voxel-wise reconstruction does not fully exploit the complementary information available in NCCT. Under weak NCCT contrast, low-level intensity patterns become less informative for pathology recognition, limiting the capture of lesion-specific discriminative features. In this paper, we propose the Liver Frequency-Spatial Unified Self-Supervised Pretraining (LiFUSE). The frequency branch applies random band-stop masking and spectrum reconstruction to encourage the model to capture subtle differences between lesions and surrounding tissues, while the spatial branch performs hybrid-granularity masked volumetric reconstruction to accommodate lesions of diverse sizes and morphologies. Finally, a cross-domain consistency scheme couples the spatial and frequency branches, yielding more discriminative representations for hepatic structures and lesions. Extensive experiments on three clinically relevant tasks across internal cohorts and a public target-domain transfer setting show that LiFUSE consistently outperforms strong baselines. These results suggest that unified frequency-spatial learning can improve liver disease analysis on NCCT, highlighting its potential for large-scale screening.

</details>


### [237] [FALLNEST: Dual-Domain Dataset Condensation of Imbalanced IMU Data for Wearable Fall Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18260)
*Rashi Niyas P,Ritika Jain,Hitika Tiwari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FALLNEST，一种数据中心的可穿戴跌倒检测框架，将大型不平衡IMU数据集蒸馏为极小类平衡合成训练集，用32-146个合成窗口即可让TinyCNN达到93.5-95.8%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴跌倒检测面临跌倒事件稀少、标注IMU数据稀缺、在不平衡语料上训练深度模型成本高的问题。现有方法或依赖手工特征与浅层分类器（限制原始信号学习），或在全数据集上训练深度网络（需重采样且消耗大量数据和算力）。

Method: FALLNEST基于双域时间序列蒸馏（CondTSC），引入跌倒专用7通道输入（含加速度向量幅值）、类平衡K-Means初始化、带梯度裁剪的归一化梯度匹配，并结合基于MMD的嵌入对齐与多视角傅里叶域增强，使类别平衡源于蒸馏集本身而非重采样。

Result: 在SisFall、KFall和MobiFall上用严格按被试划分及自然不平衡测试集，跨5个随机种子报告准确率、Macro-F1和PR-AUC。仅用32-146个合成窗口（约训练窗口的1%）训练的102K参数TinyCNN达到93.5-95.8%平均准确率和0.89-0.98 PR-AUC，准确率标准差1.0-1.7%。

Conclusion: 实验证明该数据中心蒸馏方法有效：用极少量类平衡合成样本即可替代全数据集训练，实现高准确率与低方差，缓解了跌倒检测中的数据稀缺与类别不平衡问题。

Abstract: Wearable fall detection is hampered by rare fall events, scarce labeled IMU data, and the cost of training deep models on large, imbalanced corpora. Existing methods either rely on hand-crafted features with shallow classifiers, which limits learning from raw signals, or train deep networks on full datasets that require re-sampling to counter class skew and demand substantial data and compute. To address this, we propose a novel Fall detection framework via Normalized-gradient and Embedding matched Synthetic Time-frequency condensation, dubbed FALLNEST, a data-centric approach that distills large, imbalanced IMU datasets into tiny, class-balanced synthetic training sets. Building on dual-domain time-series condensation (CondTSC), FALLNEST introduces a fall-specific 7-channel input with accelerometer vector magnitude, class-balanced K-Means initialization, and normalized gradient matching with gradient clipping, combined with MMD-based embedding alignment and multi-view Fourier-domain augmentation, so that class balance arises from the condensed set itself rather than from re-sampling. We evaluate on SisFall, KFall, and MobiFall using strict subject-wise splits and naturally imbalanced test sets, reporting accuracy, Macro-F1, and PR-AUC over five seeds. A 102K-parameter TinyCNN trained on only 32 to 146 synthetic windows (about only 1% of the training windows) reaches 93.5-95.8% mean accuracy and 0.89-0.98 PR-AUC, with standard deviations of 1.0-1.7% in accuracy, demonstrating the efficacy of our approach.

</details>


### [238] [Traditional and Recent Approaches for Infrared Small Target Detection : Overview, Challenges and Perspectives (Part 1)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18276)
*Thierry Bouwmans,Anastasia Zakharova,Belmar Garcia,Marwa Chendeb El Rai,Meghna Kapoor,Badri Narayan Subudhi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文是一篇红外小目标检测的综述，按数学模型、机器学习模型和信号处理模型分类梳理传统与近期方法，讨论其应对的关键场景，并提供资源、数据集、库及未来方向。全文分两部分：Part 1 关注传统及低秩稀疏分解（模型驱动）方法，Part 2 关注深度学习（数据驱动）方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外小目标检测广泛用于地面、空中、海上监视等民用与军事场景，检测静态或运动目标。该领域已有约30年研究历史，近二十年因低秩稀疏分解（模型驱动）与深度学习（数据驱动）兴起而成果显著。现有文献虽有多篇综述，但缺少覆盖全领域的整体性回顾，故本文旨在提供完整综述。

Method: 按建模方式将文献方法分为数学模型、机器学习模型和信号处理模型三类；再按各方法声称可应对的关键困难场景进行讨论；并整理现有资源、数据集与工具库。综述分两部分：Part 1 覆盖传统方法与低秩稀疏分解（模型驱动方法），Part 2 覆盖深度学习方法（数据驱动方法）。

Result: 提供了红外小目标检测领域传统与近期方法的完整分类与对比框架，涵盖挑战场景分析，并汇总了可用资源、数据集和库。

Conclusion: 总结了若干有前景的未来研究方向，为红外小目标检测的后续研究提供整体性参考。

Abstract: Infrared small target detection is often used in different civilian and military applications to detect static or moving targets in scenes like in ground, air and sea surveillance. First valuable research has emerged since 30 years ago and the two last decades witnessed very significant publications in this field following the rise of low-rank and sparse decomposition (model-based methods) and deep learning models (data-based methods). Furthermore, several surveys can be found in the literature but none of them addresses an overall review in this field. In this context, the aim of this paper is to provide a complete survey of the traditional and recent approaches in infrared small target detection. First, we categorize the different approaches found in the literature in terms of the modeling models, i.e mathematical models, machine learning models and signal processing models. Then, we have discussed them in terms of the critical situations that they claim to handle. Furthermore, we present the available resources, datasets and libraries. Then, we conclude with several promising directions for future research. For the sake of reading, this survey is divided into two parts: 1) "Traditional and Recent Approaches for Infrared Small Target Detection : Overview, Challenges and Perspectives (Part 1)" about conventional and low-rank and sparse decomposition (model-based methods), and 2) "Traditional and Recent Approaches for Infrared Small Target Detection : Overview, Challenges and Perspectives (Part 2)" about deep learning models (data-based methods).

</details>


### [239] [Efficient Scene-Text Refinement via Pixel-Space Grouped Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18284)
*Yaoli Liu,Guo-Hua Wang,Yiyang Chen,Qing-Guo Chen,Zhao Xu,Weihua Luo,Yao-Xiang Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PixText 将局部场景文本精修建模为像素空间的分组图像生成，仅生成目标区域并保持背景不变，配合 MeanFlow 四步生成，在文本精修基准上大幅超越基线且高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成图像整体布局与内容连贯，但小文本和复杂字符的局部错误影响可读性与实用性。场景文本精修需兼顾准确性、保持性与效率。现有 VAE 方法重建时易丢失细小笔画，全图编辑又会改变背景颜色。

Method: PixText 以原图为条件，在像素空间中进行分组图像生成，只生成目标区域、保留未编辑内容；并采用 MeanFlow 实现四步生成。同时提出 Text Refinement Bench 基准，用于评估生成图像中的文本纠正与图像保持能力。

Result: 在该基准上，PixText 达到 83.06% 字形通过率与 94.66% 精确目标字符 OCR 准确率，而最强基线分别为 36.77% 与 52.31%。其四步 MeanFlow 变体仍保有 81.84% 通过率，每图摊销生成成本低于 1 GPU 秒。

Conclusion: PixText 通过局部像素空间生成有效解决文本精修的准确性与保持性问题，并在效率上具备实用性，同时提供了相应评测基准。

Abstract: Generated images often have coherent layouts and visual content, yet local errors in small text and complex characters still limit their readability and practical use. Scene-text refinement requires accuracy, preservation, and efficiency. Existing methods based on variational autoencoders (VAEs) can lose fine strokes during reconstruction, while full-image editing can shift background colors. In this work, we introduce PixText, a model that formulates local text refinement as grouped image generation in pixel space. Conditioned on the original image, PixText generates only the target regions, preserves unedited content, and uses MeanFlow for four-step generation. We further introduce Text Refinement Bench to evaluate text correction and image preservation in generated images. On this benchmark, PixText achieves 83.06% glyph pass and 94.66% exact target-character OCR accuracy, versus 36.77% and 52.31% for the strongest baseline. Its four-step MeanFlow variant retains an 81.84% pass rate with less than one GPU-second of amortized generation cost per image.

</details>


### [240] [LongForcing: Scaling Temporal Manifold Matching for Long-Horizon World Rollout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18289)
*Ziyu Zhu,Zhaoxu Sun,Fan Jiang,Mengchao Wang,Junfeng Ni,Ting-Bing Xu,Zhicheng Liu,Mu Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LongForcing通过将教师监督扩展到长学生自回归rollout，实现时间轴上的分布匹配，缓解长时域世界模型中的渐进漂移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散模型在超出训练窗口后会出现渐进漂移：画质下降、运动不一致、物体身份崩溃。根源是"监督时域差距"——短窗口蒸馏缺乏对后期rollout状态的直接监督，匹配短窗口边缘分布无法确定长时域行为。

Method: LongForcing是一种蒸馏方法，将教师监督扩展到较长的学生自回归rollout。保留短时域teacher-forcing和因果ODE蒸馏阶段，在最后分布匹配阶段用扩展时域的双向教师替换realscore教师，对长学生rollout用标准分布匹配公式监督，不改变学生架构也不增加推理成本。

Result: 在文本到视频生成上取得VBench Quality 85.52；在交互式动作条件世界模型中，WorldRoamBench 60s动作分数提升23.9分。定性压力测试可采样长达24小时的交互rollout。

Conclusion: 沿时间轴扩展监督是迈向长时域世界模型的实用步骤。

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion models have emerged as a foundation for long-horizon world rollout. However, when extended far beyond their training windows, these models suffer from progressive drift: visual quality deteriorates, motion becomes inconsistent, and object identity gradually collapses as generated frames are recursively reused as context. We study a supervision-horizon gap: short-horizon distillation leaves later rollout states without direct supervision. We show that matching short-window marginals need not determine long-horizon behavior, while longer on-policy trajectories expose delayed failures to training. This motivates temporal manifold matching at scale for long-horizon world models. Building on this insight, we introduce LongForcing, a distillation approach that scales teacher supervision to long student self-rollouts. LongForcing preserves the short-horizon teacher-forcing and causal ODE-distillation stages, while replacing the realscore teacher in the final distribution-matching stage with an extended-horizon bidirectional teacher. The long-horizon teacher supervises extended student self-rollouts using the standard distribution-matching formulation, without modifying the student architecture or increasing inference cost. We evaluate LongForcing on text-to-video generation and interactive action-conditioned world models, achieving a VBench Quality score of 85.52 and improving WorldRoamBench 60s action score by 23.9 points in first-person ablations. Qualitative stress tests further sample interactive rollouts up to 24 hours, far beyond the training horizon. These results suggest that scaling supervision along the temporal axis is a practical step toward long-horizon world models. The project website and code are at https://github.com/Fantasy-AMAP/LongForcing.

</details>


### [241] [Unsupervised and Supervised Approaches for Infrared Small Target Detection : Overview, Challenges and Perspectives (Part 2)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18291)
*Thierry Bouwmans,Anastasia Zakharova,Belmar Garcia,Marwa Chendeb El Rai,Meghna Kapoor,Badri Narayan Subudhi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于深度学习的红外小目标检测方法，按建模策略分类，讨论其应对的关键场景与性能评估，并展望未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督式深度学习兴起后，研究者尝试用CNN、GAN、Transformer、混合专家及图/超图神经网络等方法解决红外小目标检测问题，且性能已超越传统无监督及低秩稀疏分解方法。为系统梳理该领域深度学习模型的研究进展，作者撰写本综述。

Method: 对文献中的深度学习方法进行系统性总结：首先按建模模型/策略对各类方法进行分类，随后依据它们声称可处理的关键情形展开讨论，并介绍针对性的性能评估方式。

Result: 提供了深度学习红外小目标检测的完整综述，涵盖方法分类、所应对的关键场景及性能评估，并总结出若干有前景的未来研究方向。

Conclusion: 深度学习模型在红外小目标检测中已超越传统无监督方法，本综述为其分类、评估与未来发展提供了系统参考。

Abstract: Since the rise of supervised deep learning modelet dets, researchers has attempt to solve infrared small targection using various models like conventional neural networks, GANS, Transformers, Mixture of Experts and recent graph/hypergraph neural networks. Moreover, these deep neural networks methods surpassed the traditional unsupervised models and the low-rank and sparse decomposition unsupervised models reviewed in "Traditional and Recent Approaches for Infrared Small Target Detection : Overview, Challenges and Perspectives (Part 1). In this context, the aim of this paper is to provide a complete survey of the deep learning models in infrared small target detection. First, we categorize the different approaches found in the literature in terms of the modeling models of strategies. Then, we have discussed them in terms of the critical situations that they claim to handle. Furthermore, we present the specific performance evaluation. Then, we conclude with several promising directions for future research.

</details>


### [242] [Robust Logical Anomaly Detection under Capture Shifts via Synthetic Anomalies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18295)
*Ufuk Efe,Alptekin Temizel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出逻辑异常检测方法在采集条件变化下鲁棒性差，并提出一种基于分类器（由语言模型生成合成异常）的框架来提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有逻辑异常检测器依赖测试图像与正常参考图像的距离打分，在标准基准（如MVTec LOCO）上近乎完美，但对其在采集条件变化下的鲁棒性缺乏考察。

Method: 提出一个不依赖与正常数据比较的框架：语言模型从产品描述中推导异常类型和编辑指令，图像编辑器将其应用于正常图像生成合成异常，分类器学习区分合成异常与自动发现的正常子模式。

Result: 在异构扰动协议下，正常参考方法损失9.7至34.1 AUROC点（CSAD从97.7%降至68.1%，SALAD从96.4%降至62.3%）；本框架在干净图像达94.9%、扰动下94.1%，无扰动增强训练时仍达92.5%（干净93.2%）。

Conclusion: 干净的基准分数可能掩盖对采集条件的强敏感性，从正常图像和规则违背两方面学习决策可降低这种敏感性。

Abstract: Detectors of logical anomalies, such as missing, extra or wrongly combined parts, usually score a test image by its distance from normal (good) images. They achieve near-perfect image-level AUROC on standard industrial anomaly detection benchmarks such as MVTec LOCO, but their robustness to changes in capture conditions remains largely unexamined. Under a heterogeneous perturbation protocol in which each test image is randomly kept unaltered or subjected to a brightness reduction or a zoom-out transformation, the normal-reference methods we tested lose 9.7 to 34.1 AUROC points, and two of the strongest, CSAD and SALAD, fall from 97.7% and 96.4% to 68.1% and 62.3%. We propose a framework in which a classifier, trained on normal images and on synthetic anomalies, scores a test image without comparing it with normal data. From a short description of each product, a language model derives anomaly types and editing instructions, an image editor applies them to normal images, and the classifier learns to separate the edited images from automatically discovered normal sub-modes. With its full training recipe, the framework reaches 94.9% AUROC on clean images, 1.5 to 2.8 points below SALAD and CSAD, and 94.1% under the perturbation protocol. Trained without brightness or zoom-out augmentation, so that neither test perturbation occurs in training, it still reaches 92.5% (93.2% clean). These results suggest that clean benchmark scores can hide a strong sensitivity to capture conditions, and that learning the decision from both normal images and rule violations may reduce it.

</details>


### [243] [AffectWorld: Benchmarking Emotion-Driven Future World Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18305)
*Changhao Song,Yacong Chen,Hui Gao,Yazhou Zhang,Peng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AffectWorld基准，评估世界模型从情感→行动→世界状态的因果链建模能力，揭示现有模型在情感驱动世界演化上的显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界演化既受物理规律也受人类情感影响，但现有世界模型基准主要评估物理合理性与感知质量，忽视情感驱动的世界演化，缺乏相关测试。

Method: 构建AffectWorld基准，含1,498个日常视频样本，标注情感、行动倾向与原子世界状态约束；设三项任务（世界状态一致性、情感-行动一致性、因果一致性）及233个反事实子集，定义8项指标加1项反事实指标，结合自动视觉评估与人类校准的VLM/LLM判断。

Result: 五个SoTA世界模型（如Cosmos3）世界状态变化得分为0.100-0.280，反事实情感敏感度得分仅0.027-0.070（[0,1]），在生成情感驱动行动后果及适应情感干预方面存在显著差距。

Conclusion: AffectWorld为推进能够捕捉情感如何塑造未来物理与社会状态的世界模型提供了测试平台。

Abstract: The world encompasses both physical environments and human actors. Predicting its evolution requires understanding both physical laws and how emotions shape human actions. However, existing world model benchmarks primarily assess physical plausibility and perceptual quality, leaving emotion-driven world evolution largely untested.We introduce AffectWorld, a benchmark that evaluates the chain Emotion-Action-World State through three tasks: World-State Consistency, Emotion-Action Consistency, and Causal Consistency. It comprises 1,498 everyday video samples annotated with plausible emotions, action tendencies, and atomic world-state constraints. A counterfactual subset of 233 samples tests whether changing an agent's emotion under fixed scene and event conditions yields corresponding changes in actions and consequences. We define eight metrics across the three tasks and an additional counterfactual metric, combining automatic visual assessment with human-calibrated VLM/LLM judgments. Evaluations of five SoTA world models (e.g., Cosmos3) required world-state change scores of 0.100-0.280 and counterfactual emotion sensitivity scores of 0.027-0.070 on a [0, 1] scale. These results reveal substantial gaps in generating the consequences of emotion-driven actions and adapting future predictions to emotional interventions. AffectWorld provides a testbed for advancing world models that capture how emotions shape future physical and social states.

</details>


### [244] [Omni-RS: Unifying Multi-Task Understanding and Generation in Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18323)
*Pan Yin. Shuai Yang,Yuanhao Liu,Datao Tang,Jialin Li,Hao Wang,Pei Liu,Kaiyu Li,Cong Zhao,Xiangyong Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Omni-RS统一框架，支持4个遥感理解任务和5个生成任务，通过场景引导两阶段训练和多任务数据集UniGen实现理解与生成一体化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感应用常需同时具备对同一地理场景的理解与生成能力，但现有方法多孤立处理理解或生成，已有统一遥感模型生成能力仅限文本到图像。
