Geometry foundation models recover this structure from RGB, but existing methods either fuse the estimated features with visual tokens and learn to use them from question answering alone, or align VLM latents with geometry features. Capturing geometry, however, does not ensure its use: zeroing the aligned latents of a released feature-alignment model leaves its accuracy unchanged. We introduce GeoReader, a framework that learns geometric representations for the VLM that reads them. First, we train lightweight Geometric Readers to produce continuous input tokens, supervising them with geometry descriptions decoded by a frozen VLM, so that the Readers express geometry in a form the target model can already read. We then freeze the Readers to preserve this interface while fine-tuning the VLM on spatial QA, with geometry injection conditioned on the question. Geometry descriptions serve only as training targets: at inference, the VLM answers directly from the continuous geometric tokens without generating a geometry chain of thought. With geometry predicted from RGB images, our approach reaches 94.3 on MindCube-Tiny with Qwen3-VL-4B and, with a multi-source QA mixture, an average of 72.5 across six additional spatial benchmarks with Qwen3.5-9B. These results suggest that a useful geometric representation is one the receiving model can use, not merely one from which geometry can be reconstructed.

</details>


### [281] [CryoTracer: A Synthetic-to-Real System for Filament Instance Tracing in Cryo-EM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18696)
*Radosław Kuczbański,Wojciech Kozłowski,Mateusz Grzesiuk,Konrad Karanowski,Lukas Frey,Andrzej Rzepiela,Maciej Zięba*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CryoTracer 提出一种从合成到真实的框架，用程序化显微图生成、基于 Transformer 的折线预测和样本特定微调，将冷冻电镜中的丝状体直接表示为有序中心线折线，实现端到端实例级追踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冷冻电镜颗粒拾取常被建模为边界框目标检测，适合球形颗粒但不适合可横跨整个显微图的丝状体。现有方法先做局部预测再后处理连接，将检测与实例构建解耦，在间隙和交叉处产生歧义，且难以保持同一丝状体的实例一致性（尤其对多晶型样本）。而专家中心线标注稀缺且昂贵。

Method: 采用合成到真实的框架：(1) 程序化生成多样丝状体几何、交叉与成像条件的显微图；(2) 用 Transformer 进行折线预测；(3) 用有限的完全标注实验显微图做样本特定微调。将丝状体表示为有序中心线折线，并用方向不变的离散 Fréchet 目标优化几何，使追踪方向无关。

Result: 在既有数据集和新构建的专家标注数据集上，CryoTracer 在实例级丝状体恢复上优于现有颗粒检测方法。

Conclusion: 端到端折线预测是检测-连接流程的实用替代方案，可在有限显微图标注预算下恢复完整丝状体实例。

Abstract: Particle picking in cryo-electron microscopy is commonly formulated as an object-detection problem in which individual particles are represented by bounding boxes, a natural representation for globular particles but a poor match for filaments that can extend across an entire micrograph. Existing methods reconstruct filament trajectories by linking local predictions in a separate post-processing stage, decoupling detection from instance formation and creating potential ambiguities at gaps and crossings. Directly preserving filament identity is particularly useful for polymorphic specimens, where segments originating from the same filament should remain associated. However, learning such instance-level representations is challenging because expert centerline annotations are scarce and costly to obtain. We introduce CryoTracer, a synthetic-to-real framework that addresses this limitation through procedural micrograph generation, transformer-based polyline prediction, and specimen-specific fine-tuning. Procedural pretraining exposes the model to diverse filament geometries, intersections, and imaging conditions before adaptation using a limited number of fully annotated experimental micrographs. CryoTracer represents filaments as ordered centerline polylines and optimizes their geometry using an orientation-invariant discrete Fréchet objective, enabling consistent learning regardless of tracing direction. Evaluation on established and newly expert-annotated datasets shows that CryoTracer improves instance-level filament recovery over existing particle-detection approaches. These results establish end-to-end polyline prediction as a practical alternative to detection-and-linking pipelines for recovering complete filament instances with limited micrograph annotation budgets. Code and data are available at https://github.com/cryotracer/cryotracer

</details>


### [282] [AIRRI: Towards a Human-Readable, Machine-Resistant Reading Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18724)
*Emory Salaun,Syed Ayon,Kaleel Mahmood,Shaun Wallace*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AIRRI框架通过图像变换和对抗攻击降低图像转文字系统可靠性，对抗AI辅助作业完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像转文字系统和LLM的进步使作业材料易于被自动转录和求解，对人类评估公平性构成威胁，需要生成抗机器识别的教育内容。

Method: AIRRI采用两类技术：(1)图像变换，含字符/词/行间距、字符不透明度变化、条纹覆盖、图像噪声；(2)决策型对抗攻击RayS、SurFree、ADBA。用Tesseract、EasyOCR、GOT-OCR2三个系统评估。

Result: 字符错误率上升：EasyOCR 6.9%→55.9%，Tesseract 2.3%→100%，GOT-OCR2 30.7%→48.1%；词错误率上升：EasyOCR 24.9%→96.7%，Tesseract 10.7%→100%。ADBA降级效果最强，RayS和SurFree较温和。EasyOCR和Tesseract比GOT-OCR2更易受扰动。

Conclusion: AIRRI能显著降低图像转文字到LLM流程的可靠性，增加AI辅助作业完成的难度。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of image-to-text extraction systems and large language models (LLMs) poses new challenges for educational assessment by making assignment materials easy to transcribe and solve with minimal human effort. To address this problem, we present AIRRI (Artificial Intelligence Resilient Reading Interface), a framework for generating machine-resistant educational content, a dataset of higher-education assignments for evaluation, and an initial evaluation of the human readability of AIRRI machine-resistant text designs. AIRRI employs two categories of machine-resistance techniques. First, AIRRI applies image transformations including character spacing, word spacing, line spacing, character opacity variation, stripe overlays, and image noise. Second, AIRRI applies decision-based adversarial attacks: RayS, SurFree, and ADBA. We evaluate AIRRI against three image-to-text systems: Tesseract, EasyOCR, and the transformer-based GOT-OCR2 model. Our results show that AIRRI substantially degrades automated text extraction performance. AIRRI increased character error rates for EasyOCR (6.9% to 55.9%), Tesseract (2.3% to 100.0%), and GOT-OCR2 (30.7% to 48.1%). Word error rates increased for EasyOCR (24.9% to 96.7%) and Tesseract (10.7% to 100.0%). Among the evaluated decision-based attacks, ADBA consistently produced the largest degradation in image-to-text performance, while RayS and SurFree produced more moderate reductions. EasyOCR and Tesseract were generally more susceptible to both image-based and adversarial perturbations than the transformer-based GOT-OCR2 model. Our findings suggest that AIRRI can significantly reduce the reliability of image-to-text-to-LLM workflows, increasing the effort required for AI-assisted assignment completion.

</details>


### [283] [Segment Where You Look: Attention as the Interface Between Language Models and Segmenters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18729)
*Eslam Abdelrahman,Hamza Omar Alkaf,Yuheng Li,Siddharth Srinivasan Iyer,Nanxuan Zhao,Eli Shechtman,Krishna Kumar Singh,Mohamed Elhoseiny*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AttnSeg，用[SEG] token对图像token的注意力作为像素解码器的空间提示，替代无空间信息的单[SEG]隐状态。在Sa2VA上ReasonSeg提升6.4 gIoU、GS-Eval提升10.2(4B)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指代分割VLM把指代位置压缩成单个[SEG] token的d维隐状态，无空间索引，却让从未见过文本表达的解码器自行恢复指代范围。作者假设VLM本身已计算了更好的信号：即[SEG] token对图像token的注意力，天然定义在patch网格上。

Method: 冻结VLM探针实验验证注意力优于隐状态后，AttnSeg用该注意力替代[SEG]嵌入作为解码器提示：学习一个带符号的注意力头组合构成稠密空间提示，并加入注意力引导目标直接监督。采用两阶段训练：先让VLM在无解码器情况下学习定位，再训练解码器精修。

Result: 探针显示注意力比[SEG]隐状态多18–22 gIoU，集中于后层，且不依赖图像增益。应用于Sa2VA：4B上ReasonSeg提升6.4 gIoU、GS-Eval提升10.2（相比同数据微调模型为4.2和9.6），14B上为8.8和11.3；物体部件、多物体、单物体任务提升13–18 gIoU，stuff区域提升可忽略。

Conclusion: [SEG]注意力是比隐状态更有效的空间定位信号，AttnSeg以轻量改动显著提升指代分割，尤其在需精确指认的物体任务上，而stuff区域解码器本已能自行恢复。代码与模型已开源。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) that segment from language must pass the location of the referent to a pixel decoder. The dominant interface compresses it into the hidden state of a single [SEG] token, a d-dimensional vector with no spatial index, and leaves the decoder, which never sees the expression, to recover the referent's extent. We show that the VLM already computes a better signal: the [SEG] token's attention over the image tokens, which is defined on the patch grid by construction. Probing a frozen segmentation VLM, we find that this attention yields 18 to 22 more gIoU than the [SEG] hidden state when read by the same probe, that it gains nothing from the image where the hidden state does, and that it is concentrated in the late layers. Building on these findings, we introduce AttnSeg, which replaces the [SEG] embedding with this attention as the decoder's prompt. A learned, signed combination of the attention heads forms a dense spatial prompt, and an attention-guidance objective supervises it directly. A two-stage recipe first trains the VLM to localize without the decoder, then trains the decoder to refine, within the budget of standard fine-tuning. Applied to Sa2VA, AttnSeg improves ReasonSeg by 6.4 gIoU and GS-Eval by 10.2 at 4B (4.2 and 9.6 over the same model fine-tuned on the same data), and by 8.8 and 11.3 at 14B. The gains reach 13 to 18 gIoU on object parts, multiple objects, and single objects, where the referent must be singled out, and are negligible on stuff regions, which the decoder already recovers unaided. Code and models are available at https://eslambakr.github.io/attseg_project_page/

</details>


### [284] [Neural Block-wise Auto-exposure for Single-Shot High Dynamic Range Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18733)
*Zhengguo Li,Chaobing Zheng,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出神经块级自动曝光算法，将临时图像及其曝光时间映射到16x16块的曝光时间，探索raw图像优于sRGB，可缓解BOR、块效应和细节丢失问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 块级变化曝光单张拍摄可捕捉HDR场景外观，但存在亮度顺序反转（BOR）、块效应以及高光/阴影区域细节丢失问题。现有自动曝光算法多用sRGB图像，但其亮度测量能力有限，难以准确学习高光/阴影块的曝光时间。

Method: 提出神经块级自动曝光算法，输入临时图像及其曝光时间，输出所有非重叠16x16块的曝光时间。临时图像可为sRGB或raw图像，并对比两种方案，重点探索raw图像因更优的亮度测量能力带来的优势。

Result: raw图像作为临时图像时，块曝光时间学习更准确，最终HDR成像系统在高光/阴影区域捕捉到更多细节。实验结果验证了所提神经块级自动曝光算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 神经块级自动曝光算法可有效解决BOR、块效应和细节丢失问题，采用raw图像作为临时图像能进一步提升块曝光时间预测精度和HDR场景细节还原。

Abstract: Block-wise varying exposures are effective for cap- turing appearance of a real-world high dynamic range (HDR) scene via a single-shot. However, brightness order reversal (BOR) artifacts, blocking artifacts and loss of details in highlight/shadow regions are possible issues for block-wise varying exposures. In this paper, a novel neural block-wise auto-exposure algorithm is proposed to address the issues. The proposed algorithm maps a temporary image and its exposure time to the exposure times of all non-overlapping 16 X 16 blocks. The temporary image can be either an sRGB or a raw image. Although the sRGB image is widely adopted in existing auto-exposure algorithms, exposure times of blocks in the highlight and shadow regions might not be accurately learned due to the limited radiance measurement capability of the sRGB image. This paper thus also explores the feasibility of temporary image as a raw image. Due to the better radiance measurement capability of the raw image, the exposure times of blocks are learned more accurately, and more details in the highlight/shadow regions of the real-world HDR scene are captured by the resultant HDR imaging system. Experiment results validate the proposed neural block-wise auto-exposure algorithm.

</details>


### [285] [Concealed Handgun Detection using WiFi Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18736)
*Erik Persson,David Gustafsson,Tomas Wilkinson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出利用WiFi信道状态信息(CSI)检测隐藏手枪，构建WiFire数据集和WiFireNet模型，验证了WiFi-based隐蔽武器检测的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统摄像头监控无法检测遮挡或隐藏的威胁，金属探测器要求逐人通过狭小通道，易造成拥堵且不适用于大流量人群，因此需要一种能应对遮挡、不受空间布局限制的补充性安全检测手段。

Method: 提出从WiFi信号中估计信道状态信息(CSI)来检测隐藏手枪；构建了WiFi信号与RGB图像对齐的WiFire数据集；并设计了利用视觉监督信号的深度学习模型WiFireNet。

Result: 实验结果表明，WiFi信号可以用于检测隐藏手枪，具备作为监控摄像头和金属探测器补充模态的潜力。

Conclusion: WiFi-based隐蔽手枪检测可行且有前景，作者公开了数据集、模型和代码以促进后续研究。

Abstract: While conventional camera-based surveillance systems and metal detectors can provide a sufficient level of security when used appropriately, they have fundamental limitations that reduce their applicability. Camera-based systems are unable to detect occluded or concealed threats and metal detectors require each person to enter through a narrow opening one at a time, leading to congestion issues and limited use in large crowds. A WiFi-based security system has the potential to be a complementary modality that is able to handle occlusions or concealment and is not hindered by strict spatial configurations. In this paper, we propose use Channel State Information estimated from WiFi-signals to detect concealed handguns. To this end we collect a dataset of aligned WiFi signals and RGB images that we call the WiFire dataset. We also propose a deep learning model called WiFireNet tailored to detect concealed handguns in WiFi signals using vision-based supervision signals. Our results show that concealed handgun detection is possible in WiFi and has promise as a complementary modality to surveillance cameras and metal detectors. We release the dataset, model and code to facilitate the further research into WiFi-based handgun detection.

</details>


### [286] [Context-aware Bayesian Fusion for Multimodal Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18741)
*Arunachalam Venkatachalam,Mhafuzul Islam,Marwan Yusuf,David Van Hamme,Ben Stoffelen,James Foresi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种上下文感知的多模态后融合方法，通过轻量门控网络结合概率校准框架，无需重训练即可在恶劣环境下动态调整预训练检测器输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶需可靠检测行人与道路使用者，但在恶劣天气、低光照下预训练检测器和车载传感器性能显著下降。且为每种场景重训练模型面临数据、算力和时间的巨大限制。

Method: 在概率校准框架内集成轻量门控网络的多模态后融合方法。门控网络提取环境上下文，据此动态调制预训练目标检测器的检测输出，无需重训练检测器。在修改版KITTI数据集上评估，对相机图像和激光雷达点云进行不同雾浓度增强。

Result: 相比非自适应后融合基线相对提升约14%，在不同雾浓度下实现稳定的平均精度56%。

Conclusion: 上下文感知的自适应后融合能有效缓解恶劣环境对检测性能的影响，且无需重训练即可提升鲁棒性，为自动驾驶安全检测提供实用方案。

Abstract: Reliable detection of pedestrians and road users is essential for the safe operation of autonomous vehicles. However, this task becomes increasingly challenging under adverse environmental conditions, such as poor weather and low lighting, where the performance of state-of-the-art pre-trained object detectors and on-board sensors tends to degrade, which limits their effectiveness and reliability. Moreover, retraining models for each operating context imposes significant constraints due to high data requirements, computational resources, and time. Considering this, we propose a novel context-aware multimodal late fusion approach that integrates a lightweight gating network within our probabilistic calibration framework. The gating network extracts environmental context and dynamically modulates the detection outputs of pre-trained object detectors based on the inferred context, without retraining of the detectors. Our methodology is evaluated on a modified KITTI dataset, in which the camera images and lidar pointclouds are augmented for varying fog levels. Experimental results show that our approach yields an approximately 14% relative improvement over non-adaptive late fusion baselines, achieving a consistent average precision of 56% across different fog levels.

</details>


### [287] [LayerCanvas: Infographic Generation in AI Learning Assistants via Hybrid Layer-Set Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18763)
*Keshav Gupta,Jeremy Owen,Christopher Lloyd,Shaunak Mishra,Manav Sharma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LayerCanvas 提出一种混合方法，将 SVG 作为语义、结构与排版程序，用扩散模型美化非文本内容。它把 SVG 元素划分为 z 序、互不重叠的元素集合，通过并行通信的 RGB 生成流生成可独立编辑并确定性重组的分层图像，文本仍由矢量元数据渲染，从而兼顾丰富视觉合成、精确排版与局部编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线学习平台需要 AI 助手生成个性化信息图，但自动生成有效信息图很难，需兼顾视觉丰富性、精确排版、结构化布局与可继续编辑性。现有方法各有局限：SVG 保留几何、层级和文本但美学灵活性有限；栅格扩散模型外观更丰富却把内容和布局压平成单图；用栅格化 SVG 条件化扩散模型也无法解决，因为栅格只保留大致位置，丢失元素归属、遮挡关系和可修改重组所需结构。

Method: LayerCanvas 保留 SVG 作为语义、结构和排版程序，用扩散模型仅对非文本内容进行美学化。不同于生成单张压平栅格，它将 SVG 元素划分为 z 序排列、彼此不重叠的元素集合，并通过相互通信的并行 RGB 生成流处理。所得图层可独立编辑并可确定性重组，文本则从保留的矢量元数据渲染。

Result: LayerCanvas 在文本保真度上显著优于最先进的栅格图像生成器，同时取得最高的整体美学质量。论文还展示了非破坏性内容编辑、本地化、素材复用和动画等能力。

Conclusion: LayerCanvas 通过 SVG 语义结构与扩散美学化相结合的分层生成方式，在保持排版准确和可编辑性的同时提升视觉质量，为信息图自动生成提供了兼具丰富合成、精确文本和局部编辑的可行方案。

Abstract: Online learning platforms are increasingly deploying AI assistants to provide personalized, on-demand tutoring. These can leverage infographics to communicate complex information and improve the learning experience. However, automating effective infographic generation remains a challenging task, requiring a combination of visual richness with precise typography, structured layouts, and continued editability. Existing representation methods satisfy these goals only in part: Scalable Vector Graphics (SVG) representations preserve geometry, hierarchy, and text but provide limited aesthetic flexibility, while raster diffusion models produce richer appearances by flattening content and layout into a single image. Conditioning a diffusion model on a rasterized SVG does not resolve this conflict because the raster preserves approximate placement but discards element ownership, occlusion relationships, and the structure needed to modify and recompose the result. We introduce LayerCanvas, a hybrid approach that retains SVG as the semantic, structural, and typographic program while using diffusion to aestheticize non-text content. Rather than generating one flattened raster, LayerCanvas partitions SVG elements into z-ordered sets of mutually non-overlapping elements and processes them through communicating parallel RGB generation streams. The resulting layers are independently editable and deterministically recomposed, while text is rendered from preserved vector metadata. Our approach combines rich visual synthesis with accurate typography and localized editing. LayerCanvas substantially improves text fidelity over state-of-the-art raster image generators while achieving the highest overall aesthetic quality. We further demonstrate non-destructive content editing, localization, asset reuse, and animation.

</details>


### [288] [MIST: Multi-Scale Spatial Context for Gene Expression Prediction from Histology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18769)
*Koushik Howlader,Tirtho Roy,Md Tauhidul Islam,Wei Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出MIST，一种多上下文空间Transformer，通过在每层加入局部k近邻注意力和切片全局汇总两条上下文路径，从H&E图像预测空间基因表达，在多个器官队列上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer对spot做自注意力却忽略spot在组织中的空间位置，而spot的分子状态依赖多尺度的组织上下文，因此需要引入空间上下文建模。

Method: MIST在自注意力主干每层增加两条路径：(1)坐标不变的局部路径，在空间k近邻spot间做注意力；(2)切片路径，将全切片摘要广播到每个spot。模型单次前向即可预测所有spot。坐标仅通过成对距离进入，使局部路径在图像特征固定时具有平移、旋转和反射不变性。

Result: 在HEST-1k六个器官队列的队列内评估中，10/50/100基因面板的平均Pearson相关分别比最强基线提升9.7%、11.6%、11.4%，且平均MSE和MAE最低；留一器官迁移提升6.0%；表达衍生域恢复最佳（各聚类分辨率下ARI和NMI最高，84%留出切片NMI超过最接近基线），并重现了选定癌症标志基因的空间表达模式。

Conclusion: 在普通Transformer主干中加入局部与切片上下文是组织学预测空间基因表达的有效方法。

Abstract: Predicting spatial gene expression from standard H&E images could extend molecular analysis to tissues without spatial transcriptomic measurements. A standard Transformer applies self-attention over spots but ignores where each spot sits in the tissue, whereas a spot's molecular state depends on tissue context at multiple spatial scales. Building on this insight, we introduce MIST, a Multi-context Spatial Transformer that augments the self-attention backbone with two context pathways applied in every layer: a coordinate-invariant local pathway that attends over spatial k-nearest-neighbor spots, and a slide pathway that broadcasts a whole-slide summary to every spot; the model predicts all spots in a single forward pass. Because coordinates enter only through pairwise distances, the local pathway is invariant to translations, rotations, and reflections when image features are fixed. In intra-cohort evaluation across six human organ cohorts from HEST-1k, MIST improves mean Pearson correlation over the strongest evaluated baseline by 9.7%, 11.6%, and 11.4% for 10-, 50-, and 100-gene panels, respectively, with the lowest average MSE and MAE. MIST further improves leave-one-organ-out transfer by 6.0% over the next strongest baseline, attains the best expression-derived domain recovery (highest mean ARI and NMI at every clustering resolution, and higher NMI than the closest baseline on 84% of held-out slides), and reproduces spatial expression patterns of selected cancer-marker genes. Together, these results show that adding local and slide context to a vanilla Transformer backbone is an effective approach to spatial gene expression prediction from histology.

</details>


### [289] [The Federated Tumor Segmentation and Classification (FeTS++) Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18771)
*Wenyi Tang,Carla Pitarch-Abaigar,Siddhesh Thakur,Verena Chung,Konstantinos Koukoutegos,Alexandros Karargyris,Holger R. Roth,Spyridon Bakas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FeTS++ 2027是FeTS挑战赛的新一届，基于BraTS 2025多机构MRI数据，新增分类任务并迁移至NVFlare框架，聚焦联邦学习下的分割与分类方法评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机构集中式数据面临隐私与协作障碍，联邦学习可在数据本地保留下训练模型；既有的FeTS挑战赛需要更大规模、更多样化队列及更真实的验证环境来推动医学影像联邦学习方法发展。

Method: 基于BraTS 2025 Lighthouse的临床多机构多参数MRI数据（涵盖胶质瘤与脑膜瘤，来自北美、欧洲、亚洲和非洲机构），搭建于NVIDIA FLARE平台；Task 1评估机构级分割模型的知识聚合策略，Task 2在联邦协议下进行胶质瘤与脑膜瘤分类（实体标签与来源站点高度混淆）；按模型效用与效率感知评分排名。

Result: 论文摘要主要介绍挑战赛设计，尚未给出实验或评测结果。

Conclusion: FeTS++ 2027通过扩展队列、新增分类任务并采用NVFlare基础设施，深化了真实世界联邦学习的验证，为医学影像联邦方法提供新的基准平台。

Abstract: International challenges have become the standard for developing and benchmarking biomedical image analysis methods using centralized multi-institutional data. Federated learning (FL) describes a paradigm-shift on multi-institutional collaborations, where models are trained across institutions while their data are always retained locally. The Federated Tumor Segmentation (FeTS) challenge, first held in 2021, was the first challenge dedicated to FL in medical imaging and editions in 2022 and 2024 pushed the frontiers of real-world method validation. FeTS++ 2027 returns after a two-year gap with a substantially larger and more diverse cohort, and with the two "pluses" that name the challenge: an official classification task alongside segmentation, and a move of the infrastructure to NVIDIA FLARE (NVFlare). Built on the clinically acquired, multi-institutional multi-parametric MRI data of the BraTS 2025 Lighthouse challenge, FeTS++ spans gliomas and meningiomas across patient cohorts from institutions in North America, Europe, Asia, and Africa, and focuses on ranking submitted methodological approaches according to their model utility and an efficiency-aware score. Task 1 asks participants to find the best strategy for aggregating knowledge from per-institution segmentation models, while Task 2 asks them to distinguish glioma from meningioma under the same federated protocol, in which the entity label is heavily confounded with the site of origin.

</details>


### [290] [GAD: Geometry Attention Distillation for Consistent Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18779)
*Junfeng Ni,Ziyu Zhu,Fan Jiang,Yu Liu,Chiyu Wang,Jiawei Zhang,Zhaoxu Sun,Mu Xu,Siyuan Huang,Yixin Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Geometry Attention Distillation (GAD)，将冻结几何模型的注意力对应关系蒸馏到视频扩散模型的注意力中，在多个视频backbone上提升长程场景一致性和时序一致性，且不增加推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型能生成视觉上吸引人的短视频序列，但难以在时间上保持场景几何和物体结构。作者发现当前视频注意力无法识别跨帧可靠对应关系，响应分散在多个区域，而可靠跨帧关联对随场景结构形成的信息交换至关重要。

Method: GAD将冻结几何模型注意力中的对应关系迁移至视频注意力。对应关系从干净视频中提取，连接跨帧的同一静态表面或运动物体。由于结构形成在高噪声时始于深层、低噪声时始于浅层，因此根据噪声时间步选择学生层进行注意力蒸馏。

Result: 训练后视频注意力更一致地聚焦对应位置。在三种视频backbone上均提升了长程场景一致性和动态时序一致性，几何增益随生成长度增加。在Wan2.2 5B上，GAD在两个长程基准上将跨帧匹配准确率相对Wan基线提升至少30%。

Conclusion: GAD无需额外推理模块即可获得上述增益，为高效且一致的视频世界模拟提供了有前景的方法。

Abstract: Video diffusion models generate visually compelling short-term sequences but often struggle to preserve scene geometry and object structure over time. In this paper, we first demonstrate that current video attention cannot identify reliable correspondences across frames, with responses dispersed across multiple regions even after recognizable scene structure has emerged. We argue that reliable cross-frame associations are essential for information exchange as scene structure forms in video generation. Thus, we introduce Geometry Attention Distillation (GAD), which transfers correspondences from a frozen geometry model's attention to video attention. Extracted from clean videos, these correspondences link the same static surface or moving object across frames. Since structure formation begins in deeper layers at higher noise and shallower layers at lower noise, we apply attention distillation at a student layer selected according to the noise timestep. After training, video attention focuses more consistently on corresponding locations. Experiments across all three video backbones demonstrate improved long-range scene consistency and dynamic temporal consistency, with geometric gains across generation lengths. On Wan2.2 5B, GAD improves cross-frame matching accuracy by at least 30% over the Wan baseline on both long-range benchmarks. Notably, GAD achieves these gains without additional inference modules, offering a promising approach to efficient and consistent video-based world simulation. Project page: https://fantasy-amap.github.io/GAD/.

</details>


### [291] [Trust Before Autonomy: Rethinking Agentic Remote Sensing for Crisis Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18789)
*Georgios Angelidis,Aggelos Psiris,Spyridon Evangelatos,Thomas Lagkas,Panagiotis Sarigiannidis,Iraklis Varlamis,Georgios Th. Papadopoulos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文主张EO智能体的自主性应"靠表现挣得"而非默认赋予，并通过对12项agentic RS/EO/GIS基准研究的元分析，证明现有证据只支持辅助性角色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前agentic遥感的评测只关注能回答多少问题，而危机响应真正依赖的是答案可靠性以及可安全交托给智能体的决策比重；能力分数无法反映可信度、校准与弃答行为。

Method: 对12项经同行评审的agentic RS/EO/GIS基准研究做证据性元分析，检查重复运行、可弃答任务与校准度量；并据此提出三项EO专属信任属性、面向危机EO任务的自主性阶梯，以及在上线前回放存档应急响应的就绪协议。

Result: 仅1项重复运行、3项含不可答任务、无一项测量校准；任务成功比工具调用正确率高出至多54个百分点，与专家轨迹的差距随轨迹长度从7pp扩大到56pp，能力与弃答无关（Spearman r为-0.25至0.50，均不显著）。

Conclusion: 按可靠性、认知诚实与可验证性授予自主性的立场成立；就现有证据而言，agentic EO智能体只应承担辅助性角色，尚不足以支持自主决策。

Abstract: Agentic remote sensing is evaluated on how many questions it can answer, whereas crisis response depends on whether its answers can be relied on and how much of the decision can safely be handed to the agent. This paper argues that \emph{autonomy should be earned, not assumed}: An Earth-Observation (EO) agent should be granted autonomy in proportion to demonstrated reliability, epistemic honesty and verifiability. The position is supported by an evidential meta-analysis of twelve agentic RS/EO/GIS benchmark studies from peer-reviewed venues. Only one repeats runs, three contain unanswerable tasks and none measures calibration. Their own tables show that completed workflows are often not correct (task success exceeds tool-call correctness by up to 54pp on identical queries), that the gap to an expert trajectory widens overall with trajectory length (from 7 to 56pp), and that capability does not predict abstention (Spearman r from -0.25 to 0.50, all n.s.). The paper then contributes three EO-specific trust properties, an autonomy ladder for crisis EO tasks, and a readiness protocol that replays archived emergency activations before any live use. In this context, current evidence justifies assistive roles only.

</details>


### [292] [Registration is a Representation Problem: Solving Whole-Body PET/CT Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18804)
*Neel Dey,Adrian V. Dalca*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出利用互补的解剖表示（anatomix基础模型的细粒度体素语义 + 现成分割模型的粗粒度标签）驱动GPU加速迭代配准求解器，结合多阶段混合框架与病灶体积保持策略，在Learn2Reg 2026 PSMAReg挑战赛中夺冠，且无需任务特定训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始图像强度作为图像间相似度代理效果差；辅助分割标签虽提供语义身份，但只能赋予粗粒度身份，且往往只覆盖部分结构（尤其在全身场景下），难以满足精细解剖匹配需求。

Method: 使用3D基础模型anatomix提取跨体积一致的细粒度逐体素语义表示，并结合现成分割模型获取粗粒度解剖身份；这些表示驱动GPU加速迭代求解器FireANTs，采用混合多阶段框架——第一阶段从密集多通道特征估计对齐，第二阶段利用anatomix与分割损失精化形变；同时开发针对病灶的分量级体积保持方案，防止配准引入非生物性肿瘤体积变化。

Result: 在Learn2Reg 2026 PSMAReg挑战赛排行榜中取得第一名，且无需任何任务特定训练。

Conclusion: 使用互补解剖表示进行纵向全身PET/CT配准是有效的，可实现最先进性能；病灶体积保持对避免非生物性体积变化至关重要。代码与可视化见http://www.neeldey.com/reps-for-reg。

Abstract: Raw image intensities are a poor surrogate for similarity across images in correspondence problems. Further, while auxiliary segmentation labels can provide semantic identification and improve matching, they can assign only coarse identities to fine-grained anatomy and often cover only a subset of structures, particularly in whole-body settings. We present an approach that instead establishes correspondence using complementary representations of anatomy and demonstrate its utility for longitudinal whole-body PET/CT registration in the Learn2Reg 2026 PSMAReg Challenge. Our 3D foundation model, anatomix, is used to derive fine-grained, per-voxel semantic representations that are consistent across volumes, and we further employ off-the-shelf segmentation models to obtain coarse anatomical identities. Together, these representations drive a GPU-accelerated iterative registration solver (FireANTs) in a hybrid multistage framework: the first stage estimates alignment from dense multi-channel features, after which a second refines the deformation using both anatomix- and segmentation-based losses. Lastly, because registration should not introduce non-biological tumor volume changes across time, we develop lesion-specific component-wise volume preservation schemes. Without any task-specific training, our system won first place in the PSMAReg Challenge leaderboard. Code and interactive visualizations are available at http://www.neeldey.com/reps-for-reg.

</details>


### [293] [DuplexBridge: Coordinating Understanding and Generation for Unified Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18819)
*Yidi Liu,Jie Xiao,Chaojie Mao,Jingfeng Zhang,Xueyang Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DuplexBridge用统一模型自身的理解能力为其生成能力增强提示，通过在理解与生成角色间建立源锚定桥与互信门控，实现可分别监督的联合适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂图像编辑需同时具备忠实的任务理解与准确的执行，但现有方案在编辑失败时无法区分是理解还是生成出错，导致责任不可归因、监督信号混淆。

Method: 提出DuplexBridge：无需外部理解模块，用统一模型自身理解为生成增强提示。源锚定桥明确目标、末态变化与保持约束；理解仅以对原始请求的忠实度监督（不看生成图），生成以实际收到条件的执行情况监督；互信检查选择性将全局任务反馈注入各角色，同时保留独立有效的局部监督。并构建CE-Bench基准（1200个任务，衡量任务导出的变化与保持要求的联合满足）。

Result: 三种模型配置下，完整系统较直接编辑器提升完全合规率21.75–25.75个百分点；在KRIS-Bench、RISEBench、ImgEdit、GEdit-Bench上也有增益。以同一监督Wan-Image初始化，门控联合训练达77.08%完全合规，无门控为70.92%，而生成局部监督改查原始任务且保留双门控为75.00%。

Conclusion: 结果支持为理解与生成分配独立的局部责任，并采用选择性共享反馈来实现联合理解-生成适配。

Abstract: Complex image editing requires faithful task interpretation and accurate execution, but a failed edit does not reveal which responsibility failed. We present DuplexBridge, which uses a unified model's own understanding capability to enhance prompts for its own generation capability, without an external understanding module. A source-grounded bridge specifies targets, final-state changes, and preservation constraints. Understanding is supervised on fidelity to the original request without observing the generated image; generation is supervised on execution of the condition it actually receives. Reciprocal trust checks selectively admit global task feedback to each role while retaining independently valid local supervision. We introduce CE-Bench, a 1,200-task benchmark measuring joint satisfaction of task-derived change and preservation requirements. Across three model configurations, the full system improves complete compliance over direct editors by 21.75-25.75 percentage points, with gains also observed on KRIS-Bench, RISEBench, ImgEdit, and GEdit-Bench. From a common supervised Wan-Image initialization, gated joint training reaches 77.08% complete compliance, compared with 70.92% without gates and 75.00% when generation-local supervision instead checks the original task with both gates retained. These results support distinct local responsibilities and selective shared feedback for joint understanding-generation adaptation.

</details>


### [294] [OmniCompass: A Holistic Benchmark for Omni-Modal Video Grounding and Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18829)
*Xuyang Liu,Shihao Wang,Xinyu Wei,Minghan Li,Zeqing Wang,Zuhao Yang,Zichen Wen,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OmniCompass，一个全模态视频定位与理解整体基准，含VL与AV两条评测轨道，每轨含时间/空间定位与问答三任务，覆盖短中长视频，50个MLLM评测显示开源模型落后且定位仍具挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频理解基准仅覆盖部分评测维度，无法系统诊断全模态视频理解能力，因此需要能同时评估视觉与视听联合证据、且覆盖定位与问答多维度能力的整体基准。

Method: 定义两条评测轨道：OmniCompass-VL评估视觉语言模型（需视觉证据），OmniCompass-AV评估全模态模型（需视听联合证据）。每条轨道含时间定位(TG)、空间定位(SG)、问答(QA)三类互补任务，分别考察目标何时出现、在哪、发生什么。QA在VL下设5个感知与3个推理类别，在AV下设3个感知与2个推理类别。VL含820视频2460实例，覆盖多领域及短/中/长三种时长；其叙事与音频丰富子集构成AV（240视频720实例）。每个实例在纯文本与文本+图像两种配对设定下评测，用文本及视觉语言指导指定同一目标。所有实例均人工标注并多轮质量验证。

Result: 对50个MLLM的评测显示：开源模型仍落后于专有模型，尤其在视听理解方面；即使领先模型在精确时间与空间定位上仍具挑战；其预测在配对目标设定间仍存在不一致。

Conclusion: OmniCompass提供了覆盖全模态、多任务、多时长及配对设定的一致性视频理解评测框架，揭示了当前MLLM在视听理解与时空定位上的明显不足，为后续模型诊断与改进提供系统化依据。

Abstract: With the rapid development of multimodal large language models (MLLMs), evaluating their video understanding capabilities has become increasingly important. Existing video understanding benchmarks cover only part of the evaluation dimensions, limiting systematic diagnosis of omni-modal video understanding. We introduce OmniCompass, a holistic benchmark for omni-modal video grounding and understanding. OmniCompass defines two evaluation tracks: OmniCompass-VL assesses vision-language models on tasks requiring visual evidence, while OmniCompass-AV assesses omni-modal models on tasks requiring joint audio-visual evidence. Each track includes three complementary tasks: temporal grounding (TG), spatial grounding (SG), and question answering (QA), assessing when a referenced target appears, where it is, and what happens. QA covers five perception and three reasoning categories in OmniCompass-VL, and three perception and two reasoning categories in OmniCompass-AV. OmniCompass-VL contains 820 videos and 2,460 instances across domains and three duration types: short, medium, and long. A narrative- and audio-rich subset forms OmniCompass-AV with 240 videos and 720 instances. Each instance is evaluated under two paired settings: text-only and text+image, specifying the same target through text and visual-language guidance. All instances are manually annotated with multi-round quality verification. Evaluations of 50 MLLMs show that open-source models still lag behind proprietary counterparts, particularly in audio-visual understanding. Precise temporal and spatial grounding remains challenging even for leading models, and their predictions still exhibit inconsistencies across the paired target specifications.

</details>


### [295] [Game2Real-Bench: Do Toy Games Reveal and Transfer to Real-World Visual Reasoning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18834)
*Qingan Liu,Juntong Feng,Yue Zhu,Yujie Cheng,Xinzhe Han,Shuai Hao,Yunzhi Zhuge,Haiwen Diao,Huchuan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Game2Real-Bench基准，研究程序化游戏任务与现实世界视觉推理能力间的对应关系，并给出数据集与迁移实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM视觉推理评测依赖真实数据或程序生成任务，但尚不清楚玩具游戏中的能力是否对应真实世界推理，以及游戏样例能否支持迁移。

Method: 构建覆盖细粒度感知、空间推理、约束推理和序列规划四类能力的基准：1000个可执行验证的程序生成游戏样本 + 400个按共同推理需求（而非视觉相似）对齐的真实样本；评测25个代表性VLM，并在4个模型上做one-shot游戏到真实迁移研究；另构建约24K验证游戏样本、18个任务、65个QA家族的Game2Real-Dataset。

Result: 25个VLM上游戏与真实表现高度一致，Pearson与Spearman相关均达0.935；one-shot迁移中，约束推理在多个模型上提升，而细粒度感知随游戏示范下降。

Conclusion: 游戏任务可作为真实世界视觉推理的有效代理，但迁移效果因能力类型而异，需针对不同能力设计迁移策略。

Abstract: Visual reasoning is an important capability for vision-language models (VLMs) to understand visual evidence, spatial structure, constraints, and state changes. Existing evaluations mainly rely on real-world data or procedurally generated tasks. Real-world data preserve natural complexity, while procedural games provide controllable generation and verifiable solutions. However, it remains unclear whether abilities measured in toy games correspond to real-world visual reasoning and whether game examples can support transfer. We introduce Game2Real-Bench, a benchmark that studies these connections across four capability groups, including fine-grained perception, spatial reasoning, constraint reasoning, and sequential planning. We construct 1,000 procedurally generated game samples with executable verification and curate 400 real-world samples aligned by shared reasoning requirements rather than visual similarity. We evaluate 25 representative VLMs and observe strong game-to-real consistency, with Pearson and Spearman correlations of 0.935. We further study one-shot game-to-real transfer on four models. Constraint reasoning improves for several models, while fine-grained perception declines with game demonstrations. We also construct Game2Real-Dataset with nearly 24K verified game samples across 18 tasks and 65 QA families. Our benchmark, dataset, and code are available at https://github.com/QingAnLiu/Game2Real-Bench.

</details>


### [296] [Plan-Bench: Can Existing VLMs Go Well Beyond Perception to Reason and Plan in Embodied Worlds?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18836)
*Juntong Feng,Qingan Liu,Jingru Dong,Yue Zhu,Weiming Zheng,Yujie Cheng,Xinzhe Han,Shuai Hao,Yunzhi Zhuge,Haiwen Diao,Huchuan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Plan-Bench基准，在隔离动作执行影响的情况下评估VLM面向具身环境的高层推理与多步规划能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在视觉感知与空间推理上表现优异，被寄望作为具身AI的高层规划器。但物理任务需要整合空间信息、推理时序状态并在多种约束下生成多步计划，当前VLM能否独立于执行可靠地支持这类推理与规划尚不明确。

Method: 构建Plan-Bench，覆盖三个互补维度、16个子类、699个精选样本，隔离动作执行的影响；对28个VLM进行广泛评估，并开展规模效应与消融研究。

Result: 评估显示模型仍有提升空间，最强模型仅达75.0%准确率；模型规模扩大总体上提升相关能力；消融表明更大模型作为高层规划器的潜力递增。

Conclusion: Plan-Bench为评估VLM面向具身环境的推理与规划能力、及其作为物理任务决策者的潜力提供了轻量级探针，基准与代码已开源。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have achieved strong performance in visual perception and spatial reasoning, leading to growing interest in using them as high-level planners for embodied AI. However, physical tasks require models to integrate spatial information, reason over temporal states, and generate multi-step plans under various constraints, raising the question of whether current VLMs can reliably support such reasoning and planning independently of execution. We introduce Plan-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating the high-level reasoning and multi-step planning capabilities of VLMs relevant to embodied worlds, while isolating the influence of action execution. It covers three complementary dimensions, spanning 16 subclasses and 699 curated samples. Extensive evaluation of 28 VLMs reveals room for improvement, with even the strongest model achieving only 75.0% accuracy. We further find that model scaling generally improves these capabilities. Ablation studies also indicate larger models show increasing potential as high-level planners. Overall, Plan-Bench provides a lightweight probe for evaluating VLMs' reasoning and planning capabilities relevant to embodied environments and assessing their potential as decision makers for physical tasks. Our benchmark and code are available at https://github.com/MinneFeng/Plan-Bench.

</details>


### [297] [RepProbe: Can Video Language Models Count Repeated Actions? A Diagnostic Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18844)
*Mahsa Khoshnoodi,Sarah Adel Bargal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RepProbe基准（42K视频），发现视频语言模型能编码重复结构却无法在输出中可靠报告计数，失败主要发生在输出阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频语言模型虽能流畅描述动作，却无法可靠统计重复次数，作者欲探究该计数失败的具体机制与位置。

Method: 构建含1-20次完整重复的42K真实与合成视频片段基准，控制片段位置、时间覆盖和时长；用线性探针与模型输出对比，并分析表示与输出的关系。

Result: 高计数时准确率崩溃，且非帧采样所致。表示中计数信息仍可线性解码，但输出集中于少数偏好数字，形成表示-输出解离；该现象在真实与合成视频上均成立。

Conclusion: 模型能编码重复结构，但未能在输出中可靠报告，失败主要源于输出阶段而非表示阶段。

Abstract: Repetition counting requires identifying and enumerating action cycles over time. While state-of-the-art video language models describe actions fluently, they fail to reliably count how many times an action repeats. To investigate this limitation, we introduce RepProbe, a benchmark of 42K real and synthetic video clips containing exactly k in [1, 20] complete repetitions, with controls for clip position, temporal coverage, and duration. These controls allow us to study the counting failure directly and locate where in the model it arises. Counting accuracy collapses at high counts across the video language models we evaluate, and controlled experiments show this collapse is not explained by frame sampling alone. We compare model outputs with linear probes trained on the final-layer representations used to generate those outputs. We find a consistent representation-to-output dissociation: substantial count information remains linearly decodable even when models fail to report the correct count. Further analysis shows this failure arises primarily at the output stage, where answers concentrate on a small set of preferred numbers rather than the available count information. The dissociation holds on both real and synthetic videos and is not explained by clip duration, position, or scene content. These results indicate that models encode repetition structure but do not reliably report it in their outputs. Project page: https://arenaa.github.io/RepProbe-webpage/

</details>


### [298] [The Last Remote Sensing Model Built by Humans? Casting Any Earth Observation Task as Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18845)
*Sijie Zhao,Zeqi Zhou,Heli Qi,Xuanchao Fu,Zhuo Zheng,Junshi Xia,Junjue Wang,Weihao Xuan,Naoto Yokoya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种新范式：将可验证的EO任务抽象为harness（智能体可用的工具/资源）与verifier（成功判定），让通用编程智能体自主递归构建并改进EO模型，在光学、SAR与多光谱时间序列四类基准上超越SOTA，且仅用约1%标注即保持大部分精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球观测（EO）模型目前仍依赖人工构建：每个新任务需专家设计训练配方并在基准上反复调优，过程耗时且难以迁移。作者希望验证这一人工迭代回路能否交由智能体完成，从而将小样本学习、跨域适应等原本分立的研究问题统一为harness与verifier的选择。

Method: 把任意可验证任务表示为harness（定义智能体可使用的工具与资源）加verifier（定义成功标准）。EO任务的验证可通过标注基准、独立传感器、物理模型或同一地点的重复观测实现。仅提供这两部分，通用编程智能体即可自主构建EO模型，并基于自身输出递归改进。

Result: 在涵盖光学、SAR与多光谱时间序列影像的四个EO基准上，智能体构建的模型超越当前最先进水平，并且仅使用约1%的训练标签即可保持大部分精度。

Conclusion: 确立了EO的新范式：人类负责设计harness与verifier，智能体负责构建模型，使EO模型能随智能体能力提升而持续改进。

Abstract: Earth observation (EO) models are still built by hand. For each new task, experts design a training recipe and tune it against a benchmark. We show that this loop can be handed to an agent. Any verifiable task can be cast as a harness and a verifier, where the harness defines what the agent may use and the verifier defines what counts as success. Every EO task can be made verifiable, through labeled benchmarks, independent sensors, physical models or the repeated observation of the same place. Given only these two components, a general-purpose coding agent builds the EO model and improves it recursively on its own outputs. Settings long treated as separate research problems, from learning with a handful of labels to adapting across domains, become choices of harness and verifier. On four EO benchmarks spanning optical, SAR and multispectral time-series imagery, agent-built models surpass the state of the art and keep most of that accuracy from about 1% of the training labels. This establishes a new paradigm for EO, in which people design harnesses and verifiers and agents build the models, so EO models improve as agents do.

</details>


### [299] [WorldRecon: Generative Multi-View Mesh Reconstruction for Large-Scale 3D World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18846)
*Mengchen Zhang,Yang Li,Chunshi Wang,Tong Wu,Chunchao Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorldRecon是一个从无位姿多视角RGB图像重建大规模完整3D场景网格的生成式多视角重建框架，基于TRELLIS.2并引入分块生成与邻居感知潜变量修复，在合成与真实室内场景上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从无位姿多视角图像重建大规模场景的完整高质量网格，需在忠实几何基础上合理补全未观测或稀疏捕获的表面，现有方法难以兼顾几何精度与场景级连贯性。

Method: 先用重建模型从输入图像估计相机位姿和粗糙含噪全局点云作为全局脚手架，并划分为重叠空间块；为原生3D生成模型TRELLIS.2配备基于重建的条件架构，从局部点云裁剪和块相关图像观测生成每块（稀疏结构、细节几何、PBR材质）；引入邻居感知潜变量修复与因果世界生成流程，跨重叠块传播结构、几何与材质上下文。

Result: 在合成和真实室内场景上均达SOTA，Chamfer距离分别降低14.9%和43.1%，F-score分别提升17.3%和12.0%，展现强sim-to-real泛化能力。

Conclusion: WorldRecon实现了可信且完整的生成式大规模场景重建，验证了分块生成与跨块上下文传播在扩展多视角生成式重建中的有效性。

Abstract: Reconstructing complete, high-quality meshes of large-scale scenes from unposed multi-view images requires faithful geometric grounding while plausibly completing surfaces that are unobserved or only sparsely captured. We present WorldRecon, a generative multi-view reconstruction framework for recovering complete, large-scale 3D scene meshes from unposed RGB images. WorldRecon uses a coarse, noisy global point cloud and camera poses estimated from the input images by a reconstruction model as a global scaffold for whole-scene generation and partitions it into overlapping spatial blocks. To robustly leverage these imperfect geometric priors, we equip the native 3D generative model TRELLIS.2 with a dedicated reconstruction-based conditioning architecture that generates each block from its local point-cloud crop and block-relevant image observations, producing sparse structure, detailed geometry, and PBR materials. To maintain scene-level coherence, we introduce neighbour-aware latent inpainting and a causal world-generation procedure that propagates structural, geometric, and material context across overlapping blocks. Together, these designs scale multi-view generative reconstruction to large-scale scenes, yielding coherent, unified meshes. Extensive experiments demonstrate that WorldRecon achieves state-of-the-art reconstruction performance, reducing Chamfer distance by 14.9% and 43.1% while improving F-score by 17.3% and 12.0% over the strongest baselines on synthetic and real-world indoor scenes, respectively, thereby demonstrating faithful and complete generative reconstruction as well as strong sim-to-real generalization. Project page: https://kszpxxzmc.github.io/WorldRecon-website/.

</details>


### [300] [PixelMoE: Scaling Pixel-Space Diffusion with LLM-Style Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18857)
*Ruixiao Dong,Jianmin Bao,Bozhi Luan,Zijie Meng,Xin Tao,Pengfei Wan,Kun Gai,Li Li,Houqiang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PixelMoE 将标准 LLM 风格 MoE 迁移到像素空间扩散，实现稀疏容量扩展，在 ImageNet-1K 上取得 FID 1.47 (256²) 与 1.53 (512²)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素空间扩散模型无需学习 tokenizer，可直接建模原始图像，但现有扩展方式多为稠密策略，模型容量与激活计算量强耦合，扩展代价高。

Method: 采用标准 LLM 风格 MoE（Token-Choice 路由、细粒度路由专家、共享专家、基于 bias 的负载均衡），无需扩散专用路由机制或辅助路由目标；并提出 Pixel-Space Hierarchical Flow Matching，沿去噪轨迹改变空间分辨率，从全局结构到细节逐步精化。

Result: 在相同激活参数量下，PixelMoE 在各模型规模上均优于稠密版本；ImageNet-1K 类条件生成 FID 达 1.47 (256×256) 和 1.53 (512×512)，且未使用表示对齐或感知损失。

Conclusion: 标准 MoE 可有效迁移至像素空间扩散，实现稀疏容量扩展，性能可比肩领先的像素空间与潜空间生成模型。

Abstract: Pixel-space diffusion models provide an appealing alternative to latent-space diffusion models by directly modeling raw images without relying on a learned tokenizer. However, scaling pixel-space models has predominantly followed dense strategies, tightly coupling model capacity with active computation. In this work, we show that standard LLM-style Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures can be effectively transferred to pixel-space diffusion. Building on this finding, we introduce PixelMoE, a pixel-space generative framework that sparsely scales model capacity with standard LLM-style MoE, without diffusion-specific routing mechanisms or auxiliary routing objectives. Specifically, PixelMoE combines standard Token-Choice (TC) routing with fine-grained routed experts for input-adaptive sparse computation, a shared expert for capturing common representations, and a bias-based load-balancing strategy. To complement sparse capacity scaling, PixelMoE further introduces Pixel-Space Hierarchical Flow Matching, which varies spatial resolution along the denoising trajectory from global structure formation to fine-grained detail refinement. At comparable active parameter counts, PixelMoE consistently outperforms its dense counterparts across model scales, demonstrating effective sparse capacity scaling in pixel space. On class-conditional ImageNet-1K, PixelMoE achieves FID scores of 1.47 at 256x256 and 1.53 at 512x512 without representation alignment or perceptual losses, comparing favorably with leading pixel-space and latent-space generative models. Code and models will be released soon.

</details>


### [301] [Visual Tokens on Demand: Visual Budgeting for Scalable and Efficient Multi-Image Reasoning in Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18858)
*Aimon Rahman,Joymallya Chakraborty,Vidit Bansal,Abhishek Dan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出mmVB，一个轻量级即插即用模块，通过跨图像上下文为每张图像分配查询条件化的token预算，无需重训练基座模型也无需访问注意力矩阵，将视觉token减少9倍，在八个多图像推理基准上保持94.4%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型多针对单图像输入优化，多图像推理成本高（视觉token激增）。现有多数token压缩方法面向单图像（冗余主要是空间和局部的），而多图像推理面临跨图像冗余、证据稀疏分布、激进压缩丢失关键细节等新挑战。此外现有方法要么无需训练但推理低效（依赖注意力矩阵，与FlashAttention不兼容），要么需重训练基座模型。

Method: mmVB：轻量级即插即用模块，利用跨图像上下文为每张图像分配查询条件化的token预算，仅增加1.6M可训练参数，无需重训练基座模型，也无需在推理时访问注意力矩阵。

Result: 视觉输入token减少9倍，time-to-first-token快5倍，prefill FLOPs减少7倍，同时在八个多图像推理基准上保持94.4%的全分辨率准确率。

Conclusion: mmVB实现了可扩展的多图像推理与理解，在不重训练基座模型、不访问注意力矩阵的前提下大幅降低计算成本，同时基本保持准确率。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models have advanced rapidly, but most remain optimized for single-image inputs. Multi-image reasoning is costly because each image adds many visual tokens, increasing encoding and attention demands. Existing techniques that address the above issue, including visual token pruning, token merging, visual token compression, learned resampling, hierarchical compression, retrieval-augmented visual reasoning, and memory-based architectures, have largely been designed for single-image scenarios, where redundancy is primarily spatial and local. Multi-image reasoning introduces additional challenges: redundancy may occur across images, task-relevant evidence may be sparse or distributed, and aggressive per-image compression can remove details needed for comparison, counting, temporal reasoning, retrieval, or evidence grounding. Moreover, existing token-reduction methods are often either (i) training-free but inference-inefficient, typically relying on accessing attention matrices to get token-importance that may be impractical to access with memory-efficient attention implementations such as FlashAttention, or (ii) training-dependent, requiring the base MLLM to be retrained or adapted to a new compressed-token paradigm. In this work, we study scalable multi-image reasoning and understanding without retraining the base model and without accessing attention matrices at inference time. To this end, we propose mmVB, a lightweight plug-and-play module that assigns query-conditioned per-image token budgets via cross-image context, adding only 1.6M trainable parameters while reducing visual input tokens by 9x. This reduction yields 5x faster time-to-first-token and 7x fewer prefill FLOPs, while maintaining 94.4% of full-resolution accuracy across eight multi-image reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [302] [PLAYGROUND: Progressive Layout Generation with Render-Grounded Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18865)
*Jaeho Lee,Junhwan Heo,Junghyun Park,WonJun Moon,Eunju Yang,Seungho Jang,Seongchan Kim,Seungryong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PlayGround框架，通过渐进式设计循环动态适配当前画布，实现可编辑图形设计的布局生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于MLLM的方法采用迭代生成与视觉细化，但高层计划在执行前已预先确定，无法随设计演变自适应调整。

Method: PlayGround包含渐进规划器、布局生成器和视觉验证器，并由结构感知检索器引导：检索器先推断粗略布局结构以检索相似参考设计；每轮规划器结合当前渲染画布与剩余元素规划下一功能组及其构图，生成器预测可编辑布局属性，验证器评估并反馈。另构建两阶段数据生成流程，用于提取功能组和轮级规划轨迹以进行步级监督训练。

Result: 在Crello和LICA数据集上实验证明优于现有方法。

Conclusion: PlayGround通过渐进自适应规划提升了可编辑图形设计的布局生成效果，代码与权重将公开。

Abstract: Generating editable graphic designs requires arranging visual and textual elements into a coherent composition while preserving their content and editability. Although recent MLLM-based approaches employ iterative generation and visual refinement to reflect the real-world design workflows, their high-level plans are predetermined prior to execution, limiting their adaptability as the design evolves. To address this limitation, we propose PlayGround, a progressive layout generation framework that dynamically adapts to the current rendered canvas. PlayGround establishes a progressive design loop comprising a Progressive Planner, a Layout Generator, and a Visual Verifier, guided by a Structure-Aware Retriever. Given an initial set of unordered elements, the Structure-Aware Retriever initially infers a coarse layout structure to retrieve structurally similar reference designs. Subsequently, at each turn, the Progressive Planner examines the current rendered canvas alongside the remaining elements, forms the next functional group, and plans its composition. The Layout Generator then predicts editable layout attributes, while the Visual Verifier evaluates rendered candidates and provides feedback when refinement is necessary. To train the Progressive Planner with step-wise supervision, we construct a two-stage data generation pipeline that extracts functional groups and turn-level planning trajectories from completed designs. Experiments on Crello and LICA demonstrate the effectiveness of PlayGround over existing methods. Our code and weights will be publicly released.

</details>


### [303] [Dog-Breed Text-to-image Flow Matching from Pretraining to Fine-Tuning on 4 Consumer GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18870)
*Reyhaneh Esmailizadeh,Xingjian Leng,Zhanhao Liang,Liang Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MiniDog是一个可在4张3090 GPU上用55分钟完成扩散Transformer预训练、15分钟微调的轻量级框架，旨在降低该领域的入门门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人们普遍认为训练文生图扩散模型需要大量算力，这阻碍了初学者自行训练模型。作者希望降低入门门槛，让初学者也能训练自己的模型。

Method: 数据上使用ImageNet中2.6万张狗图片加caption用于预训练，用FLUX.1-schnell生成的2千张图片微调，并预计算缓存图文特征；架构上采用E2E-INVAE语义潜空间、22M参数小transformer、小型Qwen文本编码器和in-context conditioning；训练上使用iREPA和EUPE感知编码器加速。

Result: 在四张3090 GPU上可在55分钟内完成预训练，再花15分钟完成微调，并提供FID、HPSv2、PickScore及多种VAE重建指标的评估支持。

Conclusion: MiniDog证明用有限算力即可训练扩散Transformer，降低了文生图模型训练的门槛，适合初学者上手。

Abstract: We generally perceive that training text-to-image diffusion models requires heavy compute: it is true if we aim at a comprehensive frontier model. This understanding prevents people from training their own models when getting started in the field. We develop MiniDog, a framework that enables pretraining diffusion transformers in 55 minutes and fine-tuning in another 15 minutes on four 3090 GPUs. For data, we use 26k dog images from ImageNet and caption them for pretraining and use 2k images generated by FLUX.1-schnell for fine-tuning. We precompute the image and text features and cache them for efficiency. For architecture, we use E2E-INVAE for its semantic latent space, a small transformer with 22M parameters, a small Qwen model for text encoding, and in-context conditioning. For training, we use iREPA and an EUPE perception encoder for acceleration. For evaluation, our repository supports FID, HPSv2 score, PickScore, and various VAE reconstruction metrics.

</details>


### [304] [Cepheid: Decoupling Scale from Structure for Cross-Family Motion Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18871)
*Chih-Hai Su,Tsung-Wei Ke,Yu-Lun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 从稀疏顶点位移恢复稠密网格运动时，残差先验跨几何族泛化差、且残差幅度随观测加密而跨族剧烈变化。本文解耦残差结构与物理尺度，学习无量纲先验。统一模型在所有评估族（含未见的布料与腿式身体）上超过调和扩展，且无需骨架、配准或跨网格对应，可直接泛化至动物点跟踪视频与机器人抓取软体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏到稠密的运动补全中，在单一几何族上训练的残差先验，往往在跨族场景下不如无参数的调和扩展；跨族训练还面临缩放障碍：残差幅度在不同几何族间相差一个数量级以上，并随观测变密而趋于零。该尺度依赖未知的目标运动，测试时无法直接测量，因此需要一种与物理尺度无关的先验。

Method: 将残差结构与物理尺度解耦：学习一个无量纲的残差先验，从可观测量中估计振幅幅度，并用仅在训练源上拟合的轻量级校正项进行修正。

Result: 统一模型在所有评估的几何族上均超过调和扩展，包括未见的布料与腿式身体；无需骨架、配准或跨网格对应。同一先验无需重训练即可应用于点跟踪的动物视频与机器人抓取软体场景。

Conclusion: 通过解耦残差结构与尺度并学习无量纲先验，实现了跨几何族稳健的稀疏到稠密运动补全，具备良好的泛化能力与免重训练的迁移性。

Abstract: Sparse-to-dense motion completion recovers dense mesh motion from sparse vertex displacements. Residual priors trained on one geometry family frequently fail to beat parameter-free harmonic extension off-family. Moreover, cross-family training meets a scaling barrier: residual amplitude varies by more than an order of magnitude across geometry families and collapses as observations become denser. Since this scale depends on unknown target motion, it cannot be measured directly at test time. We decouple residual structure from physical scale by learning a dimensionless prior, estimating amplitude from observables and a lightweight correction fitted only on training sources. Our unified model surpasses harmonic extension across all evaluated families, including unseen cloth and legged bodies, without skeletons, registration, or cross-mesh correspondence. The same prior applies without retraining to point-tracked animal video and a robot grasping a soft body. Project page: https://su-terry.github.io/cepheid-website/

</details>
